IRFAN ALI NABAZAGA sunan shi Kenan kakanshi NABAZAGA bafullatanin bazabarme ne kamar yada mukasani fulanin ma kala kala ne akasar Niger a Baban birnin NIAMEY a wani yanki da akecema BALLEYARA iyayenshi acan suke kasancewar azamin da ba'abama boko karfiba yasaka diyawa basa saka yaransu a boko to haka takasantce ga iyayen NABAZAGA su biyu iyayensu suka Haïfa shine baba ,saï kanninsa mai suna JAURO tunsna kanan iyayensu suka rasu sukabarsu ahannun kanin mahaifinsu kasancewar shi kanin babansu bai taba samun haihuwa ba yasaka yarikesu da amana duk dakarancin ilimi awancan zamani haka baihanna yaturasu makaranta bokon ba da islamiya saï dai shina NABAZAGA yana tsananin son boko sabanin kaninshi jauro dayatsani boko yafiso yyi kiyo dabobin da iyayensu suka bari Wanda sunbarmusu dukiya mai tarin yawa ahaka suke tafiya in anturasu jauro saï yace ma yayanshi yyi GABA shiyana zuwa dayatafi kuma bayasake dawo saï dai yabi mutananen da suke musu kiwo ahaka NABAZAGA yakamala primary dinsa zaishi collège sanin cewa babu collège anan garin yasaka kanin babansu yamishi cukucuuku ganin cewa yana son karatun yakaishi can wata makaranta tagwanmnani a nan Baban birni na NIAMEY makaranta kwanace ahaka yake karatunshi har allah yasa yakamala collège din dasakamako mai kyau yanada kwakwalwa sosai kulum shi yakecin nadaya dayake collège din ne kuma saï yayi wani jarabwa kuma yaci ahaka yasamu yyi yatafi aikin lawyer yyi jarabawa kuma yasamu yatafi inda yayi karatun lwayensgi yafuto Baban lawyer mai zaman kanshi alokacin yanada shekara ishirin da tkwas to bayan yafuto yaje gida hakika dan uwanshi jauro yyji dadi dashi da iyayensu shikuma jauro y'a cigaba da kulamusu da dabobinsu ,Kasu tille Abokin kanin mahaifinsu ya'nada ya'ya biyu fatima da shatu inda suke tsananin kaunarsu to alokacin ne kuma kanin babansu yanema musu auren fatima da shatu inda suka kasanxe kyawawan gaske alokacin fatima nada shekara sha shida itakuma shatu nada sha hudu ahakan Alabama NABAZAGA fatima shikuma jauro akabashi shatu dama awanxe lokacin yarinya iyayenta suke mata zaben miji to wata daya akasaka tunda dama shi NABAZAGA a can NIAMEY yake aiki to zaikoma kafin lokacin auren da kwana uku saï yazo ahaka lokaci yeketa tafiyar har yau biki inda aka daura auren abisa sadaki mai tsada irin nafulani aka kai kowace gidanta NABAZAGA aka kaimishi fatima axan NIAMEY DIN awata unguwa mai suna banifandu kusa dawata kasuwa da'akecema marché bonkaney ahaka shinma jauro suke zaune anan da dankewayensy Wanda aka gyarashi tsaf aka saka shatu cikinsshi dukan masayon soyaya suke nuna wajuna kowa yana tatalin kowa duk dadai ba'auren soyaya sukayi ba ana haka fatima yasamu ciki inda itama shatu take dauke danata ama akwai tserawa wata uku ahakan har lokacin aihuwa fatima tasantalo danta fari Tass mai kama d ubansa sunyi murna kwarai ranar suna yaro yaci sunan ALI kasancewar sunan mahaifinsu gareshi suka sakamishi Abbi yataso cikin gâta da kaunar iyayenshi ....🖊

tagabatar dakanshi tace yaya bazan boyema ba ina sonka tun ranar danafara ganinka naji ina son kazama uban ya'ya na saï da ta ida bayaninta sanan yanisa yace" kiyi hakuri banada ra'ayin zama da mace fiyeda dya"yana gama fadar haka yakashe wayarshi bayan kwanan biyu kwatsam saigashi yazoma zainab damagarshi aure zai kara badan tagazba koda wani Abu zaiyine sabida sunnar ma'aik ce allah sarki zainab mai hakuri kawai saï tace allah yasanya alkairi jikinshi yyi sanyi domin yasan doli zataji ciwo shikaran kanshi wllhi baisan miyasaka yakeson auren ba haka kawai lokaci daya yaji son auren (kankara)tafiyar awa daya da rabi sukayi kafin suka isa Baban makaranta wada tundaga ganin makarantar kasan dataci kudi waw suka furta suduka banda IRFAN dayace "tasarki yataba ga uban gijin talikai" zuwa hostels din tafiyar minti ashirin ce sauko sukayi suna karema makarantar kallo komi yayi daidai kayansu akadoka aka wuce dasu inda zasu zauna kowane daki dama mutum biyu ne aciki IRFAN wucewa akayi dashi daki mai lamba 38wanda aciki dama dalibi dayane saishi IRFAN dayazo yanzu ,d'à salam yashiga dakin nasu "wa'laikum salam " aka amsa masa "mai kulada daliban ne yace " Samir ga bako nan ma mutum duk d'à daturanci yayi maganar ama shi yaji komi tunda dama turanci kai ba'amagan hannu irfan yabashi suka gaisa "Samir din ne yace" sannu d'à zuwa dan Uwa sunana SAMIR kuma nima dan kasar da matsalarta kar ta tunaresu tako aminci suna zauna lfya itada mijinta shekararsu biyar da aure sanan allah yabasu haihuwa inda suka samu mace suka sakama nasreen ,nasreen nada qhekara hudu allah yayima iyayen Malan rasuwa zasukai ziyara akauyensu dake kusada THAOUA' allah sarki abban yayi kuka sosai haakama mama kasancewar suna tsananin kaunarta kmr dai Umar din to bayan rasuwarsune abba yatataro iyalinshi yakoma zinder din kasancewar yabar yan Uwa acan saidai yanakaimusu ziyara ,shekara nasreen hudu sanan allah yakara basu wata indasykatasamata rufaida  saï bayan shekara uku sanan kuma sukasamu Imran auta iyensu suna tsananin kaunarsu mama kuwa iyayenta daransu kuma tana tafiya dubasu duk da abunan basu canjamata fuskaba sudai kartayarda abunda abban yakemata tatunkarasu su nasreen ma sunazuwa can kuma kakaninsu suna kaunar su ahaka yaran sukatashi cikin kaunar iyayensu suna zaune adan gidansu Wanda abba ne yagina musu mai danki uku aciki da bayi daya dayan nasu abbane dayan kuma nasreen ce da rufaida dayan kuma kayan tarkacensne shima Imran kayansa anan suke dakinsu nasrren din

Nasreen ce saké da wata atamfah wada takarbeta masha Allah saï dan kalabi datayane akanta ,doctor kuwa saké yake da bakin wado da blue din riga saï rigarshi ta likitoci dayahauda daga sama wada masha Allah tayimishi kyau yafuto a Doctor shin.

Daga gefe kuma su Abbi ne dasu Mama suna kalon yada zata taka 
Wasu Nos ne guda uku tsaye kusada Nasreen wada take zaune bisa keken guragu saï IRFAN dayake gefenta

Karafunan yafuda mata Wanda wurin debewar tasha kuka sa ihu saï Allah yasakamata takefadi Wanda zafin hakan ne ya SATA fadar haka ,koda yake dama Nasreen akwai tsiwa lol🤣🤣..

Su Abbi duk sun tawsayamata bare rufaida da la maman sarkin tausayi har kuka sukayimata diyawa sun tawsayamata danma likita ce ansan zafin ciwo duk Wanda zai wuce saï dai yayi mata fatan ..
Tsawon minti talatin sanan aka kare kuka takeyi duk fuskar ta tajike sharkab da ruwan hawaye kai harda majina lol😜

Bayan ankarene yace ma Nos din sukamata ta tamike taimaka mata sukayi tamike dafarta daya ,dayan kuma mai ciwon taki ajeta bayan ita akeso tataka 
Umarni yabata data fara taka kafar ama ina kukanta kawai takesha tana Harar gefe .
Ganin zata batamasa lokaci yasashi cewa"ke malama kitaka kafar nan inbaso kikeyi naciremiki itaba" cikin tsawa yakarasa hana zabgamata harara 

Abbi ne yamatso yakama hannunta daya ,dayan kuma ya umarci IRFAN dayakama shi .
Badan yaso ba yakama itakuwa ganin Abbi ne yasakata yin karfi ta aje kafar dan takawa tayi saï kuma tadauke kafar tana hawaye

Abbi ne yace"yawa yaya kara takawa zakisaba ai nasan yata mai kokarice "jin yabonda da Abbi yakemata kuma bata so wanan mugun yaciremata kafar yasakata jin karfi batasan sanda tataka har sau uku ba.

Tapa mata su charifa sukayi ganin irin yabata din dasuke yasakama tacema Abbi yasaketa zata iya haka kuwa yasaketa ,shikuwa IRFAN tun kafinma takarasa yasaketa Wanda badan Abbi bai saketaba datafadi 

Tatakawa takeyi su sharifa na natapa mata .wani dutse ne tadan taka Wanda yasakata yin baya zatafadi rumtse idonta tayi jin wani irin zafi sanan ga faduwar datasan bamakawa sai tayi da karfi ta runtse idonta ,
Sukuwa su Abbi dagudu sukaje dan tareta sai dai kash...

Itakuwa jin takai seconde talatin batajita kasaba ysakata Budé idonta  Doctor tagani rungume da ita dumu dumu kamr wata matar shi .
.
Kalonsu Abba yayi yana wani tunani..

Itakuwa ganin su Abba na kalonsu yasakata tadan tureshi ,Wanda shikuwa IRFAN wani  irin yajishi jin yarungumi abunda sukafi komi daga mishi hankali jin tana kokarin kwace kanta yasashi sakinta yana wani zabgamata harara ama baya barin su Abba sugani ,

Shikuwa ganin yada su Abba da Abbi sukedan fira ,saï Ammi da Mama suma sunadan zantawa bisa ciwon nata .
Su shaarifa ko saï tapa ma Nasreen dinsuke suna dariya tsaki yaja babuwanda yajishi yajuya yayi tafiyarshi office dinsa ,zama yayi bisa kujera mai jujuyawar nan yana tunanin fah inyaci gaba dazama haka wlh cutuwa zaiyi,Ruwa yadoko a firgen dinsa Wanda yake shake da kayan fruits da ruwa  sai jus ,ruwan yakurba yana mai sauke ajiyar zuciya ,yakai minti talatin sanan yaji kwankwasawar kofa izini yaba Wanda bakowa bane face Abba da Abbi

Tashi yayi tsaye domin girmamawa garesu zama sukayi sanan shi yazauna

"Doctor yanzu ya'ake ciki gameda patient dinmu " cewar Abbi 
Wani papier yadoka yayi rubutu yakai minti uku sanan yamika ma Abbi ,
Kanshi nakasa yafara fadin"dafarko Alhmdllah zance saboda jiki yayi kyau dama kafarce kuma ahida karafunan so yanzu tarika tatakawa kadan kadan zata saba ahaka incha Allah kuma duk bayan sati uku zatarika zawa ana gasamata ita incha Allah komi zaiyi daidai"

Yakarashe fadar hakan yana mai don kalon wayarshi data fara ringing bai dokaba saima kashewa dayayi ,Abba ne yace" gaskiya ba'abunda zance daku sai Allah yabiyaku ,awanan zamani da talaka ba'abakin komi yakeba ,mai kudi yataka talaka yakashe rai karshe yayi tafiyarsa KO ansan shine ba'adokar wani mataki,nagode sosai Allah yasaka da alkairi BARRISTER Na-san duk tarbiyarka ce "

Abbi ne yace"haba Malan ai anzama daya mune da godiya kam danmu muka fara laifi ,Allah dai yabata lfya "da Ameen duk suka amsa banda IRFAN daye latsa wayarshi ,mikewa sukayi shima yabisu abaya koda sukazo Nasreen din tadan zauna asaman keken guragun motoci biyune su Nasreen daya su Abba daya gida akawuce dasu Nasreen din ,suma su Abbi nasu gidan suka wuce .

Doctor kuwa ciki yakoma yaci gaba da aikinshi wayarshi sai kira keshigo wa yaki dagawa ma ahaka sai biyar yatashi gida yawuce yayi wanka yadoki daya daga cikin wayar yaci gaba da aiki ,yadan jima yana aiki sanan yaji Kiran Kakarshi fatima yagani doka yayi  hade da salama yana fadin" yadai uwar gida ko kina kewata ne nazo, dan naga hajiya karama tafiso na (cewa da matar jauro ) fadamin naji" yafada yana dan murmshi 

Jin kamr anyi shirune yasakashi fadin "kinajina kuwa uwar gida" dan nisawa tayi tace."inajinka mai gida ,kakan kune ba lafiya jikin yamatsa,kuma sai sunan ka yake kira "

Takarashe fadar hakan muryarta da dan rauni ...
Shikam dama mafarkin dayakeyi da kakan nashi yasan ba lfya ,duk da in yakira yana cemasa lfya klw yake ama har cikin zuciyarshi dama bai yardaba ,doli ma yaje dubo kakanin nashi.

Jin shirun yayi yawa yasakata cewa"kar kafawa Abbin ku Na-san shi da rashin juriya bare akan babanshi danhaka karkanuna masa komi"

"OK jibi zanbiyo jirgi incha Allah ,dan Allah ki kula dashi sosai " cewar IRFAN din 
"To babu laifi su jauro ma ai duk sunzo ".
Masha Allah yace " duk la gaishemi. Dasu "cewar DOCTOR.
Kashe wayar yayi ,jin anfara Kiran sallah yasashi doro alwala yanufi masallaci,bai dawo ba sai bayan Kiran isha'i ..

Koda yazo bangarenshi yawuce dan yana so yayi buking din jirgi a internet mai zuwa CAPITAL DIN NIAMEY direct ,yakoyi sa'a yasamu sai dayatabatar komi yayi dai dai sanan yakira Ammi yace" Ammi a aikomin da abinci na nan ina wani aiki ne bazan samu damar ci ading ba yaw"
"OK" tace masa bayan kamr minti goma yaji ana kwankwasa kofa ,izin yaba .
Al'moctar ne doke da wasu kuloli masu shegen kyau salama yayi daganan yagaishe da yayanshi ,shikuwa IRFAN ko kalonshi baiyi ba yana latsa wayarshi yace"ya karatun ,fatan kana adu'oin koda yaushe duk da bansanka da wasa da addini ba"

"Inayi yaya incha Allah, kuma karatun Alhmdllah" OK yace 
Shikuma yaci gaba da aikinshi ,shikuwa ganin yamasa shiru yasan bazai saké maganarba fita yayi yakoma part dinsu ..

Shikuwa docteur abincin yadan tsakura dama bawata yunwa yakeji ba ,aikinshi yaci gabadayi saï karfe daya na dare sanan yasake wanka yafeshe jikinshi da turare yasaka kayan barccinsa,adu'aa yayi sanan yalulube abinshi ,can cikin barcci yafara fadin"oh yes ahhha washhh dadi wai ,baby dadi wayo durinki dadi ahh wayo zaizo baby ,zaizo ahhh gayanan yehhhh wayo burana zaici dadi ahhh baby fuck me .ahhh ,"gwatso yake yi shi kadai bisa makeken gadonsa saï zufa ke karyo masa duk dasanyin climatiseur din dayake dakin..
 daga gani kasan mafarki yakeyi kuma mafarkin ma nacin mace lol su Doctor manya🤣😜

Yakai minti ashirin ahaka saï can yace"oh dadina washhh ahh kibari nakara baby kinji"

Dan Jim yayi sanan yace "OK "
Yakai minti uku sanan yayi wata irin zabura yana adu'aa saï zufa ke karyomishi "inallilahi lahawlah walakuwati illah-billah" yafada dafe kanshi yayi yana tunanin wanan wane irin mafarki ne yasan ba tantama yabata wandonshi ,tsaki yaja yace"gaskiya wlh dasake " 🤣🤣🤣🤣kunaji doctor fah

Book 1
Page 15
Koda yasauka Jauro yatura adaukoshi wani cousin dinsa ,yana zuwa kuwa dama naganeshi sabida ai sunada number juna shine dai bayda wani lokacinsu ,gaisawa sukayi bayabo babu falasa ,tafiyar minti talatin da biyar yakaishi ,masha Allah famille sunyi murna daganinshi,dakin NABAZZAGA aka kaishi ,konce yata rardashi yadan kishingida hajjo na kusan shi ,tana dama masa fura 

Da salama yashiga dakin,NABAZZAGA naganishi yafara kokarin Mike wa ,da Sauri yazo yarungumeshi sundan jima ahaka sanan yace"maraba da jika nagari sannu da zuwa"cewar NABAZZAGA 

murmushi yayi Wanda yake futo da tsananin kwarjininshi yace"maraba da kaka na gari abin son ko wane jika"yafada yana sunbatar goshinsa .

"Naji dadi ganinka Allah yadafamaka adukan lamuranka,ya kareka da kariyarsa"

Da ameen hajjo ta ansa dan dukowa yayi kusanta kasancewar ita tana saman kujerun ne masu laushi dafa kanshi tayi tana tanbayr ya yabaro yan uwanshi.

Itama albarka tasaka masa .tsohuwace tashigo wato matar jauro tanafadin "dan nema ni konema na bakayi ko" 
"Haba amaria ta taya zanmanta dake ,jira nake uwar gida ta salameni sai naje wurinki" yakareshe fadar haka yana rungumeta .

"Oh oho na'aza kamanta dani nama saki biyu" cewar tsohuwar kyakyawa Duk data tsufa ama kyawunsu nan masha Allah.
Itama dai albarka tasakamishi tnamai masa fatan alkairi dashi da yen uwansa gabadaya


Jauro ne yashigo yace"ai sai kubar Abokin nawa yahuta haka ko" kasancewar dama sungaisa tun awaje 

Daki da'aka kaishi jauro yanunamasa 
Shikuwa yadawo sukaci gaba da hirarsu .


Nasreen kuwa yaw wani bala'i tatashi dashi yaw,jitake Duk Wanda yamata maga jitake kamr tamamakeshi ,rufaida ce tashigo tace "Anty nazo kibiyamin karatun nawa ,wlh yaw maths ne kuma kinsan bana ganewa" haranta tayi tace"mtsss ke zo kiyi tsalan kodo anan" 
Kalonta Rifaidan tayi tace"Anty kiyi hakuri inamiki wani Abun kinji" wata tsawa tabugamata Wanda yasakata fara tsalen kwadon.
Dama tasan Duk randa zata fara ganin al'adarta ranan da bacin rai taketashi .

Rufaida kuwa baiwar Allah sai nishi take hadawa tana kuka kasa kasa dan tasan idan ma Mama taji ta iya cewa laifi Tamata,kuma da'bi'ar gidansu ne baba bayama yaro fada Abba ko Mama susabaki sai dai in abin yayi ywa

Hararata tayi tace"bacemin dagani "aikuwa dagudu tafuce tana kukanta.

Itakuwa Nasreen kuka tafashe dashi tarasa ina zata saka kanta

Tana cikin kukan taji zuuu akasanta tasan shine yazo ,kukanta tacigaba dayi takai minti goma sanan ta tsagaita tadoki audugar mata tadoki sabon pant bayi tawuce tasake sabon wanka jikinta tagyara tsaf ,dakin tadawo tashafamai tasaka wata duguwar riga ta atamfah masha Allah tayi kyau sosai ,tanagama taji cikinta yamurda dama tasan randa take farawa da randa take karewa saitasha azaba,wani lokacin har suma take.

Abba babu asibitin dabaikaita ba idan ma anyi goji sai ace lfyarta kalw ,akwai wani Doctor ma dayacemusu inba aure akamata bazata dainaba 
Abba yayarda da maganr Docton ama ba yanzu zaima Nasreen aureba dan alokacin tanada shekara goma sha biyu ne .

Ahaka dai sukenema mata na galgajiya shima dai sai ahankali ,to idan yatasamata wani lokacin sai dai taganta agadon asibiti da Karin ruwa

Yaw ma haka takasnce konciya tayi adan madaidaicin gadansu tana murkususu kuka tafara tana Kiran Abba .
Mama ce tajiyota tace"ciwon ne halan ,Allah yanunamin lokacin dazaki yi auren nan indai shine samun lafiyarki"
Wani dan magani data ansomata tabata tasha Allah da ikonshi ko ko minti biyar batayi ba tafara Hama ,dan dama datafara haman nan hartayi so hudu to cikin zaidaina sai barcci.

Yanzuma barccin ne yadawketa .ajiyar zuciya Mama tayi tana tunanin gaskiya zata samu Abba tamasa magana yakamata suma Nasreen auren nan indai zata samu lfya tunda dama ansha zawa tanbayar aurenta sune dai suke kiyawa sunaganin batakaiba.

Yanzu kam lokaci yayi dazata nunawa Abba hakan...

_🍂"ALƘALAMINMU🖋️ ƳANCINMU"🍂_


Book 1
Page 16
Yau kwanan Doctor biyu da zuwa ,kuma Nabazzaga da sauki, yau da la'asar zaune suke a falon Uncle MUHAMMAD k'anin Abbi shi yake bi mishi sai Anty Mariam in baku manta ba 'kanin Abbi ne uwarsu 'daya uba d'aya.

Sai Uncle Imran ,Rabi'ou y'ay'an Jauro sai Malik wato dan Uncle MUHAMMAD shima kusan sa'an IRFAN ne amma ya bashi shekara d'aya ,kasancewar shima miskilin ne kamar IRFAN ya saka ta su ta zo d'aya kaf a cikin cousin d'inshi .

Nabazzaga ne shigid'e yana d'an nishi ,kalonsu yayi yace "ina so duk ku bani Hankalinku anan "

"Da farko Malik ka bud'e mana taro da addu'a "

Adu'aa yayi suka shafa sannan Nabazzafa ya fara magana kamar haka "da farko abunda zance shine nagodewa Allah ,d'a ya bani tsawan Rai har na zo wannan lokacin ,na ji dad'i da Allah ya bani y'ay'a kamar ku masu ladabi da biyaya, Allah ya albarkaceku gaba 'daya daga ku har iyalanku" dan nisawa yayi sannan ya ci gaba da fad'in "hakika mun taso Ni da d'an uwana marayu ,amma duk da haka kawunmu ya rik'emu amana kuma Alhmdllah ,ga 'dan uwana nan shi ka dai ya rage mun ina fatan ko bayan raina ku cigaba da d'aukansa tamkar mu biyu ne Na-san zai iya ,idan kuma shima rai yayi halinsa to kuji tsoron Allah kuzartar da hukunci Allah kamar yanda yake a tsakanin ya'yanku da al'ummar da suka rataya a wuyanku" dan tari ne ya sarkeshi ruwa Jauro ya miko masa ya kur'ba sannan ya ci gaba da fad'in 


"IRFAN hakik'a na san ka kasance d'a mai ladabi kamar mahaifinka ina so kowa da yake wurin na yaji wasiyi ci na da zan bari na san zaku cika mini al'kawarina,kayi min alkawari gaban iyayenka da ubangijin ka cewa zakayi min abunda na umarceka, IRFAN naso na ga aurenka amma na san ba lalle ba ne,ina so na baka umarni da ka yi aure nan da wata  biyu ,idan ba haka ba ina umarta ka daka auri y'ar uwarka Mufida wato 'yar kawunka Imran,kowa ya shaida matu'kar bai kawota ba nan da wata biyu ko bayan raina ku cika mi ni alk'awarina ,"

Dan nisawa yayi ya kalli IRFAN d'in da ya shiga wata duniyar tunani ,shi ina zai samu matar aure nan da wata biyu kuma har su fahimci juna ,kuma wai in bai kawo ba za'a bashi wata ko miye ma sunanta oho"

Murya Nabazzaga yaji yana fad'in "IRFAN ba za ka yi ba ne naji kayi shiru?"
Hannun Kakan nashi ya kama yana fa'din "mi zai Hana kuwa Abokina na ,kuma ai zaka samu lfya insha Allah cuta ba mutuwa ba, za ka ga kama kunanka ba ma aurena ba " ya kareshe fadar haka yana mai rungume kakannashi .


Shima rungumeshi yayi sun ɗan jima a haka sannan ya sakeshi yace "Allah ya albarkaci rayuwaka Allah ya baka zaman lfiya da iyalanka" duk da ameen aka ansa ,jin Kiran sallar la'asar ya sakasu mik'ewa suka wuce massalaci bayan sun dawo ne shi kuma Doctor d'akinshi ya wuce .

Wanka yayi ya shirya cikin wata jallabiya ruwan toka masha Allah ta masa dan karan kyau ,yana cikin fesa turare yaji Kiran Samir dauka yayi suka gaisa yake tambayarshi jikin kakan nasu yace "da sauki Alhmdllah ,amma akwai damuwa fah"

Ya fad'a yana dan zama bakin gadon "wallahi Samir na dauki abunda yafi k'arfina" cewar IRFAN yana mai dama Samir d'in maganarsa sukayi .

Wata dariya Samir ya kece da ita yace "waw wannan irin angwanci haka ,Masha Allah angon wata biyu" ya fad'a yana wata irin dariyar shakiyanci .

Ganin shakiyancin Samir ya ke masa ya sakashi kashe wayarshi yana Jan tsaki

Computer d'inshi ya dauko ya ci gaba da aiki sai da yaji ana Kiran sallah ya dauro alwa'wala ya wuce massalaci bai dawo ba sai da akayi sallar isha'i.

D'akinshi ya wuce ya kira Abbi suka gaisa,bayan sunkare wayar ne yawuce b'angaren Abbi .



Nasreen kuwa sai bayan sallar la'sar tafarka da wani irin mafarki surutai take tana fadin "wallahi nikam ba na sonka sai wallahi Abba kar ka auramin shi mugu ne azzalumi" kuka ta fashe dashi wanda ya saka Mama tayi saurin zuwa d'akin tana fadin "subhannallahi ke Nasreen tashi miye haka ,Budé idonki" ta fad'a tana jijigata .

Budé idonta tayi tace" lahawla wala kuwati illah billah" 
Kalon Mama tayi tace" ki tashi dama baccin la'asar ai ba da'di"

Waje Mama ta wuce .ita kuwa Nasreen tsaki ta ja tana wasu suruntanta Wanda ita kadai ta san me take fadi .


IRFAN kuwa da salama ya shiga falon ,gaban shine yayi wani irin faduwa ganin uncle d'inshi Muhammad na hawaye ,saï sauran uncle din nashi da suma suke zubda nasu hawayen ,Jauro ya kalla ya ga yanda yake rungume da Nabazzaga da babu alamoun numfashi atatare dashi ,"inallilahi ,subhannallahi" cewar Irfan d'in yana mai 'karasawa kusa da Jauro yana rungumo Kakan nashi ,wuyanshi ya ta'ba yaji babu alamar Rai atatare dashi,bai san sanda hawaye suka sulalo akan kyakkyawar face d'inshi ba .

Hannunshi yaji an rike Hajjo ce girgiza mishi kai kawai ta ke yi ,Wanda idan kakali kyakkyawar fuskarta za ka ga har yanzu kyawunta na asali yana nan sai dai yanayin sufa da ya kamata, rungumeta yayi yana fad'in"kar kice min ya tafi ,Ashe dama wasiya yake bamu d'azu ,meyasa bai jira na cika mishi burinsa ba? "Cewar IRFAN d'in yana mai k'ara rungumeta .



Dan bubbuga bayanshi takeyi alamar rarrashi tace "kowa baya wuce lokacinsa ,muyi ha'kuri a yanzu addu'ar mu kawai yake buk'ata " .

Sakinshi tayi ta maida hankalinta ga kaf ahalin Wanda suke wurin tace "haba yaya Jauro kai ne fah Babba kaine ya kamata ka lallashemu haba ,kuyi ha'kuri Babanku addu'ar mu kawai yake buk'ata ,shine gatan da za ku nuna masa " ta fada wasu hawayen suna sulalo mata Allah sarki dan Adam ba a bakin komi yake ba .

Ambulance tazo aka tafi dashi hospital d'in domin akai shi gidan sanyi .

Duk Wanda ya kamata ace ya sani duk iyalanshi wanda ma suke nesa gobe zasu karaso,Sai da Abbi tsohuwa ce tace masa "yazo kaf shi da iyalanshi ,ta fada masa lfya klw" amma Abbi ai shi ba yaro bane ya fahimci wani Abu jikinshi kuma yayi sanyi.


Ko da akayi sallar subahi mokota da manyan-manyan kasar duk sun iso sabida Nabazzaga mutimin mutane ne barema talakawa 

Fadar irin mutanan da suka halarcci zana'izarshi bata lolaci ne kai har SEMI président ISSOUFOU MAHAMADOU ya zo ,Abbi yayi kuka daya iske wanan babban rashin da yayi amma addu'a kawai zasuyi masa shine gatan da zasu nuna masa.

"Allahu akbar" Sai dai muce Allah ya jikan Nabazzaga .

Yau kwana uku da rasuwar shi,kasancewar ya bada wassiyi cin cewa idan ya mutu kwana uku kawai za'a yi masa na zaman makoki ,kowa ya watse duk mai son yi masa addu'aa to ya aika masa ita KO gidansa ne zata riskeshi .

Anyi masa sauka al'kur'ani da sadaka ,y'a y'an sama sundaw al'washin Gina masa massalaci banda Wanda yayi akala sun kai biyar damun Allah ya kai lada a kabarinsa,wato sadakatil jariya Kenan zasu masa.

Zaune suke kaf famille din daga yara har manya Malik din ne dai ya bu'de taro da addu'aa bayan haaka Jauro ne ya fara bayani kamar haka "da farko ina mi'ka godiyata ga UBANGIN AL' ARSHI Wanda rayuwa da mutuwa take hannunsa ,ina so kusani duk Wanda ku ka ga ya tafi lokacinsa ne yayi bai yi Sauri ba,mu kuma da mukayi Sauri ba mu yi jinkiri ba, dan haka ina so kusani munyi babban rashi ,amma mi zamu masa addu'ar mu ita yafi buk'ata " dan nisawa yayi ganin yada Abbi yake zubda hawaye kalonsa ya mai da gareshi yace"haba Ali ka dau hakuri kana Baba ai kaine za ka ma kannanka fad'a ,Ashe idan mutum ya ara maka Abu ,ya dawo ya karbi abunsa ba za ka mayar masa ba?" 

"Allah aro ya bamu kuma ya karb'a shi ai ya cika da kalma shahada muna masa kyakkyawan zato,muyi fatan idan ta mu ta zo mu cika da imani" ya fad'a yana mai jinjina kansa .

Da ameen suka ansa ,sanan yace"ya fada min cewa idan akayi ukun mutuwarsa na fadawa kowa yaje gidansa daga can kowa yayi masa addu'ar zata iskeshi,Allah ya muku al'barka gabaki dayanku,Allah ya abarkaci zuri'arku". Da ameen suka amsa suna mai masa godiya da zumar gobe duk zasu watse saï in sunzo sadkar arba'in .


IRFAN ne zaune a harabar gidan yana shan iskansa da jus dinsa a hannu sai wayarsa dayake latsawa ,wani uban hone yaji Wanda ya sakashi Jan tsaki ,bai ida ba aka saké wani ,kasancewar daga inda yake duk yana ganin komi ya sakashi kafa idonsa ya ga wane marashi hankali ne ,inba marass hankali ba ka zo gidan mutuwa kana zubama mutane irin wanan haukar" mtsss"ya sake Jan tsaki.

Mai gadin ya dan zagane shiya saka bai yi Saurin bud'ewa ba, ya san mutum gudane mai wannan irin haukar cewar mai gadin ,budewa yayi wata bakar mota tasako kai acikin harabar gidan  baka jin komi sai karar kidi da yake tashi ,ankai minti uku kafin Wanda yake cikin motar ya futo .

Wasu fararen kafafu ya ga sun sauko sanye dawasu takama bakake masu dan karan tsini ,wata farar budurwa na ga ta sako wada akala shekarunta bazasu wuce 25year ba, farace Tass kamr yanda kaf ahalin yawancinsu fararene sanye take da wani dogon wando baki gine saï bakar Riga tayane kanta da bakin kalabi ,idonta sanye yake dawasu bakaken glasse saï hanunta dayake doke dabakar jaka ,doguwace bata da jiki kamr kabusata tafadi ,kasancewarta fara saï shirin yayi mata kyau sosai fitowa tayi tana karkada clé (makullin motar ta )tana tafe tana taunar amusugel(cingam) kai tsaye part d'in da zai sadata dana Bazzaga tawuce kasancewar tasa ba zuwa gidan .

Shi kuwa Doctor daga can ya kare mata kalo sosai wani uban tsaki ya ja ya cigaba da abunda yake   .

Tafe IRFAN d'in yake yana waya falon Nabazzaga ya shigo zai bawa su Abbi Samir ya musu gaisuwa bai lura ba sai jin yayi yayi karo da mutum Wanda ya saka wayarshi ta fadi kass kakeji ta tarwatse ,Wanda bakowa bane sukai yi karo dashi face wannan budurwar ta dazu .
Ita kuwa ganin kyakkyawan handsome gabanta Wanda kamshin turarensa ya ƙara hargitsata yasakata tsegege tana kalonsa ,Wanda shikuma IRFAN wani irin haushinta dayaji ya kamasa Wanda ya sakasa zabga mata marin daya sakata dawo hayacinta .

"Tu es folle(ke mahaukaciya ce) wannan wane irin rashin wayo ne " ya fada yana wani irin zabga mata kalon kaskanci ,MALIK ne ya zo wucewa ya ga yanda sukayi cirko cirko ga wayanan akasa screen d'in duk ya fashe ,kalon budurwar yayi sanin cewa ko ya tanbayi IRFAN d'in ba fad'a masa zai yi ba ya sakashi cewa"MOUFIDA  lfya miye yake faruwa anan ?"yayi tambayar yana kalonta ganin yada fuskarta tayi ja abunka ga farar fata,

Shi kuwa DOCTOR bai bari ya karasa tanbayar taba ya doki fassasa wayarshi yayi gaba abunsa dama shi yarinyar tun lokacin da take zuba hone yaji ta bashi haushi "mtssss " ya ja tsaki ya shige falon.

Ita kuwa wada aka kira da Mufida hawaye take yi tana fa'din "yaya Malik wlh ban gani bane na bugeshi wayar ta fadi amma shine ya zabga mini mari" ta fada tana mai kara volume d'in kukanta ,hak'uri malik d'in ya bata ,ita kuwa motarta taja tana mai tuanain tayada zata gano wayeshi ,gashi ta ga yana mata kama da uncle Ali.


Yau jirginsu IRFAN yadaga zuwa ZINDER ,dan gaba daya kowa yawatse sai HAJJO da TSOHUWA da JAURO sabida HAJJO tace sai dai su zauna a gidan su ma  .


su  IRFAN sun sauka lfya ,inda kowa ya wuce d'akinshi a gajiye .
IRFAN kuwa wanka yayi ya shirya cikin wata farar gizner wada tayi masa kyau masha Allah sai kamshi yake zubawa 
Hospitalier dinshi ya wuce sabida yana son duba mutane sosai gashi yasan aiki ya taru masa a office.. 



Masoyan IRFAN yana godiya ,kai har ma da makiyansa 💗💗💗💗

Book 1
Page 17
...✍️Direct asibitin yawuce yatarar da aiki sosai ahakan yaci gaba da duba takardun wata mata Wace za'ayi ma aiki a nonon ta ,yana cikin dubawa yaga wayarsa na ringing ,reject din Kiran yayi.


Mai Kiran ya kai kira biyar sannan ya dauka ,shiru yayi daga can akace"hakika ka kyauta ,miyasa ba za ka doki kaddararmu ba a yanda tazo muna ,kasan irin son da na ke ma kane ya saka nake supporté abunda ka ke mini ,please ka Aure ni wlh ba zan iya rayuwa babu wani in ba kai ba"aka fada dagacan ,Wanda murya mace ne dagaji kuma ya rece dan ba ta jin hausa sosai.

Shiru yayi akala ya kai minti biyu sanan yace"zan miki transfert ,idan kinada wata maganar ki fada" cewar IRFAN yana mai kashe wayar nan take yayimata transfert na dubu dari biyu.

Aikinshi ya ci gaba dayi ba'a wani jimaba yaji ana knoking "yes " yafada yana mai ci gaba da duba computer shi.

"Barka da warahaka yallabai" cewar Nos din da ta shigo,hannu yadagamata yace"ya akayi?"

"DOCTOR dama sister khadija ce take son ganinka " cewar Nos din kanta akasa ,yakai minti uku yana dane danen shi sanan yace"ta shigo" fita tayi ko minti biyu ba'ayiba sai gata tashigo"sannu DOCTOR" bai dago ba yace "lafiya?"

"Dan Allah DOCTOR kayi hakuri ka taimakeni kamaidani aikina wlh ba zan sake ba na tuba ,please DOCTOR kayi hakuri" cewar Nos din da sukayi fada da Nasreen tana kuka alamar roko,

Aikinshi ya cigaba dayi,harta fuda ran zai ansa saitaji yace"kije gobe ki dawo,asibiti ba wurin fada bane"yafada yana mai mata wani wulakantacen kalo.

Godiya tayi masa kamar zatayi masa sujada ,kofa ya nuna mata da Sauri tawuce ,shi kuwa tsaki ya ja ya ci gaba da aikinsa ,sai karfe takwas na dare ya wuce gida ,har ya kusa gidan yajuya akalar motar zuwa gidansu Nasreen gidan yashiga kai tsaye da salamarsa, shiru ka ke ji babu kowa .

So biyu yana salama ciki yazo wucewa yana tunanin wata kila suna ciki ,"wayo Allah na "cewar Nasreen da take wanka a filin gidan a cewarta ba za ta shiga bayi ba aljanni yacinyeta ,shi kuwa IRFAN bai ma lura da itaba sai datayi ihun kasancewar akwai dan duhu ya saka shi bai ma lura da ita ba.

DOCTOR kuwa jin ihunta ya sakashi kalon wurin kasancewar suna kusa kusa ya sakashi ganinta tsaye tana kare kirjinta sai ihu take " wayo Allah na kadaina kalema mijina halittashi wayo Mama" cewar Nasreen din duk tabi tabirkice .


DOCTOR  kam sandar girmashi ce taharba shi harya ma manta da rabon yaji wani Abu wai shi shaawa ,gashi yaw yadawo wanan jarbaba zata jazamishi bala'i ,kallonta yayi ya ga yanda take dan tsale haka nonuwanta da suke tsaye suke tsale ,bai san sanda ya rungumeta ba ,itama rukunkumeshi tayi a cewarta ai bazai kale mata kayanta ba lol...
Shi kuwa jin cabullanta saman kirjinshi ya sakashi kara rudewa fuskarshi harta fara canja kalla ,hannunta ta sakalo ta bayanshi tana dan ihu sannu sannu,shi kuwa gogan bai ma san sanda yasake rungumota ba yana dan sauke ajiyar zuciya kadan kadan ba ,turata yayi gabagon tajinginu fuskarata ya kala da take zubar da hawaye sai KO idonta da yake runtse dan k'aramin bakinta ya kala dayajike da dan Saliva d'inta sai KO ruwan data jika akanta har saman bakinta suke saukowa.

Lips 'dinta ya kama ya hade danashi ,harshenshi yazura cikin bakinta yana wani irin zakulo salive d'inta wani irin zaki zaki yaji hade da wani irin gardi ,lebonta nakasa ya kamo yahada da nasa yana mata wani irin Salo mai gigita Wanda akema shi, jinshi yakeyi kamar bashi ba ,ita kuwa Nasreen wani dadi taji hade da zafi zafi jitakeyi wani Abu na mata yawo tun daga saman kirjinta  har izuwa kasan ta ,wani yarrr taji hade da wani Abu da takeji yana zubo mata akasanta ,jinta takeyi a saman gajimare wani zuko salive d'inta dayayi batasan sanda tarike fuskarshi ba dama dakadan ne zai gwadamata tsawo,jin kamar yana neman damkar dukiyar fulaninta ya saka ta take mishi kafa da da karfi Wanda ya sakashi sakinta bai shirya ba .

Ita kuwa dagudunta ta sabci towel dinta kasancewar dama rudewa yahana tagansa ,bayin dama akusa dashi take wanka tana shiga ta rufe gam tana maida numfashi.


Shi kuwa DOCTOR yaji zafi lokacin da tatakemasa kafar ,wani irin numfashi yake saki,ga fuskar nan tayi ja ,kalo daya zakayimasa ka fahimci ba dai-dai yake ba.
Yakai minti biyar yana mai da numfashi sannan yadan daidaita kanshi rigarshi ya gyara kasancewar Nasreen 'din tadan rukunkumeshi

Su Abba kam duk wannan abun da yake faruwa ba su ji ba kasancewar sun saka TV gaba suna kalo,da salama ya shiga dakin.


Abba ne ya ansa yana fadada murmushin shi ,har kasa ya duka yana gaishesu ansama sa sukayi cikin kulawa suna tanbayar sa yasu Abbi ,

"Lfya kalw suke ,kwana biyu dai munyi tafiya ,mahaifin Abbi Allah yamasa cikawa yaw ma mukadawo" cewar DOCTOR yana mai dukar dakanshi kasa.

"Allahu Akbar ,Allah mai girma ,gashi banji labari ba ,koshine na ga ana hasakawa jiya a labarai?"""

"Eh shine" ya bashi amsa.
"Allah sarki datijon arziki ai ina ganin ana wanoshi cikin kungiyoyi masu ba da tallafi akan marayu,da na kasasu ,Ubangiji Allah ya gafarta masa Allah yasa yahuta " cewar Abba yana mai jinjina kanshi.

"Gobe za mu zo muyi ta'aziya incha Allah" inji Abba,Mama ma gaisuwa tamishi .
Amir ne daya makale dashi yace"ye gobe za mu je in ga Anty sharifa na san zata bani chocolat " ya fada yana mai yar dariya,Abba ne ya girgiza kanshi kawai yana tunanin kurciya mai da'di in ba haka ba yaji ana maganar mutuwa shi ta chocolat ma yake
Rufaida ma gaisheshi tayi tana mai yimasa ta'aziya kasancewar sun samu ilimi sun san miye mutuwa.

Sun dan jima suna mai da zancen samnan ya mike dan ya matsu yaje gida ya sha tablettes 'dinsa jin ya da marashi take masa ciwo,sai da safe ya musu ,

Yana futowa bai ko kalli bayin ma duk daya san tana ciki,kai tsaye gida ya wuce ,da Sauri yayi toilettes yawatsa ruwa ya dan jima sannan ya futo ko mai bai shafa ba ya dauko maganinshi yasha ,kasancewar har goma tayi ya sakashi yin konciyarshi dan yasan su Abbi sunyi bacci yanzu.

Lulube kansa yayi yana mai murkususu cikin bargon sai zufa yake had'awa ya jima a hakan sanan yaji sauki barcci ne yayi awon gaba dashi,tare da wani irin mafarki mai ban tsoro ,Nasreen ce ya gani cikin wasu irin tufafi farare sai muushi takemasa hannu ta meko masa shima ya mik'a nasa zai kaama sai ga wata budurwa mai bakaken kaya tafusgi Nasreen din ,bata suka bace wai ge wai ge ya fara amma ko alamarsu bai gani ba


Sai zufa yake ha'dawa da Sauri ya farka yana mai addu'ar neman tsari daga shedan l'anane dafe kanshi yayi yana mai tunanin me wannan mafarkin yake nufi ,yana cikin wannan tunanin yaji motsi a bayan window d'inshi bai kawo komi akan sa ba ya mik'e ya d'auro al'wala ,sallaya ya shinfid'a ya fara nafilfili yana mai nemawa kansa tsari dama a halinshi gabakidaya.


Salamewa yayi zaune yayi yana jan tabzi ,wani irin iska yaji ya zo da karfi ya wuce ta gaban windo d'inshi cigaba yayi da lazuminshi a tunaninshi ko iska ne ,ba'a ji ma ba ya ga wani Abu kamar mutum ya wuce da da bakaken kayanshi,mikewa yayi yana mai neman tsari daga wurin Allah ,Budé kofar part dinshi yayi ya gewaya bayan gidan ,da torshe din wayarshi yake haskawa garin shiru ba ka jin motsin komi sai kukan karnuka da tsuntsaye,sai ko farin wata da yahaska garin bai ji tsoro ba yana tafe har yazo daidai window shi ,ya haska bai ga komi ba,gewaye gidan ya shiga yi yana mai dudu ba ko ina amma bai ga komi ba.


Part d'inshi ya dawo yana wani irin murmushi Wanda shi kadai ya san ma'anarshi .



Gaisuwa da fatan alkhairi zuwa gareki K'awata my lovely ,kin wuce kawa kin zama yar Uwa Fati Haruna ,Allah ya bar k'auna nagode.

Book 1
Page 19
....✍️Nasreen kam sai hamdallah dan yanzu kafarta da sauki ama tana dan dingisa ta ,gashi ta koma scool dan IRFAN da kanshi yaje makarantar tasu ya fada musu kada amaidata baya kuma sun yarda zatayi semestre karshe sunada yakinin zata wuce.

Zaune take cikin d'akinta wayarta ta doka ta kira wata number bugu biyu akada ga "haba Kawata wai ina kikashiga ne na jiki shiru,ko na kira number ki bata shiga?" cewar MUFIDA datake wayar da kawarta Samira

"Sorry K'awata wlh kwanan naje kauye duba Kakata kin san bata da lafiya gashi muna da matsala network" cewar Samira kawar MUFIDA .

"To naji kin san mine ne ,nayi bincike Nagano cewar YAYA IRFAN dan UNCLE ALI ne ,kin san da yake basa zawa sai anyi hutu ni kuma kinga boording nakayi shiyasaka kuma bancika zuwa gidan kakani nawa ba " ta fada tana mai cigaba da fadin" so yanzu ki fad'amin ya zanyi wallahi ni kam na kamu da sonshi ,yana da kayau da kwarjini gashi da duk qualité din da nake son mijina dasu ,so ki fad'a min yanzu miye mafita".

"Yanzu kin san yanda za'ayi mu had'u yaw da marecen nan kinga zai fi ko?" fadar Samira kenan
"OK to zanzo damarece gidan ku" inji MUFIDA.
"OK to shikenan sai kinzo " kashe wayar tayi ,tana tunanin ita fah yanzu inba Yaya IRFAN d'in ta aura ba, ba ta jin zatayi wani aure.


Abba ne zaune da wasu mutane guda biyu datijawa ga dukan alamou magana mai mahimmanci suke tataunawa dayan ne yace" to Malan a gaskiya Sani ya nuna yana matukar kaunar yar wajenka ,shine ma na ke ganin kai ne yafi cancanta musanar kafinma yafara zuwa zance wajen yarinyar ".

Dayan kuma Wanda daka ganshi kaga kamainsa da Sani Wanda yake nuna cewa lale Mahaifinshi ne " Idan har ka ga Sani yacancanta kabarshi yarinka zuwa har Allah yasa su daidaita to ka barshi in kuwa bai cancanta ba kada ka duba zumuncinmu ,Allah ne yace muzabama y'a y'an mu miji da mata tagari" fadar baban Sani din Wanda shida mokocinsa ne suka samu Abba da wannan maganar "
   
   "A gaskiya Abunda zance da ku nagode sosai,sannan Sani ba yada wani aibu da zai hana na bashi y'a ta ,bazan mata doli ba ,zai iya fara zuwa zance idan sun daidaita falilahil hamd idan kuma basu daidaita ba to Allah yasa hakan shi yafi zama alkhairi" cewar Abba kenan..


   "Masha Allah ameen mungode sosai Malan " cewar makocin nasu daya..
Da haka suka watse kowa yayi gidanshi.

IRFAN ne rike da waya yana Kiran Samir amma bai daga ba ,wayar yajefar amakeken gadonshi yana jan tsaki...

Ba 'aji ma ba yaji tana ringin Wanda yasan ba zai wuce Samir din ba watakila ya tarar da kiransa ne , saï daya masa kira uku sanan yadaga "haba my Man ya ina ta kira ka na gani kaki dagawa ,KO duk izzar ce tamotsa" cewar SAMIR d'in,
     "Ya su momy ?" cewar  Irfan 

"Su Mom lafiya 'kalau suna gaisheka " inji Samir d'in....
 Shiru ne ya dan ratsa sannan Samir d'in yace"Mike labari dan Na-san ba ka Kiran banza"

Dafe kanshi DOCTOR yayi sannan yace"wallahi Samir wasiyar nan ce take damuna ,gashi d'azu uncle ya kirani yana fad'a min wai karna manta lokaci na tafiya" ya fad'a kamar zai yi kuka.

 "IRFAN ,ko na baka shawara Na-san ba za ka doka ba dan haka ka nemawa kanka mafita " cewar Samir din.

"Ka ga ka daina jamin Rai kavfadamun kawai " inji DOCTOR yana mai da fe kanshi alamar surutun ya fara damunshi.

"kasan wani Abu kuwa ?idan nace maka ka kamu da son Nasreen za ka yarda kuwa ?"tambayar da Samir ya jefa masa Kenan .

Dip IRFAN ya doke wuta yana maijin abunda Aminin nasa yake fad'a ,shiyama rasa mizaice Wace ce ma Nasreen ,tunani ya shiga yi sai can ya tunota " Oh my God" cewar IRFAN din,Samir ne yace "yadai ,Na-san ka kamu da sonta ganin ya da kake karemata kalo idan mukaje gidansu ina fah ganin komi Abokina ,kawai ka amince in ba haka kuwa ina mai tabbatar maka sunanka sorry". 


Kanshi ya dafe ya fusgar da wata irin iska mai hucin zafi " IDAN ka gama sai anjima"fadar IRFAN kit ya kashe wayar yana Jan tsaki ,ya ji ma yana hararar wayar sanan ya shiga toilettes dan yin wanka .



Nasreen ce zaune a dan d'akinsu tana karanta wani novels na hausa mai suna "RAYUWAR IRFAN NA  MAMAN ARIFULLAH ,wani murmushi taketa saki ita d'aya wanda shigowar Rufaida ya sakata fad'in" Anty Nasreen kedawa kuma na ga littafi a hannunki ama bango kikema murmushi"
  Dan gimstewa tayi tana fadin"ki shiga taitayinki Rufi bana son shiga shara bashanu"
"Allah ya baki ha'kuri" inji Rufaida tana mai yi waje kasancewar ta doki abunda ya kawota .
Nasreen kuwa murmushita ta ci gaba daya sai can kuma ta sake Jan tsaki tace"kyawun dan maciji ba ,ga kayau amma ba hali mai kayau sai lalube y'a y'an mutane" komi ta tuno kuma oho na ga tasaki murmushi .


Abba ne da Mama yana fad'a mata yanda sukayi da ma neman Auren Nasreen din "to masha Allah ai kuwa Sani bashi da wani aibu Abbansu" cewar Maman Kenan
        "Bari na fad'a mata Na-san za tafi fahimta" cewar Abba yana mai kwalawa Nasreen din kira...
Minti daya tsakani sai gata ,gaishe da iyayen nata tayi sannan Abba yace"Nasreen ina so ki sani da farko dai burin kowane uba ya ga yarsa tayi Aure ,so nagodema Allah daya bani ku a matsayin y'a y'a na ,ina Alfahari da ku da mahaifiyarku domin sai da Uwa tagari sannan akan samu y'a y'a nagari".

Aya ya dasa sannan ya ci gaba da fadin"Abunda Nike so ki sani shine zan baki dama kifito da mijin aure ,kamar yanda mahaifin Sani ya nema ma dansa neman auren ki,na fada masa zai iya zuwa idan kun daidaita shikenan,in kuma aka samu akasin haka Allah ya had'a kowa da Rabonsa" yafada yana mai kalon réaction 'dinta .

Shiru tayi tana tunanin Aure yanzu ,ita fah bata tashi ba Allah yana gani ,karatu take so tayi tazama Babar mace irin Manyan matan nan wanda akeji dasu,sai gashi Abba yana son ruguza mata burinta.

Abban ne ya katse ta da tunanin da take yace"Nasreen ka da ki saka wata damuwa a ranki,zamuyi da wajewa akan zai barki kici gaba da karatunki ,kinji abunda baba ya hango yaro KO yahaw bene ba zai hangoba ,ina fatan bamuyi miki shishiga bâ"fadar Abba Kenan .

Da Sauri tace"kayi ha'kuri Abba bakumin shishigi ba ku iyaye nane kuma ina alfahri daku ,nagodewa Allah daya bani ku a matsayi iyayena ,sannan dama akan karatuna ne tunda zakuyi yar jejeniya na amince Abba ,kuma ka daina fadar kumin shishigi kowa ka kawomin kace na Aura Abba wlh zan Aure shi " ta fad'a wasu hawaye na zubo mata Wanda bata san kona minene ba.

"Gobe Sani zai fara zuwa wurinki insha Allah, Allah ya zaba miki abunda yafi zama alakairi ,Allah yamiki albarka dake da yan uwanki" Abba ne mai mata wanan addu'ar .

 Mikewa tayi bayan Abban ya bata umarni,d'akinsu ta shige tana tunanin rayuwa ,ita fah wlh bata taba jin son Sani a ranta ba ,tana mishi kalon yayan tane jini daya,bata ma san mizatace mishiba ita.

Doctor kuwa ya shiga damuwa sosai ,gashi kosu Abbi basu san da alkawarinsu da kakanshi ba.shiyama zai yi ne ta ina zai fara bayanawa yarinya cewa yana sonta salon ta rainashi ba ,ganin tunanin yana neman sakamishi ciwon kai ya sakashi sawakawa kansa daganan bacci ne yayi awan gaba dashi.


Mufida ce da kawarta Samira zaune a 'dakin Samira d'in Mufida ce tace"wlh Kawata sonshi nakeyi kuma ban san ya zanyi ba ,yana da taurin kai idan har zan fito ince ina sonsa Na-san raina ni zai yi ni kuma ba na son haka ,ki bani shawara Kawata" ..
"Yanzu kin san ya zamuyi kiyi ko'kari ki nemo mana number sa kinga duk abunda zai biyo baya mai sauki ne" inji Samira din

   "Shikenan Kawata zanyi kok'ari na sato a wayar Bro Malik ,dan bana so mayasan Miyake faruwa yanzu" fad'an Mufida Kenan .da haka suka ci gaba da tataunawa akan yada zasu shirya plan d'insu.


Sani ne zaune a cikin zauren su Nasreen din Wanda Rufaida ce takawo masa kujera zama da,kasancewar bayan isha'i ne ya saka Abba har ya shiga ciki, Nasreen ce sanye dawata doguwar Riga ta  atamfah wada take matsewa daga saman din nan daga kasa kuma mai budewar nan ce tayane kanta da dan mayafi ruwan madara sai kamshi taketa zubawa masha Allah Nasreen kam komi yaji .
  Salama tama Sani tana fadin"Yaya Sani ina wuni" 
"Lafiya klw ,Ama please kanwata a canja min sunan nan na yaya dan baya dadi abakinki" cewar Sani Kenan yana mai wangale bakinsa kamar Wanda akama albishir da Aljanna....

Dan dukar dakanta tayi tana wasa da kalabin kanta ,Sanin ne ya sake fadar "da farko dai k'anwata na zo da kokon bara na Na-san Abba yafadamiki abunda yake tafe dani ko, kin san ni sosai Nasreen ni dai ba wani hali ne dani ba sai rufin asirin Allah ,ama Na-san zan iya dokar nauyinki dake da y'a y'an da zamu haifa, ina son Auren ki ,na ji ma dakamuwa da soyayyarki,ama ganin cewa ba kya kula kowa yasaka nima na bar abun a zuciyata " ya kareshe yana dan satar kalonta.

"Nagode sosai Yaya Sani da k'aunar daka nuna min Ni da dangina ,sannan kuma ba abunda zance sai dai ince Allah yamuna zabi na alakiri " cewar Nasreen Kenan wada batasan mizatace mishi ba ,dan gaskiya ba za ta iya fitowa ba kiri kiri tafamasa bata son sa ,dan yayi kokari sosai akanta ya nuna mata so tun batasan kanta ba,Amma kuma ita dai wlh bata taba yi masa so na aure ba.....
  Tunanin tane ya katse lokacin da taji wani kamshi ya daki hancinta Wanda tasan mutum daya ne yake anfani dashi, bata karasa tunanita ba taji salamarsa cikin dadar muryasa wada duk lokacin da ta ji ta sai taji gabanta ya fad'i .

DOCTOR kuwa kwana biyu bai zo gaida Abba ba har yana kiransa ma yana tambayarsa KO lafiya shi kuma aiki ne ya masa yawa ,yaw kam ya samu dama shiyasa ya zo gaishesu ,tun kan ya shigo yake jin yada Sani yake wangale baki tsaki yaja inbadan yasan Yin salama wajibi bace a matsayinka na musulmi da wlh shikam ba zai yi ba.

  Sani ne ya ansa kasancewar akwai dan haske ya sakashi ganeshi wannan DOCTOR ne Wanda ya zabagama Nasreen mari ranan .shikuwa IRFAN kalo daya yamusu yadauke kai ,gidan yashi warshi yana maijin ranshi na suya shiyara sa miye duk lokacin da da zai ganta da wani sai yaji ransa na suya kuma shi kam yasan ba sonta yakeyi ba ...


Nasreen kuwa ko kalonshi batayi ba duk dayada gaban ta yake fad'uwa Wanda duk lokacin da zata ganshi KO taji muryasa sai gabanta ya fa'di.

"Yana ga kamar hankalinki yadan tashi kanwata ?,sanan wance wane ne naga kamar DOCTOR din nan na Ranan KO?" cewar Sani yana kalon Nasreen wada take sauke ajiyar zuciya akai akai.

"Eh ai shine yaka de ni ranan " inji Nasreen din
"Amma sai na ga kamar ranan hukunci daya miki bai da wannan hurimin..." fadar sanin ,tunkan ya 'karasa ta ka tseshi da fad'in "wannan ba huriminka bane yaya Sani"

Ganin kamar bata ji  dad'in maganarsa ba yasakashi cewa "kiyi hak'uri kanwata idan na miki shishigi" 

" bakomi Yaya Sani, ni zan shiga ciki dare nayi"Nasreen ce mai wannan maganar Wanda tarasa miyasa maganar Sani ta mata ciwo Wanda ta kasa boyewa harshi ya gane .

  Mikewa tayi ,shima saida safe ya mata ba dan ya so ba kasancewar bai ida fad'a mata abunda yake ransa ba .  .. 

Doctor kuwa fira sukeyi shida su Abba Wanda yanzu jinshi yake yi kamar dan gida ,Nasreen ce ta shigo tana gaishe da iyayen nata Wanda shi kuwa DOCTOR ganinta da wannan shiga Wanda KO hijab babu yasaka Ranshi baci wani irin hararenta yayi Wanda itakadai ta gani kasancewar tadan saci kalonshi.. Dauke kanta tayi kamar bata gani ba tayi cikin dakin nasu...


Sun dan jima suna fira da su Abba sanan yamusu sai da safe ya wuce gida...........


Yana zuwa gida sashensu Abbi yaje ya gaishesu daga nan d'akinshi yawuce ,yana zuwa wanka yayi ya saka kayan barcinsa wayarshi ya doka yana Kiran Samir ,ringin d'aya ya doka" Aslm Abokina ya kake?"cewar Samir 'din
"Lafiya klw " inji DOCTOR .

dan Jim yayi sannan yace"tambayarka zanyi " .
"Allah yasa na sani " cewar Samir.

"Miya saka mutum ya ke jin bacin rai a duk sanda ya ga wani mutum tare da mace?sannan miya saka gaban mutum yake faduwa inhar zai ganta ko zaiji muryata?" yafada yana dan dafe kanshi...

Wata mahaukaciyar dariya Samir d'in ya kece da ita wanda ya sa sai da yadan kauda wayar daga kunanshi ,tsaki ya ja yana mai kalon wayar kashe wayar yayi ganin kamar Samir din ya zama mahaukaci...

Sai bayan miniti biyar sannan ya sake kira Wanda yasa IRFAN d'in harar wayar sannan ya doka "yi hakuri abokina tanbayar takace na jita wata iri I am sorry dan Ammi" fadar Samir 'din yana mai da ne dariyarsa ,"yawa ka na ji ko ,duk lokacin da ka ga ka na jin bacin rai idan ka ga wani abunda kake so tare da wani ,wannan bakomi ba ne face kishi,sannan kuma fad'uwar gaba alama ce tanunana ka na tsananin 'kaunar mutumin" fadar Sam 'din yana dan dariya kasa kasa.

Kashe wayar sa DOCTOR yayi yana mai Jan tsaki Kenan wai ya kamu da son wannan yarinyar yoshi bai ga abun so ajikintaba ,Wanda yatabata inda ya ha yake mata so 'daya kasheta zai yi a yanda yake jinshi ai ko mata goma wallahi zai iya dasu ,ganin tunanin na neman sa masa ciwon kai yasa sawakama kanshi,adu'ar barcci yayi ya lulube dagan bacci ne yayi awan gaba dashi........

Book 1
Page 20
  ..........✍️Nasreen ce zaune ta fad'a tunanin anya ba akwai wani Abu bakuwa dan ita fah ta fara jin tsoron DOCTOR nan to miyasa ta ke jin fad'uwar gaba a duk sanda ta ganshi ,sannan miyasaka take yawan tunanninshi,ga son jin kamshin shi da take ,taji kawayenta a scool suna yawan cewa in kaji haka alama ce ta kakamu da son mutum ,amma ita bata yarda ba sabida ba za ta so wannan mugun ba, tun tashinta ba ta da kawa Mama ita ce kawarta duk damuwarta ita take sanar mawa ,dan haka yanzuma zataje taje islamiya,aikin ma da zata kamawa Mama tayi ,ishe Mama tayi tana dan gyaran wakenta .

 "Mama kawo nayi shine kika ce babu aiki bayan gashinan ," Nasreen ce mai wannan maganar .
"Kinga aikinyi naki bari zan 'karasa " fad'ar Mama data hana Nasreen 'din gyaran waken,ganin kuma kamar ta dage ya saka Mama sakar mata .

Hira suke danyi irin ta da da Uwa ,sai can Nasreen tace "Mama na tanbayeki kuwa?" tana dan dukar da kanta 
"To Allah yasa na sani"cewar Mama Kenan tana dan mai da hankalinta ga Nasreen d'in Wace take dan susur kanta alamar jin kunya .

" am eh ,dama Mama tanbayar ita ce ,Miyasaka kakajin fad'uwar gaba a duk sanda ka ga mutum,KO mutum ya rikama kwarjini,ko ka rika yawan tunanin shi?"
Tana mai cigaba da dan shafa kunan ta alamar jin kunya ...

Murmshin manya Mama tayi domin ta gane duk yanda akayi yarta ta kamu ne da son wani,amma bata gane ba ,ita kuwa ba za ta tanbeta in tayi tsami saji.

"Hmm Nasreen Kenan idan har mutum zai rikajin irin wa'danan abubuwan to alama ce ta kakamu da sonsa, son ma mai tsanani,Wanda mafi akasari idan d'aya na jin haka dayan ma naji" Mama ta bata ansa tana maiciga ba wanke shinkafarta.

Daga nan Nasreen tayi daki tare da tunani mi hakan yake nufi ba dai ta kamu da son Wanda take ganin mugu ba, Kenan shima yana jin abunda takeji "mtsss"
Tsaki ta ja kamar yana ganinta ta doko book d'inta tana karatu...


DOCTOR kam yanzu ya yarda da yakamu da son yarinyar nan to amma taya zai fad'ama gashi lokaci na 'kure masa ,kar yaje yace yana sonta tace ba ta son shi Daga nan fah raini ya shiga ,zai tanbayi Samir yaji duk da ya san zai yi masa shakiyanci...

Bai karasa tunanin shi ba yaji wayarsa na ringin Wanda ba'kuwar number ce,yanaganin ba'kuwar lamba ya ja tsaki sai da wayar takare ringin sanan ba'jima ba wani Kiran ya shigo ,kira ake babu kakautawa Wanda yasa IRFAN Doka ya jefar da wayar kasancewar a hau parlé ya sakata ,shiru yayi sai ji yayi ance"hai handsome ,ya kk" wani irin tsaki ya ja Wanda ko kare akama sai yaji haushi amma sai naji an koma cewa" kayi ha'kuri Bro IRFAN ,Mufida ce dama na kira ne dan na gaisheka "cewar mufida wada ta sato lambar IRFAN din a wayar Babanta Imran...shine Samira tace ta kirashi ,tayi kamar gaisheshi ne tayi ,mafarin ta kirashi Kenan...

Shi kuwa dafe kanshi yayi yana dan tatara goshinsa ,shi sai yanzu ma ya tuna da wata Mufida kasancewar rada sukadan kabrun nan ,Malik ya masa bayaninta tare da mishi shakiyancin wai ga matar shi nan.

Kashe wayar yayi yace"mtss aikin banza" ya cigaba da aikinshi a computer shi... 


Yanzu kam duk bayan kwanan biyu Sani yakanzo suyi fira Wanda ita Nasreen jinshi kawai take, shi ka kidinsa shiga rawarsa ,hakan yasa in suna fira baya wuce tace" um,aa "ko tayi murmshi ba.
Wanda ya sakashi fahimtar anya Nasreen d'in tana son shi kuwa...

Nasreen ce tafe wada kasancewar yaw asabar akwai islamiya yasakata tafiya ,wanda dawowarta Kenan ,sanye take da katon hijab d'inta wanda ya kai mata har kasa ,Sauri take zubawa taje gida ,wani mutum tagani a bayanta kasancewar da dan nisa zuwa gidansu gashi magrib takusa dan karfe hudu suke shiga su sabko shida ,yada take Sauri haka yake Sauri abin har mamaki yake bata ,so take taga in ita yake bi ya sakata rage saurin ,wanda yasaka mutumin shima rage saurinshi...
Daga nan ta fara jin tsoro kar azo yan satar mutane ne ,add'ua ta fara yi cikin ranta tana neman tsari daga mugaye.....
Ganin ba zai daina binta ya sakata zabga wa da gudu wanda shima mutumin ya bita da gudu ,gudu takeyi iya karfinta shima kuma yana biye da ita,duk iya gudunta saida mutumin nan ya ci mata hijabinta ya rik'e gam wanda dama akwai dan lungu a wurin,cikin lungun yasata ya rufe mata baki yana fad'in" yaw kam saina dandani zumar ki shegiyar yarinya kawai ,Allah yabaki kayan dadi saiki hannamu ci baki isaba" ya fada yana mai kokarin yaye mata hijabi...

Ita kuwa Nasreen jakar ta tayada tana kuka da ihu tana neman agaji wanda ya saka mutumin har yafuda mata hijabin har yana ko'karin ya ga mata rigar kasancewar batasa brezia ba


DOCTOR ne tafe cikin motar shi yana tuki hankali kwance ,so yake yaje gaida Abba inyaso yayi sallar shi acan

Wanda yau kam katari ya biyo hanyar kasancewar kamar gewaye ne ,ya ga lokacin da mutumin ya ja yarinyar duk akan idonsa ne ,tsaki ya ja yana mai ci gaba da tukinshi ,yo ina ruwanshi da rayuwar wasu yanzu haka dama sun sa ba tanbad'ewa...

Har ya d'anyi nisa kuma ya dawo ganin kamar yarinyar na kwatar kanta ne ,ba zaiso ayiwa 'kananshi haka ba ,shiya saka shima ba zai yarda ayi ma na wasu ba...

Nasreen kuwa ganin saura kadan yake mata riga ya saka saduda sunan Allah kawai take kira ,Mutumin ne yaji an wani dagashi sama an jefah gefe duk da kuwa ba za'a kirashi da mara jiki ba ,ita kuwa Nasreen jin kamar an daina kokayi da ita ne ya saka Saurin mi'kewa tana neman hijabinta wanda tsabar dimaucewa gashi kusanta amma bata ganshi ba sai kukanta take tana kare kirjinta....

IRFAN kuwa lokacin da ya futo daga motar yaji kukan yarinyar sai neman taimako takeyi yasa shi isa wurin da Sauri ya daga mutunan naushi daya ya masa ya sume nan take

Nasreen kuwa ganin IRFAN ya saka ta mi'kewa da Sauri ta tana rukunkumeshi ringumeshi tayi irin rungumar nan wada ka ga wani wanda kake kauna ,rungumeshi tayi kamar za ta shige cikinsa sai kukanta takeyi mai sauti ga hawaye da majina duk sun 'bata mata fuska glass d'inta ma na ido ya fad'i ,DOCTOR kam tun lokacin da yama mutunan naushi ya juyo gun yarinyar dan ya mata tsawa ta daina cika masa kune karaf idonshi ya sauka akanta ,sai ko yaji ta rungumeshi ,yada ta rungumeshi 'din ya saka shima zagaye hannunshi rungume da ita dan ya san tana cikin shoks ne.

Shafa bayanta yake danyi kadan kadan wanda tun ganin ita ce ya saka gabanshi wani irin fa'duwa shi yana nan yana dako Ashe akwai masu neman keta masa gona ,lale kuwa gara yayi da gaske, ganin ta dan dawo dai-dai ya sakashi Jan hannunta yasa a mota dan so yake ya ci uban wannan dan iskan yaron.

Ita kuwa taki sakinshi ganin yaban bareta Daga jikinshi ya sakata 'kara volume d'in kukanta dangani take har cikin motar mutunan sai ma iya iskota.

DOCTOR kam ranshine yayi wani irin bacewa fuskar nan tayi jajur kamar yaji sai idonshi da suka canja kala zuwa jajaye police ya kira ya musu kwatancen inda zasu sameshi...


Wurin mutumin nan ya isa naushi ya kai masa a fuska wanda ya sakashi farfad'owa ,jibgarshi yakeyi kamar Allah ya aikoshi ,bai ta'ba dukan kowa ba sai akan wannan makirin,yana cikin dukansa policawan sukazo wanda dakyar IRFAN d'in yakyaleshi ,shi kuwa mutunan bai masan duniyar da yake ba dan ya suma yafi a 'kirga yana farfad'owa

IRFAN kam motar ya ja tare da bada wani irin iska wanda ya saka Nasreen kara sautin kukanta ,wata irin tsawa ya buga mata wanda ya saka dakadan futsari yakubuce mata "mtss ki mini shuru ko na bab'alaki "

Tsit kake ji wanda sai shashekar kukanta da yake tashi ....
Kasancewar angama sallah Kenan gashi ba zai iya barinta ta shiga gida haka a firgice ba ga mutane da gulma zama su iya zarginsu ya sakashi yin parking nesa da gidansu...

Nasreen kam tayi shiru amma sai shashekar kukanta da yake tashi,IRFAN kam ya tsani hayaniya bare kuka hakan ya sakashi dafe kanshi wanda yake d'an sara masa ,gashi shi kam bai iya rarrashi ba dan ya ga alamar sai ya tausasa murya ko za ta nutsu...

Book 1
Page 21/22


    ........✍️Sunkai minti biyar a mota shi kanshi adafe ,itakuwa sai dan shashekar kukanta dayake tashi ,ganin bazatayi shirun bane yasashi dan kamo hanunta kadan yace"Zaki aure ni???"fadar IRFAN din Wanda shima baisan lokacin da maganar takubuce masa ba .

itakuwa Nasreen a bazata taji maganar shi Wanda yasakata dauke numfashinta na wasu yan dakiku,shiru sukayi kowa da abunda yake sakawa saican IRFAN yace"kije kiyi shawara da zuciyar ki,koda Mama,banyarda kiyi dakowa ba" fusgar da iskar bakinshi yayi sanan yacigaba da fadin "abun nan dayafaru kuma banyarda kifadawa su Mama ba ,fatan kin gane mi Nike nufi?" yafada yana mai dan sakin hannunta...
  Itakam Nasreen mamaki ne yakasheta itam bata gane nufinshiba Wato Auren ta kawai yake son yi wato bama ita yake so ba,lale kuwa zata nuna mishi samun mace kamarta ba sauki ne dashi ba.....

Hawayen ta tashare Tass sanan ruwan datagani agaban motar sa tadoka tashare hawayenta ...
IRFAN kam sai yanzu yake tunanin anya baiyi kuskureba mtss yaja tsaki dama Samir ne yabashi wanan shawara ,shikam bazai bata lokacinsaba wajen fadawa yarinya kalaman soyaya danshi gani yakeyi gara sai sunyi auren zainunamiki Soyaya a lokacin babu wani raini atsakaninku kun kece raini dan shi bazai doki shashancin nan ba na zubda girma ....

Ganin ankare sallah yasakashi tada motar yayi parking dai dai gidansu ,fitowa tayi sai dayatabatar data shiga gidan sanan yajuya akalar motar tashi zuwa gida gobe zaidawo gaishe dasu Abba...

Nasreen kam koda tashiga gidan Mama kadai tagani wanda taso tagano wani Abu ama Nasreen din tace bakomi hakan yasaka tabar maganar

Ruwan zafi tadaza tayi wanka ,gabatar da sallar magrib tayi kasancewar anyi isha'i yasakata hadesu ,jin maganar Abba yasakata fitowa tagaishesa ansawa yayi yana mai tanbayar ta karatu da "Alhmdllah" ta ansa daganan abinci sukaci,suna karewa  ta tatara wurin ...
Dakinsu takoma tana tunanin ina mafita ita fah wlh tasan tana sonshi to ama tanbayar itace shi yana sonta anya,inko hakane bataga dalilin dazaisa ta aureshi ba.....


 IRFAN kuwa gida yawuce wanka yayi agagauce yadauro ala'wala yatada Sallah magarib yana karewa ana Kiran isha'i danhaka kai tsaye massalaci yawuce ,ana karewa gida ya shiga part dinsu Abbi yaje yagaishesu daga nan kuma abinci sukaci sunakamalawa yama iyayenshi saida safe, part dinshi yawuce yana tunanin idanfah tace tafasa ,girgiza kansa yayi yasan Doli ma zata soshi sabida samun irinshi sai mai sa'aa da wanan tunanin yabawa kanshi karfin guiwa....
Barcci ne yayi awon gaba dashi ........

Yaw kam saura sati biyu kwananki da akabashi su cika,gashi tun randa abun nan yafaru baisake ganin ta ba ,duk yanda yaso ganinta kuwa kin bari take idan yaje gidan dataji muryasa take shigewa daki,yana son ganin kyakyawar fuskarta ,yana son ganin abubuwanan masu tsokalemasa ido,yana sonji amsar ta ,yaw kam duk yanda za'ayi yaci al'washin sai yaganeta .....

Nasreen kam da gangan takemasa hakanan sabida tana son ganin shin yadamu da ita ko kuwa....
Duk dakuwa yanda zuciyarta take azabtar da ita son ganinshi ama zata jure taga iya gudun ruwan sa...

IRAN ne zaune yaci wanka cikin wata blue din shada wada takara mishi Kyau masha Allah kamar dan indiya sai kamshi yake zubawa sumar nan tasha gyara abun sai Wanda yagani,Ammi ce tashigo falon dandama dayaje tana shiryawa shiyasa yasauko dan yabata damar shirinta zasu fita itada su Abbi kai ziyara gidan abokinsa,kamshi take zubawa kamar wata balaraba tasha wanka cikin wata giznar ruwan toka like son like Mather...
Gaisheta yayi ta ansa tanafadin "masha Allah wanan wanka haka sai kace wanda zaije zance" tafada tana zama adaya daga cikin kujerun falon, shima kusa da ita yazo yadora kafadar shi bisa dantsen hanunta shagwabe fuska yayi yanafadin "haba Ammi na dama bansaba wanka irin hakabane?" yayi maganar cikin shagwaba wanda yakaramsa zallar Kyau.

Murmushi Ammi tayi tace"ni na isa nafadi haka ,aikai kulum kyakyawa kake zama masha Allah,ama wanka nan dai yamin kamada wurin surukata zakaje" tafada tana lakuce masa hanci....
Murmushi yayi wanda Amminshi kawai yakema shi yace"to Ammi na nidai kisamin al'abarka kinjima bakisamin ba zanje "fadar IRFAN din yana wasa da zoben hanunta...
" kaga rabani da shagwabar nan kabari har matar ka tazo kayimata ,badai kunki aure ba kaida Samir sainabada sadkar ku a massalacinai" tafada tana yar dariyata wanda yasaka Abbi ma saukowa yataya danyaji abunda tafada.

DOCTOR kam kara shagwabewa yayi yaje jikin Abbi yana fadin"Abbi harda kai ko, gaskiya ni gidan Abba ma Zani nafadamasa abunda kukayimu" yafada yana narkewa ajikin Abbinshi

"Oh sorry dan Abba ayi hakuri ,ama gaskiya Amminku tafada, nima nafara tunanin haka" cewar Abbi yana mai dan sakin murmushinsu na manya

Dariyarsu Ammi keyi wanda yasaka Abbi cewa "yi hakuri DOCTOR kyale Amminku kudin ai masu tsada ne samun kamarku sai mai sa'a " fadar Abba yana mai mikewa dan ummi tasauko wanda kalo daya tamusu tadauke kai ,dan wani irin kwarjini sukamata 


Ammi ce tace"to muzamu wuce agaishemin da y'a ta ,Allah ya albarkaci rayuwarku yakareku da Sharin mahassada "" ameen "fadar IRFAN din yana mai sumbatar hannunta kanshi tadan dungure tayi gaba Abbi yabita abaya ...

Ummi ce taja tsaki wanda IRFAN din kadai yaji hakan yasashi wani irin murmushi sanan yadansha gabanta batareda yabari su Abbi sunganiba yace" haba ummi na kedawa irin wanan tsakin haka, ko hassadar ce tamotsa ganin kyakyawan ahali cikin farin ciki" yafada yana mai mata wani irin murmushi wanda takeji kamar tasa wuka tacakesa da ita 

Ummi kam murmushin yake tasaki tana fadin "mikake ci nabaka nazuba yaro,Rana dubu tabarawo rana daya tak tamai kaya kajira lokaci" tafada tanamai dan hankadeshi wanda inbadan yayi niyaba wane ita....

Murmshi yasaki yana mai bin bayansu cikin wata irin Izza da ji samartaka,gidansu Abba yazo kai tsaye ,yana shiga yayi salama bai ishe kowa ba atsakar gidan bakin baranda yatsaya nanma salama yayi yaji shiru gadukan alamu kuma akwai mutum agidan

Har yajuya saikuma yashiga babu kowa a falon sai kamshin turare mai dadi dayake tashi wanda yasashi lumshe idonsa ....

Zama yayi a falon dan yasan duk yada akayi basuyi nisa ba

Nasreen kam itadayace a gidan Sabida su Abbi sun dan gangara kasan layinsu dan zuwa ta'aziya kuma su Rufaida sun bisu,Sanin haka yasaka Nasreen fitowa falon da darma gaba duk da fadan da Mama keyimata ama bataji....

Sanin cewa itadayace a gidan yasaka nufar falon tasa abun sauraran kida akunenta wanda ga dukan alamu waka take saurare sai kada kai takeyi tandan jijiga jikinta wakar agirgiza kwankwaso take saurare hakan yasakata fara girgiza duwawun nata wanda batama lura da DOCTOR ba dayake zaune yana kalon ikon Allah ,girgizawa takeyi wanda yasaka zanin nata dan faduwa kasancewar kuma idonta arufe yake wanda dadin wakar ne yamantar da ita yana kusa .

Shikam Docteur yaw yasan yakawo kanshi ,dan yasan da babu abunda zaihana hankalinshi tashi irin wanan rawar ta kaya ai dolima hankalinshi yatashi ,wani irin girgizawa tayi wanda yasa alkalamin IRFAN harbawa ,wani Abu yaji yanamasa yawo tundaga saman kirjinshi har izuwa kasan marainansa ,wani irin shauki yakeji yana dibarsa lumshe idonsa yayi yadan Budé akanta wada yayi dai dai da zaninta yayi kasa kuma tana juyawa ne gashidama batasaka Brezia ba

Ganin abunda yafi kauna wato dukiyar fulaninta suna juyawa gasu amike sai dan girgiza wa dasuke ai baisan sanda yadake burarsa ba yana fadin"inalillahi wa'ina ilaihi raji'un" dan yasan dayaw kam yakade har buzunshi....

Sandar girmanshi ce yaji tanata haniniya kamar zata fasa wandon yasakashi dafeta yanadan nishi kadan.

Nasreen kam rawarta takesha hankali konce saida wakar takai karshe zata canja wata Kenan sai taji kamar nishi abayanta juyowa tayi karaf idonta yasauka akanshi yana mata wani irin kalo na inkinzo hannu kinbanu ,fuskar nan tashi tayi jajur jijiyoyin wuyanshi duk sun Mike ...
Wani irin ihu tasaki tana kokarin Jan zaninta kawai saiji tayi anyi jifa dashi idonta taruntse dantasan yaw kam tashigo hannu wanan kwarton iskaci zaimata gashi babu su Mama.

DOCTOR kam wani irin runguma yamata yana mai dan tureta tafada saman kujera falon mai zaman mutum uku ,dan rankwafowa yayi samanta yana kalon kyakyawar face dinta Wanda alamun tsoro yabayana akanta idonta arufe yake saidan karamin bakinta dayake makarkata.

DOCTOR jin nonuwanta na gugar kirjinshi yasakashi dan dagawa sabida yayi alkawarin bazai kara tabataba ama duk randa aka daura musu aure tashigo hannu shi ta banu.

Kirjinta yakala Wanda yaketa sama da kasa alamar dai haryanzu atsorace take sai ajiyar zuciya datake,wani irin harbawa yaji jijiyarsa tasakeyi wanda na neman agajine ....

"Bude idonki ko kuma namiki abunda bkyaso" yafada yana mai kalon dan karamin bakinta .


Idonta tabude karaf kuwa sai cikin nashi wani irin sako suke aikawa junansu Wanda abun sai su ,sunkai minti daya suna kalon juna sanan ita tayi kokarin jaye nata sabida yafara cikamata ido da ruwa, dan babu mai iya hada ido dashi inbashima yandada kwarjinin ba yaji ruwa basu cikamasa ido ba...


Dan kuzarinshi yatataro yace"wato kinga babu kowa bari kiyi abunda kike so ko saikace kina gidanki,sananan kuma tsabar kin raina mutane afalon su Abba zakizo kisaka musu kida ko ,mafarin kijawomusu aljannu ko?"yafada yanamai hararenta ...

Itakam har yanzu a tsorace take dashi ganin haka yasakashi dan komowa gefen kujerar yazauna hakan yabata damar tashi zaune yana mai kare kirjint....


Tsaki yaja yana fadin "mtsss wane dare ne jemage baigani ba ,daga yaw karnasake ganiniki da darma gaba nosense kawai" yafada yana hararenta ....
"Bacemin daganan ni" aikuwa dagudunta tayi cikin dakinsu Abbi tarufe sai maida numfashi take.......✍️
