Gaisuwa da fatan alkairi garéka my lovely son fauzan Allah yabarmin kai....



Book 1
Page 23/24
       ......✍️ Shi kam DOCTOR wani hali yake ciki Wanda ayada yakejinshi ko mata ishirin aka kawo masa zai dagargaza su ,marashi yaji tana wani murdawa Wanda ya kafe kanshi akan kujera da yake konce hannunshi dafe da mararsa nishi yakeyi kadan kadan duk yayi zufa kamar Wanda yayi sport wato motsa jiki fuskar nan tashi tayija ahaka su Abba suka sameshi..
Suna shiga falon sukaga mutum kwance sai nishi yakeyi ,Mama ce tace "subhannallahi IRFAN baka da Lafiya ne" tafada fuskarta fal da damuwa ...

Abba kam rikoshi yayi jikinshi yana tatabasa ,Abban ne ke tanbayarshi ina ke masa ciwo marasa yanuna Wanda yasa Abba tunanin to miyakawo masa ciwon ,bisa tsotsai yatabo sandar girmanshi Wanda yasa Abba dan kalon wurin ,yanzu kam ya fahimta tunda shi ba yaro bane......

Nasreen kam jin salatin su Abba yasaka fitowa dan a tunaninta ma ya tafi ganinshi tayi jikin Abba sai zufa yakeyi duk ya fita ahayacinsa ,batasan sanda tarikice ba ,da Sauri ta 'karasa wurinshi tana fadin " Abba miye ya samesa?" kamar za ta yi kuka ta fada Wanda duk dabaya cikin hayacinsa yaji dadin yada tanuna damuwa akansa ,duk da boyewar dayake bai san Abba ya ganoshi ba....

Shine yace ma Nasreen d'in tayi gagawa dauko masa Zam -Zam Wanda da Sauri ta doka ,kasancewar Abba ya mike dan doko habbatu sauda ya sakashi jingina jikinshi da nata ,hannunta ya damka cikin wani irin yanayi Wanda duk Wanda ya gansa sai ya tausaya masa.

Kanshi yake jujuyawa alamar yanajin jiki sosai ,ganin haka yasaka Nasreen zubar hawaye dan tasan yada itama take ciwon mara ,Wanda taji ance su Maza bayamusu da sauki har kashesu yakeyi hakan ya sakata tausayamasa sosai ,dan tasan idan ya mutu ita ma karshenta ya zo,kuma tasan yada su Abbi da kaf familly dinshi suke kaunar shi tasan zasu shiga wani hali....

Abban ne ya katsema tunaninta da fadin "dagoshi ya sha wannan zai ji sauki sosai "duk da ya sakimar mata nauyinshi ama a haka Abba ya taimaka mata suka kamashi ,Kafa masa kofin yayi ya dansha kadan ya 'kara langwab'ewa jikinta....

Zufa dai ke karyo masa ko minti biyar ba'ayi ba sukaji yana dan sauke ajiyar zuciya Wanda hakan yanuna barcci yadan 'daukesa ,ganin yayi barccin yasaka Nasreen dan dora masa kansa bisa kujerar Abba ne  ya taimaka mata suka gyara mishi kwanciyar shi...

Waje sukayo su duka inda Abba yake tanbayarta ko miyasameshi....
" Abba ni ban masa yanan ba "fadar Nasreen din duk da bata son yima iyayenta karya to tace musu mi.

Abba ne ya fara fad'a yana cewa" to wato da ko mutuwa yayi babu ruwanki ,kina daki me ye kikeyi a ciki,inaga dasai an zo anyi mana sata kina ciki daki KO"fadar Abban Kenan ,Mama ce tacabe maganar tana fad'in "hmm lale Abban su kamar bakasan halin Nasreen d'in ba,ya wuce kida takesha ta saka wannan abun na ya hudawa " fadar Mama tana mai harar Nasreen din dan tashama ta fada akan haka...

Abba ne yace"Allah ya shiyama ke" yafada yana mai wucewa d'akin dan ganin halin da IRFAN din yake ciki.

DOCTOR kam bai farka ba sai wajen sha biyu na dare Wanda Abba ne yake zaune akusan shi yana dan duduba wani littafi...

Dafe kanshi yayi da yaji yana masa dan nauyi ,sai yanzu abubuwan da suka faru suke dawo mishi ,yanzu da wane ido zai kali Abba ya san aishi ba yaro bane ya fahimci komi...

Abba kam bai lura da ya tashi ba yana so yaje gida dan bai kamata ya kwana musu a gida ba .

Abba ne yaga kamar yadan motsa ya saka shi aje littafinshi yana fad'in "masha Allah DOCTOR sannu ko fatan babu inda ya kema ciwo?" fadar Abba yana dan taimaka mishi ya tashi zaune sabida jikinshi har yanzu ba kwari

Kunya yaji sosai shiyasaka yadan sada kanshi kasa yana fadin "Abba Alhmdllah ,yanzu ma gida zan wuce" ya fad'a yana mai ko'karin mikewa,Abba ne ya dakatar dashi tare da fadin "haba DOCTOR ka kalli lokaci kuwa ,gashi jikin ka ba kwari ,na soma Kiran Abbinka amma ganin kadan wartsake ya sakani kyaleshi" ya fada yana mai turama IRFAN din kwanukan abinci"ai Abba gara da baka gaya masa ba kuma ai naji sauki zan iya tafiya gida da kaina" inji IRFAN d'in .

"Nibdai ban yarda ba ka kwana anan indai kadokeni kamar Abbinka" Abba ya fa'da yana mai mikewa tsaye ,d'akinsu Nasreen ya wuce yace mata ta had'o masa thé mai kauri kafin ya fara cin abincin.

Kasancewar batayi barcci ba karatu takeyi kuma hankalinta na kanshi.

Aikuwa da saurinta ta mi'ke ,falon ta futo Wanda ko kalonsa batayi ba duk da ta san shi zai iya kallonta ,dama tahada ruwan zafin ,thé d'in ta had'a masa koda ta shigo Abba na daki,dukawa tayi tana fa'din "sannu ,Allah ya ƙara Lafiya" ta fad'a tana mikewa Wanda tasan dama ba amsa mata zai yi ba,dan satar kalonshi tayi ta ga shi wayar shi ma yake dan latsawa alamar kamar bai san da mutum a wurin ba ,gashi har yadan fada cikin awa nawa,hmm tace ganin ya shareta tasan dama mai hali bai fasa halinshi,mik'ewa tayi ta kawo masa abun shinfi'da da matashi dan dama dakin guda shigin kayansu ne a ciki ,doli afalon zai konta...

A je masa tayi agefe harta juya yace "ba ki ji ba,nace ni zan zubawa kaina abuncin ne" ya fad'a yana mai cigaba da latsa wayarsa

Juyowa tayi tadan duka ta zuba masa dan waken wanda yasha kayan haddi sai kamshi yake tashi....tana zubawa ta mike tana dan gunguni kasa kasa Wanda ita kadai tasan mi take fa'da


IRFAN kam yi yayi kamar bai ji ta ba ,thé dinsa yasha ,daga nan yadanci dan waken baya son raga abun ci gashi wanan yarinyar ta saka mishi diyawa ,hakan ya cinye yana mai fad'in zai rama wataran yanada yakini ko tahalin kakane zai sameta duk da bayada yakinin zata 'daukeshi amma zai yi ko'karin ganin ya juyata yada takeso da wanan tunanin ya dauro al'wala yarama salolin da a ke binshi..

Abba ne ya tashesa da asuba sukayi al'wala massalaci suka wuce inda basu dawo ba sai da rana tadan yi haske ,kasancewar lahadi babu makaranta yasaka Nasreen itada kananta karatun al'kur'ani da hadisai inda dama sunayi ama bayan magrib ko isha'i ,ranar lahadi kuma da asuba sukeyi ,da salamar su suka shiga inda Abba ne a gaba IRFAN kuma na bayansa ....

Wata murya yaji na tashi wada kamar ana busa sarewa ne tsananin dadi ,kira'a takeyi cikin nutsuwa da kwanciyar hankali inda takebawa kowa ne harafi hakinsa ,Wanda duk yaji doli ne yaji nutsuwa na zo mishi.

Masha Allah Nasreen kamar da larabcin aka haifeta ,IRFAN d'in ne ya gaishe da Mama ta ansa masa cikin kulawa...

Su Rufaida ma gaishe da Abba sukayi sannan IRFAN banda Nasreen datake karatu ,wanda sai yanzu tadasa Aya

Gaishe da Abba tayi ,dan satar kalon IRFAN 'din tayi Wanda ya shiga falon dan ya na so yaje gida duk da ya fadama Al'moctar ya fama su Abbi yau asibiti zai kwana ,ama gara ya je gidan dai...

Dauko makulanshi yayi da wayarshi sanan yace ma Abba zai wuce Wanda babu yanda Mama ba ta yi dashi ba ya bari ya karya ama yaki hakan yasaka sukamishi fatan alkhairi....

Gida yaje ,yana zuwa wanka yayi yashiya cikin wani farin yadi mai dan zanen baki masha Allah yayi kyau sosai

Part dinsu Abbi yawuce kasancewar har goma tayi kuma babu aiki yasakashi ishesu ,gaisheda iyayen nashi yayi kasancewar yaw girkin Ammi ne dama sai inbata girki ne bata tashi da wuri,ansa mishi sukayi cikin so da kaunar dan nasu .

Su sharifa ma gaisheshi sukayi,AL' sidiq ne yace "yaya kadan fad'a kodai ba ka jin dadi" ya fad'a yana dan kalonshi ta ka san ido

Kawar dakanshi DOCTOR yayi dan bayaso su fahimci komi yace"yanayin aiki ne,fatan dai kuna karatu ko?" yafada yana mai maida hankalinshi ga sharifa wada yasan yanzu ba ta son karatu ya lura da ita...

Da sunayi suka ansa masa daga nan hira ce tabarke tsakanin iyalan wanda dama Su sharifa ne ,shikam doctor dama bai cika magana ba, ganinin ma zasu sakamishi ciwon kai yasashi mikewa yayi part d'inshi


IRFAN kam yana zuwa part d'inshi Samir ya kira wanda yadan labarta masa abunda yake faruwa "haba abokina kayi ko'karin ganin ka shawo kanta ka fad'a mata zafafan kalamai ,ama in bahaka kawai dan kace zaki Aure ni nufinka zata yarda ,bayan ba ta san ko kana sonta ba a'a,ka canja shawara nawan" Samir ne mai wannan magana dan sarai ya fahimci Abokin nashi...

"To yanzu wai so kake nace mata ina sonta ko miye " fadar IRFAN yana mai dan Jan tsaki
"Kaga bari na baka wani secret wanda nasan doli ma ta amincema " inji Sam yana mai dan dariya kasa-kasa.

Sun dan jima yana mishi bayani wanda nikaran kaina ban san misuka tattauna ba...


Yaw saura kwana uku lokaci yayi gashi mai sufar ungulun nan sai damunshi take da kira wanda ya rasa dalilin haka ,yasan ai ba ta ma ji maganar had'asu da za'a yi ba sabida kar tasashi arai kuma ya kawo wada yake so,doli yabi shawar Samir din da wannan tunanin ya shirya cikin wani irin gin din wando da bakar Riga irin mai dogon hannun nan masha Allah sai kamshi yake zubawa....


Gidan Abba ya je yau ma ya samu su suna fira wanda duk Sanin ne mai maganar sai can ta dan saka bakinta...

Da salama ya shiga gidan inda ko kalon kirki su Nasreen d'in ba su samu ba,Abba ya gaida shida Mama wanda suke tsakar filin gidan,su kuma su Nasreen suna cikin zaure ne....

Fira suka danyi sannan yace"Abba dama ina so mu fita dasu Amir har da su la mama ma zan kaisu wani wuri inba damuwa" cewar IRFAN d'in yana mai dan Sosa kanshi,"masha Allah ai kananka ne basai katanbi izini ba Yaron kirki " cewar Abba yana mai kwalawa Rufaida kira ,IRFAN ta gaida tana tambaayar sa su la Mama yace "suna lafiya"

Abba ne yace"Yayan kune yace zai kai ku wani wuri,ki fad'awa auta maza" ya fa'da yana dariyarta ganin yada take tsale tayi ciki da gudu....

Nasreen ce ta shigo tana mai yima su Abba barka da hutawa daki ta wuce inda Abba ya tsaidata yana fadin"kinga ki shirya za ku je wani wuri da yayanku"

Kamar tace a'a duk da kuwa yanda take son kasancewa dashi ,mi'kewa tayi ta shiga ciki ,bayan minti biyar sai gasu Rufaida da Auta mik'ewa yayi yana fad'in "Abba sai mun dawo" yayi gaba yana rike da hannun Auta ,inda yapaka sukaje baya ya bud'e musu su ka shiga suna ta murna ,mazaunin Dreba ya zauna ,inda ya fad'awa Al'moctar inda zasu hadu wanda dama shi zai kai su sharifa...

Tsaki ya ja kadan ganin wajen minti biyar har yakuna motar sai gata ta zo ,sanye take ciki wata bakar Abaya mai duwatsu irin mai bud'ewar nan daga 'kasa ,tayane kanta da bakin diko ,sai 'yar Jakarta da tarataya masha Allah Na fad'a ganin kyawun da tayi, dan takaminta mai dan tudu ta saka wanda yasa take tafiya kamar wata doki cikin isa da ta kama za ka d'auka da gangan ne bayan ba haka bane ,tafiyar tace a hakan ,baya ta bud'e zata shiga tajishi akule ,kamar zata juya komi tatuna oho ta bud'e gaban ta shiga inda wani kamshi yadaki hancinsa ,lumfashi ya ja sai kamshinshi turaranshi da nata had'e da sanyin ecin ya bada wani irin yanayi mai sa masoya cikin shauki

Doctor kam da 'karfi ya figi motar wanda saura kad'an ta bugu,dan hararashi tayi takasan ido .

Tafiyar minti goma takaisu wani baban hôtel wanda sai masushi ne suke shiga wurin,hôtels ne da ya ansa sunashi wanda tun daga bakin get din hôtel d'in zaka an rubuta HOTEL LE MOTEL wanda da baban harafi aka rubutashi ,ciki suka shiga inda akayin parking d'in motoci ,gaba d'ayan hôtel din kewaye yake da wasu irin fulawowi masu 'daukar ido ,daga ga matakan tsaro kota ina ,bud'ewa sukayi suka fito aikuwa su la Mama ma sun iso, da gudu taje ta rungume Nasreen d'in tana fa'din "ye Anty Nasreen masha Allah kinga yanda kikayi kyau ko kamar Aarti ta cikin poonar vivah ,fari kawai zata nuna miki" ta fa'da tana mai murna ta ,Al'sidiq da al' Moctar ta gaida sannan suma su Rufaida suka gaishesu ,ganin gogan yayi gaba ya saka Al'moctar céda suje binshi sukeyi wanda har ya ma danyi nisa dasu.....

Ganin yayi réception ya saka su binshi ,magana yayi da ma'aikatan wurin inda jikin su har karkarwa yakeyi ganin kwarjinin daya musu , 

Nasreen kam tafe take tana latsa wayar ta bata lura ba taji tayi gum da Abu wanda ,ma'aikacin wurin ne ke tafe da shayi kasancewar shima yana Sauri ya saka bai ganta ba sai jiyayi kofin ya fad'i a kasa inda yadan tabamata kafa ....

"Inallilahi " ta fad'a tana mai dan yarbe hannunta ,da Sauri ya kai hannun zai ta b'ata yaji an rik'e hannun DOCTOR ne dan harararshi yayi ,shi kuwa dukawa yayi yana bashi ha'kuri ...

Ita kuwa kasancewar shayin yadan sameta ya saka ta dan ihu ,toilettes tace a nuna ma da Sauri tayi wurin ,inda ganin ran IRFAN d'in a 'bace ya saka su bashi hak'uri ....

Ita kam ta kai minti biyar sannan ta futo tana dan goge hannunta da dan lotus ,wayam tagani babu wanda ta sani ,Dube-Dube ta fara kar dai ace sun tafi sun barta ,dube Dube ta ke yi har ta fito waje duk da ba mutane sosai amma ba ta ga mai kama dasu sharifa ,idonta ne ya ciko da ruwa kar azo dama da ya kawota nan dan ya mata wulakanci ne ,wani dan wuri ta gani hakan ya saka ta bi ta hanyar tana shiga ta ga wani irin haske wanda wurin ga dukan alamu kamar in za'ayi invité ne ko wani taro na musamman ga kujeru nan sai flwawer da aka gewaye wurin.

Ciki ta shigo kasancewar wurin ya doki hankalinta sosai Saï da ta kawo tsaka taji dip an doke wutar sai duhu,wani irin fa'duwa gabanta yayi wanda ya sakata yin addu'aa tarufe idonta gam.

Lalube ta fara dan samo wayarta da ke cikin jaka a tsora ce take sai add'ua take rungumeta taji anyi ta bayanta wanda yasaka ta yin ihu tare da yada Jakarta ,baki taji an rufe mata gashi an rungumeta tsam wanda in badan tana a tsorace ba da ta gane kamshi turarensa ...

Wutar aka maido inda tayi Saurin Budé idonta ,photunan ta ta gani ko ina inda lokacin da taka ririye wani wuri tana ihu lokacin da da a ke mata pansements ,wani wuri kuma tana gida zaune da book tana karatu ,wani rukuma tun tana yarinya ne ,juyo da ita yayi gabanshi yana kallon cikin idonta ,ita ma shi take kalo wani irin sako suke aikawa junansu ,sunkai minti biyu ahakn sanan ya hure mata ido da iskar bakinshi wanda ya saka ta dan zabura ," Na-san zakiyi mamakin meya saka duk wad'annan abubuwan faru ,farkon tambayar ki Kenan " IRFAN ya fad'a yana mai aika mata da wani irin kalo wanda ke rikita wanda a kema shi...

"Ina Son ki yayi kadan wurin fad'a miki k'aunar da nake miki,ban ta'ba son wata ya mace ba bayan ke,tun ranar da ido na yayi tozali da ke naji zuciyata ta harba wanda ya tatabtarmin da akwai wani Abu da zai shiga tsakanimu ,kece ya dace ki zama uwar y'a y'a na ,ina miki Son So ,ke'din ta dabance anan "yayi nuni da zuciyar shi...

" ina Son ki zaki Aure ni?" ya fa'da ya fad'a yana mai dukawa kasa ya fudo wani irin zobe na gold yana mai kamo hannunta ya saka mata masha Allah tsaf ya kama hannunta ,wani ya fudo ya bata yace "idan har kin yarda zaki Aure ni nima ki saka min " ya fa'da yana mai shagwa'be fuskar shi ...

Nasreen kam yayi surprendre dinta wasu hawaye taji suna zubo mata wanda ta rasa na miye ,Ashe dama shima yana sonta ba ita kad'ai take wahala ba ,tafin dataji anayi ne ya katse mata tunaninta ,su Rufaida ne da wasu mutane wanda bata san ko su waye ba ,wani irin kuka ne ya kwace mata wanda bata san sanda ta duka ba irin position d'inshi ta rungumeshi tana mai k'ara volume d'inta.....

Kan'kameshi tayi sosai kamar wani zai kwacemata shi 
Shi kam bubuga bayanta yakeyi yana fa'din "miye to ko ba kyaso nane"ya fad'a cikin rada

Girgiza mishi kanta tayi sannan ta'amshi zoben hannunshi ta saka mishi ,mikewa sukayi inda taji ana mata happy birthday Wanda ita tama manta yaw birthday ta......

Wurin yanka cake sukaje inda wani katon cake ya bayyana irin mai hawa ukun nan sunanta ne a sama an saka " HAPPY BIRTHDAY NASREEN" hannunta ya aza saman nashi suka yanka cake d'in ,dan gutusowa yayi zai kai mata baki sai ya shafa mata shi a gefen fuskarta ,tafi aka saki inda ita ma ta lakuto ta shafa mai a kumatunshi tafi aka k'ara saki...

Su laa Mama ne su ka zo cake d'in ta saka musu a baki sannan IRFAN d'in ya doki wani baban GIF ya bata wanda daga gani kasan kyautar ta musaman ce ,su ma su laa Mama bata GIF d'in sukayi......

Daga nan hannunta ya kama sukayi waje gaban motar ya abunta ya kama sukayi waje gaban motar ya bud'e mata ,ya jata da wani irin gudu....


PLEASE IN KIN SAN THANK KAWAI ZA KI CE KI RI'KE ABUNKI BA NA BU'KATA ,SANNAN BA NA SON STIKER ,IN BAZA KIYI COMMET BA KIYI SHIRU ABUNKI....


Ga masu bukatar gyara ko magana dani su tuntub'eni kai tsaye ta wannan lamba...99174891

[9/4 20:29] Ummu Safiyya Adam😍🥰😘: DOCTOR IRFAN 🥼

🍂"ALKALAMINMU YANCINMU🍂


Book 1 
Page  25/26
     .....✍️ Direct gida ya wuce da ita parking yayi a bakin kofar kasancewar har isha'i ma ta gota ,yasan su Rufaida Al'moctar zasu maidosu.


Nasreen kam ta rasa wane irin farin ciki take ciki.

Kallonta yayi yada ya ga duk ta wani ta kure a jikin kujerar kamar ba Nasreen din da yasani ba mai tsiwa da rashin dokar wulakanci ba ,murmshi yayi yace"ina son ki sabida Allah ,ki Soni sabida shi,zan fad'awa Abbi Na-san za su yi murna idan su ka san wacece surukar tasu" ya fad'a yana mai dan kamo lebensa na kasa ya ci za alamar akwai wani Abu a ranshi..

"Ina so ki zama mata ta rufin asiri ,Wace zata rik'e min SIRRI na bata reda ta fad'awa ko da mahaifiyar tace ba ,Na-san zaki iya shiyasaka na zabeki a cikin su " ya fad'a yana mai dan karkato fuskarshi dan yaleki tata

Nasreen kam yaw jinta takeyi awata duniya ta daban ,ita ce yaw DOCTOR IRFAN mai ji da kudi da kasaita da izza yake fad'awa wannan kalaman har yana rokonta ,ita kam mizatacewa Allah bayan ta masa godiya.

Katse mata tunaninta yayi yana fadin "yalabiya lafiya dai ko har kin fara tunanin yaran da zamu haifa ne kyawawa masu kama dake" ya fada yana dan murmushi, Nasreen kam ba ta san sanda murmushin ya kub'uce mata ba Wanda yasa dimple 'dinta lotsawa ,mota ya ga tayi parking kusa da tasu wada yasan su Rufaida ne aka maido ganin haka yace"to HAJIYA  sai na kiraki,yaw nima nake so ki fad'amin irin kaunar da kikemin Na-san ai kema kin kamu kinga yaro hadade mai jini a jika ko ba haka?" ya fada yana mai daga mata gira daya sama Wanda hakan dayayi ba 'karamin birgeta yayi ba harta dan shaga da kallonshi ,da Sauri ta maido hankalinta tana fadin "Nagode " bu'dewa tayi Wanda su Rufaida na shiga ita ma ta bi bayansu ,su la mama aka fara ajewa sanan su Rufaida dan su basu lokacin firantawa kuma plan din ba za su fad'awa kowa ba....

DOCTOR kam yaw jinshi yakeyi cikin nishadi ,gida yazo wanka yayi ya saka kayan barccinsa ya wuce part d'insu Abbi ko da yaje ma sunyi barcci dan babu kowa a falon
Part d'inshi ya koma gado yahaye yana mai dana Kiran wayarta kasancewar dama Rufaida ta turo masa ita 


Nasreen kam ko da taje ma d'aki ta wuce tahaye gandonsu ,Rufaida ce datake zaune tace" Anty ki yarda ki Auri Yaya IRFAN ,wlh bashi da matsala yana da kirki kema sheda ce " ta fad'a tana mai rungume yar uwartata 

Nasreen d'in ma rungumeta tayi tana fad'in "wato daku aka shirya plan d'in ko" ta fad'a tana mai dungure kan Rufaida din, " E mana Anty duk shirin mune har shayin da aka watsa miki,Wanda an dan samu kuskure ya sameki " cewar Rufaida Kenan

"To naji uwar surutu tashi kiyi karatu ko" inji Nasreen d'in .
Bata karasa ba taji wayarta na Ringing 
"My King" ya fito  baro baro akan wayarta Wanda ta san Rufaida ce zata saka,tasan kuma baya Wuce DOCTOR tunda yace zai kira...'

Bata daga ba sai da yayi kira biyu wai ita jan aji kar yarainata 

Shi kam DOCTOR kira biyu dama yakeyi ,ganin bata doka ba yayi tsaki yana mai jifa da wayar ya lulube daga nan barcci ne yayi awon gaba dashi

Nasreen kam niyyar ta ana ukun zata doka sai bai kira ba ,ganin har minti talatin bai kira ba ya sakata fad'in "haba dan Allah ka kira mana ,kar aje yayi zuciya ne" cikin ranta take wannan maganar, ganin ba zai kira ba ya sakata jin ba dadi aranta bargo taja daganan barcci yayi awon gaba da ita...

Yaw ne lokacin da akadibarma IRFAN Wanda yasan doli uncle d'in nashi zai kirashi....
Zaune yake wajen 'karfe biyar na yamma a cikin farfajiyar gidan nasu Wanda dama wurin anyi shine dan shakatawa,wayarshi yaji tana ringin kasancewar yana dan aiki ne a computer shi ya saka ya dan share dan a tunaninshi Samir ,tunda sunyi zai kirashi...

Sai da yayi mai kira biyu ana ukun ya duba Wanda ya ga sunan Uncle Muhammad baro baro akan wayar gabansa ne ya fadi ,amma sai ya dake ya dauka ,gaishe da uncle d'in nasa yayi cikin ladabi kamar yanda ya kema su Abbi,ansa mishi yayi yana tambayar shi klw suke....

Uncle d'in ne yayi gyaran murya yace" IRFAN ya maganar mu kasan ba zamuyi wasa da wasiyar mahaifin mu, yanzu Mika yanke ne?" fadar uncle d'in,shiru yayi nadan seconde sannan yace"Uncle na samo yarinyar"ka dai Abunda IRFAN din yace ,Uncle ne ya jinjina kanshi a cikin ranshi kuwa fad'i yake"Allah ya shiryaka IRFAN koma wacece sai tayi ha'kurin wannan miskilancin naka" amma a fili cewa yayi"to masha Allah ,Allah ya sanya Alkairi zan kira Abbinka duk yanda mukayi kuma da sauran uncles dinka zakaji" ya fada yana mai yanke wayar

IRFAN KAM aikinshi yaci gaba dayi hankali konce ,gashi yana son Kiran yarinyar nan yana tsoron rainin hankalinta ..


Nasreen kam yanzu hankalinta konce ,ama in tatuna Yaya Sani sai taji kamar bata kyauta ba sam
Zata bashi hakuri damasa addu'aa Allah ya bashi ta gari...

Yaw kwana uku bai kirata ba kodai yayi fushi ne ,ita kam wlh ba za ta kirashi ba in ya ga dama ya kira ,duk da kuwa yanda take son jin muryashi mai dad'in gaske,da son ganin kyakyawar fuskarshi mai tsananin kyau da kwarjini amma zata share ita ma ai ta iya tsaki taja Wanda yasa Mama kallonta...

Dan ta lura kwanan nan akwai abunda yake damun yar tata.

Amma zata saka ido ta ga iya gudun ruwanta.

Da misalin k'arfe takwas IRFAN da Abbi ,Ammi and Ummi ne zaune a cikin babban falon nasu gyaran murya yayi sannan yace"da farko dai ina so nagodewa Allah da kuwa iyayena ,burina na Biyu zai cika Alhmdllah,abunda yasa na tara ku anan dama maganar IRFAN ne da Baba Wanda tun kafin ya rasu sukayi abinsu ba tare da sanina ba kuma naji dadin haka"labarta musu abunda ya faru yayi sanan ya cigaba da fadin "Sai mugodewa Allah da hakan ta faru ,Allah ya ma albarka Allah ya sanya Alkhairi ,sannan magana ta gaba ina neman wata Alfarma a wurinka " ya fada yana kalon IRFAN d'in,shi kam Wace Irin alafarma Abbi zai roka da zai kasa yi masa ai ummarni kawai zai bashi...

"Abba ka bani umarni kawai zan kasance mai biyaya a gareka" cewar IRFAN d'in Wanda ya matsu yaji mi Abbin sa yake so yayi masa shi cikin gagawa

"Kunyi da Baba cewar idan ka kawo mata ba zaa Aura maka Mufida ba ,ama in tanbayeka kana ganin kayima Uncle dinka Imran adalci zai ga Kenan kamar kafi son bare akan yarsa ,ba xa muji dad'i ba idan dan uwanmu bai ji dad'i ba,Imaran yana sonka ,kuma Baba ma yana son Auren ka da Mufida ,ama baya son yima katsalandan ne shiyasa ya baka wannan choice d'in, munyi shawara da 'yan uwana akan cewar zamu hadaku Aure da yar uwarka ,sai ka ha'da da zabinka" Abbi ya kareshe maganar yana kallon yanda IRFAN d'in ya wani hade rai kamar bai ta'ba dariya ba yasan dama za'arina ,yasan danshi mai adalci ne zai yi ko'karin kotanta adalci a tsakanin su ,sannan yana da halin da zai iya rik'e koma mace hud'u ne...

Ammi ce tace"ina da magana Yaya" ta fada yanainta duk ya canja tasan IRFAN d'inta mai biyaya ne ba ta so a cutar dashi abunda ya saka yayi Saurin nemo budurwa Wanda ita KO waya bata tabajin yayi ba tasan alama ce ta baya son Mufida ,ba za ta so amishi rashin adalci ba dukda mufida diyar yayan tane Uwa daya uba daya ama ba za ta so amma dan nata mafi soyu ba a cikin ranta adalci ba ,in bayaso za ta san yanda za ta yi ta hana abun ba tare da kowa yaji ba dad'i ba.

Abbi ne ya katse mata tunaninta da fad'in "ki dakata tukun har na ji daga bakin shi" cewar Abbi...

IRFAN kam ya rasa a wace duniya zai saka kanshi haba shifah wlh da mace daya yatsara rayuwarshi ,bayada ra'ayin mata biyu,ina zai iya raba adalci koyayi sai wata ta ga ba'ayi mata adalcin ba, duk da kuwa yasan baya son Mufida amma hakan bashi zai sa idan tana karkashin ikonsa ba yatauye mata hakinta ba, Abbi ne ya katse mishi tunani da fad'in "shiyasa tun farko nace Alfarma ,in ba za ka iya ba ka fadamin "

"Abbi na amince Allah yasa hakan shi yafi zama alkhairi" fadar IRFAN d'in Wanda yasan ba zai iya kin neman Alfarma mahaifinshi ba...

Abbi ne yace "to Alhmdllah naji dadi sosai son Allah yama albarka yahada kan zuri'arka ,saï magana tagaba wacece dayan surukar tawa?" yafada yana mai kalonshi ..

"Abbi Nasreen ce yar gidan Abba" fadar IRFAN din 

"Masha Allah ,Alhmdllah kace Abu gida ,gaskiya naji dad'in haka Nasreen yarinyar kirki haka ma iyayenta ,to Abu yayi zanma uncles din naka magana duk yanda mukayi zakaji" fadar Abbi Wanda fuskarshi kadai zaka kala kasan yana cikin farin cikin,wurin Ammi ya jiyo yana fadin ina jinki ....

"Ama Yaya IRFAN din ne zai yi aure mata biyu lokaci daya" cewar Ammi wada yanayinta ya canja 

Tun kan ta karasama Abbi ya daga mata hannu hakan ya saka tayin shiru da bakinta ....

Ummi kam wani murmushi ta saki Wanda ita kadai tasan ma'anar sa ...

Mik'ewa sukayi kowa ya kama gabansa kasancewar Abbi ya salamesu....

IRFAN kam ya rasa wane tunani ya kamata yayi ,taya ma zai had'a mata biyu da wada ranshi yakeso da zabin iyayenshi,anya bai yi rashin hankali ba da ya yarda miyasaka ya amince wai....

Nasreen ce ta fado mishi arai yasan yada take kankanuwa nan duk bata cika kuriciyaba ama bazaa hadata da mufida ba ungozama da ita gashi kanta a waye yake taje jami'aa,anyama su Abba zasu yarda su bashi yarsu

Table din dake gabanshi ya buga da karfi Wanda saura kadan wayarshi dake sama ta fado ,da Sauri yadoki wayar yana Kiran Samir ringin biyu ya doka "haba Malan da daran nan har nayi barcci,ina ce caisse din Nasreen kuma an wuce wurin yanzu kuma miye ne" ya fad'a yana mai Hama alamar dai har yanzu barccin ya ke ji.

Wani irin tsaki IRFAN ya ja yana fadin "aisai kayi tayi ,kaga Malan wata matsala ce takuno kai " ya fada yana mai dafe kanshi

Ganin kamar seriause magana ne yasakashi Samir din mikewa zaune yana fadin "fadi Abokina inajinka"

Labarta masa komi yayi shiru ne yadan ratsa sannan Samir yace "kabari har zuwa gobe zamuyi magana kaji ka kwantar da hankalinta"

Kashe wayarsa yayi danshi kadai yasan abunda yakeji....

Da safe amakare ya tashi sallar ma a gida yayita dan bai samu barcci da wuri ba kanshi ma ciwo yake masa...

Da misalin k'arfe takwas ne yaji ana kwankwasa kofa dafe kanshi yayi dakedan saramasa yaje yabude kofar Wanda Ammi ce taye cikin shigarta ta zuwa aiki gaisheta yayi ta'ansa tana mai kamo hanunsa sukashiga ciki ,zama sukayi bakin gadon inda shi kuma yana dafe dakanshi,kan nashi tadafa tana fadin "Yaro na fadamin miye yake damunka Na-san jiya bakyi barcci ba ko, fadamin in baka son wanan Auren zansa afasa shi kaji" ta fada cikin tausayin Uwa ga danta, shi kuwa Doctor kanshi ya dora aka fadarta yana sauke ajiyar zuciya alamar dake nuna mata dan nata ahasale yake kuka ne kawai bayayi inda tafi ganema gara yayi kukan akan wannan ajiyar zucian 


Hanunta yajinmke cikin nashi sun kai minti uku a hakan sannan yace"Ammi ke uwa ce tagari kullum ke kike fadamin yin biyaya ga iyayena dama Wanda suka girmeni ,Abbi ne fah wannan shine karo nafarko da ya nemi alfarma ta kina tunanin ya kamata nayi watsi da ita bayan ina da halin da zan yi masa ita fadamin Ammi na ,Sannan NABAZZAGA yamin komi ya soni fiye da yanda yake son sauran jikokinsa kinjifa abunda Abbi yace bawai dan baya son Aure na da Moufida bane saidan kar ya shiga hakkina kin ganin na masa adalci kuwa ,kullum ina godewa ubangiji na da ya bani ku a matsayin iyayena ,iyaye na gari Wanda samunsu a wannan zamanin sai antona "ya karashe fadar haka yana mai  cije dan lebensa nakasa Wanda yake jajir dashi kamar ansama sa Jan baki ....

Hawayene yasulalo ma Ammi Wanda tasan na tausayin dan nata ne mai biyaya kanshi ta shafa tana fadin " insha Allah ba za ka tabe ba yarona kana tare da addu'aar iyayenka Allah zai baka y'a y'an da kaima zasu maka biyaya fiye da yanda kayi mana " ta fada tana mai goge 'yan hawayenta ganin haka ya sakashi Saurin girgiza kansa yana fa'din "haba miyasamu jarumar Uwa kamar Ammi na tana zubda hawayenta bayan tasan dan nata jarumine kamarta " yafada yanamai yaba murmushi akan fuskarshi...

Mikar da ita yayi tsaye yana fadin "muje yaw kinyi latti Ammi na bayan ke kike mana fada akan haka" ya fada yana mai dokar mata Jakarta hannunshi daya kuma yana makale da nata,wurin motar ta ya kaita Saïda ya tabbatar ta shiga sannan ya sumbaci hannunta yana mata addu'ar a dawo lafiya,ita ma goshinsa ta sumbata tana maidaga masa hannu ,mai gadi ne yawangalemata kofah yana mata a dawo lafiya ....


Nasreen ce zaune da littafin ahalari tana karantawa gabanta ne taji yafadi "inallilahi " taketa maimaitawa har dai taji ranta ya mata sanyi ,tunani ta shiga yi anyama kuwa DOCTOR yana sonta ,inko hakane bai kamata yaki kirantaba kuma gashi gidan yabar mazuwa batakara tunaninta ba taji auta na fadin "Anty Nasreen Yaya Sani ne yazo yana zaure " mikewa tayi tadako hijjabinta tana mamakin baiko sanar da ita zuwansa ba .

Zauren ta isheshi kujera tabasa ita ma ta zauna dan dama tana so ta fada masa gaskiya kuma ta bashi ha'kuri

Gaisheshi tayi ya amsa ranshi adan hade gyaran murya yayi sanan ya fara fadin "Nasreen wani kishin kishin nikeji wai wani yake zawa wurinki harda dokarki a mota " ya fada yana mai hade rai...

Nasreen kam kanta ta dafe tana mai tunanin sharin mutane wai yana daukarta a mota so daya fah ne ,shine har ana fa'din yana d'aukarta girgiza kai kawai tayi inda abun ya bata mata ba ta san sanda tace "Yaya Sani da kanka kake fadamin irin wadanan maganganu,ai na dauka ko masoyina ne ya fadama hakan ba za ka yarda ba amma shine kake zargina har haka bakomi nagode ,ina so nasanar dakai nazabi mijin Aure inamai baka hakuri ,tunda nake bantajin sonka ba kayi hakuri Allah ya hada kowa da rabonsa ,nagode da soyayar da ka nunamin ba zan manta da wannan ba kanann a matsayin Yaya na san..." bata karasaba taji kamshin turarensa a lokaci d'aya kuma yana mai shiga cikin gidan ko kallonsu bai yi ba....

Nasreen kam bataji dadin ganinsu dayayiba duk dabataga wani canji atatare dashi ba yada fuskar nan take ko yanzu ma haka take ....

IRFAN kam ciki ya wuce gaishe da su Abba yayi kasancewar bayan isha'i ne yana nan ,hira sukeyi kamar yanda suka sa ba za'a kace babu abunda yake damusa amma shi kadai ya sanya yakeji .

Nasreen kam ha'kuri tacigaba da bashi inda shi kuma Sani yace"Ashe ruwa baya tsami banza ,kince Doctor nan ne dayakadeki ,Ashe shine kike so kin gansa da kud'i ko bakomi nagode" fadar Sani yana mai tafiyarshi hade da buga gyanbun da karfi.

Nasreen kam bataso haka ba zataso su rabu ta lafiya amma ko yanzu zata nashi hak'uri...

Tana fitowa daga zauren shikuma yana shiga kasancewar hospital d'in yake son zuwa dan akwai patients din dayake son dubawa

Jitayi an matse ta Wanda tabare baki zatayi ihu taji an rufe mata baki jin kamshin turarensa ya sata gane shine ...

Kara matseta yayi yana fadin "nutsu man ,wato baki salami wannan mumunan ba  ko" ya fada yana mai dan murde mata hannu  ihu ta danyi tana fad'in "wayo Abba kayi ha'kuri wallahi na salameshi" jin haka ya sakashi dan sakemata hannu....

"To kasakeni zan shiga gida"ta fada tana mai  kok'arin kwacewa ,

Dan murmushi ya saki yace" da sharadin sai kince kina So na" ya fad'a yana mai dan take mata kafa ,dan kara tasaki tana fa'din shikenan to kasakeni zan fad'a" ta fad'a cikin shagwaba Wanda ya ja hankalinshi har ya matsa ,ita kuwa tana ganin haka tayi ciki da gudu tana fad'in "na masa wayo" dariya ta bashi wai ta masa wayo dan murmusawa yayi yana fad'in "hmm yarinya zaki shigo hannu" ya fada yana mai shiga motar sa ya ja sai hospital d'in sa....✍️




Ga mai bukatar gyaran kuskure hanya a bude take kutuntubeni tawanan laba kai tsaye 99174891

WANAN PAGEN GABA DAYANSA SA'DAUKAR WANE GAREKU YAN GROUP DOCTOR IRFAN INAJIN DADIN YADA KUKE COMMENT ALLAH YABIYAKU DA KAUNAR DA KUKE MA LITTAFIN WANDA NASAN K'AUNAR DA KUKEMIN CE ALLAH YA BIYAKU DA GIDAN ALJANNA NAGODE😍😍😍😍

```MARUBUTA MASU AIKI DA FASAHA DA ZALLAR TSAKWARON AMANA,  FAƊAKARWA HAƊI DA NISHAƊANTARWA GAMI DA WA'AZANTARWA HUMMM WANI ABU SAI ROYAL STAR WRITER'S          ✍🏻📚✨⭐✨  ASSOCIATION RUWA ƘAƊAI KE MAGANIN ƘISHI  (R..S..W..A)               💪💪💪💪 
         UP-UP-UP```

_🍂"ALƘALAMINMU🖋️ ƳANCINMU"🍂_

Book 1
Page 27/28
               ........✍️Nasreen kam ciki ta shige tana murmushi Wanda ya saka Mama kallonta kasancewar Abba ya shige ciki...

Addu'aa tayi mata cikin ranta Allah yadawama da farin ciki a rayuwar yarinyarta


Abbi kam ya samu iyayensa da yen uwanshi ya fad'a musu yanda akayi kuma sunji dad'i sosai sun saka Albarka cikin abun,inda sukayi sati mai zuwa tsohuwa za ta zo ita da uncle din IRFAN ,wato UNCLE MUHAMMAD and UNCLE RABI'OU dan Jauro sai Anty Fatima ita ma diyar Jauro din sai Malik din ,sun tsaida magana inda Abbi yace zai ma mahaifin yarinyar magana in dai ba'a mata miji ba, kowa yayi na'am da wannan shawara....a haka suka tsaida magana....


 Mufida ce zaune ita da Mahaifiyar ta inda taketa fada tana fa'din "gaskiya Alhaji ban yarda taya za'a hada Mufida da kishiya bayan ,ita dai yafi cancanta da ta zauna ita daya a gidan mijinta ,yarinyata ba za ta zauna da kishiya ba " cewar hajiya kaltume mahaifiya ga mufida Kenan yar kasar Maradi ce Wanda dama ansan su da massifa,
Alhaji Imran din ne wato Baban Mufida yakatse ta cikin wani irin bacin Rai yace"Aure ba fashi ,inko ki ka ga anfasa to shine ya janye ,baki isaba kisa nacanja maganar Iyaye naba" ya fad'a cikin fada Wanda ba dabi'arsa bace kasancewar shi mai sanyi akaf cikin yan uwansa,Wanda y'a y'ansa hud'u ,Jalal ,Mufida, and souhaila ...
Ganin yanda ya nuna bacin ransa ya sakata sasauta fushinta dan tasan idan ranshi yabace to baya sauraran kowa tasanshi sarai mulki takezubawa son ranta ,gata da wulakanci Wanda shiyasaka yan Uwa ba sa son zuwa gidan sabida ko sunzo inba natane ba a wulak'ance zasu koma 
"Ka dai yi shawara amma ni kam y'a ta bazata zauna da mai Mata ba" ta fada tana barin wurin

Shi kuwa Alhaji Imran girgiza kai kawai yayi yana tunanin Wace kalar mata Allah ya bashi duk abunda takeyi yana sa kamata ido ne kawai badan yana tsoronta ba ,duk abunda takema yan uwansa yasani,wanan karon kam saidai ta Mutu Aure ba fashi,mikewa yayi tareda dauro alwala dan wucewa massalaci....


Abbi ne zaune a falon su yana fadawa su Ammi yanda sukayi da yan uwansa" dan haka gobe zanje Ni da Abokina wato Yayan karime,dan mu tambayi izini " ya fada kai tsaye yana kallon Ammi wada ya ga damuwa ta nuna sosai akan fuskarta ,ama zasuyi magana dagashi saï ita ...
Ummi ce tace "hmm Allah yasa rabo ne" kallonta duk sukayi daganan Ammi ta shige dakinta ,dan yaw ita ce da girki kuma tana so suyi magana da Abbi d'in....

Wanka tayi kasancewar har sha 'daya tayi ta isko Abbi dakinshi ,thé dinsa ta'ajemasa Wanda ya zame masa al'ada kafin barcci sai yasaha doli,zama tayi bakin gadon tana fa'din "sannu da hutawa " cewar Ammi tana mai amsa jaridar dayabata ta'aje a inda yake aje documents d'insa .

Gadon tahaye ta juya masa baya ,Wanda bai sa ba ganin haka ba kanshi ya girgiza domin Mata dai abarsu da halinsu ,thé dinshi yasha sai dayakare sanan yace " wai waye ya tab'amin yar kanwata ,oum ni fah bansa ba ganin wanan juya bayan ba ,fadamin wai fushi duk na menene?" Abbi ya fada yana mata cakwulkuli dama intayi fushi daya mata hakan zata sasauto,ai kuwa zaune ta tashi tana yar dariya tare da fadin "Haba Yaya kabari kafa girma" cewar Ammi Kenan 

"A a ni dai ban girma ba ,kona nuna miki " ya fada yana dan matsowa kusanta ,dan baya tayi tana fadin "yi ha'kuri Yaya na aikai baka tsufa a wurina" dariya suka saka inda daka kalesu zakasan sudin massoyan asali ne Wanda basa tsufa...

Murmushi Abbi yayi ganin harta huce sanan yace "fadamin miye yakesaki fargaba a Auren IRFAN dinmu,bayan kinsan Yaro na mai adalci ne Na-san zai iya" yafada yana dan rungumota kadan, dan fuskanta yacanja sanan tace" Yaya ba Auren ne bana so ba ,kasan dai IRFAN Nasreen ya nuna yana so ,ita kuma Mufida zabinku ce bana so Dana ya tashi da barin jiki gobe kiyama duk danasan zaiyi iya kokarinshi ama kasan abunda akeso da Wanda ba'aso badaya bane ko,Sannan kana ganin Abba zai yarda yarshi ta zauna da kishiya bayan itadin kankanuce Mufida ta girmeta ne sa bakusa ba ,tafita sanin Duniya da abunda ke cikinta ina jin tsoro Yaya " cewar Ammi Wanda daka kaleta kasan tana cikin fargaba ...

Abbi ma tunaninshi bai wuce Kar Abba yace yarshi bazata zauna da kishiya ba ,kasancewar tayi kankanta duk da tanada dan jikinta ba ramama bace ama abun kai ba lalai bane ya yarda, murmushi ya yaba fuskarshi dan bayason taga rauni a fuskar shi yasa yace"kinga kiyimasa Adu'a kawai insha Allah komi zaiyi daidai " yafada yana mai rungume abarsa ,ganin haka yasaka nayi waje dan gadukan alamu soyayar tsofin ce tamotsa.....

DOCTOR IRFAN kuwa sunyi magana da Samir wanda ya bashi shawarwari tareda da alakawarin shima zai shigo cikin satin,da wanan shawara yadan kontar da hankalinsa, duk da wani fani yana jin fargaban yanda zai fuskance ta yafadamata yanda sukayi da famillynshi ,ama ya zaiyi doli yafadamata tunkan taji wani wuri dan maganar Duniya bata boyuwa ,shiyasaka yashirya yaw dan fadamata ,sanye yake cikin wata shada Wada akamata simple dinki masha Allah dinki yakarbesa kuwa,sai kamshi yake zubawa....

Da salama yashiga gidan su Abba na kurya Wanda jin salamashi yasaka Abba fitowa yana fadin "a'a DOCTOR shine ka tsaya bazaka shigo ba " fadar Abba din ...
"A a Abba ina Sauri inba damuwa ina son na duba Nasreen din dan tabatar da kashin yazauna ,duk da dai Alhmdllah " yafada yana mai dukar dakanshi kasa.

"To masha Allah bari nafadamata ,ai dakashigo" fadar Abba

"A a Abba adai turota din " cewar DOCTOR din 

Zaune yayi bisa kujerar filin gidan yana latsa wayarsa .

Nasreen ce sanye cikin farin hijabi sai kasa sai kamshi take zubawa na humra ...
Waje ta iskeshi sai latsa wayarshi  yakeyi

Salama tayimasa tareda gaisheshi inda baiko kaleta ba ya'ansa salamar "ina yini" tafada tana mai yimishi kalon kasa kasa.

DOCTOR kam tun fitowarta yakaremata kalo Wanda humrar datasaka tadan fara canja mishi yanayi

Nasreen kam shiru tayi dan tasan matukar bashi ne ya mata magana ba to zata bata bakinta abanza ne .

"Kada kisake saka wanan turaren ,wane ne ma yasiyamiki shi?" yatanbaya kai tsaye yana mai ci gaba da latsa wayarsa 

Nasreen kam izzar nan tashi tafara bata haushi to ina ruwanshi ji yada yakebata comment kamar wani mijinta...
"Dake nake " yakatsemata tunaninta

"Mama ce takeyimana shi" tafada tana mai dan hararenshi Wanda karaf ko suka hada ido.
Saurin sada kanta kasa tayi tana wasa da yatsu hannunta 

Shikam réaction dintama dariya yaso yabasa ama ya dake yace"kada ki sake sakawa " ya fada kai tsaye....

Ita kam mamaki yake bata wannan izzar ba za ta daukaba doli ya ajeta gefe wlh
Gyaran murya yayi sanan yadan saci kallonta ganin yada tasada kanta kasa kamar wada tama sarki karya

Shiyama rasa ta ina zai fara fada mata tsaki ya danja sanan yace " da farko dai zan fada miki wani Abu Wanda ina so kiyi ha'kuri da jinsa sabida ba laifi nane ba ,ina son iyaye na fiye da tunaninki ba na tunanin zan bijire musu sabida soyayya ,so abunda Nike son sanar dake shine Abbi ya zabamin y'ar uwata wanda wanda saidai na hadaku na Aura" yafada yana mai kalon réaction dinta


Nasreen kam jin maganganunshi takeyi tamkar aradu kodai mafarki takeyi ,Aure yar uwarshi kuma atare mafarin ya wulakanta ta Kenan dama tasan kamarshi bazaizo yace yana sonta kuma tayi Saurin yarda ,ta cuci kanta ,yaudarar kanta takeyi dan yanzu dama Aure saï dan maishi da mai ita ,ita kuwa dama mahaifinta rufin asirin Allah ne ...

Wasu zafafan hawaye tashare sanan tayi murmushin yake wanda yafi kuka ciyo ,kishiya fah su biyu Kenan ,hmm itakam bazata iya ganinshi tare sa wata ba yana mai mata wanan murmushin nashi mai kayatar wa ina sai dai ita tayafe,Soyyaya batama adalaci ba, batasan tanada kishi ba sai yanzu dataji yana fadamata wai su biyu zai Aura....

DOCTOR kam kallonta yakeyi badai hawaye ta keyi ba sabida kishinsa,wani kishi sa yake hangowa acikin idonta ashe dai tana son sa

Nasreen kam bata son yasa sabida shi take kuka hakan ya sakata share hawayenta tadan kaleshi sanan tace" hmm dama ai nayi Saurin yarda da abunda zuciyata take fada min bayan ba haka ba ne yaudarar kaina nakeyi ,ka Auri mata hud'u wannan badamuwar Nasreen bace,Nasreen dama bata sonka ,zata rayu da Wanda yasan mahimanci ta ,Wanda zai bata lokacinsa ba mutum mai IZZA ba da nuna tak'ama ba ,Allah ya bada zaman lafiya" ta fada tare da wani irin mik'ewa da Sauri zatabar wurin hawayene ke kwaranya akan kyakyawar fuskarta ....

IRFAN kam tun lokacin da ta fara yaba masa maganganun nata Wanda yakejinsu tamkar madaci ya sakashi dafe zuciyar wai fah bata Son shi ,zata rayu da wani wannan kalmomin su sukafi komi daga masa hankali,yasan za'ayi haka ama bai doka Ranta zai sosu haka hartayi tunanin barinshi ....

Ganin zatabar wurin ne ya sakashi Saurin rikota ta fado kirjinshi,rungumeta yayi yana dan bubuga bayanta Wanda ita kuwa take kiciniyar kwacewa tana dan dukanshi a kirji sai kukanta takeyi mara sauti ,bayanta yakeshafawa sunkai minti hud'u a haka sananan ta fara ajiyar zuciya tana dan shashekar kuka " kiyi Ha'kuri Ashe ba za ki kasance Matar da zata bani kwarin guiwar yiwa iyayena biyayya ba ,shin dama wani yana Auren Matar wani ne fada min ,Ashe dama zaki iya fada min bakya Sona bayan ga Son nan ina gani cikin kwayar Idonki,ashe zaki iya rayuwa da wani ba tare da IRFAN ba, sabida zai yi Aure ,komi dakike gani na Duniya yana da lokacinsa kuma yana tafiya dai dai da k'addara Kane ,ba za ki bari mu rungumi kaddarar mu ba ,Ina Son ki ,ina tsananin 'kaunar ki,Na-san Nasreen d'in Abba jaruma ce miye zai bata tsoro takai duk inda ake neman mace ,zata iya kishi dama ko wace ,fad'amin ko kina tsoron tafiki kyau ne ?" ya fada yana dan lakuce mata hanci Wanda ita kam tun lokacin da ya fara magana taji ranta yayi sanyi ,Tana Son shi sosai bata 'dauka dan ta bata ranta haka ba zai yi rarashinta ,Ashe ita din tana da mahimanci a rayuwarsa ,idan ko haka ne bakomi zata ha'kura ta Aure shi sabida cika Umarnin su Abbi.....

Dariya tadan saki Wanda yake kara mata kyau Wanda yasa IRFAN shagalta da kallonta ,dan tsilikeshi tayi a cikinsa tayi ciki da gudu Wanda ya sakashi dawo wa hankalinshi yana fad'in "washh" kasancewar yaji zafi,murmushi yayi daganan yashiga mota yana mai sauke ajiyar zuciya 

Yanzu matsalarshi daya su Abba anya zasu yarda yarsu ta zauna da kishiya ,yana fatan komi yazo mishi da Sauki...

Abbi kam sunyi magana da Abba inda yace mishi zasu zo gobe akwai magana mai mahimanci ,ai kuwa ga goben sai gasu sun zo shida Yayan karime bayan sallar magrib,cikin mutunci Abba yatarbesu Wanda bakaramin dadi sukajivtarbesu Wanda bakaramin dadi sukaji ba sabida karamasu din da yayi bayan gaishe gaishe ne Abbi yayi gyaran murya yayi add'ua sanan yace" Malam dama wurinka muka zo neman alfarma ,wada muna fatan ya zama silar shigar mu aljanna gabaki daya"cikin fara'a Abba ya amsa dan dama yana neman hanyar dazai sakawa Abba da abunda ya masa ya nuna masa karmci,ya nuna masa Tallaka da mai kud'i duk daya shi kuwa mizai saka mishi dashi,cikin murna yace"masha Allah gaskiya naji dad'i duk da bansan Wace irin alfarma bace amma Na-san ta'alkairi ce Allah yasa zan iya" Abba yafada yana mai Mika dukan hankalinsa dan ji mi za su ce.

Yayan karime ne yace"dama Alfarma ita ce yaron mu wato  IRFAN ne yace yar wajenka ta masa wato Babar dan yace akwai karamar shine muke fatan Allah yasa ba'a mata miji ba?"
Shiru Abba yayi sannan yace "to hakika naji dadi da ya kasance IRFAN ne yake son Nasreen kasancewar shi yaron kirki ,ni shaida ne akan halayensa ni mai iya bashi Nasreen ne ,amma wani hanzari ba gudu ba" ya fad'a yana mai dan kada kanshi

Abbi ne yace cikin Sauri"wane hanzari ne Malam?"ya fad'a yana kallon Abba.

" a gaskiya ba zan boye muku ba akwai dan makwafci na d'aya ke zuwa wurinta ,zan tambayeta insun dai-dai ta in kuma akwai Wanda takeso koshi DOCTOR ne badamuwa ina maraba da hakan "fad'ar Abba Kenan

" to masha Allah Allah yayi muna zabi na alakiri ,sanan muma akwai magana wada ina fatan ba za ta zamo silar lalacewar Abun ba idan yaran sun amince" cewar Abbi Kenan

Abba ne yace yana jinsu "to Malan abu na gaba shine za'a had'a da 'yar kanena "Abbi ya fad'a tare da yi masa bayanin komi,kai Abba ya jinjina sanan yace" to naji bayaninku ,Allah ya musu zab'i na alkhairi,zanyi magana da yarinyar da mahaifiyar ta dan duk suna da hakki duk yanda mukayi zakuji" inji Abba...

Godiya suka masa dagan suka mike suna masu musabaha da juna ,gida suka wuce shima Abba gidan ya shigo kai tsaye falon ya shiga yana mai jinjina ala'amarin ,shikam bazai so diyarshi tare da kishiyaba kasancewar har yanzu Nasreen bata kamali hankalin kanta ba ama in kadarra tace haka zai yi mata addu'aa .

Ajiyar zuciya yasauke yana mai sauraren yaran nashi da sukemasa sannu da zuwa gabaya kasancewar duk suna falo ,ansawa yayi yana mai samusu albarka

Mama ce ta gaisheshi abinci ta gabatar musu dashi suka ci cikin kwanciyar hankli daga nan su Nasreen d'akinsu suka wuce inda akabar Mama da Abba ....✍️


🖊️Maman Fauzan ce🖊️

_🍂"ALƘALAMINMU🖋️ ƳANCINMU"🍂_


Book 1
Page 29/30
       ..........✍️Abba ne yama Mama bayanin komi wada tashiga tashin hankali,ba'a bunda ya daga mata hankali sai jin Nasreen d'inta zata zauna da kishiya kuma kishiyar ma yar uwarsa ,duka Nasreen d'in nawa take ,gaskiya da sake ba za ta yarda ba ,tana fatan Allah yasa ma Nasreen din bata son shi bawai dan bata son Irfan ba saidan ba za ta yarda a cuta ma yarta ba

Kallon Abba tayi sannan tace "Hakika IRFAN yaron kirki ne ama gaskiya Abban su Nasreen tayi kankanta da zama da kishiya haba abun ai akwai alhaki" cewar Mama Kenan

Kallonta Abba yayi sanan ya girgiza kansa yace "kidaina fadar haka ke uwace adu'aa kawai zakiyi Allah ya zaba mata abunda yafi zama alkairi,sanan kirata zanji daga bakinta " yafada yan kalon ta

Mikewa tayi Jim kadan sai gata da Nasreen,kasa tazauna tasada kanta kasa tace"Abba gani "cewar Nasreen 

Hankalinshi yamaida kanta yana fadin " Nasreen ina so kifadamin gaskiya tanbayarki zanyi " fadar Abba Kenan "insha Allah " cewar Nasreen wada taji gaban ta ya fadi ba tare da tasan dalili ba

"Alhaji Ali wato mahaifin DOCTOR yazo min da maganar IRFAN na Son ki,shin kinsar da maganar" cewar Abba yana kalon yar tasa

Shiru tayi tana wasa da hanunta Wanda nan take Abba ya gano shirun budurwa yana nufin tayarda kanshi ya jinjina sanan ya sake cewa "kuma ba ke kadai zaki zaunaba ina fatan kinsan da wanan" cewar Abba

Kanta ta daga alamar tasani cikin sanyi Wanda yasa Mama wani irin zubura tana fadin "ba zaiyu ba inke baki son kanki to ni ina son ki,ke kinsan kuwa miye kishi dasake,ina " cikin wani irin yanayi na fada Wanda babu Wanda bai yi mamakin ta ba cikinsu sabida basu saba ganinta haka ba ,Abba ne yagirgiza kai domin ya fahimce ta yagane mitake nufi Nasreen ya bawa umarni tabar wurin mikewa tayi ta shige dakin nasu

Abba ne yayi ajiyar zuciya ya kali Mama da take dan girgiza kanta yasan yaw kam barbarcin ya tashi zuciyar gado ta motsa, cikin lalami da son fahimtar da ita yace"Haba mana fanna yakike haka gaban yarinyarki karkisa taji ba dadi mana ,kinsan dai yada take mana biyyaya KO bata taba ketare umarnin mu ba yanzu ne ya kamata mu bata dama tasan cewar lale ita ma y'a ce mai yanci ,kiyimata Adu'a insha Allah ba abunda zai faru sai addu'a ,tana tare da adu'ar mu dan haka kisa Albarka a al'amarin kiyi ta istihara kinji Matar Aljanna" ya fad'a yana murmushi ,itama murmshin tamayar masa Wanda jin da'din sunan daya kirata ne,addu'a tayi sanan tace tayarda anan ne ma Abbi yake gaya mata cikin satin nan za'a kawo sadaki da lefe Allah ya nunama na tafada tana mai mikewa dan kule falon dan dama dare yayi.

    Nasreen kam tanashiga daki ta fara tunanin kardai Mama ta hana abun bayan ita babu Wanda takejin zata iya rayuwa dashi sama da shi,kuma ma ai in dai kishiyar ce zatace yara ba musu gida ,kuma ita fah wlh ba ta ga abun daga hankali ba ai kishi shine kayi kok'arin sace zuciyar mijinka malamin su yana musu wa'azi akan haka ,komi munin mace da in dai ta iya sace zuciyar Miji ai shikenan kuma ita bataga abunda wata mace zata gwada mata ba da zai bata tsoro,tana Son shi ji take idan har akarabata dashi ba'a mata adalci ba ,hawaye taji suna sulalowa akan fuskarta ,sharewa tayi wasu na sulalowa ganin su Rufaida sunyi barcci ya sakata mikewa ta je ta dauro al'wala domin neman zabin Allah.


DOCTOR Kam hankalinshi kwance yanzu sai ko wannan abar daza'a kakaba mishi shi fah bai ga abunso a jikin wanan abar ba ,tsaki ya ja, Nasreen dinshi ya tuno wada tahada komi na mata yasan zai kwashi gara a jikinta sabida kalar mace mai dadi da angata an sani wani murmushi yasaki yana tunanin Daren farko ya san ranan kam ya ga takanshi yakice tunanin yayi ya cigaba da aikinshi dan dama aiki yakeyi a office d'in.


Su Uncle sunzo inda masha Allah Abbi dakanshi yaje dokosu dan yayi kewarsu sosai duk da ba'a zo da Hajjo ba amma kosu tsohuwar ma daya gani kamar ya ga Mahaifiyar shi ne,gidan ne yarikice da murnar ganinsu Wanda Gogan yarasa inda zaisa kanshi dan murna ,ga malik yazo ga Samir ya samu abokanan hira duk da yaw ne zawarsu gobe za'a kai sadakin kasancewar Abba ya amince musu kuma yabawa iyayen Sani dashi Sanin hakuri hakan ya saka gobe za'a kai kuma jibi shi Samir din zai koma ,shi kuwa Malik tare dai zai koma da Uncles dinsa da Dady sa su kam Jauro shida Matar tashi Abbi yace sai anyi Auren ko za su koma tunda kosunje d'in ba wani Abu suke ba ba dan sunso ba zasu zauna gidan y'a y'an nasu ba kasancewar su fulani masu kumya ,Su Abbi sun yanke yin biki nan da wata biyu in har su Abba sun amince su zasuyi komi tunda Nasreen koba Irfan zata Aura ba su masu iya yi mata ne bare kuma Nasu,Dama daga can Niamey Jauro ya bada millions biyar yace su duka a had'a musu lefe kowace na millions biyu da rabi, inda Anty Fatima wato yayar Ammi da kanta taje manya-manya shaguna da sukenan Niamey ta hado ma kowace lefe na gani nafada komi iri d'aya ne ko akwatinan lefen ma guda ne sai dai kowace da irin nata color ,ita Nasreen rose aka siyama tare da lesh kala goma,shada kala shida,meterial kala biyar ,atamfa kala ishirin sai takalama da jakukuna da mayafi kala goma goma ,sai kayan kwaliya da sauran Abu buwan lefe dai abun sai wanda ya gani ,itama Mufida nata akwatin bakakene komi yanda aka sama Nasreen haka aka saka mata Alhmdllah kaya sunyi su Ammi sun yaba sai dai ita Nasreen d'in ba'a saka mata brezia ba da pant kasancewar basu san size d'inta ba kuma da akatanbi gogan yace a bari zai siya mata ....


DOCTOR ne da Samir da Malik suna masa tsiyar wai angon mata biyu ya kusa zama magidanci yafita daga gauro shidai murmushi kawai yake musu ganin sunki barinshi ya sashi cewa "to in hitsari banza ne kaza tayi mu gani" ya fad'a yana dan kaima Malik d'in duka a muscule 'dinsa dan dama shi yafi matsa mishi ,Malik kuwa da Sauri ya dafe wurin yana fad'in "aush kai anyaba dokar 'karfe kakeyi ba irin wannan karfi haka wlh matan nan naka Na-san inka rutsa su sun shiga uku" ya fad'a yana mai murza wurin dan kam da gaske yaji zafin,Samir ne ya kece da wata irin dariya yana fad'in "tap dan Allah dai kayima y'a y'an mutane da sauki,barema Nasreen gata kankanuwa dan Allah ka tausaya musu" ya fad'a yana dane dariyarsa dan ya ga yanzu kam sun kaishi kankat

DOCTOR kam hararsu kawai yakeyi ganin ba za su daina ba yasakashi mikewa yana tunanin dan iskan yaron nan da yaso keta gonarshi,sai yanzu da Malik yayi wannan maganar ma ya tuna tsaki ya ja yana fadin zaka ci ubanka ba yanzu ba.

Abba kam ya fad'awa yan uwanshi inda aka samu dan uwanshi yazo sai bangaren Mama ma Yayanta bukar yazo Wanda dama su biyu iyayen suka haifa sai yar uwarta diyar 'yar Maman ta wada suke kira yafindo dan yanzu sunacan yankin Diffa wato kakanin Nasreen d'in ,shine da'a ka fad'a musu suka zo sai makwabtan Mama da suka cika gidan dan ganin wainar da za'atoya kasancewar Nasreen bata ma'amala da yan matan unguwar dan ita bata son tsegumi da yawan surutu shiyasaka yan matan da iyayensu suke mata lakabi da mai girman kai,suna so suga duk girman kan nan nata wazata Aura shiyasaka duk unguwar suna nan da Wanda aka gayata da Wanda ma ba'a gayata ba ,yan Matan ma wasu sun zo inda ita ko ko a jikinta tasan dama tsegumi ne zai kawosu duk da hakan bata nuna musu komi ba


DOCTOR kam yaw jinshi yake cikin farin ciki ,Kamar ranar Auren ne su Sam d'in sai tsiya suke masa shi dai sai dai yadan yi murmushi ya sharesu 

Anty Fatima da wasu kawayen Ammi Wanda ma'aikatsu daya sai makwabciyar su daya ta b'angaren mata ,Maza kam Yayan karime ne sai makwabtan Abbi guda biyu sai uncle d'insa ,mota uku sukayi inda suka samu gidan cike kamar gidan biki Wanda yabawa k'awayen Ammi d'in mamaki har suna cewa ina hankalin Ammi yaje zatabar Danta yayi Aure cikin wannan kazamar unguwar ,akwati goba sha biyu aka kai inda aka rangada guda wata daga cikin makwabtan Mama d'in ta fara ya dama 'yan unguwar habaici dan tasan basu so suga haka ba sai kuskus suke Wanda duk Mama naji ama bata da lokacinsu.

Sadaki an yanke dubu dari biyar (wato jika dari) shelar da aka sakeji na sadakin yasaka wurin kara hargitsewa masha Allah ankai lefe Lafiya  ,kuma su Mama sun bada tukuycin dubu dari da  hamsin da son alkaki guda ,an tsaida ranar Aure wata biyu sai dai muce Allah ya nuna mana 


Kawayen Ammi kam sun yaba da karamci ,inda Kud'in tukucin Mazan sukace sun yafe kawayen Ammi ne da makwociyarsu suka raba kud'in dan Anty Fatima kin karba tayi a cewarta ai danta ta kai sa lefe masha Allah zamuce .

Ummi ce zaune ita da kawarta dan bataje kai lefen ba duk da kuwa Abbi yace taje tace batajin dad'i ,hakan yasa ya kyaleta kawarta ce tace "hmm kewai haka zaki zuba ido Kenan " ta fada tana dan taunar cingam d'inta irin na yen duniyar nan ,murmushi Ummi ta saki tana fad'in "haba Kawata baa fara wasan bafah zakiga yanda zanyi warnig d'in game d'in nan ,ba'a fara komi bafah wai ko bakisan ni ba ,kowa yaci tuwo dani miya yasha " cewar karime tana latsa wayarta tare da wani irin murmushi Wanda ita dai tasan ma'anar sa daga nan suka shiga wani labari.


Yan kallon kwaf kam duk sun watse tare da habaice habaice ita dai Mama godiya ta musu tabawa Wanda ya kama ta bawa wani Abu ta bashi


Nasreen kam k'awayen ta da suka zo ganin irin kayan da aka kawo mata ya saka wasu suke ya ba mata magana cikin magana wai tana 'yar talakawa aka kawo mata wannan irin lefen ,ita dai duk ta fahimce su kawai dai bata da lokacinsu dan tasan hassada ce watacema daga cikin 'kawayen Nasreen d'in na school suke dan mai da musu ,sai magrib duk suka watse a lokacin ne Nasreen ta samu damar gyara gidan tsaf tayi mishi ko kallon kayan batayiba da Anty takecewa takala batayi ba amma taji dad'i sosai sabida dama so take ayi mata kaya na gani na fada Wanda zata ci wanka a gidanta Wanda za'aban banceta da kishiyarta ko da ba'a kawo masu yawaba zata fad'awa Mama tayi mata dan tasan zata iya ,ba yan isha i ne Abba yazo gidan a lokacin ne sukaga lefen shida bukar fatan alkairi sukayi daganan kuma aka dasa hira inda Anty tace tana so a bata Nasreen d'in idan sun shiga hutu kasancewar saura wata d'aya su shiga hutun ,Kenan wata daya zatayi acan tare dasu domin tsumata da irin nasu kayan na kanuri barema da sukaji wai da kishiya zata zauna ,Abba ba zai hana musu Nasreen ba dan dama yarsu ce amma yace zai fad'awa Abbi dan yanzu suna da hakki akanta sunyi na'am da haka inda suka ci gaba da hirar yaushar bo

Nasreen ce zaune tana tunanin yanzu Kenan nan da wata biyu  zata rabu da 
Mama duk da gari daya ne amma tasan zata yi kewar su wayar ta taji tana ringing inda ta duba taga shine doka tayi dan tana son jin muryashi kartaje wurin jan aji yaki kira dan ta fara gane halinshi 

DOCTOR kam abun da yasa ya kira ta su Samir ne suka dage sai ya kawosu duk dashi Samir d'in ya ta'ba zuwa lokacin da Nasreen d'in tayi hatsari amma ya dage shiyasaka ya kira ya fad'a mata za wasu gobe kasancewar Samir d'in ya fasa tafiya sai jibi,ba 'yan tadoka ne shiru batayi magana ba shima shirun yayi,Allah gani gareka ta fad'a cikin ranta dan tasan matu'kar ba ita tayi magana ba shi ba zai yi ba ,"aslm ina wuni"ta katse shirun dan ta san ko shekara zasuyi ba zai yi magana ba 

Shi kam jin dadar muryata ya sakashi shiga wani irin yanayi ,jin ta kara cewa "hello " ya sakashi gyaran murya yana fad'in " waslm gobe zamu zo Ni da su Samir "ya fada kit yana mai kashe wayar

Nasreen kam shekeke tayi da wayar a hannunta duk son jin murya nan tashi da take abun da zai fadamata Kenan amma ba laifin kowa ba ne natane sai ya gane kuranshi 

Ga goben ko shiryawa sukayi ba yan sallar isha'i sukaje inda tayi musu abinci wata shept Wanda yaji naman kaji da kayan miya sai kanshi yakeyi sai jus da cake da ta hada musu da kanta Wanda Abba da kanshi ya bata kud'in ba yan Mama ta sanar dashi ahaka ta jera musu shi ana dan filin gida

Wanka suka ci kowa sai kamshi yake zubawa ciki suka shiga suka gaishe da su Abba kasancewar su bukar sun kuma sai daga baya Anty zata zo taje da ita ,Imran ne ya zo ya rungume DOCTOR Wanda shima ya runguleshi yana fad'in " my lil bro kwana biyu ban ganka ba ko school d'in ne "ya fad'a yana mai kama hanunshi sukayi waje suma su Samir binshi sukayi dan sunga kujerun da aka jera musu rufaida ce tazo ta gaishesu Wanda malik ba ƙaramin birgeshi tayiba 

Nasreen ce sanye cikin blue d'in hijabi Wanda yayi tsananin yi mata kyau ba kadan ba sai kamshi take zubawa gaishesu tayi suna amsawa cikin kulawa banda gogan daya dora Imran akan cinyarsa suna game d'insu ba yan sun gaisa ne Samir yace" amaria bakaya laifi ko kin kashe dan masu gida "ya fada cikin zolaya malik ma kama masa yayi suna zolayar ta da cewa takusa zama ta su dai ,ita dai sai murmushi da take dan saki kasancewar bata san mizata ce ba ,mik'ewa tayi ta basu wuri tana fa'din" ku ci abinci zanje na dawo"
Samir ne yace "a'a kice zamu kwashi girkin amariya"
Ita dai mikewa tayi tana barin wurin Wanda irfan ya bita da harara bata ma san yanayi ba,Samir kam shi da malik abincinsu suka kwasa banda DOCTOR da yaketa maka musu harara ko a jikinsu cake d'in suka taba malik ne yace "tap bro irin wannan abincin haka wlh kahuta Na-san ita tayi ga kuma cake mai dad'i gaskiya in Mufida ta iya shikeman kai kam ka more mata" ya fad'a cikin dane dariyarsa 

Tsiya suke mishi shi dai bai kulasu ba ba yan kamar minti talatin sai gata tazo sanin cewa yanzu sun gama Wanda tasan gogan bazai ciba shiyasa ni ba dai tayi ba 

Gaishesu ta sake suka amsa suna mai mata godiya ce musu tayi bakomi dagan Samir ne yahido kud'i Wanda zasu kai dubu ishirn ya aje mata yana fad'in "yaba kyauta tukuci dan haka ga tukuici nan mun gode kisai kayan kwaliya kirikama abokinmu Allah ya nunama lokacin mu zo musha rawa " yafada wanda kasan zolaya ce ,malik d'inma godiya yayi sosai daganan ciki suka shiga su duka su kai masu Mama sai da safe 

Nasreen kam na inda sukabarta gaba sukayi inda take musu godiya ita ma DOCTOR kam ganin sun shigo cikin zauran kuma su malik d'in na gaba ita kuma dama tana bayansu yasakashi Saurin dawo wa baya wanda ya saka had'ewa da bango motsowa yayi sosai inda har suna jin lumfashin juna kallon idanunta yake, yana mai 'karemata kalo wanda kamshin humra da tasaka yana neman tayar masa da hankali

Nasreen kam idonta akule yake gam duk da tasan idonshi na yawo a fuskarta hakan bai sa ta Budé idon ba 

"Budé idonki " ya fad'a kai tsaye 
Bud'ewa tayi wanda karaf sukayi ido hud'u "baki iya gaisuwa ba ne?" ya je fo mata tambayar ,shiru tayi masa sannan ya sake cewa "ba na ce kar kisake saka turaren nan ba?" ya 'kara maka mata tambayar

Da Sauri tace"kayi ha'kuri na saba ne amma ba zan sakeba"ta fad'a cikin raw raw da ido alamar zata yi kuka ,kallon fuskarta yayi sannan yadan mareta kadan irin marin na na soyaya yace"to shagwababbiya na ha'kura kar ki sake ,kuma kud'in nan ki ajemin rabona " ya fad'a yana mai daga mata gira daya ba ta san sanda dariya ta kubuce mata ba,kallonta yayi yada take dariya sai tayi mishi kyau sosai kamar ya kama bakin nan nata ya tsotsa amma ba komi lokaci zai zo

Ganin ya shagalta da kallonta ya sakata dan gimtsewa a lokacin ne ya dawo hankalinshi yace"ki koma gida sai wani lokacin"
"Allah ya tsare" ta fad'a tana mai yi cikin gidan,murmushi ya saki yana mai fad'in amin cikin ranshi dan ba ƙaramin dad'in addu'arta yaji ba 


Koda yaje Samir ne kemasa tsiyar kodai ya tsaya latse yar mutane ne duka ya kaimasa a bayansa kasancewar wa malik d'in ne ke mazaunin direba sai Samir da ke kujera mai zaman banza shi kuma Irfan baya ya zauna,dariyarsa Malik yayi yana fad'in "kaji ba jiya ba kaine mai dariyata ba yanzu aikaji maza" ya fad'a suna bushewa da dariya gaba d'aya dagan direct gida suka wuce

Book 1
Page 31/32
            ........✍A ɓangaren Mufida ko ba karamin dad'i tajiba da jin wanan labari ,inda Momy wato mahaifiyar ta sameta tare da cemata "ko da wasa kada baban ki yatan bayeki ki amince akan Auren ki da IRFAN zanso ki Aure shi domin ya cancanta ama kinsan matsalar da akasamu kuwa?" nan ta labarta mata duk abunda yafaru

Ajiyar zuciya Mufida ta sauke inda take tunanin itakam ai ba abunda zaihana ta Aure shi ,ina ruwanta da Matar shi kawai zata fadawa Dady cewa ita ce uwar gida ma'ana dai Auren ta za'afara Daurawa

Momy kam mai sauki ce zata shawo kanta,yo ita ina ruwanta da Matar shi ,kowa yayi harkar gabansa ita ba za ta doki raini ba intama kawai uwar ta zata ci,Momy ce ta cigaba da banbaminta ta gaji ta bari ,Mufida kam wani irin farin ciki take ciki burin ta zai cika da wanan takira kawarta Samir sukaci Gaba da tataunawa


Alhaji Imran ya tanbayi yarsa kuma nan take ta amince dan haka bayan kwana biyu da kai lefen Nasreen ita ma aka kai nata ,Momy kam ganin anfi karfinta kuma Mufida naso yasa ka tayi shiru amma ba wai yana nufin ta kyale ba ,yarta ita kadai zata zauna..


Samir kai ga goben shima yashirya ya tafi ,inda Malik d'inma kwana biyu ya wula Niamey, su Jauro kam sunan inda yaw saura kwana biyu Anty tazo taje da Nasreen d'in Wanda Abba ya fad'awa Abbi kuma ya yarda ,IRFAN kai bai da labarin wainar da ake toyawa ,so biyu kawai ya taba Kiran Nasreen shima zai tambayeta ne ko akwai abunda ta ke so daga nan yake kashe wayar ,acewar sa Bashi da lokacin batawa yanzu ,idan ta shigo gidan zata gane wanene shi ,Mufida kam baitaba kiranta ba saï dai ita ta yi ta kiransa inda sai tagaji yake d'auka shima ko minti biyu bayayi yake kashe wayar

Yaw saura kwana biyu tafiyar Nasreen kuma bata fad'a mishi baganin baya da lokacinta,ita ma kam indai basu Abba ne zasu fa'da ba ba za ta fada ba shiyasani....

Mama ce zaune ita da Nasreen d'in da sanyin la'asar suna dan firan su,Mama ce tace" Nasreen " wada yasa Nasreen maida hankalinta ga Mahaifiyar tata 

"Nasreen ke yarinya ce bakisan komi ba gameda rayuwar Aure kinsan kuwa miye Aure?" Mama ta tanbaya tana saka idonta cikin Na Nasreen din Wanda ita kam Nasreen wani irin tausayinta take hangowa acikin idon Mahaifiyar tata, shiru ne yadan ratsa sanan Mama tacigaba da cewa"hmm rayuwar Aure ba kamar rayuwar dakika saba yi bace a gidanku,Rayuwar cike take da kalubale ,jarabawa,Hakuri,kar kidoka zakije ne ki kwanta hmm Aure daban ne a rayuwar y'a Mace" ta fada tana dan nisawa sanan tacigaba da fadin "idan nace jarabawa bawai irin wada kuke a scool ceba ,wanan dabance ama kafin komi abunda zance dake shine Hakuri ,idan har Allah ya baka Ha'kuri to ya baka komi ,danshi yasa ka ubangiji yace " kuyi bushara da masu Hakuri" ina so kikasance cikinsu

Hakuri dai Nasreen komi yasameki kar kimanta wanan ,ki rike Allah akomi shizai isar miki akomi ,ina mai kara jadada miki Ha'kuri " fadar Mama wada hawaye suka fara taruwa a idonta Wanda bana komi bane sai na tausayin yar tata Abu guda kulum zatarika rokar wa yar tata shine kuma hakuri.

Nasreen kam ganin hawaye a idon Mahaifiyar tata ya sakata tashi da Sauri ta rungumeta tana fa'din "kiyi ha'kuri Mama ki daina min kuka,idan kuma Auren ne ba kyaso wlh zan fasa" ita ma ta fad'a cikin kuka

Rungume yar tata tayi tana mai kara jin tausayinta aranta 

"Bakuka nake ba Nasreen dariya nake Allah zai nuna min Auren y'ata " fadar Mama tana mai dungure kan Nasreen d'in Wace itama dariyar ta subuce mata 

Imran ne yashigo yana fadin "kai kai Mama shine kika rungume wanan gorila din" yafada cikin shagwaba ta yan auta Wanda bayita cikin sangartatun y'a y'an nan ba

Ture Nasreen tayi daga jikinta tana fadin "Haba Auta na zo mana rabu da Anty ka ,kasan takusa tafiya tabarmuna gidan ko sai kayita shagwabarka dama kai da ita ne ,dan Rufaida inaga itace ta dau girman ta yabawa kanta" takarasa fadan haka tana dariyarsu ganin yanda Nasreen d'in tabishi suna zagayeta akan sai tajamasa kunne dan yace mata gorila....

Abba ne ya shigo gidan ,ganin abunda yake faruwa yasa ka Nasreen din zuwa wurin Abba tana bubuga kafafunta kasa alamar shagwaba tace"Abba kagani Imran ko wai niyake cewa gorila,kuma Mama ta hana na dakeshi" ta fada tana mai makama Imran din harara Wanda yaboye bayan Mama datahana Nasreen din masa hukunci

Abba ne yace"a'a lale kuwa yau zanyi hukunci "ya fada yana dariya dan ba 'karamin dadi yakejiba idan iyalensa na farin ciki 

Imran din ne yazo yakama kunanshi alamar ban hakuri ,Harar shi tayi sanan tayimasa dan duka abayansa tace" nahakura kanina nasan wataran sai kayita nemana karasa"Wanda fadar haka yasa ka fuskokin iyayen nata canjawa da haka ,sukayi daki inda Mama tace mata ta kimtsa duk abunda Takeda bukata dan gobe Anty zata zo kuma tafadawa IRFAN din tace "Eh ai nafada masa" jin haka yasa ma shiga sabgar gabanta dansu kam bazasu iya gayamishi ba ,tunda sunga yafara kula sarakuta dasu sai yayi kusan sati bai zo ba da waanan barcci yadoketa bayan tagama shirya duk abunda take bukata


   Anty tazo inda Ammi har gida tazo tabawa Anty kudi har dubu dari tace a gyara omata yar tata ba 'karamin dadi sukaji ba ganin Nasreen d'in tayi dacen iyayen miji na gari ,su Nasreen kam sun daga inda ta manta wayarta dama bata cika bata mahimanci ba kira'a kawai take saurare sai number Mama da Abba sai na gogan da Rufaida ta saka mata ,Nasreen kam sai dai muce Allah kiyaye hanya.


   IRFAN kam ya shiga busy sabida ga aikin hospital kwanan na kulum cikin théâter yake kuma duk matsalar mata ce ,wasu zasuje susha maganin matan nan suna tutse tutse a gabansu Wanda basu san ilah sukeyi wa kansu ba su yan dadi miji ,har abun yajanyo musu cututuka agaban nasu yasa ka musu warin gaba ,akwai Wace aka kawo mishi ,Wanda sanadin warin har office din nasa sai da yadume ko bayan fitar ta bayan cika musu file warin ya nan ,gaza zama yayi doli sai dayasa aka kara gyarashi aka saka turaren wuta da na daki ,bakaramin tashi zuciyarshi tayi ba Wanda ya saka shi amai sai da ya amaye kaf abincin dayaci sanan hankalinshi ya kwanta .


Mata sukejama kansu azaba yanzu dan Allah ina anfanin wanan ,yayita Jan tsaki ba'a dadi 

Shi kam wlh bazai yarda ba matan shi surika shaye shayen nan ,Matar kam sanadanin wani matsi da tayine Wanda dagaji ta'aikata shi shine wurin ya kemata kai kayi har infection ta kamata Wanda idan kaikayin yatasar mata ko a gaban kowa hannu takesa wa tayita kirta kirit kirit kakeji Wanda sanandin haka yasa ka shima mijin ya kamu da infection din Wanda 'karamin ciwo ya zama baba harta kai gaban na mata wari Wanda komi turaren da zata saka warin baya fita, shine sukaji labarin hospital d'in DOCTOR IRFAN Wanda sukaji ana yaba kokarin aikinsu da kuma rangwamen su shine suka zo ita da mijin,yanzu dai ya dorasu akan magunguna kafin ya ga yanda za'ayi .


Ga kuma ginin gidan Wanda yasa koshi gaba ,amma yanzu kam ankamala kuma gida d'aya ne dan a cewar sa ba zai iya zarya ba gara wuri daya ,part biyu akayi Babar kofa daya ce kowane part daki uku ne da toiles inshi,sai kitchen masha Allah komi yaji Ammi da kanta ta bada ayo musu order d'in kayan daki day daya inyaso in iyayensu zasu musu wasu ne ita ba za ta hana ba shiyasaka ya shiga busy sosai Amma yanzu Alhmdllah


Yaw tafiyar Nasreen sati d'aya kuma IRFAN ya je gidan sun dan taba hira sanan ya zo tafiya Imran na biye dashi dan yakawo masa chocolat mantawa yayi a motar .

Imran d'in ne yace" Yaya IRFAN Kenan sai Anty Nasreen ta dawo za'a muku Aure" ya fad'a yana karba chocolat 'dinshi da murna a fuskarshi.

IRFAN kam mamaki ne ya kashe shi ina Nasreen ta je bata reda ta fad'a masa ba,hmm lale kuwa zata gane kuranta 

Imran ne ya katse sa da fad'in "Nagode Yaya IRFAN" shafakanshi yayi yana mai tada motarsa ,yana zuwa gida ya kira number ta ringin daya Rufaida ta doka gaishe shi tayi ya ansama ta alokacin ne take shaida masa tafiyar Nasreen d'in fira sukadan yi daga nan yakashe wayar yana tunanin hukunci da yakama ta yayi mata ,ranshi ne ya bace wato ita tafi 'karfin ta gaya masa ko zata gane kuwa,alwala ya doro ya fara na filfili ...



Nasreen Amaria sai gyara takesha nasu na kanuri wani irin turare ne na bakar humra Wanda yalumka Wanda Mama take musu ,yaw satin ta biyu ama karkuga yanda tayi wani frech da ita tasha wanka dilka fatar ta har wani yelo yelo tayi kamar ka latsa jini ya fito masha Allah ,duk inda ta wulga kanshi ne Wanda zai tabbatar maka lale kana tare da amariya ,gashi abincin ta ma daban ne ,dan da safe apple ne abun karyawar ta tare da wani hadi na Zuma ,sai da rana kuma zata ci  abincin su wani irin tuwo tukake ne da miyar baka Wanda shima sai dai akama sa haddi na musaman ,da dare kuma kankana ce da had'in Zuma da madara lait peak shi za ta ci ci kuma magrib nayi Anty zata zubo gaemrwashi kamar mai gashi ta sa mata wani irin turare na tsuguni Wanda ita karan kanta yanzu kam taji da banbanci Wanda yasa ka ta tunanin tambayar Anty taji sabida kasanta sai bulbulowa yake da ruwa masu yawki ,Wanda ta fara tunanin kodai infection ce gareta sabida shinema yanzu daga kare tsugunin ta saka pant d'inta Kenan taji wani zu hannu ta saka taji yauki,aikuwa sai ga Anty zama tayi bakin gadon tana fad'in


"Anty ni kam na tambayeki" cewar Nasreen din ,amasawa tayi da cewa tana jin ta 

"Anty nifah ina ganin infection ne ya kamani sabida wasu ruwa Nike gani masu yauki suna fitowa ta kasana" ta fad'a tana sada kanta kasa


Wata irin guda Anty ta saki tana fa'din" masha Allah  sai inba diyar kanuri ce ba ,gaskiya wanan yaron zai more life" ta fada tare da dan taka rawa irin tasu ta kanuri

Nasreen kam kallonta takeyi dan bata gane mi take nufi ba Anty ce tace"ki kwantar da hankalinki alama ce ta nuna kedin lafiyaya ce kar ki damu da wanan " ta fada tana rungume Nasreen din wada ita dai jinta kawai take 

Yanzu damuwarta bai wuce son jin muryashi data ke son yi ba da son ganinshi tayi kewar shi sosai ,Ammi kam tana kiranta da wayar  anty wani lokacin kamar ta tanbaya ina yayi gashi ita bata da number sa cikin kai kuma Abba ya amshe wayar bare tasa Rufaida ta nemo mata ita ,yanzu haka ya ma manta da ita yana can yana bawa matar shi kulawa ita ko ko oho Kenan bata san sanda taja tsaki ba wanda sai dai Anty ta kaleta bata dai yi magana ta cigaba da had'e Haden ta Wanda shine na k'arshe kuma shi d'in samai gida kukan dadi ne sunanshi Kenan ma...


Ta b'angaren Mufida ma ba'abarta abaya ba had'i ake mata na musaman.Wanda ita gani take wanan shirmene ,hajiya tabawa mai gyaran mata akadoko take gyarata kasancewar dama ita fara ce ba laifi ta kara fari sosai  ita ma kam sai kamshi take zubawa ...


Yaw kam saura kwana goma biki kuma kowane b'angare shirye shirye yakan kama inda ta 'bangaren su Mama sun sa ankerama Nasreen din su wani irin meuble masu kyau kalar zinari masha Allah sai kujerun salon arabe suma kalar zinare sai kayan kitchen da duk abunda suka san yarsu zata bu'kata ba laifi sunyi ko'kari sosai sabida dama Abba yayi yar ajiyarsa Mama ma haka dan sunsan dama rana zata zo ...

Ta b'angaren su Ammi ma ba'abarsu a baya ba suna ta shirye shirye inda order d'in kayan datayi sun iso kuma kowace an samata nata a daki daya ,Anty fatima kam can zata tsaya ayi biki na Mufida da ita kasancewar ta zo kawo lefe shiyasaka ,uncles dinshi zasu zo su uku  sai anty Meri sai sauran  cousines d'inshi ,inda aka raba saura su tsaya anan saura suje can a ranar bikin ma amaria Mufida zata shiga jirgin 'karfe biyu inyaso da marece kafin magarib tadan huta sai ayi duk eviting din da yakamata da wanan aka cigaba da shirye shirye gidan Ammi, Umma kam sam hankalinta ba akwance yake ba kalo daya zaka mata ka fahimci haka.

DOCTOR kam ba 'karamin azalzala zuciyar shi keyi ba da rashin ganin abun kaunar shi amma zai kyaleta zata shigo hannu sai ta gayawa yan garinsu ,Samir ne da malik suka tsara eviting d'in daza'ayi inda yace shifah baya son wanan shimen Ama sun dage sai sunyi ko da biyu ne ranar sa lale da ranar Aure in yaso in an watse sai akai masa amaren shi shi dai jinsu kawai yake

Samir ya amshi number Mufida dan ya tambayeta ko akwai abunda suke so ,daga jin haka kuwa Samir tazugata akan tana buk'atar kudi bai tanbi dalili ba kasancewar yasan amare ba'ara basu da kashe kashen kud'i transfert d'in dubu hamsin ya mata

Inda ya nemi number Nasreen yace tabar aiki anan ya fada msa komi da yake duk awaya ne suke maganar sai ranar sa Hanna zasu shigo su duka suraba kati ...✍
