Book 1
Page 35/36
             ......✍ Mufida kam ba 'karamin ba'kin ciki ta ji ba daba'ayi eveting d'in nan da ita ba amma nagaba zasu gane kuransu,itama bayan isha'i akasa kata lale masha Allah amaria ta wanku sai dai jiran ranar d'aurin Auren


Ta 'bangaren IRFAN kam suna zuwa gida saura duk sunyi barcci dan haka suma 'bangaren IRFAN d'in suka je sukayi wanka ,dama su Malik kam sun gaji barcci ne yayi awon gaba dasu IRFAN kam wani murmushi na ga yana tasaki shikadai a hakan har Barcci ya zo yayi awon gaba dashi

Ta 'bangaren Nasreen kam tana shiga d'akinsu kayanta ta cire wanka tayi da ruwan hadinta wani sanyi takeji aranta takarda ta doko ta warware rubutu ne kamar haka

"Aslm Matar IRFAN ina miki sannu da zuwa daga inda kikaje kinyi naki hukunci saura nawa ,kinyi abunda kike so gud ki saurari naki hukunci" daga kasan takarda akasa MIJIN NASREEN ,ita kam har ta manta badai abun yamasa ciwo bane tap aikuwa ba ta ga abunda zaimata ,konciya tayi tana tunanin yada eviting dinsu ya kasance dazu ashe dai yana sonta ,ita kadai take tasakin murmushi "komi na Doctor mai kyau ne" tafada tana Jan bargo


Shirye shirye ya kama inda Abbi yayi carte din Aure ya rabama duk jama'arsa ,shima Samir haka yayi ya aikama su Nasreen yace tabawa su Abba godiya ta musu ,tako wane bangare shirye shirye ake inda har ALLAH ya kawomu yau ranar daurin Aure

Daurin Auren kuma dama karfe biyu na Rana za'ayi shi inda rabawa akayi za'a daura na Nasreen din kafin na Mufida ,Na Mufida acan za'a daurashi karfe hudu na marece 

Nasreen na gani cikin wani baki bakin sahari wand yayi matukar kamata kasancewar fatar jikinta tayi. Wani yelo yelo duk da ita din ba. Fara ba ce kuma ba za'a kirata da bakaba ,Anty ce tabata wani Abu a cikin Kofi tace ta shanye Wanda itakam wlh wanan abun yafara isarta dakyar tashi sai yamutsa fuska take dan maganin da dan dacinsa

Masha Allah sai anurin amarci take 

IRFAN ne nagani konce cikin bargo Wanda yanzu wajen karfe goma ne shikadai adakin yau kule kansa sai barccinsa yakesha kasancewar kwanan bayasamu barcci sosai ,hayaniyar mutane ce ta hanasa karasa barccin NASA sai tsaki yake ja tashi yayi ya dafe kansa dake dan saramasa ,bubuga kofar yaji anyi Wanda baya raba dayan biyu Samir ko Malik ne ,aikuwa murya Malik d'in yaji yana fadin "ka ga Malan ka budema mutane inbadan ma kana rago ba taya za'ayi ranar d'aurin Auren ka ka zo kayita barcci " ya fad'a yana kara sukan kofar danyasan in ba haka yamasa ba bazai zo ya bud'e masa ba

IRFAN kam sanin halin Malik din ya sakashi bude masa hararsa yayi inda shikam yashigo ciki hanunshi doke da wata gizner blue sai hulama ba blue ce sai takalma bakake ,da agogo ajemasa yayi nan bakin gado yana fadin "mtsss rago kawai " fadar Malik din Kenan 

IRFAN kam dariya yayi yana fadin "hmm rago KO kajama y'a y'an mutane zaka rago kuwa" yafada yana cire jalabiyasa yadaura zanin wanka "kaga Malan fita har nakare " yafada yana budema Malik din kofah danshi wlh ytsani yana shiryawa asashi gaba da kalo ..

Malik din ne yace "mtsss irinku ne inkun yi Aure da gajeren wando kuke yawo " yasan magana yacakamasa ama sai yashare yarufe kofar wanka yashiga cream dinshi yashafa yataje sumarshi turare yafesa sanan yasaka kayanshi ,wando ne da Riga trix car sai Babar Riga daga sama wanan shine farkon sawar Babar riga ajikinsa agogo ya daura wanan yasaka takalmansa hula yadora masha Allah nafada ganin irin kyawun da yayi ,shikaran kansa Saïda yaburge kansa jikinshi yakara feshewa sanan yadoki wayoyinsa yarufe part din ,yana son ganin Aminsa dan tun shekaran jiya rabonsa da ita 

Hakan yasa shi yi bangarensu duk da yasan akwai mutane ama shifa babu ruwanshi tunda Amminshi yazo gani duk da yasan zaisaha tsokana 

Aikuwa yana shigowa falon tsohuwa na fadin " iye kaga angon Nasra take kowa da ja'irar nan dasunanta har yanzu ban iyashiba ,wato suna neman kwacemin kai KO duk abinsu dai Nice uwar gida " tafada tana dan dariya ,shima dariyar ce takubuce masa Wanda duk mutanan dake wurin  bakaramin burgesu yayi ba ,kusanta yazo yaduka yana fadin "haba hajiyata kinsan aidama ke kadaice a zuciyata sudin me uwar gida sarautar mata " ya fada yana kama hanunta 

Diyawa dariya suka saki dan bakaramin bugesu dramar su tayiba albarka tasaka masa sanan yagaishe da mutanan dake wurin 

La mama naga taci wanka cikin blue din shada kamar dai ta IRFAN kasancewar anko din famille Kenan dagudu tazo tarungumeshi kasancewar yamike zaije dakin Ammi ,murmushi yayi yana kama hanunta ,kalonshi tayi tana fadin "yaya kasha kyau sosai ama Na-san Anty Nasreen saita fika kyau" tafada cikin yarinta ,murmushi yayi yana fadin to "uwar su rutu kinsha kyau Autar Ammi kamar na saceki" yafada yana kama hanunta suka haura wurin Ammi

Kwankwasa kofar akayi inda akabada izinin shiga ,dakin daga Ammi sai kawayenta da salama yashiga gaisheda kawayen nata yayi suka amsa cikin kulawa tareda masa fatan alkairi shidai kansa akasa ganin haka yasa Ammi janshi sukayi dakin Abbi kasancewar akulema yake


Ammi ce tabawa la mama yan kunne takaima sharifa kasancewar dama sutazo nemomata saiga IRFAN din 


Zama tayi abakin gadon shikuwa zai zauna akasa tayi Sauri tadashi tana fadin "habadai ango taya zaka zauna akasa yaw fa ranar farin cikin muce " tafada tana zaunar dashi saman gadon 

Zama yayi tare da fadin "a a nidai Ammi da kinbarni na zauna akasa ,ko sarauta na haw idan nazo gabanki doli na durkusa " yafada cikin shagwaba ,hanunshi tasa cikin nata tana fadin "to yanzu dai na yafe " kanshi yadora aka fadarta ,shiru sukayi saican yace "Ammi yau ne fah wlh Ammi tsoro nakeji ina tsananin Son Nasreen, Ammi ke shaida ce bana son Moufida kar Allah yakamani da laifi" yafada wasu hawaye suna dan sulalo masa ,kanta ta girgiza masa tana fadin "haba mi yasamu jarumin Dana naga yana neman karaya ,Na-san da na mai adalci ne ko awaje bare kuma a gidansa Na-san zaiyi adalci wa matansa ,zaka iya yaro na" tafada tana jinijina hanunta cikin nashi alamar karfafa guiwa 

"Kaganka kuwa masha Allah kafito sak kamar balarabe ,yaro na kyakywa ne masha Allah ,Allah yakauda idon makiya akanku" tafada tana dan digamasa kwalindatasaka a idonta zuwa gefen idonshi daya ,dariya yayi mai sauti sabida ba'abunda yafimasa dadi kamar yaji Amminshi nayaba shi ,itama dariyartayi tana mai rungumeshi

Kwankwasa kofar akayi inda bakowa bane sai sharifa datazo wurin Ammi dan tadaura mata dan kwali kasancewar la maman tafadama ta inda zata sameta 

Sakinshi tayi tana fadin shigo 
Sharifa ce tashigo itama cikin blue din shada dinkin iri daya da na la maman ,tana shigowa tayi arba da yayan nata aikuwa dagudu tarungumeshi ganin irin kyan da yayi ,shima rungumeta yayi yana fadin "to yar lukutar gidanmu kar kikada ni" yafada yana dungurema ta kai 
Shagwabe fuska tayi sanan tace "Ammi kinga yaya ko " dariya tabasu dan dama tatsani acemata yar lukuta ,wanan kuma dama Al'moctar ne yake fadamata insu na fada kasancewar su mabiyan juna shine fah kowa yakama idan suna son tsokanar ta zasuce mata haka

Wayar Ammi tadoka tsakansu tashiga tasaita caméra din tana fadin "Selfie" aikuwa duk dariya sukayi ta kyasta musu photon ,asidiq ne da al'moctar suka shigo inda suma sunsha wanka abinsu seflie akayi dasu daganan Ammi ta sulale itada IRFAN suka barsu 


IRFAN Kam wurin Abu kanshi yaje awani baban gida kusa da nasu nan suka kama shida abokanan shi Wanda saï tsokanarshi suke shikuwa sai murmshi yake saki Sabida zuwan wanan rana ta musaman gareshi

Bangaren gidan Amaria Mufida kam uncle Ousmane yadauka Daurin Auren karfe sha biyu ne sai gashi andaura tunda duk sunanan kuma Abu gida ne shabiyu da minti goma Naji mai shela na Fadin "Masha Allah an daura Auren Amaria Mufida Imran JAURO da angonta IRFAN ALI NABAZZAGA bisa sadaki dubu dari biyar ,Allah yabada zaman lafiya" haka mai shela yayita fada inda akashiga yiwa juna barka 

Mufida kam kawarta Samira ce tafadamata itada take waje wani katoton ajiyar zuciya tasauke tana fadin "Masha Allah ,Allah nagodema daka nunamin wanan rana" tafada tana taka rawa kamar ba Amaria ba da akasani da kunya kawayenta kuwa sai zugata suke suna fadin "kaji Manyan Mata Matar DOCTOR da girman kujeraki" sai iskanci suke iri iri 

Ta bangaren su Abbi kam ana daura Auren uncle Rabi'ou yakirashi dafe kansa Abbi yayi yana fadin "ousmane saifa bayan la'asar za'adaura na Mufida ya kukayi haka?" yafada yana tunanin tara Abba zaiji ba dadi zaiga kamar da gangan sukayishi sabida Mufida tasu ce bayan bahaka bane ,uncle Ousmane dai hakuri yabashi tare da rantsewa sundoka shabiyu ne uncle Imran ma haka yayi ta ba Abbi hakuri wlj basu sani bane kar acema dan tana yarsa ne suka hada baki ,kasancewar Abbi yasan halin yan uwansa basama sa kariya yasakashi cewa shikenan Allah yasa hakan shiyafi zama alkairi ameen suka amsa suna mai kara bashi hakuri,shidai kashe wayar yayi ta lalubo ta Abba bai yi kasa aguiba yafadama masa abunda yake faruwa

Ajiyar zuciya Abba yasauke yace "Allah yasa haka shiyafi zama alkairi da haka suka katse Kiran 


Karfe biyu nayi naga jama'a dimkim bakin kofar Abba masha Allah harda gomna yazo da manya manyar jahar ,mai shela naji yana fadin " Masha Allah an daura Auren Amaria Nasreen da angonta IRFAN bisa sadaki dubu dari biyar " kabbara kawai kakeji sai barka akema juna masha Allah ,Samir ne naga yayi wani tsale yarungume IRFAN yana tayasa murna kowa sai murna yake tayasa shikam gogon sai murmushi yakeyi hardama dariya idan kayi sa'a 

Wurinsu Abba sukaje suka gaishesu Albarka sukayita sakamishi a Auren da fatan zaman lafiya  su Malik ne suke ansawa shima dai cikin ranshi yana ansawa da haka suka kama hanyar zuwa gida danci gaba da shirye shiryen eviting din 

Ta bangaren Amaria Nasreen kam jin kawarta tafadamata Daurin Auren yasakata fashewa da kuka tanajin wani nauyi na hawa kanta kukan ta tasha inda cousine dinta sai tsokanar ta suke da kukan dadi take ,itakam Kukan rabuwa da iyayenta take dakuma kaninta duk da gari guda ne ama ai ba wuri guda bane ,ganin tana kuka taki bari yasaka Anty janta tana rarashi da kyar dai tayi shiru abinci tadebomata Wanda cokali uku kadai tayi tace amai takeji badan taso ba takyaleta 


IRFAN kam bangarenshi yadawo yasake wanka cikin wata gris din shada bakaramin kyau yayiba da haka yakoma wurin abokanshi Wanda har yanzu yadauka Da Nasreen dinshi aka fara daurawa la'sar nayi Abbi yakirashi gaban uncle dinsa yakwashi gaisuwa alokaci ne ma suke fadamasa yanda Daurin Auren yakasance

Fuskarshi tadan canja Wanda tundaga nan su Abbi suka sare da al'amarin yaron nasu Kenan dai dagaske ne yafi son Matar shi daya ganin haka uncle Muhammad yafara yimishi nasiha da kwatanta masa yin adalci atsakin matansa da ya'yansa da kwatanta masa hakinsu ,Abbi ma yakara da tashi nasihar godiya ya musu yamike yayi bangaren shi yana tunanin shikenan fah yanzu d wacan abar zaifara sauke bukar shi ,shifah wlh Ruwan nan daya tara Nasreen ce yatara masu yo yanzu Kenan sai wata Mufida tsaki yaja ba'adadi ,wai Mufida yoshi wanan ai ci guda zaimata ta mutu ,mutuwa mana baiga alamun jarumta awurin ba kuma yasha alwashin koma Wace ce ta farko bazai dagamata kafa ba wlh zai sagargaza ta ,tunda Allah yasa Matar tushe ce aishikenan tashiga ukunta ,Nasreen dinshi yatuno baiso hakan takasance ba yaso yaw yakwashi gara yanunamata irin tasa kaunar ama KO yanzu Allah yakai damo ga harawa KO baiciba yayi birgima asama murmushi yasaki,yana tuno irin dramar da za'asha idan tasan ba'itace uwar gida ba yaga nata kalar kishin murmshi yakara saki daganan ganin magrib takusa yasakashi yin al'wala ya iske su Malik inda suke 


Amaria Nasreen kam zaune take itada da Abba da Mama a dakinsu kuka takeyi kamar ance mata Mama ce tarasu Nasiha sukayi mata mai ratsa jiki bare ma Mama datasan halin Mata kuma ance mata ta girmi Nasreen dinta takuma fita wayewa ama duk da haka tabawa yar ta karfin guiwa tama ta nasihar dama baikamata tama ta ba ,Nasreen kam duk taji Abun da iyayenta suka fada kuma tayi alkawari zata yi aiki da nasihar su duk da kukan da take harda na haushin yaw Kenan bazama taganshi ba KO ,ba wurinta zai kwana ba itace a ta biyu ba komi Tana rokon Allah ya yayemata kishin nan nata kamar yanda Mama tafadamata sai ta kauda idonta ama anya zata iya kuwa kukan ta tasha Mama ma Saïda tayi hawaye da kyar Abba ya rarashesu sukayi shiru ,ganin magrib ta gabato yasaka shi mikewa yayi waje ,itama Nasreen Anty tafadamata ruwan wanka sanan tajata takaita bayi KO yaw dai dijat ce  zata mata kwaliya shada fara aka dunka musu su duka biyun har angon nasu kuma duk Ammi ce ta dunka kowace an kai mata tata Nasreen na fitowa sallah ta gabatar sanan aka fara dijat tafara tsara mata kwaliya


Ta b'angaren Mufida ma sai 'karfe shida suka shigo gidan Ammi aka wuce da ita dan KO bakomi iyayenta ne kuma yan uwanta na can babu ma inda yafi cancanta aje da ita sai nan d'akinsu sharifa aka ajeta ita da Samira sai Anty fatima da tabiyosu saiko kawar Momy daya ,a haka aka tarbesu da abunsha da na ci ,Anty fatima ce tabata umarni taje gaishe da Ammi duk da tasan ta kasancewar tana zuwa can Niamey din kuma intaje takanje har gidansu 

Gaishe da Ammi tayi da 'kawayenta cikin kulawa suka a masa mata inda sharifa ta ja ta suka koma dakinsu ,magarib nayi ta shiga wanka dama Ammi ta nemo wada zata mata kwaliya ita kwaliya aka fara mata 

Ta 'bangaren IRFAN bayan sun gama isha'i komi yadai dai ta ne ya samu ya sakeyi wanka ,shima yanzu shadace fara kal da ita da Babar Riga sai ma yafi dazu kyau sabida komi ya canja takalmi agogo da hula Masha Allah nafada ganin irin kyau da yayi 


Gidan Mama kam Nasreen ce na ga anmata wata irin kwaliya wada inbadan Nasan Da Nasreen d'in bace Amaria dabazan ganeta ba Masha Allah tasha kyau cikin farar shadata sai kuwa mayafinta fari shima sai takalmanta farare komi anmata marié Anty na shigowa tace "Masha Allah my daugther gaskiya angon nan yamore" dariya dijat tayi tana fa'din "aikam ba karya acikin maganar ki sis" da haka suka jata sai wurin gwagwaninta Wanda daga nan dama gidanta za'a wuce da ita ,nasiha suka yi mata mai ratsa jiki har ta fara kuka Anty tace idan kika bari hawayen nan suka zubo ni da ke hakan yasa ta maidasu d Sauri dan tasan Halin antyn tata akwai zafi


Ta 'bangaren Mufida ma ba 'karamin kyau tasha ba ita ma komi nata fari ne tubarikallah 


Kasancewar sai an kawo Nasreen nan ga sanan a had'asu amusu nasiha inyaso daga wurin eviting din su wuce gidansu 


Motoci aka kawo Wanda suka zo dokar Amaria aikuwa ana cewa ga motoci sun zo dokar Amaria yasa Nasreen tashi da gudu ta rungume Mama dake falon wada takejin zuciyar ta acunkushe wasu hawaye ne suka sulalo mata itama rungume yar tata tayi ,Anty ce taja Nasreen d'in tana fadin "haba Fanna ke da zaki rarasheta kuma kece mai sata kuka" ta fada tana jan Nasreen d'in wada kadan a babu tayi ihu janta tayi sai cikin mota ,direct gidansu Abbi aka wuce da ita inda duk da dare ne mutane ne jin kam a wurin ciki aka wuce da ita sai sama falon Abbi sukaje inda itadai kanta akasa bata san dawa dawa yake wurin ba ,zaunar da ita Anty tayi aka shiga gaishe gaishe inda itama Nasreen cikin sanyinta ta gaishe da iyayen mijin nata cikin kulawa suka amsa 

Mufida ,IRFAN ,Nasreen Abbi ,Ammi,Ummi,Uncles ,Jauro,Tsohuwa sai Samir da Malik nasiha suka musu mai ratsa jiki inda kowa aka bashi matsayinsa da hakin da ya rataya a wuyansa barema gogan da kowa yasan abunda yake cikin ransa da wanan aka salamesu da fatan alkairi 


Cikin wata farar mota kirar Benz IRFAN ne ya fara shiga sannan Samir yabudema Nasreen gefe d'aya Malik ma ya budema Mufida ya kasance sunsa IRFAN d'in tsaka tafiya sukeyi sai sautin kidan da Malik yasa yake tashi motoci ne akala sun kai sha biyar suka jero Masha Allah a hankali suke tafiya ,kan kowace akasa sai IRFAN dans latsa wayarshi yayi kamar ba kowa saishi kadai ,motar ce ta ba da wani irin kamshi kasancewar kowa Da irin nashi kamshin 


Gogan kam shiru yayi abunshi duk kowane b'angaren kanshi yake shaka amma na Nasreen yafima sa dad'i ,da haka suka zo cikin guest hausse d'in 

Motoci nayin parking na ga mutane duk sunyi cikin hol d'in kasancewar ya tafi da imaninsu da haka Ango dai bai fito ba sai da aka samu komi ya dai dai daita sannan na ga an bud'e b'angaren Mufida ,ita ce ta fara fitowa sannan IRFAN sai Nasreen ta daya 'bangaren 

Tsaka suka sashi inda caméra sai haskasu takeyi diyawa sunfi ganin dacewar Nasreen da Doctor Masha Allah a hankali suke tafiya ,Mufida kam hanunta tamakalo cikin nashi hannun ganin haka ya saka shi sakala nashi cikin na Nasreen,wada ita dama ba ta ga abunda yake faruwa ba ta dauka dai shine yayi niyar yin abunda kujerar da akatanadar dansu suka zauna inda sukasa angon nasu tsakiya mufida kam sai watsa hakura take inda Nasreen kam saidan murmushi da take saki wani lokacin 

Maman Haidar na gani,Ummu Safiya,Aisha Walid,Aisha Tasi'u,Aisha Auwal ,Laila ,Maman Aisha,su Ummisalma,an hayake gaban kujeru sai watsar ha'kura suke sun zo Auren dan Ammi da Nasreen d'inshi 
Daga bayansu Maman sadiq ce da Maman Zayan,Queen Sadiya,Marwanatu  sai Harar Mufida suke Wanda bansan mita tsaremusu ba 

Tadaya bangaren kuma Anty khadija kanoma ,Ammin IRFAN nagani zaune suna kalon yada shirye shiryen yake tafiya

Ko ina mutane ne Dijé ne ya'amshi abun magana ya bada umarnin yin shiru ,tsit kowa yakeji "jama'a da farko zanmika gaisuwata ga amare tare da angonsu,sannan muna Mika gaisuwarmu ga manyan baki wato Wanda suka zo spécial dan Auren dan gata muna godiya ,Programme d'inmu na farko shine mun gayato muku shahararen mawa'kin nan wato Umar M Sharif ," ya fad'a inda anan take ya bayana bisa podium d'in waka ya fara saki inda ya wake Amaria da ango aikuwa su Maman Haidar da Ummu Safiya naga sun fara tikar rawa kamar Allah ya aikosu wurin ne ya hargitse sosai inda amare suka shigo fili da ango dan tatakawa sukeyi inda Moufida ta kamo hanun IRFAN Wanda bai ri'ke na kowa ba amma ita dan iyayi sai takomo nashi Wanda baya son kumyata ta ya saka kama hannunta sukadan fara takawa Fatima saud na ga ta mitsine Mufida Wanda ya saka sakin hanun shi da Sauri aikuwa Nasreen ba 'karamin shaka tayi ba ,ama ganin yanda masoyanta suke Harar Mufida ya sakata sharewa 

Bayan ankare raye rayen ne inda su Aisha Walid suka tiki dawa aka gabatar da sauran programme d'in lokacin ne aka fara yabon abinci aikuwa su Ummu Safiya na ga suna gyara ledodinsu dan kwasar guraye ,anci ansha anyi watsi da CFA  kamar ba'a sonta sai sha biyu aka watse inda su Maman Haidar aka cika ba'kar Léda da guraye 


Kamar yanda su ka zo haka aka maida sauran inda aka dokosu ango kam direct gidanshi a kayo dashi Wanda a gajiye yake......✍

_🍂"ALƘALAMINMU🖋️ ƳANCINMU"🍂_

Book 1
Page 37/38
              ......✍direct gidansu aka ajesu inda ko Wace 'kawayenta ne suka je da ita part d'inta kasancewar dama tun jiya aka yi musu gyara .

Mufida ita da 'kawayenta biyu suka wuce da ita inda sashin nata sai ba za kanshi yakeyi masha Allah na fada ganin irin tsaruwar dakin komi na falon ruwan tokane ,dakin daya suka kaita Wanda shima sai baza kanshi yake bisa gadon suka ajeta ,aikuwa ta bud'e mayafin tana fadin "haba wlh da zafi " Samira ce takwashe da dariya tana fadin "ai wlh baki fara fadin da zafi ba keda zakuci amarcin ku ayaw " ta fada tana kara kwashewa da dariya

Ita ma d'aya kawarta ta wada cousine d'inta ce ta wajen Mom dariya suka saka ,Sanan ta tashi ta shiga bayi wanka tayi da irin turaren da Mom ta bata sannan ta shafa mayukanta tasaka wata antanfa Wanda Riga da siket ne mceaup tasakeyi kasancewar ta gwanar son kwaliya aikuwa ba laifi tayi irin nata kyan mayafi tayafa tahaye gadon tana jiran angon nata kasancewar su Samir bayan shigarta wanka Malik ya fad'a musu inbasu fito ba su tafiyarsu zasuyi dan IRFAN ne ya dage akan lale shi baza'a raka shi ba in dama Abar so ce ,Malik ne yace ai ya kamata a musu nasiha ko ya fad'a yana. Dariya hararsa IRFAN yayi yana fadin "ni da kai na na'ishesu nasiha" ya fad'a yana yin baya ganin kawayen Mufida sun fito da na Nasreen ,duk cikin motar suka shiga Samir kam dan gulma baya ya dawo dai dai kunan IRFAN yana fadin "Aboki na dan Allah kabimin kanai a hankali kaji barema Nasreen ,Moufida kam Na-san yau tuzuranci shekaru talatin da wani abu ne za'a sauke mata " ya fad'a yana barin wurin ,murmshi IRFAN ya saki sannan ya daga masu hannu 

Kanshi ya dafe daya ke dan Sara masa,shi yanzu yama zaiyini wai ,doli zaije ya duba Abar 'kaunar sa kafin ya je ga wacan ,tsaki ya ja y'a wuce sashin Nasreen d'in

Nasreen kam ko da k'awayenta suka tafi kuka tasha baka dan ba ,kukan ta take tasha Wanda bana komi bane sai na jimamin rabuwa da mahaifiyar ta yau dai gata a wata Duniya ta daban ,karar bud'e kofah taji Wanda bata raba dayan biyu shine,bata dagoba daga luluben da take ,kanshi turaren sa dataji shi yatabatar mata daya na kusanta 


Shikam IRFAN shigowa yayi sannu a hankali yake takowa zuwa wurin gadon kalon ta yake ta cikin mayafin Wanda tun a falon dama yakejin shashekar kukanta ,hawa yayi bakin gadon tayanda suke fuskantar juna hannu yakai ya yaye mayafin baya yayi dashi ,inda fuskarta tabaya kalo yake kare ma fuskarta yanda tajike sharkap da ruwan hawaye ,shibai masan ta ina zai fara lalashi ba dan baisaba ba sai ga kanansa suma aiba wai zaibata lokacinsa bane wurin lalashinsu inyayi suka kiya tsawa yake musu ,gashi bai kamata yamata tsawan ba kasancewar yasan tana kuka ne sabida rabuwa da ahalinta hakan yasashi dan bata fuska yakamo hanunta yana fadin 

"To miye nikam ,in bkyason Auren wayasaki amince " yafada cikin dan tsawa aikam Nasreen bata san sanda ta fada jikinshi ta rungumeshi tana mai ci gaba da kukanta ,bayanta yashiga shafawa Wanda hakan dayake mata yasata fara sauke ajiyar zuciya sunkai minti biyar a hakan sanan yace "in bkya son zama dani ne sai namaida ke wajen Mama " yafada yana zareta daga jikinshi

Aikuwa da Sauri tashare hawayenta cikin yarinta tace "to shikenan na yarda kamaidani wlh ina son nakoma gida" tafada cikin shagwaba 

Lale akwai Jan aiki gabanshi har yanzu Nasreen kuciya na tare da ita "to shikenan zankaiki ama da sharadin ke zakimin wanka sanan kishafamin mai kisakamin kayan barcci " yafada shima cikin shagwaba 

Dariya ce takubuce inda tadan bugi dantse hannunshi ,aikuwa wurin yadafe yana fadin "tap irin wanan kulin haka kamar hanun gardi baki isaba sai na rama " ya fada yana kokarin kamota ganin ta zile

Aikuwa falon tayo da gudu inda shima ya biyota harta kai kofar ta ji ta akule gashi yana bayanta babu damar gudu aikuwa ganin haka tasu lale tayi kasa daga jikin kofar cikin dariya take fadin "kayi hakuri na tuba wlh ba zan saké ba"ta fada tare da runtse idonta ganin ya sakata tsakan kanin hannuwanshi ,Irfan kam kallon fuskar ta yakeyi sosai yanda take dariya kamar ba ita bace ta gama kuka dazu ba 

Budé idonta tayi ganin irin kalon da yake mata ya sata dan kara mitsileshi aikuwa da Sauri ya fara yi mara tsakulkuli sai dariya take abinta yana dan tayata yasan hakan kawai ne zai ragemata zafin datakeji bare kuma in ya tafi ya barta 

Caca bar ta yayi kamar baby bai ajeta KO ina ba sai bi sa gadon Wanda har lokacin dariya take ,kasancewar a kwance take ya saka shi hawa dan samanta ya zamana suna fuskantar juna 

Wani irin kalo yake aikamata Wanda itadai tasan ma'anar shi ,itama kalon nashi take ,bakinshi ya matso kusa da nata labansa ya dora saman lebanta na kasa yafara dan wasa da shi ,dan shafawa yakeyi Wanda har ya samu nassarar ta bude bakinta ,ai kuwa harshenta yakamo yafara mata wani irin Salo Wanda yasa Nasreen fara hawa network ,salive inta yake dansha inda shima yake tura mata nashi ,Nasreen kam wani Abu taji yana mata yawo a cikin jiki Wanda bata san kona miye ba ,DOCTOR kam yafara fita hayacinsa ,hannunsa hankalinsa ne yayi matukar tashi baka dan ba domin jiyake sha'awar shi na motsawa Wanda yasan yaw kam idan bai samu abunda yake so ba kwanan shi sun kare

Yakai minti biyar suna tsotsar juna Wanda Nasreen ji take kamar kar adaina 

Hannunshi yakai yadan dagota inda ya zuge zip d'in rigarta tasaman yadanyi kasa da rigar wani kamshi ne yadaki hancinsa Wanda yasashi kai fuskar sa cikin wuyanta yana sunsuna ,kissing dinta yake yi tundaga kan wuyanta har yazuwa wurin kijinta Wanda badan brezia din datasaka ba da sai bisansu ,brezia din wada ita ma fara ce ,hannunshi yakai sama yafido kirjinta Wanda bulut suka bayana Wanda kan yamike sosai yayi baki da shi ,kalonsu yayi yaga yanda suke kalonshi kamar zasu tsokane mishi ido

Bakinshi ya kafa a wurin yafara yimata wani irin Salo halshensa yasa a wurin dan kan yana dan yawo da shi yana zagaye halshensa a wurin ,tsakanin kirjinta ya sakar mata wani irin hot kiss Wanda yasata zabura da Sauri tana neman kwacewa domin kuwa ta kasa jure wanan salon da Irfan d'inta yake mata

Tsotsar dukiyar fulaninta yake kamar yasamu halawa a swl yeke komi brezia din ya barka ta gaba da karfi Wanda yanzu kam hankalinshi yabar jikinshi nishi ya fara saki kadan kadan yana mai cigaba da yimata wanka halshensa a duk inda ya ci karo da shi 

Nasreen kam ta fita daga hayacinta kanshi tarike sosai tana kara bankaro mishi kirji domin kuwa tsumin Anty ne yafara aiki ,jin yayi kasa da kanshi yana neman yin sama da rigarta ya saka ta dawo haya cinta sai yanzu ta tuna ba fah anan ya kamata ya kwana ba tunda ba Auren ta aka fara daurawa ba ,Mama ta fadama ta cewa ta kiyaye shiga hakin yar uwar ta domin shi namiji ba ruwanshi idan kina kwatanta adalci ba zai bari a miki rashin adalci ba zaki kara shiga zuciyar sa ne ,tuno haka ya saka ta fara tureshi ,Wanda shi kam DOCTOR komi nashi ya motsa kawai so yake yaji dandanon dake kasanta 

Jin tana ko'karin ko ce kanta Wanda har ta fara shasheka yasa shi dan dawo wa hayacinsa ,sama yadawo da kanshi dai dai fuskar ta yana dan sakar mata hucin lumfashin sa ya kai minti biyu a hakan Wanda fuskarshi tadan yi ja kallonta yayi sanan cikin murya wada da gaji kasan abukace yake yace "Mine ne Nasreen d'ina ki barni na sha daga ni'imar ki pleaseee..." ya ja please d'in cikin wani yanayi ,kallonshi tayi Wanda duk tsoro ya baya a fuskar ta ganin yanayinshi dayada takejin kayanshi na harbawa sai bugun ta takeyi, cikin in ina tace "ka kayi ha hak'uri ka manta ba ni ba ce ni ke da kai yaw" ta fad'a cikin tsoro 

Rumtse idonshi yayi shi wlh har ya ma manta da akwai wata halita a gidanshi bayan Nasreen d'inshi

Wallahi an cuce shi gaskiya ,wani irin tsaki ya ja ya koma gefenta yana maida numfashi ya kai minti biyar a hakan sanan ya mik'e ya kalleta ganin yada duk ta takure kanta idonta arufe ,kiss ya sakar mata a kuncinta yana mai kai bakinsa dai dai kunanta yace "wato wayo ki ka min ko, yayi kyau yarinya zaki shigo hannu ,oya tashi kiyi wanka maza sanan kiyi alwala kiyi add'uar barcci kar ki manta kinji,tunda ke bkya bukata ta bari naje wurin Anty ki Na-san ita tafiki bukata ta" yafada yana dan lasar kunanta 

Nasreen kam jin abunda yake fadamata yasa wani kishi yata so mata Wanda bata san sanda tace "ai sai kaje kada Allah yasa ka dawo" tafada wasu hawaye nazubo mata ,shashekar kuka ta fara ,shi kam DOCTOR yasan kishi ne yake damunta ama bazai lamunci Nasreen dinshi ta raina shiba tun ba'aje ko inaba ,hakan yasa shi mikewa yadoki doguwar rigarsa da wayoyinsa yafara tafiya ko juyowa baiyi ba aikuwa ganin haka tafashe da wani irin kuka tadoki filon tajefi kofar daya fita yanzu dama can zaije yakwana yabarta anan kukanta taci gaba dayi babu mai rarashi hakan yasaka ta sawake ma kanta toilette tashiga Wanda ba'abunda ke tashi sai kanshi ga turarukan ta nan na wanka anjeramata tubewa tayi tasakar ma kanta ruwa Wanda ko abayin hawaye take saida tagaji dan kanta tayi wanka hade da al'wala ,tana fitowa mai tashafa tashafa humran ta tasaka kayan barccin ta Wanda wasu Riga mai dan gajeran hanu ce irin mai rigar nono nan ce hakan yasaka gaban rigar yafito mata da kirjinta sosai ana gani sai Wanda gajere ne ko guiwa bai kawo mata ba Wanda ya bayana Santa la santalan cin yoyinta ,gadon tahaw kasancewar tayi isha'i dama addu'oin ta tayi ta shafa taja bargo tana tuanin yanzu yana can wurin matar shi duk abunda ya mata Kenan matar shi ma sai ya mata fiye ma da nata wasu zafafan hawaye ne suka sako mata ,ta ji ma farke dakyar bacci yayi awon gaba da ita


Ta 'bangaren Irfan kuwa daganga ya mata haka yaso ya rarasheta ama baya so raini ya shiga tsakaninsu haka yasa shi kyaleta kuma yaji kuka ta Wanda ba 'karamin taba mishi zuciya yakeyi ba ,zai horata sosai ba dai tace kar Allah yasa ya dawo ba zata gane kuwa ,'bangaren Mufida yaje Wanda ya ishe falon ba kowa shiru hakan yatabatar masa tana d'akinta


Mufida kam ranta ne yafara bacewa wato dai yana can wurin wacan ibon matar sa ko ,haushi taji sosai Wanda ganin d'aya da wani Abu ya sata fara kukan ta dama anafadama ta ai yana son ta wato ita da ba matar so ba shiyasa zai mata haka wlh ba za ta yafe ba idan ya kwana wurinta kuma kamar akunen su Abbi ne

Tana cikin wanan yanayi taji bud'ewar kofa ko kalonshi batayi ba ,shi kam yana shigowa yaji kuka ta Wanda yasan dama kishi ne kuma bai yi mata adalci ba ,rigarshi ya aje da wayoyinshi shiyama manta da ledar kazar ta Wanda Samir ne yace "kawai mufida zataci ta yau tunda dama itace za'a..." bai karasaba Irfan ya buge masa bakin ,sai yanzu ma shi ya tuna kalo ya kare mata kasancewar tana akwonce kuma tabashi baya babu ma hadi domin Nasreen d'inshi ta fita da komi farin kawai zata gwada mata "tashi " ya bata umarni kai tsaye .
Ita kam tasan zai iya shuka mata rashin mutunci ya sata tashi zaune tana share hawayenta ,kallonta yayi yace "mi akayi miki na kuka ,wani ya dake kine" ku ji fah DOCTOR da tambayar gaya kamar bai san komi ba ,mufida kam rasa miza ce mishi tayi hakan yasa taji wani kuka yakwace mata wato abunda zai ce mata Kenan amai makon ya rarasheta ,shi kam wane irin ango ne ko dan itadin ba'a bar son shi bace

"Tanbaryki Nike kimin shiru " ya katseta cikin dan tsawa ,da Sauri ta girgiza masa kai alamar babu ,mi'kewa yayi ya fita d'akin da yake kalon nata ya shiga Wanda anan ne d'akinsa kuma kayansa ana aka jera masa su shiga yayi da bissmillah d'akin yayi matu'kar kyau sosai dan ba 'karamin burgeshi yayi ba ,kasancewar fourniture inda Ammi tase musu shi aka basu ama tsayin dakunan su Wanda kuma iyayensu suka semusu a ka jera ma sa nasa kayan alamar nan ne dakinsa .

Kayansa ya cire toile ya dora akugusa ya shiga wanka yakai minti ashirin sanan ya fito sai kanshi yake zubawa cream inshi ya shafa sanan ya feshe jikin shi da turaruka masu kamshi kayan barcci ya saka kalar fari Wanda Riga da wando ne har kasa ba 'karamin kyau suka mishi ba ,makulin motar sa ya doka yaje ya dauko ledar kazar da fruit ,kalon sashinta yayi yasan dakayr in tama rufe kofar shiga falon aikuwa bata rufe ba hakan yashi rufemata kasancewar yana da makulan ko wane sashe kuma dama hade suke da makulin motar shi

Dakin Moufida ya koma wada ya tarar har yanzu tana kukan ta Harar ta yayi yace "kinga malama ki rufe mana baki cikin daren nan" aikuwa tsit kakeji umarni yaba ta da ta dauro al'wala aikuwa al'wala tayi kasancewar shi yayi hijabi tasaka salaya ya shinfida musu yana gaba tana baya raka'a biyu sukai sannan ya juyo ya dafa goshinta yana yin addu'ar da ko wane ango ya kamata yayi domin neman alkhairin dake tare da iyalansa ya ji ma yana addu'a sanan ya shafa ita ma ya shafa 

Juyawa tayi tahaye gadon Wanda shi kuma yaci gaba da lazumunshi yajima yanayi sai karfe biyu da rabi sanan ya shafa kallonta yayi ya ga yanda ta takure a gadon yasan ba barcci take ba nade sallayar yayi sanan yace "sauko ki dokomin plat" yafada yana zama kujerar mai zaman mutum uku da take d'akin aikuwa da Sauri ta Mike taje ta kawo masa ,kazar ya saka a plat d'in yace ta ci ,zama tayi ta fara ci hankali konce wanda shi kam ko kalo bata ishe shiba sai da ta ci iya son ranta ko a jikinta ta sha yaghourt d'in mai sanyi gyatsa tayi sanan tahaye gadon Wanda ya buga mata wata irin tsawa "wa kike jira ya kwashe sauran ragowar ki" aikuwa da Sauri ta sauko tadoki plat d'in da ledar ta aje a kitchen ,dawo wa tayi ta shige bayi Wanda ban san mi tayi ba takai minti goma sha biyar sanan ta fito


Shi kuwa DOCTOR jus kawai yake son sha hakan ya sakashi mik'ewa ya je falon inda yaga firjen bud'ewa yayi ya ga exotic aikuwa zama yayi a ciki kujerun falon yana tunanin yada zai fara sauke nauyinshi da ala ya dora masa doli ne ya bata hakinta ama shibai san ta ina zai fara ba sabida Nasreen dinshi kawai yake jin désir akanta ,jus d'in yakafa baki Wanda ya sha kusan rabi

Ajiyewa yayi ya cigaba da Dana wayar sa Wanda game yake ,idonshi ne yaji suna mishi wani irin yaji yaji sai kanshi dayaji yana juya mishi ,mikewa yayi aikuwa yaji ya dawo sharkaf saman kujerar ,kara mi'kewa yayi ama sai ya fara ganin komi bi biyu kanshi ne yaji yayi wani irin sarawa kara mi'kewa yayi yaji yana tangal tangal kamar mashayin giya d'akin Mufida ya shiga wada tana jin yada aka turo kofar tasan babu lafiya 

DOCTOR ta gani dafe da kai sai tangal tangal yakeyi na masha ,da Sauri ta Mike tana fad'in "subhannallahi Bro Irfan dama kana shan giya inallilahi nashiga uku" tafada tana aza hannu saman kanta sai baya take ja 

Irfan kam duk yana jinta amma baya gane komi hannu yake mikamata alamar ta taimakesa dan ba 'karamin ciwo ya ke ji ba akansa ,amma ina ita baya take ja tana kuka dan gani take yaw kashinta ya bushe Ashe dama Yaya Irfan mashayin giyane ne kuma su Abbi sun sani suka barshi haka ,ai kuwa gobe zata fadawa iyayenta,Irfan kam wani irin faduwa yayi aka san Wanda da'ace farke Yake da saiyaji ciwo ,wani irin barcci ne yayi awon gaba da shi ,Mufida kam falo ta koma dan yanzu kam Irfan ya 'kara bata tsoro kukan ta tasha daga nan barcci yayi awon gaba da ita ....✍

Book 1
Page 39/40
          



........✍ Ƙarfe bakwa saura minti i'shirin ya  farka,  
      jin kan shi na sara masa yasa yabuɗe idonsa  ƙaɗan,  da Sauri kuma yasa ke rufesu jin idonshi na masa nauyi,
      Ƙara buɗewa yayi window yakala kasancewar andan yaye labulan yasashi ganin haske fau ,ai da Sauri yamike zaune dafe kanshi yayi dayaji har yanzu bai daina Sara masa ba "innallilahi wa'ina illaihi raji'un" yafada hakan yayita mai maitawa har yaji kanshi yadan yi sauki,mikewa yayi yafito daga dakin ,dakinshi yashiga da saurin yayi wanka ya dauro al'wala jalabiyar sa yasa sanan yatada sallah ,sai da yatabatr yakare yana zaune yana lazumi yatuna su Nasreen kar azo dai suma basuyi sallar ba ,aikuwa da Sauri yamike yayi falon,turuss taja ya tsaya yana kalon mufida daketa sharar barccin ta hankali konce ta takure kanta wanda ga dukan alamu bata shiryaba ya doketa ,dada bata yayi dan juyowa tayi wanda saura kadan tafado yayi Saurin tarota dan yasan hakinsa ne yace "sai kitashi ai kina ta asararen barcci har anyi sallah"aikuwa ganinshi yasata Saurin kwacewa daga hannunshi tana fadin " inallilahi yaya Irfan "tafada a tsorace ganin haka yasashi fadin " ke shiga hankalinki ba abunda zanmiki kije kiyi sallah" ai tun kan yakarasa tayi ciki da gudu dan ita wlh tsoro yake bata yanzu.

Ta bangaren Nasreen kam tun Kiran sallah farko da akayi akunanta mikewa tayi ta dauro al'wala wani zanin atanfa tasa tasaka katon hijabinta ta tada sallah bayan ta karene tacigaba da lazuminta ,ganin Safiya tafara yine yasata dauko alkur 'anin ta tana karanta wa cikin da dadar muryata inda take karanta suratul kahaf ,Irfan ne yashigo falon ganin baiganta ba yasashi tafiya dakinta tun kan yakarasa yaji  karatun alkur'ani natashi cikin wata dadar murya ,sannu a hankali yabude kofar yana budewa Nasreen yagani tana karatunta hankali konce yakai minti biyar yana sauraron ta sanan yakoma bangaren Mufida ,tarar da ita yayi zaune a falo alamar har takare sallah bai kalleta ba kamar yada bata gaishe shiba 

Sundan jima ahakan shi yana zaune yajingina bayansa da kujerar falon itama tana zaune bisa daya kujerar tana latsa wayar ta gyaran murya yayi sanan yace "Miyafaru jiya?" ya jefo mata tanbayar 

Kalon rainin hankali tamishi wai miyafaru kamar baisani ba ,shiru tamasa dan gani take intayimasa magana yamasamu dama ,aikuwa wata tsawa yabugamata wada ta sata wuntilowa daga saman kujerar 

"Kawai nima naga kashigo abuge ne " tafada tana kuka

Kalonta yayi na yan sakani shidai jus kawai yasha yatabata a cikin jus din akasaka mishi wani Abu kalonta yayi cikin wata irin tsawa yace "uban me kika sakamin acikin jus din " yafada yana hararta 

Da Sauri tace "wlh Yaya nibansama komi ba haka kawai naga kazo abuge " tafada tana neman arcewa dan bakaramin tsoro yabata ba 

Wani kalo ya watsa mata Wanda yasata barin wurin bata shirya ba ,kanshi yadafe doli dayan biyu ne KO itace tasa KO wani daban ,to in itace miye ribarta doli zai yi bincike yagano miye akasaka masa a jus din

Duba jus din yashiga yi ama baiganshi ba ,cikin firjen din yaduba jus ne Wanda alamar ba'ama tabasu ba dama duk exotic ne kasancewar yafi son shi yasa Ammi cewa azuba mishi su a firjen Wanda da saninshi akayi haka ,Jus din sunkai kala goma ,kira ya kwalamata aikuwa sai gata da Saurin ta umarni yabata ta kwaso jus din gabadaya aikuwa kap takwaso ta 'aje gabanshi Kofi yabata yace "duk jus din dasuke wurin nan ki kukurba su " yafada yana zabgamata harara

Aikuwa bashiri tafara sha jitayi cikinta yakule kasancewar bata ci komi ba ,cewa tayi "please Yaya kayi hakuri wlh cikina ya kule banci komi ba kabari nakarya " harar ta yayi ba shiri taci gaba da sha sai da tadan Dana ko wane sanan yakyaleta wada itakam lokacin abinci tashiga nema kazar jiya taci aikuwa sai gyatsa take ganin har awa guda batayi komi ba yasashi aza ayar tanbaya to indai ba'ita bace wanene ,dan yanzu kam ya yarda ba ita din bace tunda in itace bazata yarda ta shaba ,kanshi ya girgiza sanan yace "ina jus din nasha jiya " kalonshi tayi alamar bata gane ba ,hakan yasa shi cewa "jiya wani yashigo nan ne "
Girgiza mishi kai tayi ,komi ta tuno oho tayi Saurin cewa "ama jiya naga kamar an rufe kofar bayan na fito falon ,koda naje naduba kuma sainaga kofar akule " tafada tana kalonshi ,suna cikin haka sukaji kwankwasa kofa, mikewa tayi taje tabude Sharifa ce tagani tsaye da kuloli hakan yasata karbawa tana ansa gaisuwar sharifa ba yabo babu falasa ,kofar tarufe itakam sharifa tafiyar ta tayi abunta 

Kulolin ta aje saman daning din shikuma ciki yashiga yayi wanka inda itama taje tayi wankanta 

Nasreen kam sai da Safiya tayi sosai sanan tarufe kur'anin dama tayi azkar dinta ,agajiye take sosai hakan yasa ta kwanta zuwa goma ta tashi tayi gyare gyare aikuwa barcci ne yayi awon gaba da ita 

Irfan kam wankanshi yayi yaci wanka cikin wata blue din shada jikinshi yafeshe da turare falon yadawo inda yazauna bisa daning din ,Mufida naga itama ta fito cikin wata farar shada wada tadan kamata ajiki zama tayi bisa daya daga cikin kujerun daning din Wanda table ne da kujeru hudu ,IRFAN kam wayarshi yake latsawa abinci tafara zubawa yadakatar da ita da fadin" kije ki kirawo ta " ta fahimce shi wato bazai kira sunanta ba ko su sukasani ,aje cokalin tayi taje sashin Nasreen ta kwankwasa shiru ,hakan yasata shiga kai tsaye ko salama batayiba falon taduba wani bakin ciki ne yataso mata dan bata so ganin falon Nasreen dinba ahaka kasancewar ance mata yar talakawa ce shine suka kashemata kudi haka gani takema kamar falon Nasreen din yafi nata tsaruwa ,tsaki taja dudubawa tayi bata gantaba ,daki dake kusanta taduba ,kai tsaye tabude kofar aikuwa wani irn kishi ne yata so mata Ganin dakin Nasreen din ,tsaki taja kadan ,dubawa tafara yi aikuwa can tahangeta samanan gado da kayan barccin ta hijabin tacire Wanda gani take takuramata zaiyi hakan yasa tacireshi zanin kuwa wurin barcci yayi gefe dan dama Nasreen badai mugun barcci ba, ganin ta ahakan bakaramin faduwa gabanta yayi ba tabbas doli yaso wanan yarinyar ko itade take mace taga kyawunta ina ga maza barema mijinsu ai fari kawai zata iya gwadawa Nasreen din 

Wani bakin kishi ne yataso mata aikuwa da karfi tabuga kofar tayi waje ina bazata iyaba doli tabi shawara Mom dinta da kawarta Wanda ada gani take ai babu wata mace da ta isa taja da ita ashe akwai kuma kishiyar ta ,sun fada mata cewa yanzu anbar zuwa wurin boka sai takama da kissa ake korar kishiya doli kuwa zata bi wanan shawara tun yanzu zata fara kuma 


Hakan yasa ta tafiya part dinta ,yanda tabarshi haka tasameshi yana latsa wayar sa 

Dan satar kalonshi tayi taga bama ita yake kaloba hakan yasa ta cikin murmushi tace "Yaya IRFAN ,kanwar tawa tace batajin yunwa domin tama karya ashe taci fruits" tafada cikin kisa, dan kalonta yayi sanan yacigaba da abunda yake 
Zubamishi abincin tayi ,itama tazuba kowa yafara cin NASA

IRFAN kai yadan so yagano wani Abu kuma sai yashare yayi tunanin datanajin yunwa da tazo kuma yasan akwai fruits acikin firjin dinsu da kitchen abincin shi yakeci hankali konce yana latsa wayar shi Wanda chat suke da Samir dayakece masa zasu zo cin abincin uwar gida dan jibi zaikoma bazai samu cin na Amaria ba cikin murmushi yace "lale ma yaro to Uwargida sai tayi wata batayi Girki ba " yafada yana kashe datar abincinsa yaci yakoshi ,yasha ruwa 

Hakan yasashi goge bakinsa da tissu yamike dan yana son zuwa gida yagaida iyayen nashi ,wayoyinshi yadoka yayi gaba abunshi ,har yadan tafi bai juyowa ba yace "kinada bukatar wani Abu dan yaw zaki fara girki dan dama bauta kuka zoyi so kiduba abunda babu kikirani " yafada yana yi gaba abunsa 

Mufida kam dabisa kujera bata motsaba sai tsaki dataja bayan fitarshi "tabijan wai yana nufin tun yaw zan  fara girki tap,harda wani wai bauta mukazo ,tazo dai wlh dan ni ba bauta takawo niba a to " tafada tana mikewa tayi dakinta ,gado ta haw ta doki wayar ta wada Samira taketa kira kiranta tayi ta doka shewa Samira tasaki tana fadin "wu Kawata duk kashe arnawan ne ,ganinan tafe " cewar Samir da to ta amsa tana kashe Kiran .

Nasreen kai barccin ta tasha sai shadaya ma ta farka ,wanka tayi tashafa manta da humra ta wata doguwar Riga tasaka ta atanfa wada bakaramin kyau tayi mata ba dan kwalinta takashe gaba inda kitson da akayimata kanana kanana ya zubo har gadon bayanta ,sai kumshi da ba'ayimata ba ,sabida Anty tahana tace Abari sai ana gobe zata karbi girki hakan yasa ba'ayimata ba ,fitowa tayi ta shiga dakin Wanda masha Allah bakaramin birgeta yayi ba har hawaye tayi sabida yaba kokarin iyayen nata ,falon ta fito shima yatsaru sosai kitchen ta shiga inda komi ansakama ta firjin Dake kusa da kitchen din taduba taga kayan lambu gasunan ga kaji nan angyarasu komi dai ansaka sai abunda baza a'rasa ba 

Kitchen din ta fito tsintsiya tadoka duk da gidan tsap yake ama sai data kara gyarashi charbon magic tasaka tasa turaren wuta irin nasu na shuwa sai kamshi dakunan suke bayin ta shiga shima duk Saïda takara gyara su duk da jiya kadai aka fara anfani dasu ,tana karewa tasake wani wanka sabida karfe daya saura kuma bataci abunci ba sai yanzu ma taji cikinta Nama kuka ,wato yasamu Amaria har yama manta da ita ko ko lekota bazai yiba yaji yata kwana  bare ayima ganar abincinta wasu hawaye taji suna sulalo mata Mama ta tuna da lokacin abinci yayi zata tusa su agaba harsai taga sunkoshi wataran ma har kuka suke sabida koshi ama sai Abba yazo ya CeCe su ,share hawayanta tayi wani lesh tasaka ruwan kasa Wanda aka masa dinki Riga da siket masha Allah ya fito mata da ship dinta na Coca-Cola ,kitchen ta shiga firjen din tabude tadoki ayaba guda biyu da aple daya dining dinta taje kasancewar ana ganin falon daga daning din yasakata kuna pankace ciyar TV dinta arewa 24 takuna aikuwa anfara dadin kowa tanaci tana kalo Wanda aple din guda taci da ayaba daya ,dayan kuma tamaida ta firjin

Sallah taje tayi kasancewar har biyu ta gota ,sallar ta tayi hankali konce daganan tadawo falon tacigava da kalo har barcci yayi Nasarar doketa .

Tabangaren Mufida ma Samira tazo inda suka tapa Samiran ce tace "yabanga kina tafiya a lakonsheba ,kardai kicemin komi bai wakana ba " tafada tana kalon kawar tata dundaga sama har kasa ,tsaki Mufida taja tana fadin " wlh Kawata ba'abunda ya wakana tsabar iskanci ma jiya giya yasha fa "nan ta labarta mata komi,wani zabura Samira tayi tana fadin " tap wlh kifadawa Mom giyafah"tafada tana  mikewa tadoko ragowar kazar jiya taci abunta ,Nan take kuwa takira Mom doka tayi tana fadin "Mom shine ko ki kirani ko?" tafada cikin sangarta "sorry diyar kirki wlh abubuwane sukashamin kai kinsan baki sunata watsewa yanzu ya ake ciki ?" Mom tafada 

"Mom kinsan wai dama Yaya IRFAN mashayine !" tafada kai tsaye 
Cikin choks Mom tace " giya kuma ?"ta fada cikin Rudi ,"kinga zan kiraki anjuma nayi baki "kit takashe wayar 

Tsaki Mufida taja sanan tamaida hankalinta ga Samira da take yagar kaza saida taci tayi nak sanan tasha jus bisa daning din duk yabace ama Samira ko ajikinta hakama Mufida sukaciga da hirar su inda Samira tashiryama ta yanda zata rika kissa azaman su da abokiyar zamanta dayada zata juya IRFAN din aikuwa sai magrib ta tafi dan gobe zata koma Niamey din ,sunyi bankwana cikin ciki  rashin jin dadi 

IRFAN kai dayaje gida gaisheda iyayen shi yayi kuma yayi bankwanan da u'cles dinshi dan yaw suka tafi harda Jauro ama tsohuwa bataba kasancewar kafarta datekmata ciwo shine IRFAN yace zaihadata da wani Doctor yadubata ,bangaren shi yaje acan ya iske su Samir yan zuwa KO suka fara yimishi shakiyanci Samir ne yace " yanaga banga kyalin angonci ba kodai bakashiga daga ciki ba ?" yafada yana kecewa da wata irin dariya Malik ma tayasa yayi shidai zama yayi yana fadin "ai sai kuyi ,amarci yanzu nafara " yafada yana Harar Malik dayaketa dariya kamar an aikoshi ,tsaki yaja saida suka ida zolayarshi sanan ne Malik yake fadamasa shima gobe zai wuce ,shima Samir haka yafada kasancewar Mahaifinshi ma yazo bikin shima dazu yakoma Hajiyar shi ce dai bata samu zuwaba kasancewar tana da bikin yar yayar ta shima jiya akayisa ,cikin sanyinshi yace "haba dan Allah kuyi zamanku " dan bakaramin rashin jin dadi yayiba da zasu tafi suna debemishi kewa Samir ne yace "kai kuwa ango kaida kakeda Mata har biyu miye zai saka kewa " dan yagano bayaso sutafi suma inbadan aiki ba ai danan zasu dawo ,bai kulasu ba suka wuce massalaci ganin Magariba tayi sallah sukayi basu dawoba saï isha'i ,IRFAN kai gida ya wuce Wanda su Samir ne suka takurashi .


Nasreen kai sai wajen karfe biyar ta tashi da Sauri tayi al'wala sanan takabarta sallah ,tana karewa ta tashi takara gyara gidan turare tasaka na wuta sanan ta tsala wani wanka magrib tayi sanan taciga da azkar dinta har akayi isha'i tana karewa tasaka wata doguwa rigar kanti mai wasu duwatsu gaba hula tasaka akanta sanan ta feshe jikinta da turaruka sanan kuma tashafa humra ,bakaramin haushi tajiba ganin har an wuni bai lekoba wato ko mutuwa zata yi ba ruwanshi ,aikuwa zata gwada mishi ita ba kanwar lasa bace .

Fruit ta debo kamar dazu tafaraci tanayi tana kalon TV

IRFAN yanadawo wa sashin Mufida yayi har zai Budé kofa yatuna yakamata yasake dubata duk dayasan ma ai tana lafiya zaiyi maganinta dai ne 

Bangarenta yashiga Wanda yana shigowa yaji wani kanshi yadaki hancinsa ,wani sanyi yaji danshi yana  son kamshi barema wanan mai dadin falon yashigo da salamar sa ciki ciki ,falon yakarema kalo tsap angyarashi yama fi jiya dan tadan cacanja ma wasu abubuwa wuri ,ganin TV akune yasakashi kalon daning din aikuwa ita yagani tana cin Aple dinta tana kalo ,kamar bata san da halitta a wurin ba 

Nasreen kai taga shigowarshi ama tashare sabida itadai bataji salamar shiba ,ganin jalon dayakemata duk da bata kalonshi yasakata fadin "sannu da zuwa " bata Réda ta kaleshi ba .


Hakan datayi bakaramin haushi yaji ba ,tsap yakare mata kalo Wanda rigar datasa bakaramin tafiyar da imanin shi tayi ba ,ama yashare bai amsa mata gaisuwar ba sai cewa da yayi "indan kin kare kisameni a sashin Anty ki " yana gama fadar haka yayi gaba abunsa da harara tabi bayanshi ,aple dinta taci gaba daci sai data kare sanan takashe TV ta fito dan amsa kiranshi Wanda tasan aidama Auren Kenan .


Mufida kam yada Samira ta bata wurin haka tabarshi sai wanka dataci cikin wani gajeran wando iya guiwa rigar kam komi ana gani naci ki hakimcewa tayi saman kujerar tana jiran oga ....✍

_MARUBUTA MASU AIKI DA FASAHA DA ZALLAR TSAKWARON AMANA, FAƊAKARWA HAƊI DA NISHAƊANTARWA GAMI DA WA'AZANTARWA, HUMM WANI ABUN SAI ROYAL STAR WRITERS ASSOCIATION👊🏾🦋 RUWA KAƊAI KE MAGANIN ƘISHI☀️_

Book 1
Page 41/42
           .......✍IRFAN na shigowa falon yaji iskan kaza tsaki ya ja ,jiyayi kawai an rungumeshi Wanda yasan Mufida ce baya son bata kumya kasancewar tana da haki akansa shima rungume ta yayi Wanda Samira ce dama ta bata shawara ,kuma tace karma ta kuskura tafara girki idan yace miyasa kawai ta shagwabe tace "batajin dadi ne tasan ai doli zai kyale" welcom dear tafada tana rike hanunshi ,shikam kalon daning din yayi yaga wayam kalonta yayi aikuwa shagwabewa tayi tana fadin "kayi hakuri wlh banajin dadi ne " ta fada tana lafewa ajikinshi ,ban bareta yayi a jikinshi yace "Allah ya kara afuwa " ya fada yana yi dakinshi wanka yayi yasaka kananun kaya wata rose din Riga yasaka da wando iya guiwa yana fitowa kam saiga Nasreen da salam ta shigo falon

Amasa mata Mufida tayi ba yabo babu falasa tana fadin "a'a kanwata ce KO lekoni ma bakyayi ko" tafada tana zama kusa da IRFAN da ya zauna bisa daya daga cikin kujerar falon 

Nasreen kai bata dagoba ita ma cikin sakin fuska tace "wlh kuwa hakane nadan yi aiki ne shiyasaka " tafada tana zama kasa kasancewar ba'abata izinin zama ba kuma wanan ba tarbiyar su bace 

IRFAN kai dagangan yasaka kananun kayan kamar yanda Mufida tayi dan yana so yabawa Nasreen din haushi ,gyaran murya yayi sanan yace "da farko nagode wa Allah daya nunamin wanan lokacin na nauyinku amatsayinku na mata na ,sanan Abu nagaba shine da farko dai ina so nayi nasihata gareki dear kece baba " yafada Wanda dagangan yayi shi sabida Nasreen taji haushi .

Cigaba yayi da fadin "ina so kidoke ta kamar kanwar ki ,kin tsofe mata nesa ba kusa ba idan kikaga tayi badai daiba ki mata nasiha idan taki kifadamin ,bana son tashin hankali masu magana nacewa idan gida yagyaru uwar gida ce idan yabace uwar gida ce ina so kitayi yin adalci atsakaninku " yafada cikin wani irin murmushin mugunta .

Sanan yadan saci kalon Nasreen din yaciga gaba da fadin "ke kuma kece karama ki rungumi karamtar ki idan kinga abunda baki ganeba to kitanbayi yayar ki ,bana son tashin hankali kuzauna lafiya " yafada yana mai cigaba da fadin "sanan Abu nagaba kunsan zanyi ma ko Wace sati daya ne kamar yanda shari'a ta sharwanta ,sanan ina so kusani bayan kowa tayi satinta zaku rika yin kwana bibiyu ne ,sanan duk Wace Takeda girki a part dinta za'ayi breakfast kafin bakwai ki tabbatar inke keda girki kin kamalashi bana son letti zuwa aiki ,sanan idan naga ansamu matsala tafanin wani zanmishi hukunci dai dai da abunda yayi, sanan bana son kazanta da yawo idan ba makaranta ba ko aiki wanan bazan hanama duk wada Takeda buri saidai idan naga baki mai dai hankali akan ibadar Aure ba zaki fasa" cewar IRFAN

"Idan damai magana cikinku ina jinta " yafada yana latsa wayar sa 

Mufida ce tace "gaskiya hakan yayi dai dai my sugar nidai banda magana sai dai ko kanwata " tafada tana rungume IRFAN din ,shima dagangan ya rungume ta 

Nasreen kai duk taga abunda yake faruwa yo har ita za'a nunawa bariki ita ma fah tayi bokon nan sanan ankoyamata karuwancin nan dagashi har ita zasu gane shayi ruwa ne ,cikin murmshinta na yar boko tace "to gaskiya Anty nidai bani da magana Allah ya bamu zaman lafiya " ta fada tana kalonsu tana murmushi cikin dakewa tace "To my baby idan ka kare zan tafi sabida na baku waje kubaje soyaya " cewar Nasreen wada wlh a cikin zuciyar ta ji take kamar tafashe ama bata so suga rauninta tun yanzu ,zata gagasasu suduka .


IRFAN kai ba 'karamin mamaki ta bashiba yasan dakiya ce kawai da za'a bude zuciyar ta yasan da bakaramin tafarfasa takeba ama ita ala doli sai ta nuna ba abunda yake damunta zai koyi maganinta hakan yasashi cewa "gaskiya kai namatsu najini a gajimare ,dan haka na salame ki ,Allah ya bamu alkairi " ya fada yana sakar ma mufida wani zazafan kiss hade da kashewa Nasreen ido daya 

Wada Allahn Allahn take tabar wajen kar zuciyar ta ta buga ,aikuwa yana karashe fadar haka tamike tana fadin "asha amarci lafiya " tayi waje abunta 

Tana shiga falonta wani irin kuka ne ya kwace mata Wanda bana komi bane sai na kishi ,ashe haka kishin nan yake ta godewa Allah daya sa Abba baiyi ma Mama kishiya ba kuma bata fatan ayimata tasan da bakaramin cutuwa zatayiba kasancewar ta mai hakuri da kauda ido 

Ita yake fadawa yamatsu yajishi ,bakomi wlh zata gwadamishi ita din bariki ce da kanta ,zata nuna mishi ita din ruwan shuwa ce ,zata nunamishi ita Karuwa ce mai zaman kanta a cikin gidanta ganin zuciyar ta nata ta farfasa ya sakat karanto adu'o Wanda Mama tafadama dan tasan dama za'ayi haka wata rana aikuwa  zuciyar taji tana yin sanyi,mikewa tayi tashige dakinta kayan barccin ta tasaka tajima farke kafin barcci yayi awon gaba da ita.

Ta 'bangaren IRFAN kai yana ganin ta fita yasaki murmushi domin kuwa yasan ta shaka ,dan ture Mufida yayi danshifah baya son rashin kamun kai ,duk dayasan hakinta ne kuma zai sauke mata shi ,mikewa yayi yace tabiyoshi dakinshi ,dakinta taje tasaka wata  arniyar Riga barcci ,Wanda da ita da babu duk daya 

Dakinta taje ta iskeshi Wanda yake zaune bisa gadon yana duba wani littafi ,da karairaya tashigo Wanda ko kalo bai isheta ba ,hawa gadon tayi tasakalo hanunta ta wuyanshi ,shafa mishi kirji ta shiga yi Wanda nan take yaji yanayinshi ya sauya sharewa yayi ,cigaba tayi da shafa shi inda nan take ya aje littafin ya juyo kalonta yakeyi ido cikin ido Wanda yahango jaraba a cikin su yana fatan ta iya dokarshi sanan tazama kamrshi bakinshi ya saka cikin nata wani kiss yafara sakar mata Wanda yasata fara mimikewa totsar ta ya fara yi yana kokarin cire mata rigar adan dama bata saka brezia ba sai wani tsigagen wando Wanda ya shige can cikin takashin Wanda dashi da banza duk daya ciramata rigar yayi inda kirjinta yabaya karemusu kalo yayi ko rabin na Nasreen din shi basukai ba ama amike suke 

Dayan yakama Wanda yaji ko rabin cika masa hannu bai yi ba ,dayan kuma bakinshi ya kafa a wurin ya fara lasar ta tundaga kirjinta har zuwa cibiyar ta ,Mufida kam tafara fita ahayacinta dan jin salon take nadaban ne wani dadi takeji Wanda batasan yanda zata musalta shiba ,sai bankarewa takeyi kayanshi yacire inda yayi saura dagashi sai boxers Wanda ganini surarshi abayane yasaka mufida kara rudewa ,kirjin shi takala taga yanda nonuwan shi suka dan Mike ko wata mace albarka irin ingarman mazan ne inbadan tasan baya cin karfe ba datace harda artères yana yi ,cikin nan nashi a lafe kamar baya cin abunci Wanda daga cibiyarshi har zuwa cikin boxers dinsa taga dan gashi gashi Wanda suka kwanta luf da a'alama kamar baiyi kwana biyu ba dayin shavin ,kaf ajikinshi fari ne Tass kanshi ta shiga shafawa a hankali Wanda sumar tasha gyara 


IRFAN kam yaji yafara fita a hankalinshi dan dama a hannu yake sai dai har yanzu baiji alkalamin shi yamikeba kamar yanda ko kissing din  Nasreen yayi sai yaji IRFAN din shi na harbawa cigaba yayi da tsotsar cibiyarta kirjinta daya na abakinsa daya kuma na hanunsa cikin wani irin Salo Wanda sai Wanda akemashi kadai yasan duniyar daya ke tasaka mufida Budé kafafunta ,murmushi yasaki dan yasan duk Wace akama wanan salon doli tayi hakan batare da tashirya ba 

Ganin haka yasaka shi yin kasa da kanshi pant din ya yaga Wanda dama ba wani karfi ne dashi ba Mufida kam marata taji tadan murda aikuwa saiji tayi zuu Abu yazo Wanda ta tabatar période dinta ne ,ganin kamar zai sakamata hannu ya sakata matse kafafunta bai fasaba yacigaba da gangarowa har yazo dai dai fuskar ta kalonta yayi yaga yanda idonta suka kankance alamar kiris take jira, shima kam jinshi yake kamar bashi ba ya kamata yaw ya samu abunda yake so in ba haka ba ya tabatar yaw sai dai wani bashiba 

Girgizamishi kai tayi tana dafe cikinta hakan ya tabatar mishi période dinta ne yazo ,dan a stage din dayakai ta ko ita wacece doli tamika wuya ,gefenta ya kwanta sharkaf dagashi sai boxers lumfashi yake maidawa ,jiyayi mararshi ta fara murdawa aikuwa dafeta yayi zufa ce tafara karyo mishi 

Mufida kam ganin ya sauka akanta yasakata mikewa da Sauri tayi dakinta auduga tadoko ta shiga bayi ta tsapta ce kanta tare da Jan tsaki kamar zatayi kuka ,fitowa tayi tana fadin "Allah ya isa na wlh shikenan yanzu banice uwar gida ba kawai dai a idon mutane Nike haka " tafada harda kukanta kasancewar tasan kwana bakwai takeyi ,kuka tasha abunta daganan barcci ne ya yi awon gaba da ita 


IRFAN kai bai masan inda kanshi yakeba sex kawai yake so gashi yayi wasa da ita ama duk banza indama yasani bai fara ba 

Cigaba ya yi da murkususn shi sai zufe ke karyomishi duk da sanyin ecin dake dakin inbadan yasan hukunci satar kwana ba ba abunda zai hanashi zuwa ga Nasreen din shi ,dakyar yamike yashiga lalube  dakin har ya shiga toilettes shower yasakar ma kanshi dan yasan  tablettes din shi yabaro shi gidan Ammi dan baikwaso duka kayanshi ba 

Yakai minti arba'in sanan yaji dama dama fitowa ya yi yasaka jalabiya bisa gadon ya kwanta yana rike da mararshi dan har yanzu tana dan murda mishi ,dakyar dai barcci ya yi awon gaba dashi ...


Kiran sallar farko yatashi kanshi ne yaji yayimasa nauyi marar shi kai tayi sauki ama wani tauri tauri yakeji a gefen wanda yasan sperme ne yatarar mishi anan ,bayi yashiga ya yi wanka yana fitowa al'wala ya yi yasaka jalabiya kasancewar akwai massalaci akusansu yasaka shi wucewa massalacin ,baidawo ba sai da haske yafara .

Part din Nasreen yaje da clé din shi yabude dan yajita akule dan dama yabiyo dashi dan dokar kur'an inshi dayabari a mota ,yana shiga yaji muryata mai dadi tana tashi hakan yatabatar masa ta farka ,dakin yashiga yaganta da hijabinta mai dogon hannun nan kalar ganye tana rike da alkur'ani tabashi baya ba 'karamin kyau hijab din yamata ba ,juyawa ya yi yatafi part din Mufida dakinta yashiga inda yatarar da ita sai barccinta takeyi kafafunta ya bubuga duk dayasan bata sallah ama ai zata hadamusu breakfast sabida yace kar Ammi ta sake aikomusu da abunci aikuwa dakayr tatashi

Toilettes tashiga tayi wanka tacanja paid ,Riga da siket tasaka na atanfa kitchen ta shiga 

Tsaye tayi tana tunanin abunda zata girka ,wainar kwai tasoya  da dankalin turawa sai ruwan zafi na thé da tahada bakwai dai dai ta kamala ...


Nasreen kai sai karfe bakwai da rabi takare karatun ,gyaran.part dinta tayi tayi moping sosai bayan tagama ne tasake tsala wanka cikin wani bakin lesh Wanda bakaramin kyau ya mata ba tazauna falon Kenan taji kira a wayar ta Anty ce doka suka gaisa shine take fadamata gobe zata wuce kasancewar dama su sukayi gyaran gida yasaka tace ba sai tazo ba ama sauran yan Uwa zasuzo anjuma shine take fadama ta daganan salama sukayi takashe wayar ,tana kashewa sai ga gogan yakira cemata yayi "muna jiranki " kadai yakashe wayar 

Mikewa tayi tasaka hijabinta tayi part din ,Wanda sai dai Doctor yama Mufida fada sanan ta tare kazar dan acewar ta sai tasa an nemo mata yar aiki ,gaisheda su tayi tana fadin "ina kwanan ku" tafada tana zama bisa kujera daya dake daninig ,Mufida kadai ta ansa itakm KO ajikinta 

Mufida ce tahadama IRFAN nashi wanda thé ne da dankalin turawa dan bayacin kwai „

Nasreen kam da kanta tahada Wanda itama dankalin kawai sai thé dan itama wainar kwan bata dameta ba „

Haka suka zauna kowa da abunda yake sakawa aranshi sai kara cokali kakeji ,IRFAN kai mikewa yayi dan thé din nema yafi sha ,falo yazauna ya kuna TV

Nasreen kam ci tayi sosai sabida yunwar datakeji ,Mufida sai hararen kasan ido takemata Wanda bata masan tanayi ba ,tana karewa tamike falon tazo wucewa shine take fadamasa ai anjuma yan uwan Abba da Mama zasuzo ganin daki 

Yi yayi kamar baijiba hakan yasata tafiyar ta dan tasan ai yaji tsabar girman kaine


IRFAN dagan gan yashareta yajita sarai Mufida kam sai Harar Bayan Nasreen takeyi sabida bakaramin tafiya da ita kwaliyar tata tayi ba.


Nasreen na wucewa bangarenta TV takuna sai shabiyu yan uwanta sukazo sunga daki masha sunyaba IRFAN ne yashigo suka gaisa Wanda hardan kasa yaduka yana gaishesu cikin sakin fuska suka ansa 


Dakin yashiga ,hakan yasa Nasreen binshi Dubu dari da hamsin yabata yace tabasu godiya tamasa bayabo babu falasa ,fitowa yayi jin su Samir sunzo ,aikuwa sune sai iskanci suke masa shidai bai kulaba yajasu bangaren Mufida dan dama nasiha zasumusu „

Nasreen ma yan uwanta na tafiya tarakasu har kofah inda suka yimata nasiha da aiki da hakuri kudin tabasu sai godiya sukeyi suna sama zamansu albarka 

Suna wucewa tayi bangaren Mufida anan ne ta tarar dasu Samir nasiha sukamusu sosai ,sanan ne suka Mike tare da cewa to ango sai munzo sunan yan hudu ko, Mufida ce tayi zarbule tace "gaskiya kai kuzauna nan sai kunga gidan mu yacika da yara " 

Ba IRFAN ka daiba har su Samir din sunga kanta rawa yake doli sai IRFAN yasauke mata shi ,Samir ne dai yayi murmushi ,Malik dinma haushita yaji Wanda yasaka shi zabga mata harara ita ko ko ajikinta 


Nasreen kai adu'ar sauka lafiya tamusu ,inda sukayi gaba itakuma tayi part din ta 

Aéroports yakaisu dan kowa da jirgin da zai tafi dashi ,Samir zaije Maradi ,Malik kuma Niamey ,ama jirgin Malik yariga na Samir tashi ,hakan yasa suke zaman jira suna dan fira ana wurin zaman  mata fiya Samir ne yace "wlh abokina naga uwar gidan nan naka kanta na rawa ka sai kadaidaita mata shi ”yafada yana kwashewa da dariya tsaki IRFAN yaja yana fadin “a a saremata shi zanyi ,ina ruwanka inkaji haushi kaje amaka rawar kan mana ” IRFAN yafada ,shidai Samir dariyar shi yakeyi ana haka jirginsu Samir din yadaga IRFAN kai asibiti yawuce dan yadan duduba wasu abubuwa duk da yadoki hutun sati uku ama zai rika lekawa hakan yasahi nufar asibitin....✍

Book 1
Page 43/44
               .....✍"INALLILAHI IKLAS Budé idonki ki kaleni ,Iklas ki bude idonki Nice tata ki  ki buɗe idonki ki kalleni"wata mata ce wada ba za ta wuce shekara talatin rik'e da wata yarinya wada a shekaru  ba za ta wuce takwas ba sai kuka matar take risga kamar dai ance mata yarinyar ta mutu ,rungumeta ta sake yi a kirjinta tana fadin “dan Allah Iklas dina ki bude idonki Nice tata ki kinyi alkawari ba za ki barni barni sai lokacin da ya dace meya sa zaki tafi to ?" ta fada tana mai kara rushewa da kuka.


"Subhannallahi Rumaisa miye ya samu Mama na " wani mutum baki mai dan tsayi ama ba sosai ba ya fada shima cikin ru'dewa tare da karbar yarinyar ,yayi waje da ita cikin wata jar mota kirar Bugatti na ga ya saka ta baya Wada na ga ya kira da Rumaisa ita ma bayan ta shiga ta rungume yarinyar „

Kujerar dreba na ga ya zauna da karfi yafigi motar wada saura kadan ya buge mai gadin, bai zarce ko ina ba sai baban hospital din garin ,

Yana yin parkinga yafito da Sauri ya suri yarinyar sai cikin hospital din émergence yakaita Wanda da Sauri likitocin suka karbeta block suka shiga da ita Wanda naga wanan matar da namijin suka kawota zasu shiga DOCTOR din yatsaida su karaf yarufe Room din.


Matar ce wada nan take nakare mata kalo fara ce ama ba sosai ba tana da dan jikinta ama bawanda zai hanamata walwala ba ,batada tsayi sosai ,sanye take da zulmuyeyen hijabinta pink ,durkushe wa naga tayi taciga ba da rusgar kukanta cikin kukan take fadin "please Iklasi na karkibarni banida kowa sai ke sai Allah kar ki tafi kibarni kinmanta kinmin alkawari Na-san ki dacika alkawari Diyar Tata " tafada cikin fitar hayaci „

Mutumin nan ne naga yadurkusa yadago matar wada ga dukan alamu matar shi rungumeta yayi yana shafa bayanta alamar rarashi Wanda shima kalo daya zakayi masa kasan yana bukatar mai lalashinsa „


Shafa bayanta yakeyi sannu a hankali yana fadamata kalmar “Allahuma ajurni fi musibati wa ahlifni hairan minha ” ahaka kuma takarba tanayi har ta fara sauke ajiyar zuciya ,rungume da ita yazauna bisa bancin da yake wurin cikin muryasa mai natsuwa kamar dai yanda yake anutse yace "Ashe bazaki rungumi kaddara ba idan tazo mana ,Ashe idan mai aro yabaka aro kuma yace yana son abunshi bazaki mayar masa ba ,Ashe bazaki iya rarashina ba bayan kin ma Mama na alkawari idan tatafi bazakiyi mata kuka ba sanan zaki lalashe mu Nida Farid ?” yafada yana mai cigaba da shafa bayanta 


Shiru yayi yana sauran yada take ajiyar zuciya ,wani DOCTOR ne yafito daga Room din da Sauri aikuwa itama mikewa tayi ta tarbeshi tana fadin " DOCTOR ya Y'a ta take ciki kafadamin ” tafada wasu hawaye nazubo mata ,likitan ne yace “sorry yanzu dai babu abunda zance "yafada yana neman kauce mata dan ga dukan alamu cikin Sauri yake aikuwa Shako shi tayi ta ga ba tana fadin “wlh kar kubari y'a ta ta mutu in ta mutu kuma zaku bita ,kaima ai kasan bazata mutu ba ” fadar matar wada da Sauri mijin yacire hanunta daga rikon da ta ma DOCTOR din„


DOCTOR kai gaba yayi abunshi dan dama ai sunsaba da irin wada nan 

Zaunar da ita naga Mijin nan nata yayi ,rungumeshi tayi tana fadin " kanaji ko Abban Farid Iklas dina ”takara fada Wanda yasaka shi janta sai cikin wani Room wani DOCTOR ne yashigo doke da alurai aikuwa kasancewar tana ta rusgar kuka bata masan da shigowar shiba ,alura ya mata nan take naga ta kwanta sharkaf ajikin Mijin nata kwantar da ita yayi „

Yana kalon kyakyawar fuskar matar tashi wasu hawaye yadauke daga idonshi dan sai yanzu yasamu suka zo mishi „ shekara uku Kenan da Allah yajarbesu da wanan lalura ta yar tasu wada ba komi bane face ciwon anta dayake damun yar ta su Allah sarki rayuwa batada tabass baiyi tunanin zata kawosu wanan lokacin duk da bawa na kwarai kulum cikin jarrabawa yake “Alhmdllah ”naji yafurta .



SHEKARUN BAYA 

Hashim Ibrahim mai dalla dane ga Ibrahim mai dallah kakan shi mai dalla yaci sunan mai dalla ne sakama kon kudin dagareshi wanda yasha gwagwarmaya lokacin turawan malaka ,yatashi cikin gata har yayi karatushi ya Auri matar shi wada bafulatana ce suka haifi dansu Ibrahim inda yaro yataso cikin gata har Allah yasa yakamala karatunshi yazama ingénierie yayi Aure inda ya Auri wata bahausa sukasami dansu kyayawa dashi inda sukasakamasa hashim ,hashim yata so cikin kauna da dukiya tako wane pani leleshi akeyi haka yata so cikin gata ama duk da gatan nan nashi hakan baisa yasangarce ba yanada ladabi da son mutane kamar kakanshi Mai dalla da baban sa ,sai dai Mahaifiyar shi dabata kaunar talaka hakan yasaka take taka talaka bata dokeshi abakin komi ba ,hashim yayi karatunshi a Saudi  inda yakaranci shari'a har yakamala yadawo Niger a garin Damagaram wato Zinder anan mai dalla yake da iyayen nashi 

Rumaisa y'a ce ga wani baban malami mai suna Malan shehu Wanda bahaushe ne kuma baya aikin komi sai koyar da dalubai daiko zuwa gonna shida mai dakinsa sa'datu wada daga ita sai kaunar ta Iklas suntaso cikin maraici ,har Allah yasa Malan shehu ya Auri sa'adatu ,kuma yamaido Iklas wurinshi kasancewar basajin dadin zama wurin kanan mahaifin su ahaka Malan shehu sukaci gaba da zama cikin rufin asirin Allah har yasa suka sami ciki inda sa'adatu tahaifi danta Namiji suka saka mishi Umar ahaka tayi aihuwa uku bayan Umar sanan ne haihuwar tatsaya „

Iklas nada shekara ishirin wani bakin buzu yanemi Auren ta kuma Malan dayayi binci yaga mutumin kirki ne hakan yasa akabashi Auren iklas inda suka tare suma anan cikin Zinder din „

Sa'adatu tasake haihuwar Mace inda suka sakamata Rumaisa a lokacin iklas shekarata biyu da Aure bata samu haihuwa ba tunda aka haifi Rumaisa taji tana kaunar ta ahaka tun tanada shekara daya take dokar ta taje da ita gidanta itada mijinta suyi ta raino suna son Rumaisa insun doketa tun safe sai dare madara suke kadamata duk da ba wani hali ne dasu ba „

Gani haka yasaka Malan shehu cewa yabasu Rumaisa sa'adatu ma ta'amince har da kukanta dan bakaramin tausayawa yar tata tayiba ahaka su iklas suka raini Rumaisa komi suka samu itada buzu mijinta yar su Rumaisa wada masha Allah tatashi cikin ilimi da kaifin Bassira har Allah yasa takai shekara goma sha shida tazama budurwa „

Tasan Iklas ba ita tahaife taba ama ita gani take tafi son Iklas din fiyeda sa'adatu kasancewar kotaje canma gidan kamar ba gidansu ba dan arakube take duk da kuwa yanda sa'adatu take janta ajiki ahaka har Allah yasa wata rana tatafi nika tahadu da hashim yadan bugeta da mota „

Bata jimuba ama saida yakawota har gida ya yaba da hankalinta dama kuma iyayen nashi sun matsa masa yanemo mata hakan yasa yana zuwa gida yafadawa mahaifiyar sa wada tace yar wacece anan take yace mata yar talakawa ce aikuwa kaca kaca tamishi ,inda Alhaji Ibrahim yaji yace ba'abunda zaisa afasa Auren aikuwa dai sunje gidan inda suka tanbayi Auren ta kuma aka basu iklas taji dadi ama da Rumaisa ta tafi shikenan hakan yasaka ta Umar yakawo mata yar shi kasancewar har yayi Aure ,anyi Aure inda bada son mahaifiyar tashi ba tadoki karan tsana tadorawa Rumaisa mace mai hakuri da kawaici shekara su shida babu haihuwa wanda ba Gorin da mahaifiyar sa bata mata ,shikam cewa yakeyi lokaci ne sabida tsananin kaunar daya keyima Matar tasa „

Wata rana mahaifiyar shi hashim taje kasuwa wurin dawowa ta buge wata yarinyar wada bazata wuce shekara goma ba amaimakon tabawa mahaifiyar yarinyar hakuri kasancewar yarinyar tana tare da mahaifiyar ta kuma laifin mahaifiyar shine kasancewar aguje take kamar titin nata ne ,sai zaginsu data ketayi wanda sanadin haka mahaifiyar yarinyar tace “daga zuri'aki sunkare haihuwa sabida bakisan darjata ba idan kuma sun haihu bazaku rabu da ibtila'i ba sai kinyi kuka da hawayenki ” Jan yar ta tayi suka tafi abinsu „

Cigaba tayi da zaginta wada tun daga wanan rana bata manta da matar ba sabida taji tsoron kalaminta ama ahaka tacigaba da tsula tsiyarta 

Shekara shida Allah yabasu Rumaisa ciki wanda bakaramin murna sukayiba barema mahaifiyar shi wada tadoki son duniya tadorama cikin kasancewar bata saké samun haihuwa ba kuma hashim shikadai ne danta ,ta bangaren Iklas ma ba'abarsu abaya ba kulum cikin tardu kayan jego suke da mai jego har Allah yasa aka haifi yarinya wada taci sunan iklas shiyasaka suke ce mata Maman su wada mahaifiyar shi bahaka taso ba ama yazatayi tunda tana son „„


Iklas nada shekara uku suka saké haihuwar Farid wanda suka tashi cikin gata barema iklas ta tashi cikin so da kaunar famille ta gata kyakyawa da ita sai yakasance sabida kwazonta tayi farin jini a wurin dalibai hardama malamai kulum ita kecin nadaya ahaka takai shekara shida to tun daga lokacin nan kwatsam wata rana sukaga tana aman jini sai kulalai dasu fito mata aka fafu hakan yasaka suka kaita asibi wanda yatabatar musu ciwon anta ne yake damunta “

Sun shiga tashin hankali bakadan ba barema hajiya mahaifiyar hashima har kuka tayi sabida kaunar datake ma Iklas ,sunso sukaita kasar waje ama likitocin suka ce ai tunda abun bai yi nisaba zata warke ,sun dorata akan magun guna kuma Alhmdllah tana samun sauki sosai sai tayi wata uku ciwon baitashi ba idan yatashi kuma kowa sai yatausaya mata „„„


Iklas na tsananin  tausayawa iyayen nata barema mahaifiyar ta,wada inciwon nata yatashi sai anmata alura barcci sanan suke samun lafiya „

Wani lokaci ne da ciwon yatashi Wanda sanadin haka sai da takai sati bata farfado ba Rumaisa kai sai dai ayimata alura barcci ranar da ta farfado ko iyayen nata na kusa bude idonta tayi Wanda da kyar tabudeso sabida nauyin da suka mata kalon mahaifiyar ta tayi taga yanda ta rame hakan yasata cewa “la Tata miye yasameki badai kuka kika tayi ba ,ai ba yanzu zan mutu ba sai lokaci ne yayi haka malamin mu yake fada muna ,Ashe nasha barcci "tafada dan karfafawa iyayen nata guiwa iklas idan tayi magana zaka dauka Babar mace ce bayan maganar ta tafi shekarunta yawa „


Da Sauri Rumaisa ta rungumeta tana fadin " ai dama Na-san baza ki mutu ki barmu ba yanzu Mama na kimin alkawari baza kitafi ba kibarmu "tafada cikin hawaye 

Ganin haka yasaka iklas fadin " Tata?" kasancewar haka take kiranta dashi dan taji y' y'an Umar suna kiranta dashi Wanda yake nufin Anty 

Kara Kiran sunanta tayi sanan tace “tata malamin mu yace dokar alkawari idan kasan bazaka cika ba bashi da kyau kasancewar ala momin munafiki ne ,Na-san bkya so na kasance cikinsu ko ?”tayi tanbayar tana kalon mahaifiyar tata 


"Eh mana Mama na insha Allah bazaki kasance cikin munafikai ba Allah yabaki lafiya yakaremin ke kyakyawa y'a ta " cewar Rumaisa cikin hawaye 

"Zan roki Allah yabani tsawon Rai kodan ku iyaye na ,Tata kimin alkawari idan na mutu ,zaki kulamin da Dady na baza kibarshi yayi kukan rashi na ba zaki rarashe shi Na-san yada yake so na ,ina ganin tashin hankali a idonsa ,kimin alkawari zaki kulamin da Farid zaki bashi lokacinki sabida yasaba muna kokayi dashi ,zaki kulamin da tsohuwa mai ran karfe taki ta mutu tabarmu hakan " tafada tana fashewa da dariya 

Aikuwa suma dariyar sukayi Hashim din ne yadungurema kai yace “mahaifiyar tawa ai kamar yanda kike son taki haka nima Nike son tawa ”yafada tana rungumeta 

Aikuwa hajiyar ce tashigo ciki tana fadin “kaji ja'ira ko sai dai ke kirigani mutuwa "fadar hajiya wada tazo duba iklas din aikuwa wurinta taje tahaye cinyar ta ,kamar ba ita ba tun daga lokacin tasamu sauki ,saiko yaw da aka saké maidota asibitin Wanda lafiya klw take tadawo daga scool Kenan tashigo falon kawai sai taga Tafadi shine taketa rusar kukan Wanda tarasa miyakamata tayi a lokacin har hashim yadawo ya tarar da ita ahaka ...✍



Labarin fah yanzu aka fara cakwakiyar kudai kuci gaba dabina kuji mizai faru da wanan ahalin ,ina labarin DOCTOR NE ??? amsoshin ku na nan cikin pagen gaba ...
