        .........✍A jiyar zuciya ya sauke fita yayi,  lokacin Doctors din sun fito, office din doctor dake kula dasu kulum idan abun yatashi yaje ,kwankwasa wa yayi izini aka bashi yashiga.


Wani DOCTOR bak'i na gani mara jiki yana zaune bisa kujerar office d'in basa umarni zaman yabashi hakan ya saka shi zama,
Rubuce - rubucensa yayi sanan yad'ago yana kalon Hashim ,magana yafara kamar haka “da farko dai abunda zance shine gaskiya ciwon ya fara tsananta takai matakin da yakamata ayi wani abu ,da farko zanbaka hakuri Mrs. Hashim magunguna sun kare kuma ba'akawo ba ,babu wasu kwararin likitoci ,inaga kafita da ita waje kawai zaifi " DOCTOR yafad'a yana mai kalon Mrs.Hashim

Kalon shi Mrs. Hashim yayi yace "naji tun farko naso fita da 'ya ta kuka hana amma bakomi ,fad'a min yanzu ya ake ciki ne ”

" a halin yanzu dai baby Iklas zata farfad'o abun ne yadan so tasar mata wanda ga dukan Alamu tad'an kwana biyu tanajin ciwo ,Iklas yarinya ce mai juriya tana jurewa ne dan k'ar hankalin ku yatashi nikadai nasan abunda yasaka nace haka please kayi gagawar fita da ita " cewar DOCTOR din.

Dafe kanshi Mrs. Hashim yayi yana fad'in "lahaula wala k'uwati illah billah " shiru ne yad'an ratsa.

“yauwa kasan wani Abu akwai wani DOCTOR wanda ya k'ware ta fannin ilimin Matsalolin mata kuma harka ciwon hantama naji ance kwararene ,sai dai bakowa yasan da haka ba kasancewar naji ance lokacin da yadibar ma kansa na nuna shid'in yana wanan aikin baiyi ba ,sau biyu yana wanan aikin kuma Alhamudulillah da ikon Allah duk mutanen sun samu lafiya ,ka jarraba sai dai ance yanada girman kai da isa ,idan kayi sa'a zai muku aiki ,kuma bawai dan kud'inka ba domin shima din yaro ne mai jini a jika ,yana ji da ilimi ,kurciya ,sanan dukiya "fadar DOCTOR DIN 


" Abu nagaba shine zanbaka number kaninshi ,Wanda Abokin kanina ne ,tahakan zaku sami number shi ,kuma shawara shine ku  sa me shi gidanshi zai fi dan naji ance kwanan nan yayi aure "likitan yaci gaba da cewa.

" to yanzu yaushe zata farka kuma ina so kar ka sanar da mahaifiyata zanyi komai nagode "fad'ar Mrs.Hashim Kenan ,hannu yabasa suka mik'e sukayi waje dan zata samu baccin Awa hud'u kafin tafarka ....




Gidan IRFAN kuwa y kwanan IRFAN shida abangaren Mufida kuma kulum Nasreen tana zuwa yin breakfast abincin rana da na dare ma haka ,kuma DOCTOR kulum sai yaje dubata yana iske ta tana karatu Wanda itakam tad'auka ai baya zuwane ma kwata- kwata  sai da daddare kuma inya je  y ta rufe bangaren ta shima har yanzu fushin yake da ita dan baihuce ba ,Mufida kuwa sau biyu yana mata maganar tsab tace wuri abun bakaramin haushi yake bashi ba ....


Yau yakama girken Nasreen ne zaune take  ita da mai lalle sai yarfa mata Jan lalle ake wanda Mama ce ta turo a mata ,tun k'arfe biyu sai bayan magrib aka kare, Masha Allah nafad'a ganin irin yanda Jan lallen ya yimata kyau sosai anzana mata shi yayi kyau sosai kamar kalashe dubu goma tabawa mai lallen had'e da turaruka wanda dama mai lallen yar unguwar su ce„„„

Gidan ta tsaftace sosai tayi shara ta saka turare ko ina sai k'anshi yake wanka ta tsala tagabatar da salolinta sabida bata samu tayiba wata orange din shada   yasa,wani irin d'aurin kalabi tayi irin ture kaga tsiyar nan awarwaron ta tasaka sanan tasaka sark'a ta sa d'ankune simple make-up tayi ,fadamuku irin kyan datayi b'ata lokaci ne „falo tadawo TV takuna cingam tasaka abakinta tana kass kass yawu zata gwadamishi itama ta iya bariki ai wlh kowa yaci tuwo da ita miya yasha ,wayar ta taji tana ringing Mama ce ke kira aikuwa da Sauri ta doka „„


Ashe ma Rufaida ce "Nayi kewar ki Anty Nasreen gashi ina so naje gidanki amma Mama ta hana wai bayanzu ba " Rufaida tafada cikin shagwab'a harda hawayenta dan bakaramin kewar Yayar tata tayi ba ,itama Nasreen harda dan hawayen ta tace "My lovely sister nima nayi missing dinku sosai zanfad'a wa Mama sai ta barku kuzo kinji " tafada cikin kewa jitayi ankarb'e wayar Wanda Mama ce taketa fada sabida batasan Rufaida tadauk'i wayar ba 

"Nasreen ba dai har yanzu sangarta na nan ba karfe nawa Kenan ,bana son shashanci fah ki kula da auren ki karfe goma ina kikabaro min  ?" fad'a Mama tayi cikin fada „„„

Da sauri Nasreen tace "haba Mama ke ko kewata ma bakiyi ba zaki hau ni da fad'a ,kuma ai bai shigo bane  shiyasa" tafad'a cikin shagwaba 


Mama ce tace "Naji sai anjima " kit takashe wayar hawaye Nasreen takeyi Wanda kewar yan uwanta ne yadameta .

IRFAN ne yashigo da sallama Wanda inbadan tasan bayashigowa baiyi sallama ba da tace batasan yayiba ,cikin takunta na kasaita tare da Jan aji irin na manyan mata ta tako har inda yake tana kass kass da cingam tare da yimishi wani irin kallo Wanda sai Wanda akamashi zai san ya yakeji 


Ledar hannun shi takarb'a tana fad'in sannu da zuwa tare da yin gaba tana wani irin juya hips Wanda yasa DOCTOR fusgar da wani irin zazafar iska ,wurin danning din taje ta ajiye ledar ganin zai zauna bisa kujeran falon tayi Saurin riko hannunshi cikin shagwaba wada tak'ara mata kyau tace "nidai a'ah kazo muje kayi wanka kaga duk kagaji ko ” tafad'a tana bubuga k'afarta ganin tana janshi ko gezau baiyi ba 

IRFAN kuwa Mamaki ne yakashe shi to mi hakan yake nufi anya ba wani abun yarinyar nan tashirya mishiba yasan bata kyalewa amma bari yaga iya gudun ruwanta rungumota yayi Wanda itama ta lafe a kirjinshi sai dakinshi ,dama tahada masa ruwan wanka zaune yayi yana kokarin cire agogon hanunshi tsalam tayi tahaye saman cinyar shi cikin shagwaba takoma cewa “to ni miyema aikina wai komi fah nizan maka har wan...da Sauri tarufe bakinta Wanda subutar baki ne tayi ,murmushi yasaki kadan Wanda nan take kuma ya gimtse hakan yasata dan kalonshi ta lura akwai abunda yake damunshi ,amma zataji ko ma me ne ne ,ciremishi a gogon tayi Wanda yacusa kanshi cikin wuyanta sabida bakaramin dad'i kamshin turaren ta Yamasa ba da Sauri tayo kasa ta ciremasa takalmin ajesu gefe tayi mikewa yayi tsaye yarike hanunshi a kugu yana mata wani irin kalo ganin kamar tafara kame kame ,aikuwa bata isaba itace zata cire masa kayan ba bariki take son gwadamishi ba yafita iyawa karya bariki ma take murmushi yasaki ganin zata fita yayi Saurin rungumeta ta baya cikin kunanta ya rada mata " haba hajiya ta ai ba kiyi komi ba Ashe iya barikin ki anan tatsaya ,to baki isaba sai kinciremin duka kayana sanan kuma kimin wanka "  da Sauri Nasreen ta rufe idonta jin ya war ware mata d'an kwalinta bakaramin tafiya dashi kitson yayi ba kasan cewar bai taba ganinta babu d'an kwali  ba tunda tazo sai yanzu kitson yashafa yana fad'in "wan nan kitson yayi kyau masha Allah " yafad'a yana sunbatar kan nata.


Nasreen kuwa abun yafara bata tsoro karfa reshe yajuye da Mujiya ,da Sauri tafara k'ok'arin kwacewa ganin haka yasaketa ya rufe kofar da key  yana fad'in "Hajiya jiranki fah nake ina son yin wanka zafi nakeji " yafad'a yana sakar mata wani irin murmushin mugunta. 

Nasreen kam ta tsorata da lamarin DOCTOR Ashe karyar iskanci take anya zata iya kuwa? karfa yaga rauninta yaraina ta aikuwa da cikin jarumta ta matsoshi b'alle masa botirin rigar tashiga yi Wanda tayi Saurin  rufe idonta kad'an kome ta tuna kuma ta Budé da Sauri singletin ta cire masa Wanda cikin zuciyar ta ba abunda take sai addu'a ganin fadadan kirjin mijin nata Wanda gashi suka kwanta raf raf ajiki belt din tashiga zugewa Wanda bakaramin jarumta ta aroba kasa tayi da wandon Wanda da Sauri ta rufe idonta ganin dagashi sai boxer gashi ansamu akasi ta tabo kayan aiki Wanda taga suncika wurin da Sauri tajaye hanunta „„„



DOCTOR kam bakaramin jinjina mata yayi ba duk dayasan acikin ranta tsoro ne fal kallonta yake cikin wani irin yanayi jin ta tabo inda yake masa kai kai yasakashi sauke ajiyar zuciya mai karfi Wanda sai data d'ago tana kalonsa „„„


Gira d'aya yadagamata aikuwa kafin tayi wani réaction ya  juyota da Sauri zip din rigar yazuge Wanda yasa Nasreen rike rigarta ta gaba dan taga alamun kasa yake son yi da ita gashi bata saka bra ba shirinta zai wargaje Kenan 


Bata k'arasa tunaninta ba taji yayi k'asa da rigar aikuwa sai gata zigidir daga ita sai dan pant da Sauri ta sa hannu ta kare kirjinta bata ankaraba taji yayi sama da ita sai cikin toilet d'in pant din yake k'ok'arin cire mata tayi Saurin rikewa tana girgiza masa kanta Wanda har hawaye sun fara taruwa  a ciki „„„


Shower yasakar musu kasancewar ta juya baya shi kuma yana rungume da ita ta baya ,wasa yafara yimata da cibiyar ta hanunshi yake shafa mata tundaga kan cibiyar ta har yazo dai dai kirjinta Wanda har karkarwa hanunsa yakeyi wajen tabawa da Sauri ya cab'e d'aya yafara matsawa ahankali yana Sumba tar wuyanta a hankali ,Nasreen kuwa ta fara shiga wani irin hali da Sauri tace "ashhh " jin wani irin matsa daya yima boobs dinta gantare masa tayi da Sauri yajuyota yaza mana suna fuskantar juna bakinshi ya aza saman nata wani irikin kissing dinta yakeyi kamar mayunwancin zaki ,Nasreen kam tafara sarewa da al'amarin DOCTOR domin yafara kureta 

Gangaro da bakinsa yayi zuwa kirjinta daya yashiga matsawa,dayan kuma yashiga tsotsar sa kamar wani baby sai ruwan dake saukowa a saman su yana jikasu ,ganin fah yafara fita hayacinsa Wanda tanajin yanda IRFAN dinshi take dan Harbin duwawunta yasaka ta fara kuka Wanda da Sauri yafara dawowa hayacinsa kalon ta yayi yaga yanda take hawaye bakinsa yasaka yana lashe hawayen sabulu yasaka da soso yafara yimusu wankan Wanda ita dai duk a tsorace take dashi

A d'a rare take dashi wanke su yayi tas Wanda ita dai idonta a rufe yake gashi sai tabe -tabe yake mata ta matsu sukare aikuwa daukar ta yayi kamar jaririya ya fito da ita ajjiye ta yayi  saman gadon Wanda har yanzu da pant din ta shima kuma da boxer dinsa towel ya d'auko yafara gogemata jiki dashi tsap yagoge mata jiki cream dinshi yashafa musu ,wani carton naga ya d'auko wanda akayi ambalen shi bud'eshi yayi wata arniya rigar bacci naga ya fito da ita fara ce saka mata yayi ,rigar iya guiwa ta tsaya mata da ita da tsirara babu banbanci pant din ne dai sai data shige bayi tasaka shi aikuwa tana fitowa yayi Saurin rungumeta yana juyi da ita dariya tafashe da ita a hankali dan bakaramin burgeshi yaga tayi ba sabida rigar tayimata kyau sosai kamar dan ita akayi ta ,Jan hanunta yayi suka wuce falon plate ya d'auko bisa daninng din yakarasa bud'e ledar yayi gasasun kaji ne sai kamshi sukeyi kazar daya yasaka ya cicirata zama yayi nuni Yamata da tazo ta hau saman cinyar sa aikuwa ganin yakoma IRFAN dinshi na asali yayi fuska yasakata zuwa ta zauna 

Kazar ya yaga yasaka mata abaki Budé baki tayi yasaka mata aikuwa bata yarika yi sai shagwaba takeyi kamar bata son ci ,sai daya tabatar ta koshi yoghourt d'in yatsiyaya mata ya bata tasha sai gashi tana gyatsa "Alhmdllah "ta furta

Ganin haka yasakashi shima dan ci kad'an hanun shi daya yana kasan cibiyar ta sai zagayeta yakeyi wanda Nasreen tayi kamar bata ji „„„

Sai da yad'anci sanan yasha juice d'in ,sunkutar ta yayi wada a lokacin taji cikin ta ya d'uri ruwa dan tafara tsorata da la marinsa ,bai ajeta ko ina ba sai dakinshi sallaya yashinfida Zani da hijab taga yamiko mata Wanda bata san yaushe ya d'auko ba al'wala yajata sukayi sukayi brush sallah yatada wada Nasreen tagama sarewa da al'amarin mijin nata amma baza ta yarda komi zai faruba yasameta a bagas ba raka'a biyu sukayi sanan yajuyo yadafa goshinta yajima yana addu'a sanan yashafa ,


Ganin yanda duk ta tsorata Wanda yasan aidama karya iskanci take kuma yau babu fashi kulum shine kwana da ciwon mara bayan gashida gonaki har guda biyu ina abun bazai yu yu ba,kalonta yayi yace " kije ki kwanta "itama da Sauri ta kaleshi dan tagane nufinshi Kenan adak'in nan zata kwana tab gashi yasama k'ofar key„„


Bayan da ta iya bayan tabi umarni hakan yasata mik'ewa da hijabinta ta haye gadon can karshen gadon tahau ta takure wuri d'aya ,kalonta yayi kawai ya girgiza kai yaci gaba da lazuminshi bai kareba sai karfe d'aya 

Lumke sallaya yayi jalabiyar sa yacire dagashi sai singleton da boxer

Nasreen kai dama batayi barcci ba dan bataga ta barcci ba ganin ya hau gadon kuma tanajin yana addu'ar bacci shafawa yayi sanan itama yamata yashafa rungume ta yayi ta baya  cikin kunanta yazura harshensa yana mata yawo dashi da Sauri ta kara matsawa shima matsota yayi sannu a hankali kamar mai koyon magana yace " bakyajin zafi ne kike bacci da wanan kinga cire hijabin miye zaki boyemin Nida kayana" yafada cikin wani irin Salo ,


Idonta ta rufe da karfi jin ya cire mata hijabin zanin ya kwance yazama na daga ita sai rigar baccin k'afafunta yafara shafawa yana mata wani irin tafiyar tsutsa har ya gangaro kan cibiyar ta rigar ma gabad'aya ya cire mata lasar cibiyar ta yakeyi har ya gangaro kan kirjinta caraf yasakashi abakinshi yashiga tsotsa

Nasreen kai hankalinta yafara barin jikinta jin salon da IRFAN d'in nata yake mata nishi ta farayi kad'an kad'an bakinshi yahad'a da nata yafara sauke mata wasu zafafan kisss masu tsayawa a rai „„„

Kanshi tarike tashiga shafawa har izuwa gadon bayanshi DOCTOR kam yafara gigicewa dan IRFAN dinshi tamike sosai sai Harbin iska take kamar zata fasa wandon tafito fuskarshi ce tayi ja jikinshi na makarkata yayi jifa da boxer din dama singleti Yajima da jifah da ita 

Tsirara yake haihuwar Ammi da Sauri yad'an ware k'afafunta kad'an pant d'in da yayi saura yaciremata Wanda tajishi itakam zata ga iya gudun ruwanshi duk dakuwa itama dad'in takeji ,yatsarshi daya yasaka a cikin gindinta „

Wasu irin ruwa yaji suna gangarowa masu yauk'i kamar ankad'a miyar kub'ewa tara hanunshi yayi Wanda sai anbaliya ruwan sperm yake ganga rowa samanshi „„

IRFAN kai bakaramin tashi hankalinsa yayi ba bakinshi yakai kasanta harshen shi yasaka yana zuk'o ruwan wani kamshi yaji yadaki hancinsa ,lashe wurin yakeyi a slow kamar yana shan Zuma kara ware k'afafun yayi yasaka yatsarshi ciki sannu ahankali yana neman hanya da Sauri tamike zaune tare da game kafafunta jin zafi yafara ziyarta ta 

IRFAN kai ganin haka yarungumeta yana da Sauri cikin wata irin murya yace “ki kiyi hakuri ki bari kar kisa najimiki ciwo" yafad'a jikinshi na karkarwa 

Kuka tafashe dashi ganin fa yau ba sarki sai Allah gashi ance mata abun baida sauki fitar Virgin sai ansha wuya gashi yatsarshi kadai ma Saï da taji zafin haka kanshi taji saman cibiyar ta ,kara fashewa da kuka tayi tana fadin “Yaya IRFAN kayi hakuri bazan saké ba dan Allah kar kamin "tafada cikin tsoro sai yarfe hanunta take tana kuka 

Gogan kam yanzu neman hanyar shiga yake dan pénis dinsa har wani zafi zafi take masa ga ciwon mara dayaji yafara ware kafafunta yayi ganin haka yasaka ta kwala wata irin kara wada yasashi koma wa gefe yana rik'eda mararshi 

Nasreen kai rigarta take neman sakawa ganin yayi wata irin jijjiga sai mutsu mutsu yake dafe da mararshi duk da kuwa ya lulube da bargo sai zufa take karyomasa „

Da Sauri ta yi kanshi tana fadin " miye yasameka katashi dan Allah kar ka mutu kabarni bazan iya rayuwa babu kaiba "tafada cikin fitar hayaci tabi duk takanai nayeshi ,banbareta yake kokarin yi domin kara mishi bala'i kawai takeyi zamansu a kusa 

Cikin tsawa daba tayi zato ba taji yace " ke ki bacemin daga kusa dani bakida abunda zakimin idan na mutu kin huta "yafada cikin fitar hayaci ,Nasreen bata da daraba takara hayewa samanshi tana kuka fadi take " kayi hakuri fadamin me kake so zanmaka amma kar ka mutu kabarni kaji "

Ture ta yayi kad'an ya yaye bargon hanunta yakamo yad'ora saman katuwar abarsa yace "ki shamin wanan ki matsamin idan bahaka ba mutuwa zanyi " yafad'a yana samata abunshi cikin hannu ta aikuwa jin wanan katuwar abar yasaka Nasreen kwace hanunta da Sauri tana kuka dan bakaramin tsoro abun yabata ba

Kuka takeyi sosai tana so tataimaki Mijin nata amma tsoro takeji ,cikin sarkewa yace "kina so mala'iku su tsinemiki ko to kihana ni hak'k'ina " yafad'a da Sauri idonshine suka fara ka kafewa sabida yau kam yakai limite inda bazai iya jurewa ba....✍

                ...........✍Kuka Nasreen ta fashe da shi ganin yanda idonshi yafara yin sama k'ara rungume shi tayi tana fad'in “please katashi kayi nayarda ,bana so mala'iku su tsinemin dan Allah katashi ”tafad'a tana jijigashi duk tabi ta rud'
IRFAN kuwa baisan Wace duniya yake ba baya son yima ta dole k'ar tayi tunanin jikinta yake so shiyasa gara yabarta yasan kuma idan yace zai yi da k'arfi jimata  ciwo zaiyi ,jin tana cewa tayarda duk da baya hayyacinsa yaburkita ta kasa, cikin buk'atuwa da fitar haiyaci yace “kiyi hak'uri sannu sannu zanyimiki insha Allah kinji ”

Yafad'a cikin wani irin yanayi kasanta yadawo yaci gaba da tsotsar ta „„„

Yatsanshi ya zura a hankali dan yasamu hanya a tsuke yaji wajen wata ajiyar zuciya ya sauke a hankali yake tura yatsar sa cikin wani irin jin ciwo Nasreen tayi Saurin rik'e hannunshi tana girgiza masa kanta 

Ba karamin tausayi ta bashi ba ganin yada duk tabi ta tsorata yasaka shi zuwa dai dai kunanta yana fad'in “sorry a hankali zanmiki “yafada yana k'ok'arin saita alk'alamin sa goga mata pénis dinsa yakeyi a hankali yana wasa da abar sa wurin Nasreen kuwa dad'i takeji sosai kamar zata shid'e ,sai shafa kanshi takeyi sunkai minti biyar a hakan adu'ar kwanciya da iyali yayi a ransa 

Sanan yamaida bakinshi cikin nata ganin hankalinta yad'auke yafara tura abarsa aikuwa da Sauri tarik'e fillon dake saman bed d'in „„„

A hankali yake shiga Wanda yaji wurin a matse a kame ,shiga yakeyi ama abar taki shiga a hankali yafara buga mata gwatso yana neman hanyar shi 

Nasreen wani zafi taji yafara kai mata farmaki hakan yasata ta damk'e dantsan hannun shi hawaye ne suke mata anbaliya a fuska badamar k'wala kara sabida bakinshi daya ke cikin nata pus kakeji Doctor yafasa Nasreen dinshi ,wani irin zabura tayi ta kwace bakinta Wanda wani ihu da ta buga in akwai makwabta kusa zasuji shi ,Doctor kai yana shiga yaji kanshi yayi wani irin Sara mishi Wanda yasashi dakatawa yarike kan ga karar da Nasreen tasaki 

Pénis dinsa yaji tak'ame a cikin part dinta having sex yafara da ita kai da koma yafara yana Kiran sunan Allah jinshi a wata Duniya wada ko a Mafarki baiyi zato ba 

Wani irin gardi had'e da dad'i ne suka mishi yawa yarasa Miyake ji ,zufah ce ta fara karyo mishi kanshi yafara girgizawa yana dukan bed din cikin zafin sa ,

Wani dad'i yakeji yana ziyartar sa hawaye yaji suna gangaro mishi Wanda dad'in dayake kwasa bai masan yanayi ba cikin fitar hayaci yafara fadin “washh ah ah wai wayo Allah kaina ,wayo Ammi ,wayo Angel dina ,kinajin me nakeji kuwa Nasreen...”yafad'a da k'arfi tare da cigaba da buga mata gwatso sai dukan bed d'in yakeyi „„„

Nasreen kai wani irin zafi takeji a kasanta kamar ana watsa mata barkono ,wani cizo ta gatsara masa a k'irji Wanda bakad'an yaji zafin ba amma dadin dayeke kwasa yasaka shi manta wa da zafin.

Nasreen kuwa bata san duniya takeba ko lahira awa guda da rabi Kenan sai bugunta yakeyi cikin fitar hayaci kamar zakin da yayi shekaru bai ci abinci ba ,

Doctor kuwa yatafi kawai burinshi yasamu yafitar da abunda yajima yana Tarawa a marashi ,sai yayi kamar zai yi relasing sai kuma yacigaba shikaran kanshi yasan dabaya hayacinsa yana neman Wanda zai dakatar dashi ba laifinshi bane ,Nasreen kuwa yanzu ta suma dan batasan inda kanta yakeba ,wani irin ihu yasaki Wanda ya farfad'o da Nasreen suman datayi “wayo wayo wayo Allah panis d'ina zata tsinke washh wayo Allah angel d'ina naba ki kaina....ah
Yehh ...yes ...oh shet wayo nidai zan mutu zan kawo zankawo NASFAN ...” fad'ar DOCTOR Kenan Wanda shima da zaidawo hayacinsa bazai fad'amata abunda yafad'a ba „„„

Nasreen kai dama ta farfado tun ihun dayayi tanajin surutun dayakeyi amma batasan ina zata saka kanta ba wani irin abu mai dumi taji yagangara kasan Marata a hankali tanajin haka taga yarage bugunta dayakeyi kawai sai taji yakwanta sharkaf akanta gabadaya ya tulamata nauyinsa 

Wani irin suma takara yi sabida jin nauyinsa gabadaya ajikinta 

Doctor kam jin ya zubar da abunda yake damunshi shekara da shekaru yaji wani irin sanyi aranshi sakayau yajishi hakan yasa kanshi kasa sauka akanta dan sai yanzu yakejin kugunshi namasa wani irin zafi had'e da k'ugunsa.

Kuma yaji lokacin da yasakemata hanun tasaki alamar ta suma Kenan yana son zare jikinshi yataimaka mata amma shima neman agajin yakeyi cikin karfin hali had'e da sunan Allah yakoma gefenta sharkaf sai add'a da duk tazo bakinsa yakeyi ,


Idonshi ne yaji sun fara rufewa Wanda baisan dalili ba karaf yarufe idonshi wani irin mafarki yakeyi Nasreen dinshi yagani cikin wani mugun yanayi tana neman taimako sai kuka takeyi tayi jina jina da jini kamar wada aka kwato daga bakin kura ,yakai minti arba'in cikin wanan mugun yanayin „

Da Sauri yafarka mikewa yayi zaune cikin dauriya da jarumta kalon ta yayi yaga ba alamar rai ata tare da ita da Saurin shi yayi kanta ciccib'ota yayi ya d'ora akan jikinshi rungumeta yayi yana fad'in “subhannallahi llahi ke Budé idonki ,kibud'e idonki Angel d'ina nine fah IRFAN dinki ,kibud'e idonki ki kaleni ina tsananin kaunar ki kece sirri na maha din rayuwata ,bazan iya rayuwa babu ke dan Allah kitashi kirufa min asiri” yafad'a wasu hawaye suna sulalo mishi ganin irin b'arna da yayi mata bakarama bace bubuga fuskarta yakeyi cikin wani irin tashin hankali ganin ko gizau batayi ba 


Bakinshi yasaka cikin nata yana busa mata iskar bakinshi aikuwa a na uku tayi tari yadan sarke mata Budé idon ta tafarayi dishi dishi take gani har tadaina gani wata irin kishirwa takeji kamar bata taba shan ruwa ba ,hakan yasakata fara magana sannu sannu Wanda sai dayakai kanshi dai dai kunanta sanan yaji tana fadin “ruwa ru..wa ”aikuwa da Saurin shi ya d'aura towel ak'ugunshi fita yayi da Sauri ya d'auko mata masu dan sanyi jikinshi ya rungumota ,ruwan yakafa mata abaki tafara kwan kwad'a sai data sha kusan Rabi sanan tayi wani yunkurin Amai

Amai tafara yi Wanda duk ajikinshi tayi shi riketa yayi sosai sai da ta kare takoma ta lafe akirjinshi ,bayanta yake dan bubuga wa sanan yaga kamar tana kokarin rufe idon ta 

Da Sauri yamaida kanta saman filo kitchen yashiga gas yakuna ya dora ruwan zafi koma wa yayi wurinta koda yazo idonta a rufe ga hawaye sai kwarara suke agefen fuskar ta 

Hakan yatabatar masa tana jinsa Kenan jin ya kara rungumota yasaka ta fashe masa da wani irin kuka tana kokarin k'wacewa ,gam yariketa yana fad'in “kiyi Hakuri angel dina miye fad'amin naji ”yafad'a cikin wata irin murya ,cikin kukan ta mai hade da zallah kuruciya tace “bakai bane ina ta rokon ka kayi hak'uri kaki sai yi kakeyi dan baka so na ka kashe ma Mama ni katai kaci gaba da cin amarci kaida matar ka ko nidai ka kyaleni bana so ” cewar Nasreen sai dukan kirjinshi takeyi ....

IRFAN kuwa ya san yad'e bo ruwan dafa kanshi dariya ce taso kubucemishi jin tana fad'in zaije yaci amarci da matar shi bayan itace yafara cin amarci da ita ,amma bazai   fad'ama mata ba ya san kishine yake damunta„

Harshen shi yasaka yana lashe mata hawayen sai daya tabatar ya sud'e kaf sanan ya kalli fuskar ta yaga yanda duk takumbura Sumba tar ta yasakeyi yace “to minene yi shiru kinji angel dina ,ba'abunda zanmiki baza ki mutu ba kowace mace da kike gani da haka tasaba ,girman Kenan yanzu Na-san Nasreen din Abba ta girma ”yafada yana kara kai mata Sumba a goshinta 

“Sorry kinji Allah yamiki albarka Allah yabamu zaman lafiya Nagode sosai da wanan Babar kyautar da kika bani kyauta ce wada ba'ataba yimin irinta ba tunda nake a rayuwata ,kece farin cikina ,uwar gida na aduniya sanan kuma a lahira ma kece insha Allah ”cewar Doctor tare da kwantar da ita „„

Ruwan yasaka a wata Roba baba Wanda sai tafasa sukeyi toilet d'in yakai ya sirka da na sanyi kadan ,dawowa yayi ya d'auketa wada bak'aramin zafi taji ba ,cikin ruwan yasaka ta aikuwa wata irin k'ara tasaki tana yarfe hannu ganin ya dannata a ciki dannata yayi aciki sosai ya ware cinyoyinta tayanda zafin zai ratsa ta aikuwa sai hawaye takeyi tana kukan shagwab'a bakinshi yasaka cikin nata yana sakar mata kiss Wanda nan take taji tafara ambaliya kwace bakinta tayi ganin ya fara yawo da hanunshi saman kirjinta buge hanun tayi Wanda yasakashi dan tabe baki yana fad'in “Hajiyata wane dare ne jemage bai gani ba ”wasu ruwan yasake had'amata Wanda shima bakarami  zafi taji ba sai dayata batar yamata gashin dayasan ko zataji ciwo yanzu to kad'an ne sanan yace “to bari namiki wanka tsarki ko ”hararshi tayi Wanda yaso bashi dariya amma yagimtse yana mai daga mata gira sama „„

Fita yayi Wanda sai yanzu yakejin kamar sasare mishi kafafu akeyi ,bed din yaga yayi kaca kaca duk jini yabatashi da Ruwan Sperme dinsu har wurin ya dan bushe tattare shi  yayi, yayi band'aki da shi yana shiga tana k'ok'arin fitowa Wanda sai cizon baki take alamar har yanzu dai tana dan jin zafi d'aukar ta yayi ya aje bisa stood san nan yashinfid'a wani bed sheet d'in a gadon k'ara  d'aukar ta yayi ya d'ora saman gadon kanta yashafa yana kalon face dinta sai lumshe ido take alamar bacci ,addu'a yayi yashafama ta kasancewar dama daga ita sai dan towel lulubeta yayi da dara sanan yakoma bayin „

Wanke zanin gadon yayi yashanya sanan yasakar ma kanshi shower sai murmishi yake saki cikin ranshi yake fadin “ashe Aure Rahama ne wlh yanzu sakayau nakejin kaina kamar andokemin wani dutse dake saman kaina Allah nagode maka daka bani ita a Matsayin Matata Allah kabani ikon jin tausayinta da kyautata mata kabani ikon yin adalchi a tsakanin Mata na ”yafad'a yana kashe showar wanka shima yayi hade da alw'ala 

Dakin yashigo kanshi yagoge da towel daya sanan yadoki jallabiya yasaka sallaya ya shinfida yatada Salah raka'a biyu yayi na godewa Allah daya sa Matar shi bata kasance cikin Mazinata ba yana fatan Allah yakare masa ita Yakuma basu zur'ia tagari ,yajima yana adu'o'i sai da yaga karfe hud'u saura sanan lumke sallayar gadon yahau addu'a yayi ya rungume abar sa wada barcci yayi awon gaba da ita ,sai muce su DOCTOR asuba ta gari...



Ta bangaren Mufida kai bakaramin haushi taji ba dako dubata baiyi ba sai wajen sha biyu bacci ya fara d'aukar ta ,can taji ihu daya sa ta tashi zaune dan taji ihu Wanda lokacin da Doctor yabuda hanyar shine,mik'ewa tayi tayi falon k'ofar falon tabud'e kasancewar ta mara tsoro aikuwa tazo ta wucewa dan ita niyar ta ta kwankwasa musu kofa wata kila suma sunji ihun kawai sai jin kukan Nasreen tayi Wace taketa rokon Doctor, ita ba yarinya bace tafahimci komi Kenan hakan yana nufin yaya Irfan Nasreen ya fara sani wani irin kishi ne yataso mata had'e da wani irin kuka bakinta ta rufe gudun kar suji

Aikuwa awurin ta zauna tana mai rera kukan ta ko tsoro bataji bata bata kara sarewa ba sai da taji Doctor na sumbatu kunan ta ta rufe tana mai yi part dinta da gudu „

Ina da sake bazata iya ba bazata iya kalon wanan bak'in cikin ba dole ma tabar mata gidan ta kota halin kaka ne domin Irfan dan ita kadai akayishi sai sunyi kuka da idonsu sai sunsan cewa sun tabo jinin Maradawa share hawayenta tayi takwanta Wanda surutun IRFAN Din kawai takeji sunamata gizo a kune batayi bacci ba sai karfe hud'u yasaceta ...



Ta bangaren DOCTOR kai bacci sukasha mai dad'i barema shi gogan Wanda yasashi makara bai farkaba sai kusan bakwai shima motsin ta yaji tana lalube alamar alkwai abunda take nema cikin muryata dabata fita tace “lunette ina bana gani sosai kamikomin ” da Sauri yatashi dama gefen gadon ya'aza ta mik'o mata yayi tare da taimaka mata ta tashi ,sakamata yayi kanta tadafe datakejin yana d'an Sara mata haske yagani fayau hakan yasashi mik'ewa da Sauri aikuwa sai gashi kasa yafad'i sabida tsamin da kafafun sa sukayi kuma dama  ya san za'ayi haka kafafunshi yarik'e yana cije lebe

Nasreen ganin faduwar sa yasakata mikewa da Sauri har ta manta ciwon ta hanunshi tarik'e tana fad'in “miye yasameka bakada lafiya ne ”tafada cikin rud'ewa ganin yada ta rud'e yasashi karfin hali yatashi yana fad'in “bakomi kinga kaina ne yaked'an min ciwo ”yafad'a yana mai kama hannunta wadda taked'an d'ingisawa sukayi toilet al'wala suka dauro salaya yashin fid'a musu suka tada sallah bayan sunka rene yazauna yana lazumi ,

Nasreen ma kur'anin ta d'auko tafara karatunta cikin kwanciyar hankali ganin haka yasakashi mik'ewa yayi bangaren Mufida dan ya san duk yada akayi yau zatayi tsarki ko ma tayi tunda dama tace bata wu ce kwana biyar ko shida ,har addu'a yayi kar Allah yasa Nasreen din shi bata kai haka ya san da kashinshi yabushe„„

Falon nata yashiga kuma ya san zataji ba dad'i jiya da bai zo dubata ba zai bata hakuri albarkacin gara da yakwasa „

Aikam yana shiga yaganta zaune tana Dana waya taci wanka cikin wasu matsatun kaya rungumeshi tayi tana sakar masa kiss ko ajikinshi shima rungumar ta yayi yana fad'in “ya akayi yi ” kirjinta ta farara gogamishi Wanda ya san da gaya takeyi ,bai cemata komi ba sai da yaga tana kokarin dagamishi jalabiya hanunta take kokarin zurawa cikin wandonshi kamar yar hannu karaf yarik'e hannun dan ya san ko ta tada mai hankali ya san inda zai sauke nashi matsalar itafah sai shi dole kuma ai ba lokacinta bane duk da shi baya son matan nashi suka sance masu kunyar shi sabida yafi so a idon jama'a masu kunya amma a idonshi yana so suzama uwayen bariki amma bazai shiga hak'in wata ba kiss yabata agoshi yana fad'in “kibari har lokacin ki yayi bana son shiga hak'k'i ,gaba yayi abunsa yakoma part din gimbiyar sa 

Aikuwa karatun takeyi ,dukda kuwa yanda yakejinshi zaiyi kokari ya dora musu breakfast dan ya san zatace zatayi bayan kuma bazata iya ba aikuwa yana shiga yajita abayanshi tana fadin “ina kwana ”tafada tana dukar dakanta dan bakaramin kunya shi takeji ba 

Gogan kam rungumeta yayi yana fadin “fatan dai kintashi lafiya Angel dina ” shiru tamasa ganin haka yashi dagota yana kalon fuskar ta hawaye yaga tana yi 

“ya Allah yanzu kuma minene uhm fadamin gimbiya ta har yanzu fushin kike da angon naki ?”yafad'a yana matsa hanunta 

Kanshi kirjinshi tashiga bubugawa cikin shagwab'a tana fadin “bakai bane Allah nidai sai ka goyani zan yafema ”tafad'a tana bubuga kafarta 

Dariya yadanyi mai sauti yace “nawa zaki biya dan ni ba doki bane ”yafada yana mata kalon kasa da sama ganin irin kayan datasa wanda Riga da siket ne yan kanti bakaramin kyau suka mata ba 

Shagalta yayi da kalon ta Wanda tafasa wani irin ihu zatayi waje aikuwa da Sauri ya rukota yana fadin “shikenan baby na kwantar da hankalinki hau indai zaki yafemin ”dukawa yayi aikuwa zata hau taga yamata tsawo kujerar kitchen d'in tadauko ta hau Wanda sai dariyarta yake wai guntuwa ce ita itadai bayan ta hau duk da kuwa yanda kugunshi da kafafunsa kemasa ciwo baidamu ba matukar zatayi farin ciki zai mata koma minene ,kitchen d'in suka fita zagaye yafara yi da ita dariya tafara kyakyatawa yana tayata sai da tagaji dan kanta tace yasauke ta ,sauketa din yayi yana maida numfashi 

“gaskiya ke kam yar lukuta ce kin karya mun baya ”yafad'a yana rike bayansa aikuwa kukan shagwab'a tasaki tana bubuga k'afa “Allah nidai ba yar lukuta bace kaine dai dan lukuti ”tafada tana bugun kirjinshi cakulkuli ya fara yimata aikuwa sai dariya takeyi „„

Burum sukaji anbugo kofar aikuwa Mufida ce wada tazo yin breakfast wada har tara ta gota gashi ba'akirata ba kuma ita yunwa takeji hakan yasakata zuwa ko wata k'ila sungama rawa kan sunci nasu ,yau basa bukatarta 

Kalon ta DOCTOR yayi ya dan Harare ta baice komi ba sai mik'ewa yayi daya yi kitchen dan ya san dama breakfast d'in yakawota Nasreen ce tace “ina kwana Anty ”wani banzan kalo ta watsa mata afakaice ,sanan tace “lafiya lau ya ki ke ” tafad'a tana zama bisa daning din ,Nasreen kai sai yanzu ta tuna Ashe breakfast zasu had'a ta shagaltar dasu kitchen din ta iskeshi yana fere dankalin turawa kama mishi tayi ruwan zafi ya d'ora itakuma tana fere dankalin har suka soyashi juyeshi sukayi sukasa komi inda yakamata bata yayi tanakai wa Wanda sai Harar ta Mufida takeyi ganin tana dan dingisawa Wanda take gani kamar da gaya takeyi 

Zama sukayi kowa sai cin abuncinsa yakeyi shine ya fara mikewa falon yazauna sanan Mufida itama tamike tazo wuce wa ko kalo bata isheshi ba dan bakaramin haushi tabashi ba data shigo musu ko sallama babu bayaso yamata Fad'a gaban Kishiyar ta baya son raini yashiga tsakaninsu zai mata fada insun keb'e 

Nasreen kai kayan tata tara ta kai kitchen sanan tafara kokarin mopin rungumeta yayi ta baya yana fadin “to sarkin tsapta gidan baiyi komi ba zomuje akwai maganar da zamuyi da tafi wanan mahimanci ”yafada yana janta bisa kujerar falon ,zama yayi yasata atsakiyar shi sunsunar ta yafara yana fadin “nagode yayi kad'an domin kin bani abunda ba kowace budruwa take kaishi gidan mijin ta ba ,ina so zanfadamiki abubuwan da baki sani ba akan Ku karan kanku mata ,sabida dad'in da na kwasa jiya natabatar Mata ta tana d'aya daga cikin mata masu ni'ima Wanda ni'imar su naturak ce yanzu kafin nan fadamin me kike so nabaki amatsayin gift ?”

Hanunshi ta sakala cikin nashi tana fadin “ni ba na son komi Abu guda daya Nike so shine kar kajuyamin baya ako wane hali na tsinci kaina ,sannan kuma ka girmama iyaye na da yan uwana kamar yanda kakeyi ,sanan kayi adalci a tsakaninmu iyalanka da y'a y'a ka ”shikam mizai ce wa Allah daya ba shi ita amatsayin mata ga ta dai yarinya da ita amma da hankalin manya sai abunda baza arasa ba shima ya san zata daina 

Yanajin da wata ce abun duniya zata roka fiyeda mutuncin ta ,shikuwa zaiyi kokari cikamata burinta „„

Sumbatar gashinta yayi yana fad'in “angama angel d'ina nemi wani Abu "girgizamishi kanta tayi alamar babu da Sauri kuma tace “jiya naji ka kirani da wani suna miye ma na manta yawa NASFAN miye ma'anar haka ?” murmushi yasaki yakara Sumba tar ta sanan yace “NASFAN yana nufin NASRENN farkon sunnaki Kenan sai FAN din yana nufin IRFAN kinga karshen suna na ne shine in an hada zaibada NASFAN “kara lafewa jikinshi tayi hakan yasashi murmushi yana shafa kanta 

“Rufe idonki sai nace kibud'e sanan zaki bud'e” yafada yana shafa cikinta aikuwa da Sauri ta rufe hannu yazura a aljihunsa yaza ro wani dan gift wani zobe na zinari Wanda akala zaikai millions guda yasakata mata a hannu wanda a tsakiyashi an RUBUTA NASFAN  a Paris yasaka aka kera mishi shi sabida jiran wana rana „

Cewa yayi tabud'e idonta aikuwa tabud'e kalon hanunta tayi wani irin kyau zoben  ya yimata sai k'yali yakeyi wasu hawayen farin ciki ne suka zubomata koba afadamata ba tasan mai tsada ne Ashe Doctor zai zame mata haka da wani ne yafadamata haka bazata yarda ba jitayi yana sharemata hawayen 

“to minene kuma na kuka ”rungumeshi tayi sosai kamar wani zai kwace mata shi tace “nagode sosai Baby na Allah yasaka da alkairi Allah yajikan kakani ”tafada tana kara rungumeshi 

Dunguremata kai yayyi yace “rike godiyar ki ba'anan zaki nunamin ba ”yafada cikin kunanta murmushi tayi „

“yawa saura maganar mu tagaba ki saurareni da kyau kuma kiyi aiki da abunda zanfad'a inbahaka ba nacire kunan nan ”yafad'a yana kame mata kune d'aya cikin wasa.....✍

[9/4 20:38] Ummu Safiyya Adam😍🥰😘: DOCTOR IRFAN🥼

_MARUBUTA MASU AIKI DA FASAHA DA ZALLAR TSAKWARON AMANA, FAƊAKARWA HAƊI DA NISHAƊANTARWA GAMI DA WA'AZANTARWA, HUMM WANI ABUN SAI ROYAL STAR WRITERS ASSOCIATION👊🏾🦋 RUWA KAƊAI KE MAGANIN ƘISHI☀️_

*"ALƘALAMINMU YANCINMU⚔️*

Book 1
Page 51/52
               .....✍Ummi ce zaune wurin wani kasurgumin daji Wanda baka jin komi sai kukan tsuntsaye ,zaune take a kasan wata bishiya katuwa tsuntsayene keta shawagi a samanta Wanda bawai tsuntsayen bane kawai sakamon wasu da ga cikin tsuntsayen idonsu jajir kamar garwashi ,kirari ta fara kamar haka “ya daga daga ya budu ya shumbudi budi ya shude ya gulmanu bara bara ya tulbe ,boka gulmanu kana ina uban shedanu da aljanu garabuti ya dafa maka sai kayi uban gaya ”ta fada tana Duke jikinta sai makar kata yake ko tsoro ba ta yi ga duhu kasancewar magrib ta gota „

Wani ihu naji ya karkade dajin gaba daya Wanda har tsuntsayen da ke saman bishiyar suka fara saukowa kasa suna layawa kamar sarki zai shigo fada „„

Wata dariya naji an fashe da ita cikin murya mai ban tsoro “hahahahahahhaha hahahaha ”kake ji Wanda duk ya karkade dajin da ihu ,bulut na ga wani kasurgumin mutum ya baya idonshi jajaur dashi kamar garwashi wuta sunyi luhu luhu da su fuskar nan baki kirin ko ina laya ce a jikinshi ,dagashi sai wata fata daya kare kugunshi irin na Arnan daji kanshi wani dogon gashi ne „„

Wata dariya yake babaka wa kamar amasa bushara da gidan aljanna ,kuka ya koma yi mai ban tsoro ,ummi kai makar kata ta fara duk da bashi ne zuwan ta na farko ba 

Wata kwarya naga ya taba aikuwa IRFAN ne da NASREEN sai MUFIDA da suke cin abinci ,wata irin zabura Ummi tayi tana nuna kwarya alamar Karin bayani„„

Boka gulmanu wata irin dariya ya kara fashewa da ita yace “wanan iyalan kike so a tarwatsa ba asaka musu rashin kwanciyar hankali araba kan iyalan ”ya fada yana muzurai ,aikuwa da Sauri ummi tadaga kanta cikin tsoro da mamaki duk da kuwa tasan bashine karon ta na farko ba da zuwa wurinshi „

“hahaha wa inan biyu "yayi nuni da Nasreen da IRFAN da suke makale da juna yace “sungina soyayar su bisa gaskiya da Amana ,suna da riko da Addini basa wasa da ibadar su ,Abu guda zaisa a raba kansu Wanda shi zaiyi sanadiyar rabuwar su ,sanadin haka zai shiga wani hali har ya kamu da cuta wada zata aika shi bar zau hahaha ”ya fada yana nunasu da abun hanun shi na gashin fata „

“ki samu ki jawo hankalin daya matar tasa wada take wasa da sallah ita nata mai sauki ne ,ki tashi kitafi na salameki sai kin shawo kanta ta yarda dake zamu ai watar da komi hahaha ”ya fada yana ware hayensa sama ya rufe idonsa 

Ummi kirari ta shiga yi masa kamar yanda tayi da farko da baya baya ta fita sunsayen nan na ga suna binta asama har sai da tayi tafiyar minti goma kafin taka bakin titi aikuwa tsuntsayen nan naga sunkoma Wanda alama rakiya suka mata „„

Sai karfe tara da rabi tashigo gidan Abbi ta iske zaune a falon dagashi sai Asidiq Wanda hankalinsu naga ummi da suke jira barema Asidiq 

Aikuwa tana shigowa ko salama babu gaisheda Abbi tayi tana yatsina ,kanshi kawai ya girgiza bata amsa gaisuwar da Asidiq yakemata ba tashige dakinta ,kai kawai Abbi yake girgizawa itadai ba yarinya bace da za'atsaya anama ta nasiha kulum ,yayi nasihar yayi fadan ama yawo kamar wada taci kafar kare kulum ita wurin biki ko suna Kenan ko rasuwa bata bashi kulawa wada yake so wanan sai Ammi „Asidiq ne yakatsemi shi tunanin shi da fadin “kayi hakuri Abbi nakasa gane miye yake damun ummi kayi hakuri dan Allah ,wlh so tari ina tanbayar kaina anya ma ummi ce tahaifemu Nida la maman kuwa ,miyasaka ba Ammi ce ta zamo mahaifiyar muba ,me yasa kazaba mana ita a matsayin Uwa Abbi?”

Yafada yana mai fashewa da kuka da Sauri Abbi yarufe masa baki yana girgiza masa kansa, kanshi yadora saman cinyar Abbi yaci gaba da kukanshi dan shikadai yasan abunda ummin shi take kulawa,yajita tana waya wataran itada kawarta suna maganar kai Ammi wurin boka ,magana yamata cikin salama ama baisamu komai ba sai rabon Mari tareda fadin kul yafadawa wani zata daga mishi nono yasan illar furucin bakin iyaye hakan yasashi shan kukan sa yakoshi yana mai yima Amminsu adu'a da yayan shi da Abbi dan yasan sune Abin harinta ,

Abbi ne yakatse shi da fadin “kayi hakuri yaro na Uwa Uwa ce ba'acanza wa tuwo suna kayi hakuri kuci gaba da yimata adu'a ”yafada yana bubuga bayanshi ,idonshi ya goge yana mai ma Abbi sai da safe da niyar gobe zai saké yimata magana „

Abbi kai dakinshi yaje kasancewar dama girkin ta ne intazo dankanta inba tazo ba dan kanta kwanciyar sa yayi tareda nema ma matar tashi shiriya „„


Ta bangaren Iklas kuwa idonta rufe take fadin “tata ,Dady ” tafada tana motsa hanunta ,da Sauri Mrs Hashim dayke zaune kusanta ya yi kanta yana rike hanunta ,idonta ta fara budewa Wanda take ganin dishi dishi sai data Budé idon ta sosai sanan takali Dadyn nata tasakar masa murmushi ,shima murmushin yasakar mata kokarin tashi zaune takeyi ya girgizama ma kanshi Sumba tar ta yayi yadaga mata yastu biyu Wanda tagane yaranshi yana so tabashi minti biyu zaije yakirawo Doctor ne ,hakan yasaka tasakar mishi murmushi dan bakinta yama ta nauyi ,aikuwa fita yayi bayan kamar minti biyu sai gashi da Doctor shima murmushi yasa kar mata sanan yashiga dubata yana rubuce rubuce ,

Ganin tana son tashi dadyn nata yataimaka mata ta zauna tana rungume ajikinshi ,Doctor ne yace “y'a ta fadamin Miyake miki ciwo yanzu ,mi kikeji ?”yafada yana shafa kanta cikin yar muryata mai dadi ta yara tace “banajin komi kaina dai nakejin yamin nauyi ”rubutu Doctor yayi sanan yace “zai daina shima insha Allah ”

Murmushi ta mayar masa Wanda na lura dimple dinta sai da suka lotsa „

Fita Doctor yayi kalonta tamaida ga dadyn ta sanan shagwabe fuska Wanda yagane ma'anar hakan wato tatar ta take nema 

Murmushi yasa kar mata yace “Tatar ki tatafi gida yaznu zata dawo kinji”

Yafada yana daukar ta bayin yakaita ruwa yahada mata wanka yake kokarin yimata ta shagwabe fuska dan bata so suna mata wanka acewar ta tagirma 

Ganin hakan yasan mitake nufi shima shagwabawar yayi yace “kiyi hakuri namiki kinga jikin ki ba kwari ko Mama na?”
Yafada yana kokarin murzamata soso da sabulun 
Aikuwa ganin yadage yasaka barinshi,wanka yamata tayi bros yanadota a twel ,cikin dakin ya ajita mai yashafamata sanan yasaka mata wata doguwa Riga sai yanzu naga ashe Iklas bakaramin kyau ne da itaba masha Allah kyakyawar yarinya ce ,abincin yahadama ta Wanda yakamata masu irin cutar suci sanan yasumba ci kuncinta yace “zanje waje yanzu nadawo kici abincin duka kafin nadawo zankai ki gidan zoo”

Aikuwa abincin tafara ci ganin haka yayi waje dan yana son ganin in Rumaisan ta farka yasan alura yanzu ta isa sakinta 

Aikuwa yana shiga yatar da ita tana kokarin mikewa taimaka mata yayi tamike sanan yace “a hankali kar kifadi Ilkas dinki ta farka 

Aikuwa da dan saurunta suka kama hangar shiga dakin ,suna shiga tayi kan yar tata ta rungumeta anan take kuma tafashe da kuka ,Murmshi iklas tayi mai sauti Wanda yasa su kalonta hanayen su tarike cikin nata sanan tace “tata bagani ba kukan mi kikeyi ai nafadamiki ba yanzu zantafi ba kinji ,kuma kince ai ni yarinyar kirkice kinga Kenan aljanna zanje malamin a islamiya yafadamuna cewa duk Wanda akemasa zaton shiga aljanna bai kamata idan ya mutu ba a masa kuka ,kuma malamin mu yace ai mutuwa doli ce ga kowa muyi fatan muhadu acan aljanna sai muyi rayuwar mu wada babu mutuwa ko dady ”tafada tana kalon dadyn ta ,da Sauri yadaga mata kai yasan Ilkas dinshi mai ilimi ce ,gata da shiga ran duk Wanda yazauna da ita „

Yana kaunar yar sa baya son rabuwa da ita itadin jigo ce a garesu ,mikewa yayi yace zaije sallah hakan yasashi fita baiyi kasa aguiwa ba yakira Al'moctar ringin biyu yadaga gaisuwa sukayi sanan yamishi bayanin kanshi da bukatar shi „

“inkana so ma kazo nan asibitin kaga ganta ,muna tsananin kaunar yar mu ba maso murasata please badan niba kayi kodan ceton rai ”

Al'moctar jikinshi yayi sanyi hakan yasakashi cewa zaizo da dadare yaganta „

Bayan magarib kuwa sai gashi Mrs hashim dakanshi yatarbeshi da salama suka shiga dakin gaisheda Rumaisa yayi ganin ta girmeshi ne sa ba kusaba 

Iklas ce dake konce tayi matashi da kan Tata ta ta tashi zaune tunani tashiga yi kamar tatuna wani tace “yawa natuna Na-san ka Yayan la maman ko ”

Tafada cikin murmushi Wanda bata gajiyawa da yinshi ,shima murmushin yasaki sai yanzu yagane ta 
Hannu yamika mata sukayi musabaha zama yayi kusan ta sanan yarike hannun ta yace “
Yess na tuna ki kece mai shagwabar nan ko ban manta kiba ”yafada yana shagwabe wa kamar yanda takeyi inyaje dokar la maman ita ba'azo dokanta ba ,duka dariya suka saka harshi Al'moctar d'un

Mikewa yayi yana musu fatan samun lafiya har yakai kofa Ilklas tace “please kafaishemin da kawa ta ,kafada mata zanzo scool har gidama zanje na gaisheda Yayan ta mai kyau ,kaima kana da kyau ama Na-san bakai bane Wanda take fadamin ba ”tafada tana murmushi Wanda yake kara mata kyau ,kai yadaga mata yana mata alama dazai isar da sakonta 

Bayan shi Mrs hashim Yabi sai wurin motar da yazo da ita tsaya wa sukayi abakin motar Al'moctar ne yace “a gaskiya inamuku jaje abunda yafaru Allah kuma yabata lafiya ,Iklas yarinyar kirki ce nasha tafiya dokar la maman karamar kanwar mu in ishe su tare suna zaman jira nasha cewa tazo na kaita sai tace min a'a Dadyn ta zaiyi fada haka zata shagwabe fuska alamr ita dai za'a bari sai ta ga la maman ta shiga mota su daga wa juna hannu „

Insha Allah zan fadawa Yaya na yada zai fahimta kaga babu Wanda yasan ya iya aiki ta fanin nan ,baya so kowa ya sani har lokacin yayi bansan dalilin shi nayin haka ba ,shi ba mutum bane kamar kowa yana da wuyar sha'ani wlh Na-san da kyar inzai amince ama bana little Iklas ta rasa ranta zanyi komi dan ya dubata ” ya fada yana Mika masa hannu sukayi musabaha ,Motar shi yashiga yatada sai gida da zumar zaije har can gidan Yayan nasa yama sa baya ni .

Ta 'bangaren DOCTOR kuwa amarcin shi ya ke sha sallah kadai take fuda shi waje sai ko in yaje duba Mufida wada take ta cika take batsewa „

Yaw ya kama kwana hudu da angwance wa Doctor kuma har yanzu bai taba shigar Nasreen ba tun ran farko sai sai yayi romancy ama yau ya ci al'washin sai ya shiga duk da yana samun gamsuwa ama ya fi so ya shiga kogin dadi „„

Zaune suke bisa karfet d'un falon ya dora kanshi bisa matashi ita kuma tana kwance akirjinshi sai shafama sa kirji takeyi wanda ta fara harda ce salon mijin nata da inda tasan tataba masa yake jin dadi „

Kwankwasa kofar akayi kasancewar da sanyin la'asar ne da Sauri ta ta shi ta zumbula  hijabin ta har kasa ,Irfan kai dagashi dama sai wando iya guiwa rigarshi ya saka daki ta shiga, shi kuma yaje ya bude Al'moctar ne hannu ya bashi sukayi musabaha shigowa yayi Al'moctar na bayanshi zama sukayi bisa kujerar falon gaisheshi Al'moctar yayi ya amsa yana tanbayar sa su Abbi duk d kowace Safiya sai yaje dubasu ,ce masa yayi suna lafiya 

Nasreen ce ta shigo tana fadin “Yaya Al'moctar sai yau zaka zo ganinmu ko ,munyi fushi ” ta fada tana dan dariya ,shima murmushi ya saki yana fadin “kaina bisa wuya ayi hakuri Matar yaya to yanzu ba gashi na zo ba”

“gaskiya kai kafi Asidiq ka fada mishi shima na masa fushi tunda yaki kawo min su sharifa Ni da kai na ma zanje na kawo su ”
Nasreen ta fada tana  gaisheshi amsa wa yayi cikin kulawa ,ruwa ta kawo masa da jus kurba yayi sanan yace “to ina girki kanwata ta ko rewa kikemin ” yafada cikin wasa, Doctor ne datun dazu yake jinsu yana latsa wayar shi yace “ka ga Malan bamu dora tuwon dare ba kuma ko mun dora bamusa da Rabon ka ba ,ko ba haka ba hajaju ”ya fada yana dan sakin fuskar sa tare da kalon Nasreen din

“aikuwa sai dai nayi hakuri Yaya ka zo wani lokacin ”
Fadar Nasreen

“wato bayan mijin ki kike bi ko ” fadar Al'moctar dariya suka sakama sa ,gyaran murya yayi sanan yace “Yaya dama wurin ka na zo ”

Ya fad'a yana Sosa keyarshi ,Nasreen ce ta mike tare da cewa bari naje na dawo sabida ta basu damar zantawa tana barin wurin ,Irfan yace “inajinka ”.......✍

_MARUBUTA MASU AIKI DA FASAHA DA ZALLAR TSAKWARON AMANA, FAƊAKARWA HAƊI DA NISHAƊANTARWA GAMI DA WA'AZANTARWA, HUMM WANI ABUN SAI ROYAL STAR WRITERS ASSOCIATION👊🏾🦋 RUWA KAƊAI KE MAGANIN ƘISHI☀️_

*"ALƘALAMINMU YANCINMU⚔️*


Book 1
Page 53/54
                 ......✍“Yaya alfarma na zo nema wurinka dan Allah badan ni ba sai dan ceton rai da zakayi Na-san zaka iya kayi sabida ceton 'karama yarinya wada bata san komi ba sai dariya da kauna mutane na roke ka ”ya fada yana game hanayen sa alamar roko tare da zubar da wasu irin zafafan hawaye ”ba karamin mamaki DOCTOR IRFAN yayi ba ga nin hawaye a idon dan uwan nasa yasa ba karamin Abu bane ya ke saka sa kuka ba zai so yasan miye wanan abun da raunta zuciyar dan uwan nashi ,hannu yadaga masa alamar ya isa ya fadi maganar aikuwa da „
Goge hawayen sa yayi yace “Yaya wata kankanuwar yarinya ce takamu da ciwon hanta tana bukatar agaji kawar la maman ce inaje dokar ta tare Nike ganinsu dan Allah kataima keta iyayen ta suna cikin wani hali ko kai kaga yarinya sai ta shiga ranka dan Allah ka duba ta kaji ”yafada yana kokarin dukawa ,harara DOCTOR ya zabga masa aikuwa da Sauri ya koma yasa ma kujerar 

Shiru ne ya ratsa na wani sakani sanan bayan kamar minti biyar Irfan d'un yace “ka je zanyi tunani ”ya fada yana latsa wayar sa „

“dan Allah ya....”bai karasa ba irfan d'un ya dakatar dashi tare da nuna masa hanya aikuwa mikewa yayi tare da du'aa cikin ranshi Allah yasa ya yarda „

DOCTOR kai wayar shi yaciga ba da latsawa tare da tunanin wanene ma yasanar yana wanan aikin ,Aikin hadari ne dashi doli ayi daya ko rai ko mutuwa tsaki yaja wanda yasa Nasreen cewa “Baby kai da wa kake tsaki ince dai ba wanan mai jajayen kunuwan bane yasaka ka tsaki ”tafada tana shagwabewa ,aikuwa baisan sanda dariya ta kubuce masa yashiga kyakyata wa abunda bata taba gani yayi ba ,shagalta tayi da kalonshi wanda har sai dataji yana hura mata iskar bakinshi sa nan ta dawo hayacinta sabida bakaramin kyau yakeyi ba in yana dariya „

“nidai babu ruwana zan fada mishi ,wai mai jajayen kunuwa ,mijinki ma ai mai jajen kunuwa ne ”yafada yana kama kunanta ,hakuri ta shiga ba shi ,saké mata kunan yayi sa nan ya dauketa sai daki wanka suka shiga wanda dakyar tasamu yakyale ta sukayi wankan ,suna fitowa yasungumeta sai saman gado wasani yafara yi da ita dan yaw kam yaci al'washin sai yakara shiga kogin dadi „„

Tsotsar ta yashiga yi kamar mayun wacin zaki ,Nasreen ma hadin kai tashiga ba shi duk da kuwa yan da takejin d'ar a zuciyar ta ama taci al'washin zata faranta masa koda kuwa ita zata cutu ,wani irin Salo yake mata na fitar hayaci kasan ta yadawo yashiga tsotsar sa sai sude ruwan yakeyi kamar yasamu sweet „„

Nasreen kai ta shiga wani hali wanda Abu guda kawai take bukata taji yana cacakar ta wasu hawaye taji suna zubo mata dan bakaramin dadin salon da DOCTOR din nata yake mata ba takeji ,DOCTOR ma yamatsu yashiga ganin irin malala da takeyi yafiso takawo ruwa sosai shiyasaka yake daukar lokaci yana mata wasani wanda wasu Mazan sam basa bashi mahimanci bukatar su da ta biya babu ruwansu shiyasaka ake yawan samun sabani tsakanin m'aurata duk zai sanar da Nasreen dinsa komi sabida ta kiyaye „„

Wasa yafara yi da abar sa akasanta wanda yasa ta fara shidewa ,ganin kamar dangwali yake mata ba yan ita kuma takai wani limite yasaka ta kamo abar cikin bukatuwa ta fara turawa ,tana sakawa tana cirewa ahankali sabida zafin da takeji yana dan ziyar ta ta„

DOCTOR kai yakyaleta yaga iya gudun ruwanta yasan idan yafara suburbuda ta sai ta yi ihu „aikuwa ganin wasa ta doki abun yasakashi fara tura abar sa wani irin kara tasaki sabida zafin da ya ziyar ce ta ,bugun ta yafara ahankali dadi dadi zafi zafi takeji ,dantsen hanunshi tarike ,gogan kai bugunta yafara babu kya kayata wa ,DOCTOR kai jinshi cikin wata irin duniya wanda wanda yake cikin ta kadai zai fahimta yasakashi fara karyo wa da zufa duk kuwa da sanyin ecin da ke dakin ,wani irin dadi yakeji na fitar hankali kanshi yafara jujuyawa sabida baisan yazaiyi ba ya kwatan ta irin dadin dayakeji„Nasreen kai wani irin dadi takeji yana ratsata kwkwalwar ta „


“wayo Yaya irfan kayi hakuri natuba bazan saké ba kabari wayo wayo Allah na Mama ”Nasreen ce mai fadar haka sabida yanzu kai ta fara buguwa Awa daya da min tun tuka yana bugunta da abarsa ama ace har yanzu mutum bai yi relasing ba bayan ita tayi yafi so biyu ganin haka yasaka ta fara kuka dan yafara rutsa ta kuka takeyi sosai tana jujuya kanta saman filon „

Doctor kai bai masan tanayi ba dan yarasa mizai yi inazai saka kanshi ganin kukan ta kara masa fawer yake yasa kashi dokar ta saman gadon yasauko da ita ba yan shi yajingina da bango ita kuma yasaka ta gabanshi yazamana ba yan ta na gugar kirjinshi „


Kafar ta daya yadaga yadan duko kadan yada sandar sa zata shiga aikuwa ahankali yafara shiga daga nan kuma yaci gaba da bugunta yana matsar kirjinta da hannu daya dayan kuma yarike cikin ta „

Nasreen kai sai yanzu tagane abunda yasaka yafadamata shi hariji ne „yanzu ta fahimci komi kuka takeyi sosai sabida yanzu kai tasan sai yada Allah yayi da ita ,DOCTOR kai babu ruwanshi sha'anin shi kawai yakesha sai da yayi awa biyu sanan yafara sakin wani irin nishi wani irin gwatso yabuga mata wanda yasaka ta kara da karfi kwantar da ita yayi bata Réda ya zare mata pénis dinsa ba „

Kwanciyar gaba da gaba yamai da ta sabida hakan Sperme yafi shiga aikuwa wani irin dumi taji a kasanta alamar yayi relasing sai dayataba tar duk yajuye mata ruwan jarabarsa sanan yakwanta akanta sharkaf yana maida numfashi tagane shi yanzu idan yayi relasing sai ya wani kwanta mata ajiki kuma ba zai cire abar sa ba har sai yasauka ,sai da yayi minti goma sanan yakoma gefen ta „

Nasreen kai yaw tasan da taci wuya yau ma tasan doli yamata gashi jitayi yana tsotsar kirjin ta kamar wani yaro goye hawayenta yashiga lashewa a hankali yace “raguwa kawai yi guda ne fah mukayi yau anjuma zaki karamin ko Nasfan dina ” yafada tare da mata wani irin Salo mai rikitar wa ,sharewa tayi kukan shagwaba ta'za tana fadin “wlh a'a nidai kakaini wurin Mama ”tafada tana kokarin tashi zaune kasawa tayi hakan yasaka ta doli komawa ta kwanta ta cigaba da kukan ta dariya yasaki hakan yasaka ta dokar filon gadon taje fe shi dashi towel yadora akugunsa bayi yashiga yahada mata ruwan zafi dawo wa yayi ya cacaleta sai bayi „

Cikin ruwan yasaka ta kuka tafashe mishi dashi tana ririke shi dariya yakeyi yana maidata ciki cikin kunan ta yara da mata “kwantar da hankalin ki Angel dina ,daga yaw baza ki saké shiga ruwan zafi ba sai rada kikamin laifi na horaki ”yafada yana sumbatar ta ,itadai hawayenta take sharewa tana Harar sa wanka sukayi suka tare da al'wala suka yi magrib da isha'i ganin anbar su „

Suna karewa suka haw daning dama sungama abincinsu tun da rana tuwo biyu ta girka ,Mufida yakira tace takoshi ko oho suka ci gaba da cin abin cin su yanabata abaki ita kuma sai narke masa take sai da yatabatar da takoshi sanan suka ta tare wurin kayan barcci suka saka yarungume abar sa ama hanunsa akan kirjinta ta fara sabawa da salon irfan din nata ,Nasfan Asuba ta gari „


Yaw Nasreen take kare amarci kuma dama sai marece zai koma bangaren Mufida „ zaune suke ita dashi saman kujerar falon yana rungume da abar sa suna kalon wani film ibo sunacin ships gani yayi kawai Nasreen ta bare masa da kuka tana bubuga kafar ta „ cikin rudewa yace “ke lafiyar ki kuwa kodai sai namiki rukiya ” kuka takara fashewa dashi wanda babu digon hawaye ko daya shi kuma gogan sai rumgumo ta yake yana tanbayar ta lafiya 

“wlh nidai yaw su sharifa Nike son gani nayi kewar Ammi ,Allah nidai sai kakaini yanzu kuma Nike so ”tafada tana kara bubuga kafarta akasa wani irin Abu yaji yana masa yawo tundaga kugunsa har zuwa tafin kafar sa sabida komi Nasreen dinsa takeyi burgeshi takeyi kara hura mishi tsananin bukatuwa ta yake katse mishi tunani tayi da mitsileshi aciki da Sauri yace “oush sai na rama ”ganin haka yasaka ta guduwa suka fara kewaye falon ganin ba zai kama ta ba yasashi dukawa yana rike cikinsa tare da dan kwala kara aikuwa da Sauri tayi kanshi tana zuwa yayi sama da ita yana fadin “na mata wayo ”

Aikuwa dariya tasaka daki yayi da ita bakaramin dadi yaji ba da takula da yen uwansa yagane kuma kara ta masa yasan tayi kewar Ahalinta baya so sufara fitayanzu ama zai kai ta kodan yafaran ta mata itama „wata farar abaya yafi do mata wanda shine yasiya musu itada Mufida tun kan suzo rigar bakaramin birgeta tayi ba godiya ta masa sosai hade da adu'a bakaramin mamaki tabashi ba ganin irin godiyar data kemasa kamar wasa yakai hajji ,sumbatar ta yayi Kiran Mufida yayi yace ta shirya yanzu zasu fita „

Kayanshi yasaka wani farin yadi tsap yamasa Nasreen ce tashigo sai bubuga kafar ta takeyi sabida shi dama a dakinsa yake shirya wa brziya dinta tajefa masa asaman gado tana fadin “Allah ni ba zan saka wanan ba nakasa sakawa tun dazu ”tafada tana goge dan hawayenta ,murmushi yasaki domin yana son haka taha ne za'arika samun shakuwa tsakanin Mata da Miji brziya din yadoko baya tajuyama sa kasa yayi da towel din wanda nan take abubuwan sa suka bayana da Sauri yakai hannu ya fara latsawa kukan shagwba tasaki tasan yanzu zaice zai nemata duk da tunda yayi so biun na har yanzu ama yana la latse ta har sai ya gamsu ganin haka yasaka mata brezia „

Yana sakamata tabar masa towel din ta fita daga ita sai brezia da pant wanda da gangan tamasa kuma zata gane kuranta ajiyar zuciya yasauke jin yanayinshi na sauyawa ganin yan da ta yi wani irin juyi yada kugunta ya juya „

Tsap tashirya tayane kanta da kalabin abayar jakar ta baka tasaka sai glass dinta na ido data saka tana fitowa shima yana fitowa da sauri tayi kanshi tana fadin “masha Allah angon Nasreen kaga yanda kayi kyau kuwa kamar nasace ka nagudu ,nidai gaskiya kama koma kacanja wadanan kayan sunyi kyau sosai ”tafada tana dan yamutsa fusaka

Murmushin jin dadi yasaki dan yaji dadin yaba masa datayi rungumeta yayi akunne yarada mata “ai kece kika haska ka ni tauraruwar yan mata matar Aurena Nasreen ”yafada yana dan raira mata waka akune „

Dariya tasaki wanda yasa dimple din ta  lotsawa dai dai wurin ya sumbata yaja hanunta sukayi waje suna rike da hannu juna ,suna fitowa Mufida na fitowa wani irin bakin ciki ne yatokema ta zuciya da hassada ganin irin kyan dasukayi dan ko makiyi yagansu yasan sun dace „

Wata irin harara tabanka musu wanda basuma luraba Nasreen na ganinta ta kwace hanunta tana fadin “ina kwana Anty Mufida ”

“lafiya klw ”Mufida tafada tana kokarin shiga gaban motar ,Nasreen dai wanan bai dameta ba baya tabude tashiga ,gogan kam baice komi ba ya shiga tukin sa cikin kwanciyar hankali ,minti goma yakaisu gida suna zuwa yayi parking 

Da sauri Nasreen tafita daga motar bata jirasu ba tayi gaba abunta,da salama ta shiga falon aikuwa la maman na ganinta tayi kanta da gudu tana fadin “oyoyo Anty Nasreen ”tafada cikin murna ta itama Nasreen din rungume ta ta yi tana fadin “oyoyo Autar Mu ” tafada tana kama hanunta sukayi cikin falon kasancewar week-end ne duk suna gida ,
 Abbi ne yace “lale marhaba y'a ta kece a tafe ” yafada yana aje jaridar dake hanunsa har kasa ta duka tana gaisheda su kasancewar Ammi ,Abbi,har da Umma sunan Al'moctar ne kawai babu „

Cikin kulawa suke amsawa banda ummi da take ansawa ciki ciki ,bakaramin dadi sukaji ba da sukaganta cikin walwala sun tabatar lafiya dan dama Ammi tana cewa zataje taga irin zaman dasuke „

Mufida ce tashigo sai IRFAN dayake bayanta gaisheda su Abbi tayi tareda zama bisa kujerar falon,babu Wanda yadoki hakan wani Abu kasancewar su wayayi Amma Irfan bakaramin haushi abun yabashi ba „

Shima kasa yadan duka yana kwasar gaisuwa hakika sun tabatar da Aure rahama ne gashi dan nasu har wani kiba da haske ya kara alamar hankalin sa a konce yake „

Nasreen ce dake zaune kusa da Asidiq tana masa korafin bai taba zuwa gidansu ba tayi fushi shikuwa hakuri yake bata tare da fadama ta zaizo karatu ne yaboyesa ,sharifa ce tace “Allah Anty Nasreen kulum sai munce yakaimu sai yace bashida dama dagashi har Yaya al'moctar ”tafada tana Harar kasa „

Irfan dayake lura da abunda ke faruwa dama sharifa na kusan shi yabuge mata baki da Sauri ta Mike tanayi wurin Abbi „
Abbi ne yace “to kwana biyu dai yara nashan iska yanzu kazo zaka takuramusu bana so tashi ku koma gidanku ”yafada yana murmushi „

Ammi ce tace “barta ya hora ta ai dama itakadai ce marass kunya tabi ta raina Asidiq kamar wani abokin wasan ta ,tayi wa al'moctar tagani ” tafada tana Harar ta ,sharifa ce ta koma wurin Asidiq ta rungume shi tace “kaji ko Yaya Asidiq ai kasan ni ban raina ka ba nidakai mutu karaba takalmin kaza ai nizanje gidanka ma nazama yar fakon daki ”tafada tana dariya ,shima dariya yasaka suna tapasa abunsu „

Ammi ce tace “wato kunya zaku bani ko babu laifi bazan kara shiga har ka ku ba dan duk Wanda yashiga zai yi kunya ”aikuwa duk dariya aka saka a falon banda Mufida da Ke yaken doli „dama ummi tayi sama abunta „

Kasancewar Ammi ce keda girki yasakata mikewa tashiga kitchen d'un 

Tsohuwa ce tashigo tana fadin “a'a yaw kishiyoyi na ne agidan har da mijin wato kasamu yara danyu kamanta dani duk abunka dai da tsohuwar Zuma aka magani ”tafada tana zama bisa bisa kujerar Mufida ce tazo tazauna kusanta tare da rungumeta tana fadin “tsohuwa mai ran karfe nayi kewar ki ” 
Tsohuwar ma rungumeta tayi tana fadin “ba wani nan kunbi kun kwacemin Miji kizo kinamin dadin baki ,yawa kekuma Nasrana kike ko wa taso nan nagan ki da kyau Na-san dai mai gidana ya iya kiwo ”tafada tana mikowa Nasreen hannu ,Nasreen tashi tayi taje kusan ta tazauna yazamana sunsa ta atsakiya ,hannun Nasreen tarike tana fadin “tap irin wanan lukutan ci ai bazan yarda nayi fada dakeba gara dai wanan Na-san zan imata ”tafada cikin wasa jika da kaka tana nuna Mufida „duk dariya akasaka banda Mufida da bataji dadin yanda taga tsohuwa ta ja Nasreen a jiki ba „

Gogan kai bakaramin dadi yaji ba ganin basu nuna banbacin ba atsakanin matan NASA ba „

Nasreen ce ta sulale tabi Ammi kichen ,Ammi na ganinta tace “ai dama kin koma dan bazan bari kiyi komi ba kije kiyi zamanki y'a ta yanzu zankamala girkin ”aikuwa da Sauri Nasreen ta amshi albasa hanun Ammi zata fara yayankawa Ammi tarike tace “kul waya fada miki Amaria na yanka albasa ba yanzu ba ”fadar Ammi ,babu yana da Ammi ba tayi da ita ba ama sai da ta amshi girkin da wayo ta samu Ammi tabar kitchen d'in tana sama ta albarka „

Dama burabusko ne da miyar soure da alayahou tasha nama, girkinta takeyi cikin kwanciyar hankali sharifa ce ta shigo tana kama mata„

Irfan kai ya so ya kaisu gidan Abba amma yasan doli zai bari har wani lokaci sabida rana tayi Ammi za tace ya bari saï marece ,ganin Ammi ta fito ya tabatar masa da ta amshi girkin ba 'karamin dad'i ya ji ba cikin ransa kuwa albarka yasaka mata Mufida ya kala ya ga sai latsa wayar ta take yi hankali konce tsaki yaja Kazan ya mike yayi part dinsa ba'jima gidan ya gauraye da kanshi aikuwa tsohuwa ce tace “ba dai kirin kirin ba tayi mai munji kanshi saura dadi zan ci girkin Amaria na gani inkin fini iyawa ”ta fada tana dan dariya 

Asidiq ne yace “haba tsohuwa ina ke ina hada kanki da girkin Amaria ,kefah banda tuwan tuke me kika iya ”ya fada yana dariya „Abbi ne yace “tuwan tuken nan dai dashi ubanka ya girma ”dariya suka saka sai ga Nasreen tana jera abincin falon sharifa na kama mata tsap suka aje komi dan Abbi yafi son ci a kasa hakan ya saka Ammi cewa ajera kowa ma yaci kasa „

Aikawa tayi aka kira Irfan ,Abbi shida Asidiq da Irfan sai ko la mama data ce ita tare da yayan ta zata ci ,ummi da Ammi su biyu ,sai sharifa Nasreen tsohuwa da Mufida suke ci tare ba 'karamin dad'i tuwon yayi ba kowa sai yaba girkin yake tsohuwa ce tace “tuwon nan ko kadan bai yi dad'i ba ”aikuwa da Sauri sharifah tace “kuji tsohuwa bayan kin kwashi girki har sude kwano ”dariya akayi gaba d'aya sanan Abbi ya mike dan d'auro al'wala suka wuce masslaci suma su Nasreen al'wala sukayi dakin sharifa sukayi sallah banda Mufida data tafi dakin kakar ta sai marece sanan sukaje gida ba karamin albarka suka sha ba wurin surukan nasu barema Nasreen „

Suna komawa Mufida ta shiga shirye-shiryen tarba angon ta dan yaw Abu ya za gayo nasu....✍

_MARUBUTA MASU AIKI DA FASAHA DA ZALLAR TSAKWARON AMANA, FAƊAKARWA HAƊI DA NISHAƊANTARWA GAMI DA WA'AZANTARWA, HUMM WANI ABUN SAI ROYAL STAR WRITERS ASSOCIATION👊🏾🦋 RUWA KAƊAI KE MAGANIN ƘISHI☀️_

*"ALƘALAMINMU YANCINMU⚔️*

             .......✍️Alhamdulillah jikin Iklas da sauki dan har amma salemesu kuma Mrs Hashim bai fad'awa Rumaisa ba.

'Bangaren Mufida ko ba laifi ta gyara part d'inta tayi girkin shinkafa fara da jar miya ta dafa „

Wata doguwar riga ta saka tasa hula a kanta ba laifi tayi kyau „

Ta shirya komi sai karfe takwas Irfan ya shigo sai ga Nasreen bayanshi duk da ba'a tare suka shigo ba ba 'karamin haushi suka bata ba kowane zama yayi Mufida ce ta saka ma Irfan d'un, ita kuma Nasreen da kanta ta saka lomar farko tari ya sarke Irfan ,saboda yajin da ta cika da Sauri ya doki ruwa ya kafa a bakinshi„

Nasreen kai wani irin yaji taji kau cikin bakinta da ko'karin hadiyewa take sabida kar ace manakissa ce ama ina kasawa tayi doli ta zubar dashi„

IRFAN kai mikewa yayi yayi falon dan yasn idan yaci abincin nan akwai matsala doli zaisa a koya mata girki ba zai yuba shi ba sakaran namiji ne ba mai bi Restaurant yana cin abinci ba bayan gashi da Mata har biyu „

Nasreen ma mikewa tayi tayi part d'inta tana zuwa ta nemi jus tasha kusan Rabi zama tayi tana sauke ajiyar zuciya „

Ta 'bangaren Mufida kai ba 'karamin haushi taji ba ganin yada suka watse alamar abincin bai yi dad'i ba ,itada take ganin tayi kokari wanda sai da ta kira Momy tana fad'ama ta yanda zata hada abincin shine zasu mata wannan wulak'ancin tsaki cokali daya tayi ita ma ta maido nata sai gata tana shan ruwa Ashe haka sukaji ta tare kayan tayi takai kitchen „

Wurinshi ta dawo tana kwarkwasa zama tayi kusanshi tana fadin “Yaya Irfan kayi hak'uri zan koya laifin Momy ne dabata koyamin ba ”wani haushi ta bashi wato laifin Momy ne ko tsawa ya buga mata yace “ba na son shashanci kinsan Allah ki nemi makarantar koyar da Abinci ba dan ba zan zauna ina cin abincin titi ba ”ya fada yana Harar ta ita dai ha'kuri take bashi „

Mikewa yayi dakinshi ya shiga ya watsa ruwa yana fitowa ya sha cream dinshi tare da feshe jikinshi da turaruka 'bangaren Nasreen ya tafi dan yasan da matukar bai yi rarashiba ba za ta yi bacci mai dad'i ba shi kuma ba zai so haka ba „

Nasreen kai yaw taji ba dadi taya zata kwana ita kadai bayan ya saba mata da kwana tare da shi bisa fadaden kirjin shi ta saba yana shafata yanzu to ita ya zata yi wanka ta tayi tahafa humrar ta ta saka kayan barccin ta gado ta haw ta lulube yanzu duk abun nan daya ke mata haka zaiyi wa matar sa Ashe dama haka kishi yake addu'aa ta shiga yi domin samun sassaucin abunda takeji wasu irin hawaye taji suna sulalo mata ji tayi an shiga lashe hawayen da Sauri tayi ko'karin mikewa mai da ita yayi kwance yana kallon face dinta kishinsa yake hangowa cikin kwayar idonta ba 'karamin dadi yaji ba Ashe NasFan d'inshi nason shi har haka „

Kanshi ya shiga jujuya mata alamar ta daina hawayen kuka ta kare fashewa dashi ,

“fad'amin ya akayi Angel d'ina duk kishin mijin ki ne oum ”ya rada mata akunne ,aikuwa da Sauri ta dagamasa kai cikin yarinta da zallar shagwaba murmushi yayi yace “Ina Son ki yayi kadan ,ina kaunar ki ,ina Tsananin son kasancewa dake ,inki na so sai nayi barcci na anan ”ya fada yana daga rigar ta dukiyar fulanin ta ya shiga shafawa kiss ya shiga bata aikuwa itama batayi kassa aguiba ta shiga mayar masa cikin salon shi daya koya mata ita kuma take har da cewa „

Tureshi ta fara yi ganin ya fara wuce gona da iri kamar yanda bata so a shiga hakinta bazata bari a shiga na wani ba dakyar ta kwace bakinta tana kalon kwayar idonsa da ta fara canjawa cikin sha'aawa tace “Ni dai ka tashi ba na so ka tashi da shanyayen Bari jiki gobe kiyama Miji na ”ta fada cikin shagwa'ba 

Shi kam tana bashi mamaki so tari gata dai yarinya ama tafi wani Baba hankali murmushi yayi ya sumbace ta agoshi cikinta ya shafa yace “My boy kayi barcci lafiya ka kulamin da Mamar ka kar ka bari tayi kuka kaji ”ya fada yana Sumba tar cikinta Wanda ya kwanta kamar babu ya'yan hanji a ciki ,aikuwa dariya ta fashe dashi har dimple d'inta yan lobawa hannunta ta dora a saman nashi da yake kan cikin ta tace “A a Baby na ka bani dariya haba taya nike da baby a ciki na dagayi sati daya har da wani ba shi sako ”ta fada tana kalon shi „

Murmushi ya saki yace “lalle ma yarinyar nan kin raina ni kin doka ragon Namiji ne a gabanki shap shuter ne ,nasan da akwai ajiyata kuma lokaci ne zai nunamiki inajima yan hudu na zuba miki ” yafada yana kara Sumba tar cikin ta Wanda shifa da gaske yakeyi yayi ajiya yana ji a jikinshi amma zai kara mata lokaci tunda bai bayana kanshi ba „

Lulubeta yayi ya tofa mata addu'aa wutar d'akin ya kashe mata ya rufe mata falon „


Yana futa ta share hawayen ta dan ba 'karamin kishin mijinta takeyi ba adu'aa ta cigaba dayi har barcci ya dauke ta „


Irfan part d'in Mufida yaje ya tarar da an kashe komi na falon kofar ya rufe yayi d'akinshi tarar da ita yayi zaune tana latsa waya al'wala ya bata umarnin tayi shima yayi ,sanye take da wata black d'un rigar barcci da wando sai hular ta data saka akanta „


Gadon yahaw ita ma ta bayanshi ta kwanta dama DOCTOR Nasreen ta fara hawda dashi Networks juyo wa yayi hanunshi yakai ya fara kunce gaban rigar ta dukiyar fulaninta ne suka bayana bakinshi yakai cikin nata ya shiga kissing dinta da yake ita ma y'ar duniya ce maida mishi ta shiga yi aikuwa dama shi abunda yake so Kenan rigar yacire mata gaba daya wasa ya shiga yi da kirjinta Wanda ko rabin na Nasreen dinshi bai yi ba shifah yafi son manya kaya a haka dai ya shiga taba duk inda yasan zai sa taji dadi ,Mufida kai ta fita hayacin ta jin irin salon dayake mata bakinshi ya saka a kasan ta Wanda yaji wani iska iska da Sauri ya cire kanshi dan bahaka yaji ba a na Angel dinsa kanshi yaji ya bugesa ama wannan wani ba shi ba shi yakeji dauke kanshi yayi ya cigaba da tsotsar ta ama bai sake yarda yakai Kanshi ka santa ba 


Ya saba sai Nasreen ta tsotse masa abarsa hakan ya sakshi sakawa Mufida alkalamin sa a bakin ta ama da Sauri ta doke kai alamar ba za ta iya ba dan har ga Allah ita kazantar abun take gani „yaji ba dadi amma tunda bata so ba zai yi mata doli ba „

Hanunshi yakai kasanta yatsun shi biyu yasaka a part dinta Wanda yaji yadan shiga gani takawo ruwa ama ko rabin Na Angel dinsa batayi ba sabida Rijiyar angel dinsa cike take da ruwa wasu nabin wasu „

Saita abarsa yayi yafara shiga ahankali dan kar yajimata aikuwa amatse take itama  ahankali yake tafiya har yasamu yashiga ya huda ta aikuwa Kara tasaki mai dan sauti bai saurara mata ba yashiga buga mata gwatso „


Mufida kai tafara fita hayacinta jin zafin da kasanta yakeyi mata kuka tashiga yi tasan abun dama ba dadi kawayen ta nafadamata ama bata doka rashin dadin yakai haka ba kuka take ganin kusan awa guda ama baida alamun dakatawa „

DOCTOR kai yaji da banbanci so sai komi na Angel dinsa mai kyau ne yaji ba laifi tana da nata baiwar ama bata kama kafar angel dinsa ba aiki yaciga ba dayi sai dayayi awa guda da wani Abu sanan yayi relasing Wanda alokacin Mufida kai tagalabaita so sai jikinta sai makar kata yakeyi yana relasing yakwanta sharkaf ajikinta kamar yanda yakeyi wa angel dinsa minti goma tsakani ya sauka a gefen ta numfashi yashiga saukewa da kyar yamike yashiga bayi yahada mata ruwan zafi dan yasan tasha wuya kai da sannu zata saba duk zai horar da matan nasa tunda shine yafara Budé abunsa bazasu ba shi wahala ba „


Daukar ta yayi yakai bayi cikin ruwan yasakata Wanda taji zafi na ratsa ta kukanta taci gaba dayi sai dayamata ruwa biyu sanan yabarta tayi wankan tsarki shikuma dakin yadawo yaye zanin gadon Wanda babu jini so sai wanan ba wata matsala bace yasan Abudurwa yasamu matar sa kuma yaji dadi ,farkon faranta mashi din datayi Kenan wani zane yashimfida ,shigowa tayi tahaw bed din tana yamutsa fuska murmushi yasaki dama yasan burga banza takeyi sai inba tashigo hannun shi ba dariya yasaki yayi bayin shima wanka yayi ytsab tace jikinshi „

Bayan yafito yasaka jalabiyar sa yatada nafil fili tare da adu'oin Allah yabashi yin iko a tsakan kanin iyalan sa sai karfe biyu ya kwanta rungume ta yayi ganin tama jima dayin barcci ,zai yi kokarin ganin yabata kulawa ama so dai daya ne kuma Nasreen dinshi ce yakema „


Kiran sallar farko akunashi dan haka mikewa yayi ya dauro al'wala sai dayayi nafila biyu sanan ya wuce massalaci tare da tada Mufida wada takara koma barccinta ganin yatafi massalaci „

Nasreen dai yasan tatashi ama duk da haka sai da yadubata ganin tatashi kamar yanda yayi zato yasa ya wuce massalaci ”

Bai shigo ba sai karfe shida part din Mufida ya wuce doli zaiyi musu breakfast hakan yasakashi wuce wa kitchen din ya dora musu ruwan zafi thé da kwai doya ya fere ya soya musu karfe bakwai yakamala komi yajera ,dakin Mufida yaje dan dubota ko jikin ne tarar da ita yayi tana Sallah ,wato sai yanzu take kan yin sallah kanshi ya girgiza yakoma dakinshi don shiryawa don yaw zai koma office „


Mufida kai wanka tashiga itama wanka taci cikin wata tsadadar atamfa Make-up tayi ba laifi ta yi kyau cikin Riga da siket din falon ta fito ganin bayanan yasaka ta binshi dakinshi wanka taga yaci cikin farar Riga suit ne wanda sukayi matukar karbashi jikinshi yafeshe da turaruka ta Miroir yaganta murmushi yasakar mata ta bayanshi ta rungume shi tace “ goodmorning my DOCTOR “

shima juyowa yayi ya rungume ta tare da Sumba tar gefen fuskar ta yace “haw are you?” yatanbaye ta 

“ I am fine ”tafada yana rungume da ita sukayi falon „

Nasreen ce tabude kofar tashigo da salama wani irin bugawa taji zuciyar ta tayi ganin shi rungume da Mufida wani kishi ne yataso mata da Sauri ta karanto adu'aa cikin zuciyar ta take fadi “miye naki aciki Nasreen matar shi ce fah ina ruwanki ” aikuwa da Sauri ta kawar da abunda takeji tagaishesu ba yabo babu falasa suka amsa „

Gogan kai sai kalon ta takeyi dan bakaramin kyau tayi masa ba cikin Riga da wandon data saka yan kanti zama sukayi kowa yashiga cin karinsa Nasreen ce tafara mikewa tare da yimusu sai anjima ko kalonsu batayi ba ganin yanda Mufida take dibar abincin take ba shi abaki shikuwa gogan ko ajikinshi shi ba ruwanshi wallahi kowa tata ta fisheta „


Sai da yakoshi yaduba agogo karfe takwas saura goma mikewa yayi tare da dokar jakar sa ,Mufida ma mikewa tayi rungume shi tayi tare da sakar masa kiss a baki shima mayar mata yayi dan yana so ya mata adalci kodan abunda tabashi wanda yakeda wuyar samu a cikin zamanin nan wato budurci „


Daga falon bata fita ba sai ce masa datayi “sai ka dawo ” baice mata komi ba yayi gaba abunsa bangaren Nasreen yaje 

Koda yaje tana kwance saman doguwar kujera da remote a hanun ta tana kalon tashar Bollywood dan bakaramin son film Inde take ba „


Jitayi kawai anhaye mata jiki ,hakan yasakata shirin kwala kara dan batasan dashi ba ,jin kamshin turarensa yasakata gane shine shagwabewa tayi tana kauda fuskar ta gefe ganin yana son yin kissing dinta „


“wai minene Angel dina badai har yanzu fushi kike ba oum ?”yafada yana sumbar cikin ta 

“My boy kayi hakuri kaji katayi bawa Maman ka hakuri ” yafada cikin shagwaba ,aikuwa dariya tafashe da ita dan yana bata dariya idan yana wanan shagwabar murmushi yayi yace “haba koke fah Mar'atu saliha ” yafada yana daukar ta sai wajen motar sa sai dariya suke abunsu „

Sauke ta yayi yakara rungume ta itama rungume abunta tayi adu'aa ta masa tare da fadin “Allah yatsare ka Mijin mu Allah yadawo mana da kai gida lafiya cikin tarin  aminci ,Allah yabaka sa'aa asamo mana halal ,idan bakasamo ba kuma mungwa maci mu kawana da yunwa da muci haram ,Allah yatsare hanya ” tafada tana shafawa kamar kar yatafi yabarta takeji 


Shima gogan haka yakeji jin adu'aar data kemasa yasaka shi jin wani irin sanyi aranshi tare da jin kaunar Matar tasa gata dai yarinya ama da hankalin manya abunsa yakama ta ace Mufida tayi shine takeyi 

Shiga motar yayi yana daga mata hannu kiss ta huramsa aikuwa zuciyar shi yarike tare da mayar mata murmushi suka sakar ma juna yaja yatafi yana mai jin nishadi cikin ransa „


Nasreen ma part dinta ta wuce tana son Kiran Mama

IRFAN sai da ya biya gida yagaisheda dasu Abbi kuma yahadu da Al'moctar inda yace yasameshi office da rana ,aikuwa da ranan sai gashi yazo „

Bayani yamasa dallah dallah yanda zai gane kuma yafadamasa aikin da hatsari bars kuma abun karamar yarinya ,shine yafada masa ba zaiyi ba

Aikuwa kuka Al'moctar yafashe dashi yahada hanayensa yana fadin “please Yaya insha Allah babu abunda zai faru zata samu lafiya please kaji Kataimaki karamar yarinya ” bakaramin Mamaki yabashi ba ganin irin kukan da yake zurdawa zaiso yataimaki yarinyar ama bazai so ace shine silar komi ba abun ne yanada hadari „

Tsawa yabugamishi tare da nuna mishi hanyar fita aikuwa mikewa yayi yana share hawayen sa „


Kiran Mrs hashim yayi yafadamasa yanda sukayi cikin ta shin hankali yace yafada masa Asibitin aikuwa baiji ma ba yazo har office din IRFAN yakaisa „

Kwankwasa wa yayi yes yace hakan yasaka Mrs hashim shiga wani irin faduwa gabanshi yayi ganin wanda yake saman kujerar wanda yatabatar shine DOCTOR IRFAN din shine wanda yazo neman taimako wurinda “innalilahi” yashiga nanatawa a ciki ranshi anya kuwa yayi wa kanshi adalci dayazo wurin nan da niyar neman alfarma yasan bama zai taba yuwa indai mutunan nan daya ke gani ba shine wanda zaiyima yar aiki ba yasan bazai yuba „


IRFAN kai nuna shi yashiga yi da dan yatsar shi yana girgiza kansa ...✍️







Nikan miye tsakanin Mrs Hashim da DOCTOR IRFAN ????MIYASAKA MRS HASHIM KWANKWANTO HAR YANA TUNANIN DOCTOR ZAI KASA YIWA IKLAS DINSA AIKI ?

KU BIYONI A PAGE IN GABA DAN JIN YADA ZATA KAYA YAWAN COMMENT DINKU YAWA TYPING DIN DAZUKU SAMU.....
