DAN ALLAH IKUNGA TYPING MISTAKE ERREUR NE KUMIN UZURI ,WANAN SHINE FARKON BOOK DINA SO A MUN AFUWA BANA SON KORAFI👏




Book 1
Page 59/60
              ......✍️DOCTOR bak'aramin rud'ani yashiga ba ganin halinda tashiga da sauri ya k'walawa Nurse d'in kira tashigo umarni yabata da ta d'auko yarinyar takaita d'aki na musaman ,d'aukar ta tayi tayi waje da ita „

DOCTOR bin bayansu yayi taimakon gagawa yashiga bata k'arin ruwa yasaka mata sanan yasaka mata oxygène alurai yahad'a cikin ruwan yamata ,sunkai minti talatin sannan yasamu lunfashin ta yadai daita ajiyar zuciya yasauke ,jinin ta yabada izinin ad'iba ayi mata test sanan yasaka wasu nurse guda biyu kula da ita „

Yana fitowa office d'insa yashiga Al'moctar ne yabanko k'ofar babu ko sallama wani kallo yake watsa ma yayan nashi cikin wani irin zafin zuciya yace“yaushe Yaya IRFAN yakoma mara tausayi ,yaushe aka canjawa Ammi d'anta daga mai taimako zuwa mara taimako ,yaushe Yaya na mai ilimi yafara zama jahili ,yaush...”

Bai karasa IRFAN yabuga masa wata irin tsawa wada yasaka duk Wanda yazo wucewa tsaya wa yana saurare cikin bacin rai Wanda har fuskar shi tayi wani irin ja jijiyoyin wuyanshi sun mimik'e yace “kai awa ,Mika d'auki kanka ,ko kamanta nine gaba dakai, kamanta wanene IRFAN ALI ko ,har ni zaka Budé baki kafadawa kalamar jahilci amatsayinka nawa ,”yafada yana buga table d'in dake gabanshi Wanda yasaka kaf aubuwan dake table d'in sai dasuka zube kasancewar dama yana bisa kujerar irin na falo kuma akwai wani d'an table na madubi agabanshi Wwanda ya aje wayoyinshi da glass dinsa yasaka table dun tartsewa yafashe gabadaya „


Hannun shi ne yafara zuba da jini shaaaa kamar anbud'e fanfo Allah sarki jini ba wasa ba da Sauri Al'moctar yayi kanshi yana kokarin kama hanunshi tare da kiran Nurse duk yabi ya rud'e „


Tureshi IRFAN yayi yace “hmm kadamu ko ganin jini yana zuba a hanuna ,to karaya uwar dataga y'ar ta kwance rai ahanun Allah an keta mata haddi ,an mata kacakaca ,yarinya kamar la maman ,wanan uwar bata taba ci ba tasha tunda abun nan yasamu yar ta takai kara kotu ama amaimakon akwatar mata incin ta ,cewa akayi baya cikin hankalinshi kwaya aka zuba masa aka likawa wasu bayin Allah Wanda ko hanyar clubs basu sani ba, ni nan nayiwa wanan yarinyar d'inki ko Babar mace albarka bazata iya d'aukar wanan ba ,ak'arshe bak'in cikin wanan mutum wanda shine lawyen d'an iskan yaron da yayi fyad'en yayi ruwa yayi tsaki ya juya maganar ,durkushe wanan uwar taduka tana rok'onsa k'watar wa 'yar ta 'yancin ta miyasa yakasance aciki azzalumai ama wanan mutum shureta yayi kamar kashi yayi tafiyar sa ” ”yafad'a cikin k'ara buga wata tsawa wanan karan kai harshi Al'moctar d'un sai dayaja baya„

Wani rauni ne yazo mishi ,wani tausayin Yayan shi ne yashige shi sabida bai taba ganin shi cikin wanan halin ba yasan hakika abun yataba shi ta yanda ba'aza to ,duba dayanda ranshi yake abace jini ne ke zuba ahanunshi amma bai damu ba „

Kama hanun yayi ganin yazauna yadafe kanshi wasu hawaye na sulalo mishi „

Dressing d'in hanun yamasa babu Wanda yace ma wani kala Al'moctar ne yamike har zai fita DOCTOR yarik'e mishi hannu “sit down”yafad'a batare da ya kalleshi ba yace “a karshe wanan uwar bak'in ciki ne yayi ajalinta ,wanan yarinyar yoyon futsari ne yakamata da tabun hankali sabida tunda y'a keta mata hadi bata saké sanin inda kanta yake ba ,bata masan mahaifiyar ta ta rasu ba ,sabida mi su Wanda aka rufe a prison bata tare da laifin komi ba 'ya'yan talakawa ne marasa galihu ,Wanda basuda wani madogara sai Allah ,a hannu na wanan mahaifiyar ta damk'a min amanar 'yar ta basuda kowa sai Allah ,ban halarci kotun ba sabida alokacin aiki yasamun kai sai kawo wanan mahaifiyar yarinyar akayi batama san inda kanta yake ba dagak'arshe bak'in ciki yasa tamutu kafad'amin wanan mutumin kake ikirarun na yafe wa , wannan mutum kake so na taimaka a'a bazai yuyuba ” DOCTOR yafad'a yana durkushewa hade da fashewa da wani irin kuka kamar karamun yaro „

Ba A'lmoctar kadai dake saurarenshi ba har Rumaisa da tazo yanzu sabida Kiran da Al'moctar yamata sai da takai kasa ,Ashe dama abunda mijin ta ya aikata Kenan ,Ashe dama zalunci dayakeyi Kenan ,Ashe dama laifin sune ke bibiyar su ,meyasa k'addara su tazo ahaka meyasa Allah yabata miji mai son kanshi ne ,da Sauri tamike tayi waje batare da sunsan ma tanan nan ba „


Al'moctar dun ma kuka yakeyi a hankali sabida tausayin Yayan nashi abunda baitaba gani yayi ba tun tashinsu kuka haka ,sunkai minti goma ahaka sanan Doctor yashare hawayen sa glass dinsa yasaka yaja hannun Al'moctar sai cikin motar shi„


Bai zarce ko inaba sai gidan mahaukata na yara ,yana zuwa yanuna wata carte anan take suka bashi izinin shiga ciki ,office d'in likitan dake kula da ita yaje gaisawa sukayi daganan sukamik'e sai wani lungu tafe suke babu mai magana sai yara Wanda bazasu wuce shekaru goma sha biyar bane wandan kallo d'aya zaka musu kasan basa cikin hankalinsu ,“


Wani d'aki Doctor yabud'e ,DOCTOR IRFAN ne yafara shiga sanan Al'moctar wata yarinyar yahango Wace bazata wuce shekara goma sha biyu ba zaune sai taddy dake hanunta take shafawa gashin ta duk yarufe mata fuska sanye take da irin rugunan nan na asibiti Wace awanke take tsap kamar sabuwa sai d'akin dayake d'an zarnin fitsari dan ma ana kula da ita ne sosai „


jin karar bud'ewar k'ofah yasaka ta d'agowa da Sauri tana ja baya baya har takai karshen ginin teddyn tayada tashiga girgirza kanta kuka tafara yi tana fadin “a'a kar kayimun komi kayi hakuri ni kadai ce awurin inna ta ,kar ka lalata mun rayuwa wayo inna ta kizo kitaimake ni zai kasheni wayo ”yarinyar tafad'a tana had'e jikin ta wuri guda sai kuka takeyi ”

A hankali Doctor IRFAN yake karasawa kusanta yace “kanwata kalleni nine ba wanda zai yimiki komi matukar Yayan ki yana tare dake Budé idonki ki kalleni nine YAYA IRFAN ” yafad'a yana kokarin tab'ata „


Itakuwa kamar ta fahimta kukan ta tafara ragewa sanan tad'ago kanta cikin tsoro ganinshi yasaka rungumeshi da Sauri tana dariya shima dariya yasaki ,kuka takara fashewa dashi bayanta yashiga bubuga wa hakan yasaka ta yin shiru sunkai minti biyu ahakan „

Al'moctar dai ikon Allah kawai yake gani „

“yawa kinga nazo miki da friend ai kin ce dama kina son friend ko ,to gashi” IRFAN yafad'a yana nuna mata Al'moctar daya kakalo mumushi yayabawa fuskar sa „

Itakuwa kanta tad'ago takaleshi sanan takali IRFAN nuna shi tashiga yi kuma tana nuna Al'moctar yasan kama tagani duk da bata cikin hankali ta yafahimci taga kamar sune ,

Al'moctar ne ya matso hannu yabata cikin tsoro make hanun ta tayi tana kalon DOCTOR kanshi yad'aga mata aikuwa hannu ta tamik'a ma al'moctar sukayi musabaha dariya tayi Wace bakaramun tafiya tayi da imanin Al'moctar ba ,shima murmushin yasaki a hankali yace “Friends” itama cikin muryata ta yara tace “friends” tsale tahau yi tana dariya Doctor ma taya ta ya yi , chocolate d'in dayake kawo mata kulum inyazo yabata aikuwa da Sauri takarba zama tayi tahau b'arewa ganin takasa bud'ewa yasa tabata rai mikewa takoma yi Al'moctar tabawa alamar ya bud'e mata bud'emata tayi tahauci „


Ganin haka yasaka Doctor yimusu sing ne alamar su fita dan dama dahaka yake mata wayo idan bahakaba rik'eshi zatayi tayita kuka „

Office d'in DOCTOR suka koma ,IRFAN ne yace “ina fatan babu dai wata matsala ko ”yafad'a yana kalon likitan „

Glass dinsa likitan yacire sanan yace “Alhamdullillah jikin nata yana ta samun lafiya tunda yanzu tana yarda ana mata wanka ,kuma tarage kuka sai dai un abun yana dawo mata a memory dinta takeyi ,sanan fitsarin ma muna kan traitement insha Allah zata samu lafiya yoyon nan bada jimawa ba ” yafad'a yana kalon DOCTOR murmushi yayi dan bakaramun dad'i yaji ba da yaji wannan daddad'an labarin ,nan take yama likitan transfert d'in millions d'aya yace acigaba da kula da ita duk abunda ake bukata amun magana dahaka yamik'e Al'moctar yamara masa baya „

Tafiya sukeyi babu Wanda yace ma wani kala har suka iso hospital d'in „

Office dunshi yashiga yana mai dafe kanshi da yaji yana Sara masa „

Al'moctar ne yashigo a hankali bisa kujerar yazauna yana kalon Yayan nashi ,sai yanzu yafahimce shi gaskiya anyi zalunci mai yawa ,anyi makirci Wanda koshine iya abunda zaiyi Kenan cikin kwantar da murya yace “ Bro kayi hak'uri hak'ik'a na maka mumunan fahimta bai kamata nayanke ma hukunci ba batare da Na-san dalilinka nayin haka ba please kayi hakur...” bai karasa ba yadaga masa hannu tare da nuna masa k'ofar alamar yafuce ,mikewa wa yayi cikin rashin jin dadi yayi waje „


Rumaisa kai tana koma wa direct office dun mijin ta ta wuce cikin tashin hankali ta banko kofar Wanda bakaramun mamaki abun yabashi ba kasancewar yana tare da baki alama yamusu da sufita babu bata lokaci sukayi waje ”

Mari takai masa hade da fashewa da wani irin kuka tace “ashe mijin da nake tare da shi bansani ba ,Ashe mijin Dana ke tare da shi makashine ,ada ina alafahari da cewa babu Wace takai ni sa'a acikin mata Ashe kuskure nake tabakawa ,Ashe irin uban da nazaba wa y'a y'a na Kenan ,kaicona daganin wanan rana ,taya Wanda baiyi wa al'umar sa adalci ba zai yiwa iyalansa adalci Ashe haka duniya tacika da mutane marasa tsoron Allah masu son kansu ,ya Allah me nayi ka jarabce ni da irin wanan jarrabawar ” tafada tana kai wa durkushe bisa kafafunta kuka takeyi kamar Wace za'azare wa rai ,kuka takeyi na bakin ciki da tashin hankali „


Mrs hashim kai bakaramun choks yashiga ba ,bawai marin da takai masa bane tashin hankali sa a'a kalamanta sune tashin hankali sa ,kardai Matar shi mai hakuri ,mai addini ,mai kaunar shi taji wanan labarin shiko ina zaisaka kanshi ,dama duniya duk abunda kaboye wata rana doli assirinka zai tonu ama yazai yi haka Allah yarubuto masa „

Mikewa tayi cikin kuka tace “sai ka saké ni hashim bazan zauna da maha inci ba mara tsoron Allah doli zaka saké ni”

Tafada tana fita daga office dun gidan su taje ,tana zuwa dagudu tashiga falon „

Hajiya Iklas dake zaune tana kalon dadin kowa sai jitayi mutum yafado mata “subhannallahi ta furta ”dan bakaramun tsoro taji ba ,ganin tilon yar tata cikin wanan halin yasaka ta shiga cikin tashin hankali bayanta tashiga bubuga wa ruwa tamiko mata aikuwa tunda takafa kai sai data shanye Tass kara lafewa tayi ajikin Mahaifiyar tata tana sauke ajiyar zuciya adu'a tashiga tofa mata sai dataga tayi shiru dan kanta sanan tadago ta cikin nuna mata rayuwa da ma haka tagada yar hakuri ce tace “miye yasamu Ruma na tashiga tashin hankali har yasaka takasa jurewa sai datazo gareni ,shin dama bana fadamiki ba rayuwa duka dukan ta hakuri bace ,na dauka y'a ta ko mutuwa nayi bazati kuka ba sai dai tamun adu'aa fadamun miye yasameki y'a ta ”tafada tana shafa bayanta „

Kaf abunda yafaru sai da tafada mata hade da cewa “nikan bazan zauna da shi ba doli zai saké ni ,bazan zauna da azzalumin mutum ba ” hakika abun yazo mata a bazata batayi tsamani ba ko tunanin haka ,ama koma miye zai faru bazata bari yar ta tarabu da Auren ta ba kaddara ce tariga fata„


Ta bangaren Doctor kuwa baima doki Kiran da Nasreen take masa ba duk da kuwa yaga Kiran ,baya cikin yanayi mai dadi shiyasaka ya gwamaci ya shareta dan in yadauka bazataji dadi ba itama „

Nurse ce tace masa ana son ganin shi hakan yasakashi bada izin shigowa Rumaisa ce da mahaifiyar ta sai mahaifiyar Mrs hashim da mahaifinsa ganin kwarjin da mahaifin nsa yamasa yasaka shi basu izin zama zama sukayi suduka sanan Alhaji Ibrahim wato mahaifin hashim yace “da farko zan baka hakuri hakika abunda yaro na yayi bai yi dai dai ba Allah yana gani nayi kokarin ganin nabashi tarbiya kuma ba'ataba cemun ga abunda hashim yakeyi Wanda badai dai ba ,duk Wanda za'abawa hakuri to ancuce shi ne ,murama alkairi ga shari sakamakon mu yana wurin Allah da manzon sa ,hakika bansan abunda yasaka shi aikata haka ba Wanda kowa yasan ba halinshi bane kasancewar shidun talakawa yakeyiwa aiki Wanda bakaramun kaunar shi sukeba ,amma yanzu kai wanan abunda yayi banace kayafe masa ba amma kadubi girman Mahalicinka kayiwa Baby iklas aiki wace batasan komi na duniya ba ,sai kaunar iyayen nata ”yafada yana hawaye „

Karo na farko da yaji ranshi yayi sanyi akan wanan al'amarin ,Ashe dai iyayen nashi suna da kirki sai Allah yabasu da mara sanin darjar dan Adam ,ko bai rokaba yayi niyar yiwa yarinyar aiki tun lokacinda yaganta yaga hankali ta abunda yamata bada son ranshi bane ,haushin abunda mahaifin ta yayi ne ,amma zai ceto ranta kodan wanan datijjon dake gabanshi „

Ajiyar zuciya yasauke har zai yi magana Nurse tashigo tana fad'in “Doctor yarinyar nan tafarka sai kira take dan Allah kayafe wa dadyn ta ” da Sauri yamik'e yayi dak'in da take suma su Alhaji Ibrahim mara mishi baya sukayi ....✍️

Book 1
Page 61/62
             ......✍DOCTOR nashiga yatarar da ita sai kuka take idonta a rufe tana fadin “please yaya mai kyau ka yafe mishi kaji”tafada tana kara jujuya kanta bisa filon da'aka dora kanta „

Ganin halin da take ciki yasan da bata farka tsabar sa abun aranta ne datayi kanta yake shafawa cikin kunnanta yake radamata “calm dawn my sister kiyi barcci mai dadi na yafewa dadyn ki kinji ”yafada yana mai kara cigaba da shafa kanta „


Murmushi tasaki duk da kuwa har yanzu idonta a rufe yake „


Barcci takoma dudubata yasakeyi sanan ya fice daga room dun yana futa suduka sukayi ca akanshi suna tanbayar shi „

Cemusu yayi sukwantar da hankalinsu insha Allah zata farka nan da awa daya daga haka yakoma office dunshi „

Mr hashim ne yabi bayanshi kwankwasa yayi ,izini yabashi zama yayi cikin sada kai kasa yace “hakika na aikata kuskuren da har yanzu Nike kan yin nadamar shi na goyawa karya baya alhalin ga gaskiya ,nashiga tashin hankali sabida Alhaji Saleh mai kada yamun barazanar matukar ban kare dansa akotu ba zai sa adokemun y'a ,zai kasheta dama saura ce baya tunanin zatayi tsawon Rai ,Naso kwatowa yarinya hakinta ama yazanyi ina kaunar y'a ta fiyeda tunaninka zanyi komi sabida ita ,yaron dakanshi yasan ya aikata laifin domun dabakinshi yafadamun cewa yamata fyaden Wanda bakaramun tashin hankali yasaka ni ba ,doli ce tasa nabasa kariya banida wata dubara sai wanan ,Nasan baka halarci kotu ba ama Na-san kanada labarin komi sanan Na-san kaine mai kula da caisse din yarinyar na girgiza danaji ance SANADIN FYADE har takamu da cutar yoyon futsari ,sanan brain dunta yasamu matsala ,bayan kwana biyu da hakan naji labarin mutuwar mahaifiyar ta ,abun yataba ni ta inda baka zato ,nasha yin mafarfkin uwar yarinyar nan tana kuka tana fadun nakwatarwa yar ta yancinta ,daga baya nashiga neman ka domin taimakamun dan ganin an hukunta yaron nan koda kuwa zan rasa y'a ta ne da duk abunda na malaka ,Na-san banida hujoji shiyasaka nakame bakina Wanda abun yakemun ciwo azyciyata ”yafada wasu hawayen nadama da bakin cikin abunda ya aikata suna zubomasa „


Dan kalonshi Doctor yayi sanan yadauke kai kuma „


Cigaba Mr Hashim yayi da fadin “wallahi nayi nadama ashiye nake yanzu dan samun hujoji domun hukunta shi koda kuwa zan rasa rai na da dukiya ta gabaki daya”


Shiru ne yaratsa sanan Doctor IRFAN yace “hakika Allah ma muna masa laifi ya yafe mana ,na yafemaka saura hakin yarinyar nan ,sanan zan taimaka maka da hujoji dan ganin an hukunta wanan yaron ” 


Mr Hashim murmushi yasaki najin dadi sanan yahada hanayenshi biyu alamar godiya „


Bayan awa daya Iklas tafarka dadynta ta rungume tana fadin “Dady yaya mai kyau ya yafema ,Karoki yafiyar Allah yanzu dama malamin mu yana fadamana mu aikata alkairi ko bayan ranmu za'atuna damu ,yace duk abunda mutum yagirba shi yake samu ,ina son la dady na zanroki Allah ya yafema kaji ”tafada tana kara rungumeshi kanta yashiga shafawa yana murmushi yagodewa Allah daya bashi ita a matsayin y'a ,koda batada cikakiyar lafiya yanada yakinin zata fi wasu y'a y'a masu lafiya „

Dubata nurse tayi taga komi Normal dahaka tafita takaima Doctor komi Wanda yake shirye shiryen tafiya gida ganin isha'i ta wuce „

Gidan shi yaje direct yana zuwa bangaren Mufida yaje yadubata wace taci kwaliya sai kanshi take zubawa cikin kanan kaya rungumeshi tayi tana masa sanun da zuwa hanunshi tagani daure da bandege da Sauri takama hanun tana fadin “Bro miye yasamu hanunka ”tafada cikin damuwa tare da rike hanun nashi „

Murmushi yayi yana Jan hancinta yace “miye yasamu kanwata ta rude kamar ba Matar Doctor ba ” itama murmushin ta mayar masa tana jun dadin yanda yake kula da ita koda kuwa tana da yakinin baya son ta „


Sai da safe yamata tare da sakar mata kiss yayi part dun Nasreen Wace tajima da bushewa da jiranshi taci wanka cikin wasu matsatsun kaya Wanda suka fito masa da komi nata tanajin karar Budé kofa ta rufe idonta dan dama kwance take bisa doguwar kujerar falon „

Kalonta yayi ya kali TV dun dayake kune murmushi yasaki yasan dama za'arina yasan fushi take dashi „


“wayo Allah hanuna wash wai Allah na ”yafada yana yarfe hanun nasa mai ciwo tare da sakin kara mai sauti „

Aikuwa da Saurin ta tamike tana yi kanshi cikin rudewa tarike hanun tana fadun “subhannallahi miye yasamu hanun waye yajima ka ciwo,?”tafada tana kama hanun nashi tare da busa masa iskar bakinta tana bubuga bayanshi „


Kalonta yakeyi cikin so da kaunar ta dayakeji kulum suna nunkuwa aranshi ji yada ta rude akan wanan yar jimuwar da yayi „

Itakuwa batama lura da kalon daya kemata ba sai rudewa datasakeyi tana kokarin dagashi kara yakara saki cikin shagwaba yace “wayo hanuna kinga nayanke da glass kof dun yafashe ahuna wurin shan shayi wayo kaina yakemun ciwon ciki ”yafada yana kokarin shige wa jikinta ganin haka yasakata itama rungumeshi akirjinta tana fadun “Sorry kaji Baby na bari nahadama Ruwan wanka sai kasha magani ko ,Kaci abinci ”tafada tana kokarin zareshi ajikinta kara makalewa yayi yana sunsunar dukiyar fulanin ta da tasakashi a tsakiyar su wai ita ala doli rarashinsa take „


Makale kafada yayi kamar wani yaro yace “nidai a'a sai dai ki goyani bazan iya tafiya ba”yafada yana shagwabe fuska „


Da Saurin ta ta zareshi tare da juya masa baya alamar ya haw dun ,saura kadan dariya ta kubuce masa ama ya gimtse yafara kokarin hawa dan gwadata yasan ko kafa daya yadora mata saitaji ajikinta bareshi ita ala cilass zata kula da miji bashida lafiya „


Kafarshi yafara dorawa kuma yasakar mata nauyi ai batasan sanda ta koma kasa zaune ba tana rike ban ta sai nishi take „

Cikin gimtsewa da dariyar datake neman kubuce masa yace “yadai Kenan bazaki kaini ba komiye ?”yafada yana yamutsa fuska „

Cikin Sauri takara mikewa tace “kayi hakuri gaskiya kayi kato dayawa bazan iya goyaka ba ” yamutsa fuska yayi yazauna kan kujera yace “shikenan jeki nafasa zan kwana anan kuma bana son abincinki”


Ganin kamar yayi fushi yasata zama kusanshi tana fadun „kayi hakuri to kuma aikaine kamun girma ,zan kamaka sai Karike kafaduna ” kanshi yajuya daga face dinta yace “shikenan nine katon ko badamuwa ai kitafi abunki bana so ” itama cikin shagwaba tace “to kayi hakuri sai nakamaka kaji ” Doctor kai bakaramun dadi yaji ba itada yakamata ace tana fushi ,shine yakoma fushi gashi sai lalabasa takeyi kamar wani yaron goye ,gashi yana son kwasar gara dan haka yasan mizai yi „

Cikinshi yarike hade da sakin kara yace “wayo cikina yanamun ciwon kai ”kara rudewa tayi tace “Baby ciki kuma yake ciwon kai ” bai kulata ba sai shigewa jikin ta dayake Wanda kamshi humrar datasaka yake kara gigitashi „


Abinci taje tazubomasa Wanda shinkafa da miyar agushi ne sai kamshi yakeyi yana rike da cikinsa tashiga bashi tare da lalashinsa tana kaimasa abaki shikuwa sai shagwaba yake zuba mata „


Sai yayi ta tarin karya yana rike ciki yana kwasar lomar abinci itakuma tausaya masa kawai takeyi, sai yayi kamar zaiyi dariya kuma sai yayi fuska da haka sai data tabatar yakoshi sanan yakwashe kayan takai kitchen„

Yana ganin tashiga kitchen dun yace “kai Alhmdllah Allah nagode ma”dahaka yakoma rike cikinsa „

Nasreen nadawowa tasamu dakyar yamike sukaje dakin  ruwa ta hada masa bayi takaishi ai kuwa shagwabewa yayi yace “to da hannun mai ciwo kike so nayi wanka ”ya fada yana kokarin biyo bayanta „

Tasan nufin shi wato so yakeyi ita zata masa wanka komi ,bayanda ta iya rigar ta taimakasa masa ya cire haka sai data cire masa komi Anka wurin boxer tace ita sam bata san zancan ba shima cewa yayi ya fasa wanka tunda gashi dai su kayan nashi na ciki y'a zai yi Kenan ba zai wnke su ba komi ,ai kuwa ba karamun kunya ya bata ba ta ga alamar mijin nata dan duniya ne „

Babu yanda ta iya da rumtse ido ta cire masa yace kuma ita za ta masa wanka babu yanda ta ita cudasa ta shiga yi ama da tazo wajen abubuwan sai tayi kasa shi kuwa yana ankare da ita dariya ce take neman kubuce masa „


A haka dai yace sai ta wanke wajen ko kuma shi yayi da hanunshi mai ciwo ganin haka adadare tawanke wajen Wanda kulum abar tashi tsoro take bata tana mamakin yanda akayi ma take shiga har ta iya dauketa „


Suna fitowa mai ta shafa mishi kayan barccin sa ta saka masa shi kuwa yasamu abunda yake so sai shagwabe mata yakeyi „


Ita ma dakin ta ta koma tasaka wasu kayan barccin Wanda suka tafi da imanin Doctor har bai san sanda yafurta “fatabaraka-llahu ahsanil halikin ”murmushi tasaki gadon tahaw ta lulubeshi wai ita ala doli zata sa shi barcci bayan ga abunda yake so nan „


Rungume ta yayi yana cewa “Sleep ”tare da tofe su da addu'aa yaso kyaleta yaw „

Amma cikin dare Abu yaciyoshi har sai da ya samu ya mata wayo tare da rike ciki ya samu ya kwashi gara wada tasashi sumbatu da jinshi awata duniya daban yakai minti arba'in yana sasakar ta sannan ya samu yayi relasing cacabar ta yayi suka yi wanka „

Rungume abashi yayi haunshin nakan dukiyar fulaninta ahaka suka yi barcci cikin so da kaunar junan nasu

Asuba nayi ya tadata ya wuce massalaci ,sai daya wuce part dun Mufida ma yatada ta sai da yaga ta mike sannan yawuce massalaci ,bai dawo ba sai karfe bakwai kamar kullum yana son biyawa gida ya gaida su Abbi sanan yaje ya duba Iklas da sauran patience dinshi ,agagauce yayi wanka baiga Nasreen ba yasan tana kitchen sai daya shirya ya isketa ta bayan ta yazo ya rungumeta yana sakar mata kiss a wuya yace “good morning dadi na ”murmushi tayi dan ba karamun dadi sunan ya mata ba ,shima ya lura da hakan cikin so da kaunar sa tace “good morning my dear” kasko ya gani Ashe wai fankasu takeyi Wanda ya cika gidan da kamshi miyau ya hade yasan zaiyi dadi gashi ba zai samu damar ci ba „

Ganin ya hade miyau ta doki daya tasaka masa a baki wani irin dadi yaji yana girgiza kai yace “gaskiya nidai ina son ci kuma kinga nayi letti ko ” shagwabe masa tayi tace “ni dai a'a ka tsaya Kaci kaji ” hakuri ya bata tare da kissing dinta yace “ki ajemun idan na dawo zan ci ”ganin kafun ya dawo zai iya hucewa ya sakata ce masa ya bata minti uku ,kula ta nema mai kyau ta zuba masa ,falon tatar dashi tare da cemasa “muje ”bin bayanta yayi kamar rakumi da akala„

Bayan motar ta saka mishi shi tace “gashi nan Idan kaje kaci ”cikin shima shagwar da ita kadai yakewa yace “haba sai kace wani baby ”sumbatar shi tayi tare da fadun “Eh mana ai kai baby nane ko ba haka ba ”ta fada tana daga masa gira sama murmushi yayi mai sauti dan ba 'karamun dad'i yaji ba yace “hakane kam ,yawa ki kulamin da kanki da baby na kinji ”ya fada yana daga mata hanun cikin Sauri ,itama daga masa tayi tare da masa addu'a cikin ranta „



DOCTOR na zuwa gida ya gaishe da iyayen nashi agaguce kasancewar suma zasuje aiki ,a haka ya wuce office dunsa bai tsai tsaya ba ya duba patince dinshi „

Iklas ma yaje ya dubata Wace ba karamun dad'i taji ba daya yafe wa iyayen ta godiya ta masa sosai Wanda ya ji yarinyar ta kara shiga ranshi ,sai 'karfe biyu ya samu ya ci abinci Wanda duk da yanzu zai ci da zafinshi„


Kakanin nata ne suka zo dubata Alhaji Ibrahim da Mijin kakarta Buzu har da Mr Hashim sukeje office dun Doctor dan akwai bayanin da zai musu ,kallonsu yayi daya bayan daya .......✍

              ......✍"Da farko dai zanso sanar daku abunda yake haddasa ciwon hanta sannan kuma hanyoyin da ake gane ankamu da ciwon , sannan ku sani aikin ciwon hanta yanada matuk'ar tashin hankali idan anyisa dole ayi d'aya kodai mutum yarayu kodai yamutu ,har yanzu bawai ansamu tak'amaiman maganin ciwon hanta bane ,sai ta wanan aiki dole za'asamu sa hanun iyayen mutum biyu ko kuma wani najikinshi tareda yimasa addu'a ”yafad'a yana jujuyawa bisa kujerar sa mai jujuyawa „

Wasu zafafan hawaye ne suka zubo ma Rumaisa domin gani take kamar har tarasa 'yar tata ne mr. Hashim ne yarungume ta tare da bubbuga bayan ta „

“Courses of hepatitis (abubuwan dake kawo ciwon hanta )

1-alcohol consumption (shine yawan shan giya idan mutum yazama na yana yawan shan giya har tamasa yawa zai iya kamuwa da cutar hepatitis)

2-sexual contact(wurin saduwa ga mutumin dayake dauk'e da cutar zaisa kaima ka kamuda cutar hepatitis)

3-shearing syringe (yima wani alura da akama wani yana kawo cutan hepatitis)

4-From mother to baby viral infection (Ma'ana in Uwa tana dashi zata iya shafawa yaron cikinta ) wanan sune abubuwan dasuke kawo cutan ”yafada yana kalon Alhaji Ibrahim„

Cigaba yayi da fad'in “Symptoms of hepatitis (alamomin ciwon hanta )

1-loft of appetite (ma'ana mutum ya just bayajin d'and'anon komai a bakinsa, duk dad'in abu ba za ka ji shiba )

2-Dark Urine (Zaka futsarin mutum yanayin baki bakamar yanda kasaba ganinshi ba wanan alama ce ta hepatitis)

3-joint pains (Mutum zaiji gab'ob'in sa namasa ciwo hannu ne koma ina wannan alama ce ta hepatitis) 

4-low grade fever (ma'ana kowane lokaci mutum zaiji zazzab'i ajikinshi ) wannan sune alamomin kamuwa da cutar hepatitis ”cigaba yayi da fad'in „

Hanyoyin dayakama abi gudun kamuwa da ciwon hepatitis

1-get the vaccine of the disease (Ma'ana kasamu rigakafin shi wanan cuta yanada ga cikin abubuwan dake kareka daga wanan cuta )

2-use a condom during sex (ana so mutum yayi anfani da condom sabida koda mai cutan yana d'auke da shi bazai yi tasiri ba )

3-don't share a need to other person (Ma'ana kar kayi anfani da alura da'akama mai cutan hepatitis)

4-don't use an infection person items (Ma'ana kar muyi anfani dakayan mai d'auke da wannan cuta kamar reza ...) wannan sune abubuwan dake kare mutum daga kamuwa da wanan ciwon ”


Cikin gamsuwa da zancenshi su Alhaji Ibrahim suke daga mishi kai „

Shikuwa bayani yaci gaba dayi kamar haka “Test of hepatitis (kalolin gwaje gwajen ciwon )

1-Hbsag and Hcv (mataki na farko Kenan dan dubawa shin akwai cutar ko babu shine gwajin farko dayakama ayi)

2-hepatitis profile (Ma'ana in angwadaka anga kanadashi to sai ama profile shi kuma shine zai nuna wanan abun yashiga jikin la ko bai shiga ba ,shin système dinka suna da karfin dazasu kareshi tayanda bazaima hanta ila ba ko basuda shi duka wanan gwajin shi ne zai nuna maka hakan )

3-LFT liver function test (Ma'ana wanan ciwon yakama hanta ne tayanda bazai raba jini ba wanan shine abunda gwajin yake dubawa )wanan Kenan ”yafada yana gyara zaman glass dinsa na ido 

“Iklas yarinya ce maishiga rai zanso namata aikin nan amma Nasan da had'ari ko ni da nake DOCTOR nasan had'arinshi amma kuyi addu'a Allah yasa ayi a'sa'a ”yafad'a yana turomusu wani file alamar insun anmince susaka hannu „

Hannu na karkarwa Mr Hashim yaja file d'in gabanshi da Biron kalon kowa yakeyi awurin alamar a tsorace yake Mahaifin sa ne ya bubuga bayan sa alamar k'arfafa masa da sauri kuwa yasaka hannu „

Rumaisa kuwa kuka take mara sauti domin itakad'ai tasan abunda takeji ,hanunshi yad'ora saman nata dayake karkarwa suduka biyu yayi sing „


Kuka Rumaisa tafashe da shi had'e da mik'ewa tayi waje ,Mr Hashim ne yamike Mahaifinsa ya girgiza masa kai alamar ya k'yaleta yasan idan ba kuka tayi ba bazata ji sasauci ba yasan kuka ma rahama ne „


Tana fita Mr Hashim ya ce yana son zuwa yaga yarinyar yarok'i gafara ta duk dayasan bata cikin hankalinta DOCTOR ne yace “kuyi hakuri ba yanzu ba ,” babu yanda basuyi dashiba amma yak'i hakan yasa su tafiya gida da zumar gobe zasu dawo tunda gobe za'amata aikin akabar Rumaisa da Mahaifiyar ta Iklasa sai shi Mr Hashim „

Iklas ce rungume ajikin dadyn ta hak'ik'a tana tsananin tausayin iyayen nata barema data ta Wace tasan zata sha kuka idan tarasa ta ,amma tanaji ajikin ta kamar bazata rayuba ,fira sukeyi mai dad'i na shirmen da Farid yake tabkawa domin sud'ebe mata kewa sunsan idan abunda Iklsa takeso bai wuce amata firan Farid d'in nasu ba „


Shiru tayi Wanda yasaka dady nata shafa kanta yace “cutie na yanaga kinyi shiru ko bacci kikeji ne” hanunwan dadyn nata takama tad'orashi dai dai zuciyar ta tace “fad'amun mekakeji daka dora hanunka a saitin zuciyata ?”

Wani irin fad'uwar gaba ne yariski Mr hashim dan ji yayi zuciyar yar tashi yana bugawa nafitar hayaci ,murmshi tasaki ganin yashiga tashin hankali tace “dady na kasan bugawar micece zuciyata takeyi kuwa ?”tafad'a tana kara koma jikinshi takwanta tace “bugawar Tsoro ne ,inajin Tsoro dady na ,amma bana mutuwa ba sabida nasan nayi abunda ubangiji na yace nayi ina sallah, tata tace duk mai sallah aljannar yan sallah daban take ,sannan kuma kamanta nima ina Azumi ,sanan ina zuwa islamiya ,sanan banama kowa rashin kunya ina so naje naga ya aljanna take ,Abu guda yake sani fargaba dady ance duk mai sabawa iyayensa Allah yana fushi dashi ,kufad'amin kun yafe mun wata k'ila nataba muku wani laifi Wanda ba dai -dai ba ”tafad'a tana sauke ajiyar zuciya ,yanzu kai ba dadyn nata ba kad'ai hatta Tata ta sai da takalleta ,Iklas tana bata mamaki idan tayi wani Abu kamar ta wuce shekarunta kalamanta bak'aramin dagula lissafin iyayen nata yayi ba „


Mr Hashim ne ya tataro jarumtar shi ganin hawayen matar tashi yasan takaraya yace “kwarai kuwa my hapiness duk Wanda yake wad'annan zaishiga aljanna madaukakiya ,sanan Iklas yarinayr kirkice mai bin umarnin iyayen ta bata saba musu suna lafahari da ita sosai ”yafad'a yana k'ok'arin maida hawayen dasuke son zubo mishi „


Jikin mahaifiyar tata takoma hanun ta ta mak'ala cikin nata tace “tata na roke ki wata alafarma ”Iklas tafad'a tana kalon Mahaifiyar tata ,kai kawai Rumaisa take iya d'aga mata dan idan tace zata saké magana to kuka ne zai biyo baya „


“tata na ga Amanar Dady na nan nabaki karki bari yayi kukan rashina ,ki kulamin da Farid idan yagirma kice masa Antyn sa nason sa sosai ”tafad'a tana k'ara kamo hanun dadyn nata dayanzu kai hankalinshi yayi k'ololuwar tashi tace “Dady ga tata na nan ka kulamin da ita da Farid d'ina kamaye masa gurbina kai kad'ai zaka iya wasa dashi kaga kafi momy fara'a ”tafad'a tana murmushi baisan sanda murmushin shima yakubuce masa ba ,Rumaisa kai yanzu kuka kawai takeson yi hakan yasata mik'ewa zata fita taji anrik'e hanun ta ,Iklas ce rungume ta tayi takamo dadyn nata shima tahad'asu wuri guda yazamana sun Sakata atsakiya “kiyi kuka agabanmu zamu rarasheki nasan me kikeji Momy na ,”Iklas tafad'a aikuwa wani irikin Kuka Rumaisa tafashe dashi Wanda yasakata rungume yar tata kam ajikin ta kamar Wani zai kwace mata ita „


Mr hashim kai kukan zuci yakeyi yasan alhakin 'yar mutane ne ke bibiyarsu „


Takai minti biyar tana kuka sanan tatsagai ta ,“Momy ?”Iklas tafad'a tana murmushi „

Itama Rumaisa Murmushi tasaki sabida babu yanda batayi ba Iklas Tarika Kiran ta Momy amma tak'i ,sai yau “Momy na barcci nakeji ”tafad'a tana kwantawa ajikin Mahaifiyar tata ,gyara mata kwanciyar tayi ta sumbace ta akunci da goshi tareda yimata addu'a tashafa mata ,Mr Hashim kai mik'ewa yayi yafita daga dak'in kozaiji sanyi aranshi „


IRFAN ma yana komawa gida duk jikinshi amace yake ,Wanda ganinsa awanan halin yasaka Nasreen ganewa har tana tanbayar sa bai boye mata komi ba yafad'a mata bakaramin tausayawa iyayen yarinyar tayi ba da ita kanta yarinyar ,ruwa tahada mishi yayi wanka jalabiyar sa baka yasaka Abinci sukaci harda Mufida datazo ,suna k'arewa takoma part d'inta ,sai karfe d'aya sannan suka kashe kalon dasukeyi hakan yasaka Irfan tafiya dubo Mufida koda yajema tayi bacci gyara mata kwanciyar ta yayi ya yamata addu'a tareda sumbatar ta yakule mata part d'un „


Nasreen ma kayan baccinta tasaka tashafe jikin ta da humra bakaramin kyau kayan suka mata ba,Irfan ne ya rungume ta yace “kinyi kyau irin babu wace ta isa takamo ki ”yafad'a yana sakar mata kyayawan kiss ,taji dad'i sosai dan tana so tayi Abu danshi kuma ya yaba ,yau dai bai nemata ba sabida yakamata yabarta ta huta bawai dan baya bukatar ta ba „

Rungume abarsa yayi tareda zura hanunshi cikin rigar ta yana taba cabulan ta bata hanashi ba saima daga rigar datayi tasakama sa D'aya abaki aikuwa kama wa yayi kamar wani baby ahakan sukayi barcci dan dama dashi yake kwana „


Asuba nayi yatashe ta kamar kulum ya wuce massalaci Mufida ma saida yatasheta ,karfe bakwai yadawo kamar kulum yau ma bai wuce kicthen dunba shiryawa sa yashiga yi sai da ya kamala ,ganin takwas saura gashi tara zasu shiga tiyata yasan Nasreen zata tsaidashi akan lalle sai yaci abinci shikuma baya son saba alkawari barema akan aikinsa „


Hakan yasakashi lalabawa tareda ajemata letter a inda zata gani yatafi sai jin karar motar shi tayi aikuwa da Sauri ta fito koda take zawa yatafi abunda harga Allah bataji dad'i ba amma Gabin abunda yarubu ta mata a letter din yasaka ta jin dad'i tareda yimasa adu'ar neman sa'a akan aikin dazaiyi yaw „


Ta bangaren Mufida kai Momy ta takirata tace “fad'amun haryanzu yana shan giyar ne ?”


“Haba Momy yadaina ”tafada tana gunguni dan yanzu kai tana matukar kaunar mijin nata ,bata son ganin aibunshi 

“tsaki Momy taja had'e da fad'in “kidai yi taka tsantsan da kishiyar taki naga alamar muguwa ce ”da Sauri Mufida takatse ta da fadun “a'a Momy fah nidai bangani ba ,ni yanzu wanka zanyi sai anjuma ”tafad'a tana kashe wayar .....✍

BOOK 1
PAGE 65/66


              ......✍sosai tashiga cikin tashin hankali ganin abun ƙaunar nata adamuwa „

„Iklas ta tafi Nasreen ,ina tausayawa iyayen ta ,haƙika abun tausayi ne ,inajin ta kamar  maman ”yafaɗa yana sauke ajiyar zuciya haɗi da shafa kanta „

“Allah yajikanta Allah yasa ta huta ,Allah yabawa iyayen ta haƙurin rashin ta ,hakika abun atausaya musu ne ,kayi haƙuri kaji ”Nasreen tafaɗa tana kallonshi „

Ɗan sajenshi dayasha gyara irin na ƴan gayu tashiga shafawa hannun ta yariƙe yana kallon cikin idon ta ,Bakinshi yakai kan nata yashiga sakar mata wani irin hot kiss ,itama mayar masa tashiga yi sosai tabashi haɗin kai ,Wanda yasa har yafara manta damuwar dayake ciki ,rigarta yaɗaga sama itama kayan tashiga ciremishi anan suka fara tsotse su har sai da sukayi saura daga su sai boxer Wanda ita kuma pant ɗin kaɗai yarage mata kasancewar dama yace tadaina saka brezia in suna tare tayadda daya zo basai yasha wahala ba „

Gabaɗaya sun fice a hayyacinsu dan yanzu Doctor yakoyama jarababbe babu salon dabai koyamata ba nashi „

Bakin ta tasaka takamo harshen shi tashiga gwadamasa itama yanzu tafishi kwarewa sunkutar ta yayi yatafi sai daƙin su cigaba yayi da tsotsar ta yana tsafata ya tsotse ta sanan yanemi hanyar shi dayake tsananin jin daɗin shigar ta wacce ako yaushe jiyakeyi kamar kara matse mishi ita akeyi ga daɗi dayakeji har cikin kwakwalwarshi ahaka yafara havin sex da ita „

Yau kai Doctor ba sauƙi domin bakaramin cin ta yakeyi ba Nasreen ma bakaramin daɗi take jiba sosai take bashi haɗin kai wurin kwasar gara ,sau biyu yana relasing Wanda a yanzu a ƙalla yakai awa biyu kuma cikin su sau biyu kawai yabar ta huta sai yaci gaba „

Wani irin riƙewa taji bayanta yayi kara tasaki mai karfi tana tureshi daga jikin ta amma ina gogan naku bai masan tanayi ba sai da ya ƙara minti talatin da biyar yayi relasing sauka yayi akanta yana sauke numfashi sumbatar ta yayi tare da rungume ta „

Nasreen kai ta galabaita iya galabaita cikin kuka tace “wayo Allah na wayo Mama kizo kitaimakeni ciki na ”tafaɗa tana riƙe ciki ,bai ɗauka dagaske abun haka yakeba yaɗauka dai shagwabar ta takeyi ,sai dayaga fah kukan dagaske takeyi tana riƙe ciki sanan yamike da Sauri a gagauce yayi wanka jallabiyar sa kawai yasaka kayan ta yasaka mata yasunkuce ta sai mota asibitin shi ya wuce da ita dan bakaramin ruɗani yashiga ba ganin Angel ɗinshi cikin wanan damuwar yana fatan Allah yasa dai ba wata matsalace babba ba „

Yana zuwa office ɗinshi yashiga da'ita sister Khadija ce ta biyoshi jinin ta yaɗiba yabata yace taje lab maza ta dubo masa yana jiran result ,allura yayi mata ko minti biyar ba'ayi ba yaga bacci yayi awon gaba da ita ,kyakywar face ɗinta yakala yaga yanda tayi fayau kamar ba itace tayi kwaliya dazuba har taɗan faɗa ,wani irin tausayin ta ne yaji yasan tana haƙuri sosai dashi dan dama itace mai ɗauke masa lalurar sa ,Mufida kai idan yaje da anfara zata fara kukan gajiya dole yakyale ta relasing ya tara sauran sai yazo wurin ta yake saukewa ,gashinta daya barbaje akan filon ya shiga shafawa haɗe da sumbatar kuncinta „


Sister Khadija ce ta kwankwasa shine yabata izinin shiga haɗe da result ɗin tamiƙo mishi ,tana fita yabuɗe file ɗin wani irin ihu yayi hade da faɗuwa kasa yana mai yin sujada da duk addu'ar datazo bakinshi ,abun dayake gani dagaskene shine zai zama uba ,shi Irfan kuma abar kaunar shi ce zata haifamishi shi wani irin taku yayi sai saman gadon rigar ta yadaga yana sunbayar ta haɗe da shafa cikin ,jiyake duk duniya babu wanda yakaishi farin ciki ayau duk da damuwar Rashin Iklas Nanan a cikin ranshi amma yasamu abun da yawargaza wanan ƙuncin dayake ciki „

Allah mai iko yaketa nanata wa haɗi da kai mata kiss tako ina Wanda harɗan motsawa takeyi amma kuma sai takoma sabida aluran baccin dayamata cikin sati biyu ashe shiɗin  jarumi ne ya'iya aiki „


Ta ɓangaren iyalan Iklas kuwa dadyn ta dakanshi taɗuki gawar ta batare da yayi sa kowa magana ba yasaka ta abayan mota suma su Alhaji Ibrahim binshi sukayi sai mahaifiyar Rumaisa da'akabari tare da ita koda zata farfaɗo ,

Bayan awa ɗaya tafarka cikin kuka take faɗin “haba Iklasi na kar kitafi kibarni ,ya kike so nayi da rayuwata ”tafaɗa hawaye na gangaro mata kuma idon ta arufe wanda ga dukan alamu mafarki takeyi „

Murmushi tasaki a lokaci ɗaya kuma hawaye na zubo mata tace “namiki alƙawari insha Allah zancika ”tafaɗa tana kara faɗaɗa murmushin ta „

Mahaifiyar tata ce ta riƙe hanun ta tana tofah mata addu'a dan taga alamar mafarki ƴar tata takeyi „

Kai tashiga girgiza kuma hawaye nabin fuskar ta tace “a'a a'a”dahaka ta tashi da Sauri zaune ,kanta tashiga dafewa wanda yake tsananin aara mata sai yanzu komi ke dawo mata akai mahaifiyar ta takalla dake damƙe da hanunta tana tofah mata addu'a tace “iklas ta tafi ko Mama dama nasani tafaɗamin bazata yi tsawon rai ba ”tafaɗa wasu hawaye na gangaro mata ,rungume ta tayi tana shafa bayan ta sunkai minti uku komi ta tuno kuma ta mike da Sauri tana faɗin “Mama tashi muje naga Yata nayi mata addu'a ,nayi mata ganin karshe ,kar akaita gidan ta nagaskiya batare da mungana ba ”tafaɗa tana neman mayafin ta da hanu tariƙeshi Mahaifiyar nata nabin ta abaya „

Adaidai ta suka ɗauka sai gidan su wanda yacika maƙil da jama'a dan rasuwar babu wanda bai jita ba family Ibrahim mai dallah ,ga malaman makarantar su da ɗalibai duk sunzo „

Rumaisa bata ma ganin gabanta bare ta lura da masu mata ta'aziya ,gidan tashiga har falo mutane ne ,ɗakin ta ta shiga wanda Hajiyar Ibrahim takama hanunta har ciki takai ta ,Mr Hashim tagani zaune yana karanto addu'a tare da tofa ma yar tasu dake kwance an rufeta da suturar da akewa kowa idan zaije gidan sa nagaskiya „
Dabas tafaɗi gaban gawar murmushi tasaki hade da kalon kyakyawa fuskar yar ta su wace kamar ka kirata ta amsa ,fuskar ta sai annuri takeyi har wani Kyau ta ƙara sumbatar ta tayi agoshi kasancewar dama fuskar ta abuɗe take tace “Iklasi na nayafemiki ,baki taɓa ɓatamun da rai ba ,Allah ne shaida ,ina ƙaunar ki yaya ta Allah yasa can tafiye miki nan ina fatan Allah yayi gagawa ɗaukar rayuwa ta dan naje natarar dake mu rayu acan ,”tafaɗa tana murmushi wanan karon kai harda hawaye a fuskar ta kara sumbatar ta tayi haɗe da jan mayafin ta rufe mata fuska „

Baƙaramin mamaki tabawa Mr Hashim ba ,hakan yana nufin tasa wa kanta salama Kenan ,baiyi tunani ba ,baiyi tsamani ba ,Ashe dama Rumaisa zata iya haƙuri rashin yar tata har haka ,Alhaji Ibrahim ne yashigo da wasu mutum huɗu akasaka ta makara ,Mr Hashim dai yana zaune a wurin kwata -kwata jiyakeyi kamar ba shi ba shikenan duniyar kenan rayuwa dama batada tabass „

Rumaisa ce ta rungume shi da ƙarfi tana rufe idon ta wani irin kuka ne yazo mishi ,yasakeshi da ƙarfi Wanda yasa Rumaisa ƙara ririƙe shi tana sauke ajiyar zuciya dan bata taɓa ganin Mijin nata awannan halin ba ,kuka yakeyi kamar ƙaramin yaro harda shassheƙa tana tso ta rarashi mijin ta amma itama tana buƙatar mai rarashin ,sunkai minti ashirin ahaka sannan Alhaji Ibrahim yashigo yasamesu ahaka rungume da juna ga Rumaisa da kifa fuskar ta aƙirjinshi ,shikuwa sai kuka yakeyi wanda yanzu yakoma shasheƙa kamar ba namiji ba ,“Haba Hashim dakanka ,kai dayakamata ace ka rarashi matar ka kaine kake kuka agabanta haba, kuyiwa yarinyar ku addu'a mana ku kyautata mata zato ,sai dai muyi fatan tamu tazo da Kyau ,tunɗazu mukejira Mutafi bunneta  amma kana zamanka bayan gatan da zaka nuna mata shine kasata da hannun ka a cikin gidan ta nagaskiya ”Alhaji Ibrahim ne mai wanan maganar ,yana kallon ɗan nasa dayake ta kuka „

Rumaisa ce ta Miƙe kamar zata faɗi ta shiga toilette ,shikuwa mahaifin shine yakamashi yatafi dashi ,ankai Iklas gidan ta nagaskiya Wanda duk inda aka Budé ruwa ne ake gani aramin da aka tona kuma ruwan irin masu sanyin nan ne ,so uku ana tonawa ana ganin haka ana hlun ne malamin yace asakata haka wannan rahama ce ta ubangiji dayayi mata „

Aikuwa haka Mahaifin ta dakanshi yasakata bayan angama bunneta ne Malam ya shiga yima mutane wa'azi haɗe da nuna musu wanan ishara kaɗai ta ishesu su na kyautata ma yarinyar zato insha Allah dahaka yayi nasiha sosai Wanda ta ratsa mutanan wurin sosai harda masu kuka ,bayan kowa ya watse akabar Mr Hashim Wanda yajima yanama yar tasa ad'u'a sosai sanan yamike ya shiga mota yakama hanyar gida yana mai tunanin rayuwa shikenan fah yanzu Iklas dinshi ta tafi sai dai Allah yajikanta da rahama „


Ta ɓangaren Doctor kuwa nan take yakira Samir yafaɗamishi Wanda baƙaramin murna yatayashi ba ,Irfan ɗin ne yace “To inkaji haushi dai kayi Auren ko sai mun Haïfa mun baka ?”yafaɗa cikin zolaya dariya suka saki suduka sanan

Ne Samir yace “Allah yasawake nayita zama har kwan naku yakoma gudan jini har ahaifeshi yagirma sanan nikuma Samir na Aura impossible ”yafada ya fashewa da dariya ,shima Irfan dun dariya yayi daganan yakshe wayar „

Nasreen kai kwanan ta tasha sai karfe bayan Magrib tafarka tare da bude idon ta tana kalon saman dakin kanta ne taji yamata wani irin nauyi sanan ,Irfan ne yashigo yana ganin tana kokarin mikewa yataimaka mata ta zauna rungume abar sa yayi yana fadun “fatan dai ba inda yakemiki ciwo ko baby na ”yafada yana kalon ta ,kanta ta nunamishi dake dan Sara mata hakan yasa yafahimci Kenan kanta ne ke ciwo ,

Kayansu ya fara tatarawa saida yatatara komi ya zo daukar ta yayi kamar baby ya'aza kafada sai cikin mota duk da kuwa kalon da mutane kemusu bai damu ba ,tuki yakeyi yana sakin murmushi ganin haka yasa ta tace “haba wai miyene naga kanata kalo na kana murmushi ,bayan kamar bakaine naga kana hawaye dazuba ,kuma ma ni miye yake damuna ?”tafada cikin shagwaba har hawaye sun fara zubo mata „

Yasan dama Zaisha fama da rigima “wani farin ciki ne nake ciki mara misaltuwa Wanda nasan kema inkika jishi sai kin tayani murna ,kuma abun da kedamunki yanada nasaba da farin ciki na kedai karkice komi sai munje gida ”yafada yana kamo hanun ta yasumbata ,bata kara magana ba harsai da sukaje gida part dinta ya wuce da ita wanka yayi mata dakanshi yasaka mata kaya hanun ta yakamo sai falo kalon ta yayi yace “fadamun me kike son ci ”dan satar kalonshi tayi sanan tayamutsa fuska tace “Nidai kafadamun miyene wai ”tafada tana hawaye ,hawayen ya shiga lashewa yace “Albishirinki ?” dan yamutsa fuska tayi sanan tace “goro ” 

“Fari ko ja ?”yakara tanbayar ta yanzu kai tara kai wa makura ama ta d'Aure tace “fari ”

“kinaɗauke da ciki na sati biyu zamu zama iyaye muma insha Allah ina cikin farin ciki ”yafada yana shafa cikin ta „

Kass kakass kakeji karan faduwar kof ,gabadaya suka juya suna kalon wanene wanan ......✍🏻

INSHA ALLAH BAYAN SALLAH IDAN MUKA DAWO DAGA HUTU ZAN FARA POSTING BOOK 2 RANAR 10/05/2022,INA MASOYAN DOCTOR IRFAN NA MAMAN FAUZAN KU HANZARTA DAN FARA BIYAN KUDINKU 


BOOK 1
PAGE 65/66
              ......✍DOCTOR nazuwa aka fara shirye shiryen shiga da ita opérations Room ,Babu Wanda baizo ba à famille „

Rumaisa ce rungume da yar tata kam taki sakin ta ga likitoci nan Wanda zasu shiga da ita Room d'un ama tahana ,Mr Hashim ba irin rarashin da baiyi mata ba ama taki sakin yar tata „babu wanda baisa bakiba ama gam tariketa „

Iklas ce take kokarun tashi daga rungumar da mahifiyar tata tamata tace “Momy na kinajin tsoro ne kada na mutu ko, ama ni ba tsoron mutuwar nake ba ,ina tsoron tashin hankalin dazu shiga ne ,kinaji ko kibari muje amin aikin nan inkuma kinfi son ganina haka sai afasa kinji kidaina kuka ”tafada tana share ma tata ta ido da hawaye kebin fuskar ta ‚girgiza kai Rumaisa tayi tace “a'a bazama ki mutu ba y'a ta zaki rayu tare damu ko ?”tafada cikin confidente„

Murmushi Iklas tayi tare da sumbatar kuncin mahaifiyar tata ,Sakin ta tayi tajuyo wurin dadyn nata da idonshi suka kada sukayi jajur sabida tashin hankalin daya keciki “dady kasiyamun chocolat kaji insha Allah zan fito lafiya kumin adu'aa kawai ance adu'arr iyaye bata faduwa kasa banza ko ”tafada tana rungume shi ,shima dukawa yayi daidai tsawon ta yasum bace ta agoshi ,wurin kakanin nata taje takama hanun Mahaifiyar Mr Ibrahim tace “miye naga kin wani marairai ce sai kace bake bace mai muna fada in munje gidanki ,Ashe dai nidin yar baiwace kina so na ko ”tafada tana dariya Wanda sai da dimple dinta y'a lotsa dariya ce tasubuce ma hajiyar Hashim harda sauran mutane wurin „


“to maza Jena ku kulamin da koshiyoyi na ,kudan basu kulawa kadan ama kartaki tawa dan Nice uwar gidan ku anan duniya dakuma can lahira ko bahaka ba ?” Iklas takoma fada tana kama hanun Mahaifin Hashim da Baba Rumaisa tare da daga musu gira sama „

Wani irin rauni ne y'a mamaye su dan kai abunda Iklas take kamar bankwana take musu „

Cikin dauriya Mahaifin Mr Hashim yace “haba sudin me aikece gaba kan komi yar tsaka rai ”yafada yana yimata kiss akunci „


Wurin mai sunan ta taje wato mahaifiyar Rumaisa tace “tsohuwa mai ran karfe dubeki duk kinbi kin ki tafiya kibarmin miji na ,to nizan taf.....”da Sauri tarufe bakinta „wurin kowa tsit da abunda yake saka wa azuciyar shi „


Hanunun Nurse d'un takama hade da sakar musu murmushi Wanda yasaka wasu daga ciki zubda hawaye hanu tadaga musu ,bata juyo ba tacigaba da tafiya rike da hanun Nurse d'un sai dakin théâtre ,Sauran Nurse d'un kuma duk suka shiga cikin Room d'un „


Rumaisa ce tafashe da wani irin kuka tana rungume mahaifiyar tata tace “shikenan Mama Iklas zata tafi tabarni wanan bankwa takeyi ,Ya Allah kar ka kashemin y'a ta Y'a Allah kabarta da ranta ni kadoki nawa, y'a Allah banida kowa sai kai gareka nake neman taimako ”tafada tana kara fashewa da kuka „


DOCTOR IRFAN karshen shiga Room d'un „

Awurin kowa yazauna zugum sai adu'aa dasukeyi acikin ransu dakuma abayane „


Acan cikin dakin théâtre kuma Iklas sakanta ta a'amshi Riga mara lafiya wata blue Wace mara lafiya yake sakawa kamar dai yanda za'amata théâtre itama „


Gadon tahaw dakanta tare da zama hanu tadaga sama Nurse d'un suna tsaye suna kalon ikon Allah ganin wanan yarinyar mai courage Wace basu taba ganin irin ta ba “Ya Allah hakika ni baiwar kace yar bawanka ,ya Allah naroke ka idan rayuwata tanada anfani ya Allah kafudani lafiya ,idan kuma batada anfani ya Allah ka kasheni kasakani a aljanna madaukakiya jannatul fidausi ,kabawa iyaye na hankurin rashi na banida tsumi banida dubara sai taka ”tafada tana shafawa „


Masu rauni daga cikin Nurse d'un saida sukayi hawaye Jikin su yayi sanyi da ameen duk suka amsa suka fara harhada alurai da oxygène hade da sauran kayan aiki „

Dai dai nan DOCTOR yashigo sanye da rigarshi ta likitoci hade da bavette inshi dayasaka yarufe hancinsa zuwa bakinshi „

Ganin shi yasakata yin murmushi ,shima murmushi yasakar mata„kwanciya tayi akan gadon kowa a wurin rufe hancinsa zuwa bakinsa yayi da takunkumi ,kowa yana wurin daya dace dan gudanar da nashi aiki fara wa kawai suke jira kusa da ita DOCTOR yamatso sosai yace “kanwata ?”  

“Na'am Yaya mai kyau ”itama tafada tana kalonshi hade da sakar masa murmushi „

“ina fatan kinshirya ko ”yafada yana shafa kanta ,murmushi tasake yi tace “Eh nashirya ”tafada tana daga mishi gira sama Wanda yazama da'biar ta bata masanin tayishi ,saita wata na'ura yayi dai dai cikinta hade da zura kanta cikin wani wuri Wanda kan nata ne kawai aciki alurai yashiga hadawa „

Kalon hanunta yayi da yajimke zanin gadon cikin wata irin murya ta tsoro yaji tace “Yaya Mai kyau?"takara Kiran sunan shi ,hakan yasashi dakatawa yace “Na'ama kanwata fadamun miye yasami jarumar yarinya mai suna Iklas ”yafada yana zuwa dai dai kunanta dan jin mai zata fada “inajin tsoro  ne ”tafada masa dai dai kunansa „

Wani irin bugawa yaji zuciyar shi tayi ,cikin karfafa wa patience dun nashi guiwa yace “ba ganiba karkiji tsoro kituna irin kaunar da iyayen ki sukemiki zakiji karfin guiwa ”

Aikuwa wani courage tasamu hade da dagamishi kai alamar tashirya ,hakan yasashi fara wa da bissimillah hade da yimata alurai ,ad'ua yaji tanayi har yaga tarufe idonta alamar ta tafi hutun wani lokaci „

Kowa matsowa yayi tare da fara nashi aikin ,aiki sukeyi cikin nutsuwa da kwanciyar hankalin bakajin kara komi sai wata na'ura da ke nunamusu numfashin ta yana wane mataki ne „

Awa biyu suka dauka suna aiki ,Alhmdllah kowa yafurta cikin zuciyarshi ganin angama kuma numfashin ta yana tafiya yanda yakamata dinke wurin akayi bayan sun gama ne yacire game d'un yasaka ahanunshi hade da wata na'ura kamar torche daya makala agoshinsa „

Toilettes dun bayin yashiga y'a wanke hanayensa ,yana fitowa yabada umarni sukai ta dakin hutu ,yana fitowa gabadaya famille sukayi kanshi cikin kuka Rumaisa tarike hannunsa tana fadun “Doctor kafadamun y'a jikin y'a ta take karkacemun dai narasa ta ”tafada tana kalonshi „

Murmushi yasaki hakan yabasu damar cewa komi lafiya aikuwa duk hamdllah sukayi ,basu karasa ba wata nurse tazo da gudu tana fadun DOCTOR numfashin ta nayun kasa ,ai dagudu yayi cikin Room d'un Rumaisa na mara masa baya tun kafin ta shiga wani nus yatsaida ta aikuwa wurin tafadi asume gabadaya sukayi kanta hade da Kiran wasu likitocin aka shiga bata taimakon gagawa „

DOCTOR nashiga yaga numfashin ta nakasa wata n'aura yashiga Dana mata akirji da Sauri hawaye yaga suna gangarowa akan fuskar ta ,bai fasa ba yacigaba da danawa numfashin nata ne yacigaba dayi kasa tun yana 70har yadawo 20 ganin haka yasakashi kara shiga tashin hankali cikin Rudi yace “one ,two,trix”yadana abun da karfi ama haka numfashin ta keyin kasa har yadawo 10wani irin faduwar gaba ne yariskeshi dan yasan yanzu kai bashida wani hope na zata rayuwa da ganin yakai 5yasakashi danawa da karfi ama tsik yakoma 3 na'ura tadaina kara „

Cigaba yayi da danawa cikin fitar hankali ,ganin arude yake yasaka wasu daga nus dun Maza suka ririkeshi dabaya dabaya yashiga yi Allah yasa akwai kujera awurin yafadi dabass ,wasu hawaye ne yaji suna zubo mishi ,kanshi yashiga girgizawa hade da fadun “a'a kuce mun bata mutu ba ,a'a ”yafada yana kaiwa kujerar naushi da hanun shi mai ciwon „

kanshi yadafe hawaye yakeyi badan Iklasa ba yasan Iklas yarinyar kirki ce batakai kwasar zunubai ba sanan kuma yana mata kyakyawan zato ama kuma iyayen nata sune abun tausayi ,rufe mata fuska sukayi hakan yakara tabatar mishi dagasken Iklas Rai yayi halinsa “inalilahi yashiga nanatawa aranshi ”mizaici wa iyayen Yarinyar nan ,yace musu Iklas ta rigamu gidan gaskiya komi ina bazaiyu ba bazai iya ba kanshi ya shiga gorgizawa yana tunanin tashin hankalin da zasu shiga „

Garam yaji an turo kofar bakowa bane saï  Mrs Hashim ganin mutum kwance a lulube Wanda ysan da'an rufeka da kyalen nan har fuska alamar matace Kenan wasu hawaye yaji suna sulalo mishi ,wurin gadon yaje yagirfana kanshi yadora hade da fashewa da wani irin kuka Wanda yasaka DOCTOR fita waje bazai ita jure ganin wanan ba ,bazai iyaba ,yana fita su Alhaji Ibrahim sukayi kanshi bai ko kelsu ba yayi gaba abunshi da Sauri fuskar nan tasa yayi jajur alamar yana cikin wani haki „

Office dinsa ya shiga kayanshi yatatara yayo hanyar gida shima yana bukatar rarashin ,dan kwanankin dayayi da ita bakaramun shiga ranshi tayiba dakayar da taimakon Allah yasamu yakai gida part dun Nasreen yaje ,yana shiga yaji kanshi turaren wutan datasaka yadaki hancinsa „

Falon babu kowa wutar ma ankashe saï duhu daya mamaye falon, bisa kujerar yazauna hade da saule kafafunshi kasa yadafe kanshi ,

Nasreen kuwa tana daki wanka tayi sosai tashafa humra duk datasan da marecen nan zata saki aiki ama bashi zaisa tazauna ba kanshi ko wanka ajikin taba „

Wasu Riga da wando tasaka yan kanti Wanda suka fitoma daduk wani diri nata ,falon ta fito duk dabatasan zwashi ba ,tazo ne takali TV tunda batada aikin yi ,TV takuna batareda takuna wutar falon ba „

A hankali ta zauna kusa da shi ,shikuwa duk dayaji kanshi turaren ta da kuma karan TV baisa y'a kaleta ba ,jin kamar Abu mai taushi Wanda kamar mutum kuma kamar hannu bayasan tasan ita kadai ce a gidan ,kara tabawa tayi taji dai wanan hanune domin yanzu kai yatsunshi ta kama wata irin kara ta saki hade da kokarin mikewa ,ganin irin firgita datayi yasashi kamo kugunta da Sauri tafado saman cinyarshi ,rungume ta yayi kama itako bata ma lura da kanshi shiba shiyasaka tafi tunanin aljani ne ne ,ganin duk tafirgice yasashi kai bakinshi dai dai kunan ta yana fadun ”Nutsu man matsoraciya Mijin ki ne ”ya fada yana sunsunar wuyanta ,sai yanzu taji hankalin ta ya kwanta ama wallahi ita kai taji tsoro ai ta dauka aljani ne ,ajiyar zuciya ta shiga saukewa ,shi kuwa DOCTOR sakin ta yayi ganin tana muku mukyto mikewa ,Wutar falon takuna ganin shi zaune dafe dakai yasata sauke ajiyar zuciya ”

Cikin shagwaba tare da bubuga kafafun ta kasa tace “Wannan ai mugunta ne wallahi ni na dauka aljani ne ”ta fada tana Harar shi ,dan dagowa yayi ya kalleta kuma ya maida kanshi saman kujerar ,

Kallo  ta karemishi kaf ta fahimci yana cikin damuwa ne hakan yasata zuwa tahaye masa cinya hade da dauke hannunshi da yadora akan kanshi tace “miye ya samu gwarzon mijina na ga tashin hankali akan kyakyawar fuskarshi ,zan iya sanin Wace damuwa ce ta maidamin shi haka ”ta fada tana kallonshi hade da kwantawa saman kirjinshi taji yanda bugun zuciyar shi ya karu....✍

BOOK 1
PAGE 69&70
            ........✍️Mufida ce tsaye Wace tazo duba KO lafiya har yanzu bai shigo ba kuma taga dare nayi bayan taji motsin zawar shi cikin mota ,shine tana shigowa taji IRFAN nafadawa Nasreen karuwa da sukasamu dama tafe take tana kurba thé dinta shine jin abunda yafada yasata yarda cof glass din yafashe„


IRFAN ne yawatsama wani Irin kalo yace “wane irin rashin hankali ne Zaki shigomana babu Ko Salama ?”yafada Yana hararta ,wani Irin kishine yataso ma ,wata Irin tsanar Nasreen taji Wanda Bata taba jinshi ba tayaya wanan yarinyar zata Riga ta dokar ciki ina impossible tafada cikin ranta ,“kayi Hakuri Yaya IRFAN ban lura bane nayi TUNTUBE bara nakwashe ”tafada tana kokarin neman tsinstiya dan takwashe „

Nasreen kuwa da gudu tashige dakin ta bin bayan ta IRFAN yayi dan baiga alamar tayi farin ciki da wanan kyautar da Allah yabasu ba „


Nasreen kai bisa gadon ta tahaye tana kuka sosai Taya zata Doki ciki tun yanzu bayan Bata kare karatun ta ba ina bazai yuba zata gayamishi Bata so yacire mata har idan tacika burin ta zata yita haifa masa y'a y'an „


Tsaye IRFAN yayi Yana Binta da kalon mamaki danshi yakasa gane inda tadosa ,“kukan mi kike bayan abun farin ciki ne yasame mu ”yafada kai tsaye Yana kalon ta ,Bata dago ba hakan yasashi karasa wa bakin gadon yazauna talabota yayi tafado jikin shi kalon face dinta yayi yace “fadamun Angel Dina miye yake damunki ”yaje homata tanbayar kai tsaye „


“Yaya IRFAN please ka ciremun cikin nan Bana son shi yanzu ,ni karatu zanyi saina cika buri na ,dan Allah ka ciremun shi wallahi Bana so”tafada harda shasheka „


IRFAN kai bakaramin tashin hankali yashiga ba jin abunda take fada wanan wane Irin rashun hankali ne matsalar  Auren Yara Kenan danyan kai Ama bakomi zai rarashe ta cikin dane fushin sa yace“haba Nasfan Dina ai ni nace zanbarki kiyi karatun ko,to kiyi Hakuri ki haifa mana babyn mu inyaso shikenan harkiga karshen biro Babu matsala”irfan yafada Yana shafa cikin ta„„


Wani Irin zabura tayi ta tashi daga jikin shi tana birgima tace “wallahi nidai a'a bazan yardaba mafarin karatuna ya lalace ko”tayi maganar cikin kuka „

Yanzu kai tafara kaishi makura shima cikin hassala Wanda Bata taba ganinshi ba yace “kar Allah yasa ki hakura ,wallahi kinji na rantse wani abun yasamu cikin nan ki kuka dakanki ana rarashin ki kina botsarewa KO stupide kawai”yafada Yana hararn ta taré da jan tsaki yafuta Yana buga kofar dakarfi „


Karo na farko dayaji tabatamishi Rai tun Auren su aidama Auren yarinta Kenan ,part d'un Mufida yaje Wace tacika saura kadan tafashe „


Yana shigowa baiko kaleta ba yayi dakinshi wanka yashiga yasakar wa kanshi shower Yana tunanin abun yi karfa yarinyar nan dagaske take taje ta barar masa da cikin sa ,tsaki yaketaja Babu adadi Yana fitowa Yaga Mufida zaune cikin wasu damamun Kayan barcci ,tsaki yadan ja shima kayanshi yasaka tahaye gadon „


Ganin kamar Yana cikin fushi yasata hawa gadon tahaye jikinshi kamar wata mage bashida abunda yawuce yarungume ta KO zaiji sanyi arasnhi ,sajen shi tashiga shafawa cikin Salo da kissa tace “Namijin Duniya miye yasameka naga ranka abace Allah yasa dai lafiya ”tafada tana shafama masa kirjinshi da gasai sa suka kwanta lup lup gwanin sha'wa „


Tana da hakkin sanin damuwar shi hakan yasashi fadun “please kima kanwarki Magana karta ciremun cikin nan ina son shi sosai fiyeda komi aduniya ,yarinta nadamun ta kimata Magana wata kila ke zata saurareki ”yafada Yana sa bakinshi cikin nata ya tsotsa Itama mayar mishi tayi sunkai akala minti biyar sanan ta zare bakin ta ahankali tace “kayi Hakuri Yaya zanmata Magana kuma zata ji kurciya ce zata daina ,zan haifa maka dozin KO duk shekara kake so kaji ?”tafada cikin kissa „


IRFAN kai bakaramin dadi yaji ba dama shiyasa akace mata biyu sunada dadi ,idan wata tabata maka wata zata faranta maka ,gashidai Nasreen tabatamasa Rai sosai Ama yanzu dayazo wurin Mufida har zuciyar shi tayi sanyi ,kiss yasakar mata agoshi yace “Allah yamiki Albarka Nouroul Hayat ” wani Irin dadi ne yamamaye zuciyar Mufida kamar tazuba Ruwa akasa tasha Ashe dai Yana son ta ,wani lokacin inyayi wani Abu sai taga kamar Baya son ta ,wani lokacin kuma sai ta karyata hakan „


Kissing dinta yafara Itama tashiga mayar masa da Irin salon dayakoyamusu gabadaya kowace tana kokarin ganin ta hardace abunda yake yimusu shima yanajin dadin ganin basa kauyanci wurin abunda yafi so Wanda diyawan mata basayin shi ,matsalar shi Daya da Mufida da anfara zatace tagaji ahaka dai suka lula duniyar M'aura ta„


Da safe kamar KULUM Yana wuce ma sallaci sai Daya Biya Yaga kotatashi kuma Alhmdllah Yaji karan Ruwa yasan tana toilette,yanada wowa yashiga part d'un Mufida agagauce yayi wanka yashirya baiganta akithen ba Haka yasashi lekada dakin ta barcci yatarar tanayi hankali kwance kiss kawaii yasakar mata yasan ya wahalar da Ita jiya wajen round uku yayi yasan tayi kokari sosai ,kuma bakidamu ba dako tayi girki KO kartayi dan Yaga kwanan nan Nasreen Bata cin abinci koda kuwa itace ta girkasa ,Yana futa gidansu yabiya sanan yabiya gidan Abba yagaishesu Wanda yawanci yakanje Can dama ,agurguje yamusu Salama sai gidansu Iklas dan baije jan'iza ba ,hakan yasa yaje gidan Wanda har yazu cike yake damu tane gaishe Su yayi taré da Mika t'aziyar gaisuwar shi ga iyalan gidan yadan jima ,zai tafiya Mr Hashim yabishi abaya hade da fadun “mun gode sosai Doctor Allah yabiya ,maganar Yarinyar nan insha Allah zanshigar da kara har sai naga ankwatar mata yancinta ” murmushi IRFAN yasaki yace “kabar shi Allah shizai mata sakaya ”daga Haka ya mika mishi hannu sukayi musabaha shi kuma ya shiga motar shi ya ja sai asibitin ,duduba patience dinshi yayi daga nan ya wuce asibitin da ake kula da da yarinyar Yana zuwa Aka kaita wurinshi „


Tana ganinshi ta saki dariya hanun shi takama Wanda shima ba karamin mamaki ta bashi ba kasa ta nuna mishi ya zauna ,bai yimusu ba duk dakuwa Kayan dayake saké farare ne kuma wurin ma duk da ana tsabata ce shi ba zai rasa datti ba Amma Haka ya zauna murmushi tsakeyi tace “yawa ina Kayan kwaliyar dakace zaka somin ,kabani ni yaw bazan sha chocolat ba ”ta fada tana lalubar shi KO zata ga Kayan kwaliyar ,ba karamin mamaki yasha ba shi kuwa YAUSHE ma yazo jiya Kenan hakan Yana nufin akwai Wanda yazo wurin ta jiya kuma yamata alkawari harda wani Kayan kwaliya „


Murmushi yayi ya lakuce mata hanci yace “yi Hakuri my sweet sis ,Yayan ki yayi mantuwa Ama anjima zan kawo Miki ”ya fada Yana kalon ta ,Rai tabata taré da juyamishi Baya tace “ni dai katashi tunda kai baka cikin masu cika alkawari ,kuma Inna ta tafadamun cewa rashun cika alkawari yanda daga cikin alamomin Munafiki ”wani Irin dadi yaji ga Dukan alamu ana samun Nasarra tunda tafara tuno da Inna ta „


Hannun ta yakama yace “Ama kuma Allah yace Yana son masu tafiya KO ,da masu Hakuri shine zai biyasu yamusu sakaya mafi alkairi ”ya fada Yana kallon ta 

Dariya tayi har farar hakwaran ta suka bayana tace “shikenan nayafe maka ”ta fada yayin da take doko Teddy ta tafara yimata kitso „


“yawa kanwata fadamun kin cemin Innar ki ce ta miki duk wannan bayanin KO ,kin tuna da Ita KO ”ya fada Yana kallon ta danjin abunda zatace ,cikin yima Teddy tata kitso batare da ta kaleshi ba tace “Eh kamar ina tunanin akwai wata Wace nataba Kira da wanan sunan ”mikewa yayi batare da yace kala ba yayi waje hade da rufe kofar „„„


Wurin Doctor dake kula da'ita yaje dan Yana son Karin BAYANI sosai bisa Wanda yazo dubata har ya mata alkawari Kayan kwaliya Shiga offici d'un yayi bayan anbashi ou izini zama yayi Yana fadun “ina son Karin bayani bisa mutumin Daya zo wurin ta ”


“Ai Doctor inaga wanan yaron ne dukuka zo taré dashi mai kama dakai d'un ”kenan Al'moctar ne yazo dubata daga Haka bai saké cewa komi ba ya mike ,bai zarce KO ina sai gidanshi„


Ta 'bangaren Mufida kai Bata Farka ba sai kusan sha biyu wanka tayi tadan shahsre wurin tana karewa yadoki wayar ta ,Ummi takira Wace ta'amishi number Mufidan awurin ta asace ranar dasukaje gaida su Abbi ,sosai tahada da kissa wurin jawo hankalin Mufida Wace suke kokarin shirya plan dan ganin Sun kori Nasreen Ama Ummi Bata nuna kiyayar datake wa IRFAN ba duba dayanda take son shi , shiyasaka tasakawa Nasreen ido saosai dan ganin yanda zasu samu wata baraka a Tsakinta da MIJIN nata tahakane sukuma zasu ai watar da shirin su „


Magana sukayi akala minti goma sanan naga sunkashe wayar murmushi naga tanayi tayi part d'un Nasreen cikin wasu tsukakin Kaya „


Tana zuwa ta shiga kai tsaye , Nasreen na kwance a bisa doguwar kujera tana kalon wani américaine film taga mutum tsaye saman kanta ,ganin Mufida ce yasata tashi zaune tana kokarin gyara dikon ta tace “a'a Anty dakanki shigo ki zauna ”ya fada tana kalon ta  ,zama Mufida tayi cikin kissa tace “a'a kanwata kisha kwancinki lafiya ai angayamin abun Arzikin da muka samu kuma naji Ance kinuna bakya so haba kanwata abunda dubunan mutane suke nema ki kwantar da hankalinki zakiyi karatu namiki alkawari insha Allah ”ta fada tana kama hannun Nasreen d'un cikin kissa „


“ni fa Anty ba haihuwar bace Ba na so karatuna nake tsoro Allah ni dai ba zan haihu ba ”ta sake fad'a tana hawaye 
“kar Allah yasa ki haihu kuma maganar nan har gaban Abba ”Irfan ne mai wanan maganar Wanda shigowar shi Kenan yaje b'angaren Mufida bai ganta ba shiya saka yayo nan shine ya tare hirar da suke .......✍️

              ......✍sosai tashiga cikin tashin hankali ganin abun ƙaunar nata adamuwa „

„Iklas ta tafi Nasreen ,ina tausayawa iyayen ta ,haƙika abun tausayi ne ,inajin ta kamar  maman ”yafaɗa yana sauke ajiyar zuciya haɗi da shafa kanta „

“Allah yajikanta Allah yasa ta huta ,Allah yabawa iyayen ta haƙurin rashin ta ,hakika abun atausaya musu ne ,kayi haƙuri kaji ”Nasreen tafaɗa tana kallonshi „

Ɗan sajenshi dayasha gyara irin na ƴan gayu tashiga shafawa hannun ta yariƙe yana kallon cikin idon ta ,Bakinshi yakai kan nata yashiga sakar mata wani irin hot kiss ,itama mayar masa tashiga yi sosai tabashi haɗin kai ,Wanda yasa har yafara manta damuwar dayake ciki ,rigarta yaɗaga sama itama kayan tashiga ciremishi anan suka fara tsotse su har sai da sukayi saura daga su sai boxer Wanda ita kuma pant ɗin kaɗai yarage mata kasancewar dama yace tadaina saka brezia in suna tare tayadda daya zo basai yasha wahala ba „

Gabaɗaya sun fice a hayyacinsu dan yanzu Doctor yakoyama jarababbe babu salon dabai koyamata ba nashi „

Bakin ta tasaka takamo harshen shi tashiga gwadamasa itama yanzu tafishi kwarewa sunkutar ta yayi yatafi sai daƙin su cigaba yayi da tsotsar ta yana tsafata ya tsotse ta sanan yanemi hanyar shi dayake tsananin jin daɗin shigar ta wacce ako yaushe jiyakeyi kamar kara matse mishi ita akeyi ga daɗi dayakeji har cikin kwakwalwarshi ahaka yafara havin sex da ita „

Yau kai Doctor ba sauƙi domin bakaramin cin ta yakeyi ba Nasreen ma bakaramin daɗi take jiba sosai take bashi haɗin kai wurin kwasar gara ,sau biyu yana relasing Wanda a yanzu a ƙalla yakai awa biyu kuma cikin su sau biyu kawai yabar ta huta sai yaci gaba „

Wani irin riƙewa taji bayanta yayi kara tasaki mai karfi tana tureshi daga jikin ta amma ina gogan naku bai masan tanayi ba sai da ya ƙara minti talatin da biyar yayi relasing sauka yayi akanta yana sauke numfashi sumbatar ta yayi tare da rungume ta „

Nasreen kai ta galabaita iya galabaita cikin kuka tace “wayo Allah na wayo Mama kizo kitaimakeni ciki na ”tafaɗa tana riƙe ciki ,bai ɗauka dagaske abun haka yakeba yaɗauka dai shagwabar ta takeyi ,sai dayaga fah kukan dagaske takeyi tana riƙe ciki sanan yamike da Sauri a gagauce yayi wanka jallabiyar sa kawai yasaka kayan ta yasaka mata yasunkuce ta sai mota asibitin shi ya wuce da ita dan bakaramin ruɗani yashiga ba ganin Angel ɗinshi cikin wanan damuwar yana fatan Allah yasa dai ba wata matsalace babba ba „

Yana zuwa office ɗinshi yashiga da'ita sister Khadija ce ta biyoshi jinin ta yaɗiba yabata yace taje lab maza ta dubo masa yana jiran result ,allura yayi mata ko minti biyar ba'ayi ba yaga bacci yayi awon gaba da ita ,kyakywar face ɗinta yakala yaga yanda tayi fayau kamar ba itace tayi kwaliya dazuba har taɗan faɗa ,wani irin tausayin ta ne yaji yasan tana haƙuri sosai dashi dan dama itace mai ɗauke masa lalurar sa ,Mufida kai idan yaje da anfara zata fara kukan gajiya dole yakyale ta relasing ya tara sauran sai yazo wurin ta yake saukewa ,gashinta daya barbaje akan filon ya shiga shafawa haɗe da sumbatar kuncinta „


Sister Khadija ce ta kwankwasa shine yabata izinin shiga haɗe da result ɗin tamiƙo mishi ,tana fita yabuɗe file ɗin wani irin ihu yayi hade da faɗuwa kasa yana mai yin sujada da duk addu'ar datazo bakinshi ,abun dayake gani dagaskene shine zai zama uba ,shi Irfan kuma abar kaunar shi ce zata haifamishi shi wani irin taku yayi sai saman gadon rigar ta yadaga yana sunbayar ta haɗe da shafa cikin ,jiyake duk duniya babu wanda yakaishi farin ciki ayau duk da damuwar Rashin Iklas Nanan a cikin ranshi amma yasamu abun da yawargaza wanan ƙuncin dayake ciki „

Allah mai iko yaketa nanata wa haɗi da kai mata kiss tako ina Wanda harɗan motsawa takeyi amma kuma sai takoma sabida aluran baccin dayamata cikin sati biyu ashe shiɗin  jarumi ne ya'iya aiki „


Ta ɓangaren iyalan Iklas kuwa dadyn ta dakanshi taɗuki gawar ta batare da yayi sa kowa magana ba yasaka ta abayan mota suma su Alhaji Ibrahim binshi sukayi sai mahaifiyar Rumaisa da'akabari tare da ita koda zata farfaɗo ,

Bayan awa ɗaya tafarka cikin kuka take faɗin “haba Iklasi na kar kitafi kibarni ,ya kike so nayi da rayuwata ”tafaɗa hawaye na gangaro mata kuma idon ta arufe wanda ga dukan alamu mafarki takeyi „

Murmushi tasaki a lokaci ɗaya kuma hawaye na zubo mata tace “namiki alƙawari insha Allah zancika ”tafaɗa tana kara faɗaɗa murmushin ta „

Mahaifiyar tata ce ta riƙe hanun ta tana tofah mata addu'a dan taga alamar mafarki ƴar tata takeyi „

Kai tashiga girgiza kuma hawaye nabin fuskar ta tace “a'a a'a”dahaka ta tashi da Sauri zaune ,kanta tashiga dafewa wanda yake tsananin aara mata sai yanzu komi ke dawo mata akai mahaifiyar ta takalla dake damƙe da hanunta tana tofah mata addu'a tace “iklas ta tafi ko Mama dama nasani tafaɗamin bazata yi tsawon rai ba ”tafaɗa wasu hawaye na gangaro mata ,rungume ta tayi tana shafa bayan ta sunkai minti uku komi ta tuno kuma ta mike da Sauri tana faɗin “Mama tashi muje naga Yata nayi mata addu'a ,nayi mata ganin karshe ,kar akaita gidan ta nagaskiya batare da mungana ba ”tafaɗa tana neman mayafin ta da hanu tariƙeshi Mahaifiyar nata nabin ta abaya „

Adaidai ta suka ɗauka sai gidan su wanda yacika maƙil da jama'a dan rasuwar babu wanda bai jita ba family Ibrahim mai dallah ,ga malaman makarantar su da ɗalibai duk sunzo „

Rumaisa bata ma ganin gabanta bare ta lura da masu mata ta'aziya ,gidan tashiga har falo mutane ne ,ɗakin ta ta shiga wanda Hajiyar Ibrahim takama hanunta har ciki takai ta ,Mr Hashim tagani zaune yana karanto addu'a tare da tofa ma yar tasu dake kwance an rufeta da suturar da akewa kowa idan zaije gidan sa nagaskiya „
Dabas tafaɗi gaban gawar murmushi tasaki hade da kalon kyakyawa fuskar yar ta su wace kamar ka kirata ta amsa ,fuskar ta sai annuri takeyi har wani Kyau ta ƙara sumbatar ta tayi agoshi kasancewar dama fuskar ta abuɗe take tace “Iklasi na nayafemiki ,baki taɓa ɓatamun da rai ba ,Allah ne shaida ,ina ƙaunar ki yaya ta Allah yasa can tafiye miki nan ina fatan Allah yayi gagawa ɗaukar rayuwa ta dan naje natarar dake mu rayu acan ,”tafaɗa tana murmushi wanan karon kai harda hawaye a fuskar ta kara sumbatar ta tayi haɗe da jan mayafin ta rufe mata fuska „

Baƙaramin mamaki tabawa Mr Hashim ba ,hakan yana nufin tasa wa kanta salama Kenan ,baiyi tunani ba ,baiyi tsamani ba ,Ashe dama Rumaisa zata iya haƙuri rashin yar tata har haka ,Alhaji Ibrahim ne yashigo da wasu mutum huɗu akasaka ta makara ,Mr Hashim dai yana zaune a wurin kwata -kwata jiyakeyi kamar ba shi ba shikenan duniyar kenan rayuwa dama batada tabass „

Rumaisa ce ta rungume shi da ƙarfi tana rufe idon ta wani irin kuka ne yazo mishi ,yasakeshi da ƙarfi Wanda yasa Rumaisa ƙara ririƙe shi tana sauke ajiyar zuciya dan bata taɓa ganin Mijin nata awannan halin ba ,kuka yakeyi kamar ƙaramin yaro harda shassheƙa tana tso ta rarashi mijin ta amma itama tana buƙatar mai rarashin ,sunkai minti ashirin ahaka sannan Alhaji Ibrahim yashigo yasamesu ahaka rungume da juna ga Rumaisa da kifa fuskar ta aƙirjinshi ,shikuwa sai kuka yakeyi wanda yanzu yakoma shasheƙa kamar ba namiji ba ,“Haba Hashim dakanka ,kai dayakamata ace ka rarashi matar ka kaine kake kuka agabanta haba, kuyiwa yarinyar ku addu'a mana ku kyautata mata zato ,sai dai muyi fatan tamu tazo da Kyau ,tunɗazu mukejira Mutafi bunneta  amma kana zamanka bayan gatan da zaka nuna mata shine kasata da hannun ka a cikin gidan ta nagaskiya ”Alhaji Ibrahim ne mai wanan maganar ,yana kallon ɗan nasa dayake ta kuka „

Rumaisa ce ta Miƙe kamar zata faɗi ta shiga toilette ,shikuwa mahaifin shine yakamashi yatafi dashi ,ankai Iklas gidan ta nagaskiya Wanda duk inda aka Budé ruwa ne ake gani aramin da aka tona kuma ruwan irin masu sanyin nan ne ,so uku ana tonawa ana ganin haka ana hlun ne malamin yace asakata haka wannan rahama ce ta ubangiji dayayi mata „

Aikuwa haka Mahaifin ta dakanshi yasakata bayan angama bunneta ne Malam ya shiga yima mutane wa'azi haɗe da nuna musu wanan ishara kaɗai ta ishesu su na kyautata ma yarinyar zato insha Allah dahaka yayi nasiha sosai Wanda ta ratsa mutanan wurin sosai harda masu kuka ,bayan kowa ya watse akabar Mr Hashim Wanda yajima yanama yar tasa ad'u'a sosai sanan yamike ya shiga mota yakama hanyar gida yana mai tunanin rayuwa shikenan fah yanzu Iklas dinshi ta tafi sai dai Allah yajikanta da rahama „


Ta ɓangaren Doctor kuwa nan take yakira Samir yafaɗamishi Wanda baƙaramin murna yatayashi ba ,Irfan ɗin ne yace “To inkaji haushi dai kayi Auren ko sai mun Haïfa mun baka ?”yafaɗa cikin zolaya dariya suka saki suduka sanan

Ne Samir yace “Allah yasawake nayita zama har kwan naku yakoma gudan jini har ahaifeshi yagirma sanan nikuma Samir na Aura impossible ”yafada ya fashewa da dariya ,shima Irfan dun dariya yayi daganan yakshe wayar „

Nasreen kai kwanan ta tasha sai karfe bayan Magrib tafarka tare da bude idon ta tana kalon saman dakin kanta ne taji yamata wani irin nauyi sanan ,Irfan ne yashigo yana ganin tana kokarin mikewa yataimaka mata ta zauna rungume abar sa yayi yana fadun “fatan dai ba inda yakemiki ciwo ko baby na ”yafada yana kalon ta ,kanta ta nunamishi dake dan Sara mata hakan yasa yafahimci Kenan kanta ne ke ciwo ,

Kayansu ya fara tatarawa saida yatatara komi ya zo daukar ta yayi kamar baby ya'aza kafada sai cikin mota duk da kuwa kalon da mutane kemusu bai damu ba ,tuki yakeyi yana sakin murmushi ganin haka yasa ta tace “haba wai miyene naga kanata kalo na kana murmushi ,bayan kamar bakaine naga kana hawaye dazuba ,kuma ma ni miye yake damuna ?”tafada cikin shagwaba har hawaye sun fara zubo mata „

Yasan dama Zaisha fama da rigima “wani farin ciki ne nake ciki mara misaltuwa Wanda nasan kema inkika jishi sai kin tayani murna ,kuma abun da kedamunki yanada nasaba da farin ciki na kedai karkice komi sai munje gida ”yafada yana kamo hanun ta yasumbata ,bata kara magana ba harsai da sukaje gida part dinta ya wuce da ita wanka yayi mata dakanshi yasaka mata kaya hanun ta yakamo sai falo kalon ta yayi yace “fadamun me kike son ci ”dan satar kalonshi tayi sanan tayamutsa fuska tace “Nidai kafadamun miyene wai ”tafada tana hawaye ,hawayen ya shiga lashewa yace “Albishirinki ?” dan yamutsa fuska tayi sanan tace “goro ” 

“Fari ko ja ?”yakara tanbayar ta yanzu kai tara kai wa makura ama ta d'Aure tace “fari ”

“kinaɗauke da ciki na sati biyu zamu zama iyaye muma insha Allah ina cikin farin ciki ”yafada yana shafa cikin ta „

Kass kakass kakeji karan faduwar kof ,gabadaya suka juya suna kalon wanene wanan ......✍🏻
