PAGE 69&70
            ........✍️Mufida ce tsaye Wace tazo duba KO lafiya har yanzu bai shigo ba kuma taga dare nayi bayan taji motsin zawar shi cikin mota ,shine tana shigowa taji IRFAN nafadawa Nasreen karuwa da sukasamu dama tafe take tana kurba thé dinta shine jin abunda yafada yasata yarda cof glass din yafashe„


IRFAN ne yawatsama wani Irin kalo yace “wane irin rashin hankali ne Zaki shigomana babu Ko Salama ?”yafada Yana hararta ,wani Irin kishine yataso ma ,wata Irin tsanar Nasreen taji Wanda Bata taba jinshi ba tayaya wanan yarinyar zata Riga ta dokar ciki ina impossible tafada cikin ranta ,“kayi Hakuri Yaya IRFAN ban lura bane nayi TUNTUBE bara nakwashe ”tafada tana kokarin neman tsinstiya dan takwashe „

Nasreen kuwa da gudu tashige dakin ta bin bayan ta IRFAN yayi dan baiga alamar tayi farin ciki da wanan kyautar da Allah yabasu ba „


Nasreen kai bisa gadon ta tahaye tana kuka sosai Taya zata Doki ciki tun yanzu bayan Bata kare karatun ta ba ina bazai yuba zata gayamishi Bata so yacire mata har idan tacika burin ta zata yita haifa masa y'a y'an „


Tsaye IRFAN yayi Yana Binta da kalon mamaki danshi yakasa gane inda tadosa ,“kukan mi kike bayan abun farin ciki ne yasame mu ”yafada kai tsaye Yana kalon ta ,Bata dago ba hakan yasashi karasa wa bakin gadon yazauna talabota yayi tafado jikin shi kalon face dinta yayi yace “fadamun Angel Dina miye yake damunki ”yaje homata tanbayar kai tsaye „


“Yaya IRFAN please ka ciremun cikin nan Bana son shi yanzu ,ni karatu zanyi saina cika buri na ,dan Allah ka ciremun shi wallahi Bana so”tafada harda shasheka „


IRFAN kai bakaramin tashin hankali yashiga ba jin abunda take fada wanan wane Irin rashun hankali ne matsalar  Auren Yara Kenan danyan kai Ama bakomi zai rarashe ta cikin dane fushin sa yace“haba Nasfan Dina ai ni nace zanbarki kiyi karatun ko,to kiyi Hakuri ki haifa mana babyn mu inyaso shikenan harkiga karshen biro Babu matsala”irfan yafada Yana shafa cikin ta„„


Wani Irin zabura tayi ta tashi daga jikin shi tana birgima tace “wallahi nidai a'a bazan yardaba mafarin karatuna ya lalace ko”tayi maganar cikin kuka „

Yanzu kai tafara kaishi makura shima cikin hassala Wanda Bata taba ganinshi ba yace “kar Allah yasa ki hakura ,wallahi kinji na rantse wani abun yasamu cikin nan ki kuka dakanki ana rarashin ki kina botsarewa KO stupide kawai”yafada Yana hararn ta taré da jan tsaki yafuta Yana buga kofar dakarfi „


Karo na farko dayaji tabatamishi Rai tun Auren su aidama Auren yarinta Kenan ,part d'un Mufida yaje Wace tacika saura kadan tafashe „


Yana shigowa baiko kaleta ba yayi dakinshi wanka yashiga yasakar wa kanshi shower Yana tunanin abun yi karfa yarinyar nan dagaske take taje ta barar masa da cikin sa ,tsaki yaketaja Babu adadi Yana fitowa Yaga Mufida zaune cikin wasu damamun Kayan barcci ,tsaki yadan ja shima kayanshi yasaka tahaye gadon „


Ganin kamar Yana cikin fushi yasata hawa gadon tahaye jikinshi kamar wata mage bashida abunda yawuce yarungume ta KO zaiji sanyi arasnhi ,sajen shi tashiga shafawa cikin Salo da kissa tace “Namijin Duniya miye yasameka naga ranka abace Allah yasa dai lafiya ”tafada tana shafama masa kirjinshi da gasai sa suka kwanta lup lup gwanin sha'wa „


Tana da hakkin sanin damuwar shi hakan yasashi fadun “please kima kanwarki Magana karta ciremun cikin nan ina son shi sosai fiyeda komi aduniya ,yarinta nadamun ta kimata Magana wata kila ke zata saurareki ”yafada Yana sa bakinshi cikin nata ya tsotsa Itama mayar mishi tayi sunkai akala minti biyar sanan ta zare bakin ta ahankali tace “kayi Hakuri Yaya zanmata Magana kuma zata ji kurciya ce zata daina ,zan haifa maka dozin KO duk shekara kake so kaji ?”tafada cikin kissa „


IRFAN kai bakaramin dadi yaji ba dama shiyasa akace mata biyu sunada dadi ,idan wata tabata maka wata zata faranta maka ,gashidai Nasreen tabatamasa Rai sosai Ama yanzu dayazo wurin Mufida har zuciyar shi tayi sanyi ,kiss yasakar mata agoshi yace “Allah yamiki Albarka Nouroul Hayat ” wani Irin dadi ne yamamaye zuciyar Mufida kamar tazuba Ruwa akasa tasha Ashe dai Yana son ta ,wani lokacin inyayi wani Abu sai taga kamar Baya son ta ,wani lokacin kuma sai ta karyata hakan „


Kissing dinta yafara Itama tashiga mayar masa da Irin salon dayakoyamusu gabadaya kowace tana kokarin ganin ta hardace abunda yake yimusu shima yanajin dadin ganin basa kauyanci wurin abunda yafi so Wanda diyawan mata basayin shi ,matsalar shi Daya da Mufida da anfara zatace tagaji ahaka dai suka lula duniyar M'aura ta„


Da safe kamar KULUM Yana wuce ma sallaci sai Daya Biya Yaga kotatashi kuma Alhmdllah Yaji karan Ruwa yasan tana toilette,yanada wowa yashiga part d'un Mufida agagauce yayi wanka yashirya baiganta akithen ba Haka yasashi lekada dakin ta barcci yatarar tanayi hankali kwance kiss kawaii yasakar mata yasan ya wahalar da Ita jiya wajen round uku yayi yasan tayi kokari sosai ,kuma bakidamu ba dako tayi girki KO kartayi dan Yaga kwanan nan Nasreen Bata cin abinci koda kuwa itace ta girkasa ,Yana futa gidansu yabiya sanan yabiya gidan Abba yagaishesu Wanda yawanci yakanje Can dama ,agurguje yamusu Salama sai gidansu Iklas dan baije jan'iza ba ,hakan yasa yaje gidan Wanda har yazu cike yake damu tane gaishe Su yayi taré da Mika t'aziyar gaisuwar shi ga iyalan gidan yadan jima ,zai tafiya Mr Hashim yabishi abaya hade da fadun “mun gode sosai Doctor Allah yabiya ,maganar Yarinyar nan insha Allah zanshigar da kara har sai naga ankwatar mata yancinta ” murmushi IRFAN yasaki yace “kabar shi Allah shizai mata sakaya ”daga Haka ya mika mishi hannu sukayi musabaha shi kuma ya shiga motar shi ya ja sai asibitin ,duduba patience dinshi yayi daga nan ya wuce asibitin da ake kula da da yarinyar Yana zuwa Aka kaita wurinshi „


Tana ganinshi ta saki dariya hanun shi takama Wanda shima ba karamin mamaki ta bashi ba kasa ta nuna mishi ya zauna ,bai yimusu ba duk dakuwa Kayan dayake saké farare ne kuma wurin ma duk da ana tsabata ce shi ba zai rasa datti ba Amma Haka ya zauna murmushi tsakeyi tace “yawa ina Kayan kwaliyar dakace zaka somin ,kabani ni yaw bazan sha chocolat ba ”ta fada tana lalubar shi KO zata ga Kayan kwaliyar ,ba karamin mamaki yasha ba shi kuwa YAUSHE ma yazo jiya Kenan hakan Yana nufin akwai Wanda yazo wurin ta jiya kuma yamata alkawari harda wani Kayan kwaliya „


Murmushi yayi ya lakuce mata hanci yace “yi Hakuri my sweet sis ,Yayan ki yayi mantuwa Ama anjima zan kawo Miki ”ya fada Yana kalon ta ,Rai tabata taré da juyamishi Baya tace “ni dai katashi tunda kai baka cikin masu cika alkawari ,kuma Inna ta tafadamun cewa rashun cika alkawari yanda daga cikin alamomin Munafiki ”wani Irin dadi yaji ga Dukan alamu ana samun Nasarra tunda tafara tuno da Inna ta „


Hannun ta yakama yace “Ama kuma Allah yace Yana son masu tafiya KO ,da masu Hakuri shine zai biyasu yamusu sakaya mafi alkairi ”ya fada Yana kallon ta 

Dariya tayi har farar hakwaran ta suka bayana tace “shikenan nayafe maka ”ta fada yayin da take doko Teddy ta tafara yimata kitso „


“yawa kanwata fadamun kin cemin Innar ki ce ta miki duk wannan bayanin KO ,kin tuna da Ita KO ”ya fada Yana kallon ta danjin abunda zatace ,cikin yima Teddy tata kitso batare da ta kaleshi ba tace “Eh kamar ina tunanin akwai wata Wace nataba Kira da wanan sunan ”mikewa yayi batare da yace kala ba yayi waje hade da rufe kofar „„„


Wurin Doctor dake kula da'ita yaje dan Yana son Karin BAYANI sosai bisa Wanda yazo dubata har ya mata alkawari Kayan kwaliya Shiga offici d'un yayi bayan anbashi ou izini zama yayi Yana fadun “ina son Karin bayani bisa mutumin Daya zo wurin ta ”


“Ai Doctor inaga wanan yaron ne dukuka zo taré dashi mai kama dakai d'un ”kenan Al'moctar ne yazo dubata daga Haka bai saké cewa komi ba ya mike ,bai zarce KO ina sai gidanshi„


Ta 'bangaren Mufida kai Bata Farka ba sai kusan sha biyu wanka tayi tadan shahsre wurin tana karewa yadoki wayar ta ,Ummi takira Wace ta'amishi number Mufidan awurin ta asace ranar dasukaje gaida su Abbi ,sosai tahada da kissa wurin jawo hankalin Mufida Wace suke kokarin shirya plan dan ganin Sun kori Nasreen Ama Ummi Bata nuna kiyayar datake wa IRFAN ba duba dayanda take son shi , shiyasaka tasakawa Nasreen ido saosai dan ganin yanda zasu samu wata baraka a Tsakinta da MIJIN nata tahakane sukuma zasu ai watar da shirin su „


Magana sukayi akala minti goma sanan naga sunkashe wayar murmushi naga tanayi tayi part d'un Nasreen cikin wasu tsukakin Kaya „


Tana zuwa ta shiga kai tsaye , Nasreen na kwance a bisa doguwar kujera tana kalon wani américaine film taga mutum tsaye saman kanta ,ganin Mufida ce yasata tashi zaune tana kokarin gyara dikon ta tace “a'a Anty dakanki shigo ki zauna ”ya fada tana kalon ta  ,zama Mufida tayi cikin kissa tace “a'a kanwata kisha kwancinki lafiya ai angayamin abun Arzikin da muka samu kuma naji Ance kinuna bakya so haba kanwata abunda dubunan mutane suke nema ki kwantar da hankalinki zakiyi karatu namiki alkawari insha Allah ”ta fada tana kama hannun Nasreen d'un cikin kissa „


“ni fa Anty ba haihuwar bace Ba na so karatuna nake tsoro Allah ni dai ba zan haihu ba ”ta sake fad'a tana hawaye 
“kar Allah yasa ki haihu kuma maganar nan har gaban Abba ”Irfan ne mai wanan maganar Wanda shigowar shi Kenan yaje b'angaren Mufida bai ganta ba shiya saka yayo nan shine ya tare hirar da suke .......✍️

BOOK 1
PAGE 73&74
            ........✍️Mufida kai yanzu ta fara koyon girki kuma Alhmdllah ta kware sosai harma da wasu abubuwan sosai tashiga gyara kanta da abubuwan da ake koya musu ,kuma ba laifi yanzu tana kokarin jure jure lalurar Mijin nata shima kuma yaji dadi sosai dan yanzu dadin ta yakeji sosai ,yaji kwanan nan kuma test dinta yacanja har ma taso tafi Nasreen Yana zargin Anya bama ciki ne da'ita ba kuwa Ama zai jira LOKACI ne zai nuna musu Haka „


Danshi yasa yau ma yabaro Nasreen dan yakwashi gara Mufida dan dama girkin tane ,sosai kuwa ya gurzata sai Daya cita Iya cinsa sanan yasara ra mata ,shima yayi musu girki yaw komi ma ganin bakaramin wahala yabata ba Magrub nayi yaje gidan su Nasreen yadokota wace yanzu cikin ta yakai wata biyu Amma su Ammi basu sani ba kasancewar hijab takesawa tana karewa shima kuma bai fadamusu ba yafiso yayi musu surprise Randa zasugani da idonsu zasufi farin ciki „„„


Ta bangaren Mara lafiyar IRFAN kuwa yanzu jiki yayi sauki dan tafara dawowa dai dai dan sotari tana kuka tana Kiran Inna ta Wanda inba Al'moctar ne yazo ba bazatayi shiru ba,yanzu harma tafi sabawa da Al'moctar d'un bisa Doctor IRFAN„„„




Nasreen ce zaune cikin falon ta tana cin dan wake da yaji dan tana cin yaji sosai danma DOCTOR Yana hanamata intaga bayan sai tayita cin abunta ,citake tana karawa hade da Shan yaji wani Irin banko kofah da'akayi batasan sanda ta tsure ba hade da Kiran “innallilahi”tana kokarin mikewa „„„


IRFAN ne yashigo fuskar nan tashi tayi jajur idonshi ma Haka kamar wani namijin Zaki yashigo hannun sa rike da wata kyakyawar Baby wace akala bazata wuce shekaru shida ba ,kyakyawa ce sosai sai dai ba za'akirata da fara ba kamar yanda baza'akirata da baka ba „„



Wani Irin faduwar gaba Nasreen taji Wanda tunda take Bata taba yin irinshi ba sosai taga yarinyar Tamata kama da IRFAN kamar antsaga kara komi nasu iri Daya sai dai Ita wanan Bata kaishi fariba „„


Tsab takarewa yarinyar kalo wace sai Share hawayen ta takeyi tana dan ja dabaya dabaya ganin Irin kalon da Nasreen kemata Wanda KO kyabtawa batayi „„„


Sosai Nasreen tashiga cikin tashin hankali gurum taji Ankara banko kofar anshigo Bakowa bace face Mufida wace dan kwalin ta yake ahannu sai Jakarta ma dateke hannun ta ,kanta duk ya hargitse kamar wata sabon kamu sai kuka take ,cikin falon tashigo tana fadun “Dan Allah Yaya IRFAN kayi Hakuri laifi nane laifi nane nasan bancanci kayafemun ba Ama kadan saurareni kaji dan Allah kayi Hakuri ”tafada tana durkushewa tsakar falon „„


Nasreen kai sororo tayi tana kalon Mufida wace yakoma kamar wata sabon kamu ,“Dan Allah kayi Hakuri wayo ni kaina nashiga uku ina neman tonawa kaina asiri ”tafada tana mai mikewa taje bakin kofar tashi tana bubugawa Ama duk Banza ,ganin hakn bazai fisheta ba yasata mikewa da gudu kuma tayi part dinta tana kuka „„„


Nasreen dai yar kalo takoma to Miyake faruwa kuma ga Dukan alamu akwai gagrumar matsala “innalilahi”tashiga maimaitawa dabasss takoma ta zauna tamaida hankalin ta akan yarinyar sosai sanye take cikin wata yar rigar kanti Iya guiwa mai shudin fari da blue sai takalmin ta masu dan tudu da karamin hijab dinta sosai tayi kyau yarinyar„„„


Kanta tahada da guiwar ta sai kuka take ahankali kusan minti ishirin Nasreen tamike kitchen tashiga ta wanke hanun ta yada tabar yarinyar Haka ta tarar da Ita „„„


Wurin ta Takara sa dukawa tayi hanun yarinyar ta banbare daga duken datayi ,kalon cikin idon yarinyar take wace Itama kalon ta takeyi sai shasheka takeyi ,murmushi tasakar mata hawaye tashiga sharema yarinyar ,Itama yarinyar kamar jira take ta rungume Nasreen d'un sosai ta lafe akirjin ta ,duk da kuwa yanda zuciyar Nasreen ke Jarabawa hakan bai yabata karba yarinyar ba Itama ta rungume bayan ta tashiga bubuga wa hakan yasa yarinyar yin shiru „„


Fuskar yarinyar tadago ta keleta taré da sakar mata murmushi tace “kidaina kuka ai beauty bata kuka ”itama yarinyar murmushi tayi Wanda yakara mata kyau tace “Anty kicewa uncle IRFAN yamaidani wurin Anty Nafissa duk da tana dukana kuma Bata min dariya Ama ina son ta ”yarinyar tafada tana kalon ta „„


Murmushi Nasreen tayi tace “shikenan yanzu Tom kidaina kuka ko sanan bara nakuna Miki carton kiyi kalo kinji beauty”tafada tana mai kama hannun ta tadora saman kujerar ,hade da kunan mata TV tasa mata cartoon d'un aikuwa murmushi tasaki taré da maida hankalin ta kan TV d'un „


Nasreen kai dakin ta ta wuce ta Doro alwala ganin Magrub tayi wanka tayi tasaka wasu Kayan tashafe jikin ta da turaruka tagabatar da sallah bayan takarasa ne taga yarinyar tamike tace “Anty ina son nayi al'wala nayi sallah naga kinshiga daki kuma ba kyau shiga dakin mutum batare da izinin shi ba ”tafada tana karasowa kusan Nasreen d'un ,murmushi kawai Nasreen tayi tareda kama yarinyar takaita toilette dinta bakaramin mamaki tasha ba ganin tayi alwala dai dai salaya ta shinfida mata aikuwa sallar ma dai dai tayita „


Nasreen kai zaune tayi tana lazumi har Aka Kira isha'i Ita ma yarinyar zama tayi tana tataba yatsunta alamar lazumin take Tare suka kabarta isha'i suna karewa Nasreen tayo falon dan kota na tunanin baije massalaci ba ,tana zaune yazo wuce wa dawowar sa daga massalaci har yanzu fuskar shi adaure bai KO kalli Nasreen d'un ba yayi gaba abunshi dakinshi yakoma ta kule „

Nasreen da kalo tabi bayanshi tana fatan koma minene yake damun Mijin nata yazo masa da sauki ,yarinyar tajawo suka dawo falon danwaken tasaka mata ,kai yarinyar ta girgiza alamar a'a Nasreen ce tace “bakya son cin wanan ”kai ta girgiza alamar batajin yunwa ,sobo tazuba mata shima dai kurba kawai tayi kalo takuna cartoon Nasreen kai sai satar kalon yarinyar takeyi „„„„


Wajen karfe goma har barcci yayi awon gaba da Ita ,dokar ta Nasreen tayi dakin ta takaita ta Dora saman gadon hade Dayi mata addu'a tashafa mata ,bargo taja mata takashe wutar dakin falon takoma tayi tunanin binshi Ama gara ta kyaleshi koma minene inyayi wari zataji Ama tashiga damuwar sosai da ganin halin da MIJIN nata take ciki anan tayi kwanciyar bisa doguwar kujera tajima kafin barcci yayi awon gaba da Ita „„„



IRFAN kai tunda yashiga Daki bayan isha'i bai leko ba kwance yake bisa makeken gadonshi kanshi na kalon saman Rufin dakin ,tunani yashiga yina rayuwa sosai hankalin shi yake tashe yanzu shi menene Mafita mai yasa Mufida zata masa Haka ,ya su ABBi zasuji ,ya su Abba zasu dokeshi ,wane Irin kalo Duniya zatamishi ,bama wanan ba uwa uba Nasreen dinshi yasan baimata adalci ba sam zai Iya rasa komi nashi Ama bazai so yarasa Nasreen dinshi ba itace komi nashi yatabka Baban kuskure Wanda kulum yake rokon Allah Daya yafemasa yasan Allahou gafuru Rahim ne ......✍️

BOOK 1
PAGE 71&72
            ........✍️ IRFAN ya fada yana mai watsa mata harara baki ta murguda masa cikin gunguni tace “to ai sai nagani in cikin a jikin ka yake ba ”ita ma ta fada tana mikewa tsaye dan barin wurin ganin Irin kalon da yake mata „„


Wani Irin tafarfasa yaji ransa namasa kamar ya babaudeta yakeji har zai bita Mufida ta rike shi cikin Kissa tace “Haba Jarumin Maza kayi Hakuri mana a hankali zamu bita,idan mukace zamu bita da garage abun bazai yi kyau ba ,kayi Hakuri Kar ka fadawa su Abba insha Allah kabani kwana biyu zata dawo dai dai insha Allah ”ta fada tana kai masa kiss „„

Ajiyar zuciya ya sauke koba komai yaji sanyi a Ranshi ,daukar ta yayi kamar baby suka wuce part dinta dan dama Ita yazo nema ,Mufida ansamu abunda akeso sai kara lafewa datayi ajikin shi „„„


Yana zuwa part dinshi ya dauketa sai murmushi take yi shima murmushi yake yasakar mata Yana fadun “bara naje nayi wanka ” ahaka taimaka masa yayi wanka duk dakuwa yaso suyi wanka Ama ta nuna bazata Iya saké wani wanka ba yanzu ,Yana fitowa yasaka jalabiyar sa abinci yaci Wanda har yaznu dai aslw ,yasa annemo mata makaranyar koyon girkin jibi zata fara zuwa „„


Ga gobe ma kafin ya wuce aiki sai dayaje duba Nasreen koda tagaishe shima ciki ciki ya'ansa ,itakuwa goganyar KO ajikin ta sai tabe baki datayi tace “Allah yatsare hanya”daga haka taja bakin ta tayi shiru


Shikuwa mamaki take bashi wato dai har yanzu tana kan bakanta KO babu komi zai bawa Mufidan kwana biyu Yaga ni dan yabata matsayin ta na Uwar gidan shi,idan Babu wani canji zai Doki Mumunan mataki akan haka„„„


IRFAN n'a futa Mufida taje bangaren Nasreen wace ta isko kwance sai tana Waya ga Dukan alamu da kanan nata take Waya takai minti biyar sanan takashe wayar tana murmushi takali Mufidan tace “Aya sorry My Anty na barki ke Daya KO kiyi Hakuri please ”tafada tana hada hanu almar roko „„„


Kalon ta Mufida tayi tace“haba bakomi kanwata fatan kina lafiya ya babyn mu ”tafada tana kalon cikin Nasreen d'un Wanda yadan tasa ga alama zatayi baban ciki sosai „


Fuska ta Bata tace “Allah Anty ni Bana son cikin nan ”tafada tana harar cikin „„„


Cikin ranta Mufida tace“sha sha kawai ai Zaki gane inkina son shi KO bakya son shi ”afili kuwa sai cewa tayi “kinaji ko matso nabaki shawara ”tafada tana kai kunan ta dai dai Na Nasreen ,bansa miyafada mata ba naga tayi murmushi tace “kuma fah hakane Ashe Anty na nagode sosai kuma zanbashi Hakuri insha Allah”tafada tana shafa cikin ,wani harar Mufida tazabga mata Ama Bata Bari tagani ba tawaske tace“yawa kokefah yanzu tashi nakama Miki sauran aikin naga bakiyi ba ”babu yanda Nasreen batayi da Ita ba Tabari Ama tace sam Bata yarda ba ahaka taka ma mata sukayi komi godiya sosai Nasreen tayi mata tana yaba kirkinta duk da so Daya ta taba Bata mata Rai Ama shikenan ta yafe mata kuma tunda ta sauko zata bita sau da kafa su hade kansu „„„


Kamar kulum yau ma koda yadawo Bangaren Mufida yaje kota kan Nasreen baibi ba yakama gabanshi sukasha soyaya su da MUFIDA wanda har yanzu baiji Yana kaunar ta ba Yana dai kokarin ganin yasauke nauyin dake kanshi na Matar shi kuma Yaga tana kokari dasu duk data fahimci yafi son Nasreen Ama har yanzu Bata nuna masa ba ,sabida shine shima yake kokarin Yaga yabata farin ciki koda kaso talatin ne acikin Dari „„

Yau ma suna tashi sai dayaje yaduba lafiyar Ta Yaga tamayi sallah sai cikin ta dayake dan jujiyawa duk dagashi KO Wata baiyi ba zai mata scanning yagani miye aciki insha Allah „„„


Wurin aiki yaje Ama sai dayaje yagaida Iyayen nashi harda su Abba Wanda yanzu yasa anfara Gina musu sabon gida ba taré da sunsani ba kuma shawara Abbi ce ,sanan yafadawa dan uwanshi Mahaifin Mufida kasancewar shima danashi Arzikin baiji haushi ba sai fatan aklairi da yayi kuma har millions biyu yanada akara Babu yanda Abbi baiyi dashi ba yace aishi dan Allah yayi godiya yamasa sosai ,shima kuma yakira IRFAN yafada masa Wanda bakaramin dadi yaji ba kuma yaji ya kara kaunar uncle din nashi shima dakanshi yakira shi yama godiya ,shidai cewa yayi bakomi yiwa kaine kuma dama suna gaisawa da Momyn Mufida ne kawai basa gaisawa „„„


Wurin aikin shi ya wuce bai dawo ba sai karfe 20 na dare yana dawo ya wuce massalaci ,Yana dawo yaje part d'un Mufida yaduba ta ,sanan yaje na Nasreen kamar ko yaushe wutar akshe kuma dakin sai kamshin dadi yakeyi dakinshi ya wuce wanka yayi ,sanan yasaka jalabiyar sa computer shi yadoko yaci gaba da aikin shi ,dan baiga abunda zai nema wurin Nasreen d'un ba „„


Nasreen kai duk taga lokacin da yashigo wato bazai nemeta ba KO,wanka taci tsaka humra ta mai sanyi kanshi wasu damamun Kaya Kaya tasaka Wanda suka dameta sosai Ita karan kanta sai dataji kunya ,Ama Haka ta daure kule dakin tayi ,dakin tashiga direct„„„


IRFAN kai yaji duk da yajin kanshi humra datake sawa hakan baisa yajuyo ba „„„

Nasreen kai tayarda Mijin ta fushi yake da Ita sosai ,hakan yasa taje bayanshi ta rungumeshi kirjin ta tashiga shafamishi ahankali,gogan naku kuwa sosai yafara hawa network dama yayi kewa Ama sai yadake yashare Yana so yanuna mata kuskuren shi „„„


Nasreen kai ganin Yaki kulata yasata kwantawa a kirjinshi gabadaya ,hanun ta ya banbare daga rikon data mishi yadoke computer shi yadawo falo yaci gaba da aikin shi „„


Nasreen kai taso tayi fushi Ama tadane tabi bayan shi tana Harare Harare falon tadawo computer Daya aza 'saman table tadoke tace “haba dan Allah wai miyene kake ta fushi kayi Hakuri to bazan saké ba ”tafada tana chagwabe fuska hade da kwantawa saman kirjin shi ,kanshi yakawar gefe Bata Reda yasa hannu ya kamota ba yace “please tashi ”yafada a hankali dan Baya son yima ta tsawa bare asamu matsala ,boturan jalabiyar gabansa tashiga budewa tarike hannun ta Yana girgiza mata kai „„„


Yanzu kai ta yarda yayi fushi sosai ,Bata kara sa tunanin ta ba taji yadoketa ya'aje saman kujera yadoki computer shi yakoma daki Yana shiga yahaye gadon yayi addu'a taré da jan bargo ya lulube ,kashe wutar dakin yayi ya lumshe idonshi duk dakuwa yanda yake son jin ta ajikin shi Ama zai hakura sai tagane kuranta dan Kar tasake aikata kuskure Irin wanan „„

Nasreen kai yanzu hawaye take ta tuno maganar Mama datace mata ,Kar takuskura ta kwanta Mijin ta Yana fushi da Ita gashi ita har sunyi kwana biyu ma basa magana yanzu Ita yazatayi binshi tayi dakin tareda kuna wutar dakin ,tasan da dama idan takuna doli zai bude idonshi dan Baya son barcci acikin basket ,ganin kusan minti Daya Bata kashe wutar ba yasa shi bude idonshi bai kaleta ba yace “malama kije dakinki ki kwanta kikashemun wutar dakin ”wasu hawaye ne suka sulalo mata Ashe dagaske yayi fushin tunda gashi har baya son jin muryata ,kashe wutar dakin ahankali ta haw gadon yazama na suna fuskantar juna dan Itama yanzu tana daga kwancen ne „

Ganin Haka yasashi juya mata Baya ,wasu hawaye ne sukara sulalo mata kuka tafarayi har da shasheka tana kalon keyar shi dayabata , IRFAN kai ba 'karamin ta bashi kukan nata yakeyi ba ,shi yanzu baimasan kukan mitake ba ,kuka take har da jan majina ,zaune ya tashi yakuna wutar dakin yace “dan ALLAH dai ki barni na huta haba ki barni nasamu nutsuwa man ”ya fada Yana dafe kanshi „



Nasreen ma mikewa tayi tana shasheka harda majina tace “to bakai bane inata baka Hakuri kaki ka hakura ai nace nafasa cire cikin ko ,ba zan cire ba tunda kana so Ama fushin na menene ?”ta fada tana goge hawayn ta ,wani Irin farin ciki ne yaji tunda tace ba za ta zubar ba ai shikenan yaji dadi sosai kamar yatika rawa ,Ama ya dake yace “kinga malama ki shafamin lafiya zanje Can wajen Mufida Ita tasan darja Y'a Y'a zanje Can tayita haifamin Kona Auro wata bashi kenan ba kekuma sai kici karatun ko ”ya fada Yana kokarin koma kwanciyar sa „


Nasreen kai bakarau Dukan ta maganar shi tayi ba hakan yasa ta cewa “dan Allah kayi Hakuri wallahi zanci gaba da haifama KO dozin kake so Ama ka zauna damu mu mu biyu daga ni sai Anty Mufida zamuna haifama KO dozin dozin ne dubu dubu Malala gashin rago”tafada tana rungume shi hade da fashewa da wani Irin kuka„„


IRFAN kai saura kadan dariya ta subuce masa Ama ya dake ya share yace “a'a ni dai na yafe gobe zanje dake nazubar da cikin ”ya fada Yana kokarin banbareta ,gam tarike shi tace “wallahi nidai a'a ainace ma nafasa kayi Hakuri ”ganin ta saduda sosai yasashi fadun “to shikenan kimin alkawarin ba zaki saké ba kinji ”rungume shi tayi kam kamar wani zai kwace mata shi tace “nayima alkawarin ba zan saké ba ”yaji dadi sosai yasan wanan duk aikin Mufida ne darajar ta ce yaji takaru sosai a ranshi da haka ya fara canja salon dan dama a matse yake dauriya ce kawai yakeyi „„


Da haka ya samu ya lalatse ta yabiya bukatar sa Wanda yaji ba karamin sugar Takara ba ,sai wajen karfe biyu ya sarara mata wanka sukayi ya rungume abar sa Yana Sama ta Albarka ,ita dai gaskiya batajin dadin sex d'in yanzu ta rasa komiye kodan cikin ne oho„„

Haka rayuwa ta cigaba da tafiya cikin kwanciyar hankali tsananin masoyan kuma itada Mufida Zaman Mutunci suke yi sun hada kansu IRFAN bakaramin dadi yaji ba kuma yaji darjar Mufida sai dada haw hawa take agunshi , IRFAN yakai Nasreen da Mufida gidan su Nasreen dun kuma su Abba sunji dadin ganinsu ahaka albarka suksaka musu sosai Mama taji dadi kuma ta fahimci diyar tata cikine da Ita „


Wurin komawa ya biya da Mufida yabar Nasreen d'un ta wuni dan yasan sunyi kewar ta sosai ,kuma yasan ai Suna bukatar tattaunawa da yar tasu hakan yasashi tafiya Ita ma Mufida ta bishi da haka suka barota bayan Magrub zai zo ya tafi da Ita „„.....✍️
